33: const natural = new Intl.Collator(undefined, { numeric: true, sensitivity: 'base' });
34: 
35: export function naturalOrder(a: string, b: string): number {
36:   const tokens = (s: string) =>
37:     s
38:       .normalize('NFC')
39:       .toLowerCase()
40:       .replace(/\b(chapter|ch)\.?\s*(?=\d)/g, 'ch ')
41:       .replace(/\b(volume|vol)\.?\s*(?=\d)/g, 'vol ')
42:       .match(/\d+(?:\.\d+)?|[^\d]+/g) || [];
43:   const category = (s: string) =>
44:     /^(prologue|opening)\b/i.test(s) ? -1 : /^(extra|bonus|special|epilogue)\b/i.test(s) ? 1 : 0;
45:   const categoryDiff = category(a) - category(b);
46:   if (categoryDiff) return categoryDiff;
47:   const aa = tokens(a),
48:     bb = tokens(b);
49:   for (let i = 0; i < Math.min(aa.length, bb.length); i++) {
50:     const x = aa[i],
51:       y = bb[i],
52:       nx = Number(x),
53:       ny = Number(y);
54:     const both = x.trim() !== '' && y.trim() !== '' && Number.isFinite(nx) && Number.isFinite(ny);
55:     const result = both ? nx - ny : natural.compare(x, y);
56:     if (result) return result;
57:   }
58:   return aa.length - bb.length || natural.compare(a, b);
59: }
60: 
61: export async function naturalSortPaths(paths: string[]): Promise<string[]> {
62:   if (paths.length <= 1) return paths;
63:   if (desktopAPI.naturalSortPaths) {
64:     try {
65:       return await desktopAPI.naturalSortPaths(paths);
66:     } catch {
67:       // fallback
68:     }
69:   }
70:   return [...paths].sort(naturalOrder);
71: }

113: export class DesktopHandle {
114:   readonly kind = 'file';
115:   constructor(
116:     readonly name: string,
117:     readonly absolutePath: string,
118:     readonly size: number,
119:     readonly modified: number,
120:     readonly width?: number | null,
121:     readonly height?: number | null,
122:   ) { }
123: 
124:   get absPath(): string {
125:     return this.absolutePath;
126:   }
127: 
128:   get lastModified(): number {
129:     return this.modified;
130:   }
131: 
132:   async getFile(): Promise<File> {
133:     if (!desktopAPI.isDesktop) throw new Error('Desktop API not available.');
134:     const buffer = await desktopAPI.readFile(this.absolutePath);
135:     if (!buffer) {
136:       throw new Error(`Failed to read file from desktop path: ${this.absolutePath}`);
137:     }
138:     return new File([buffer], this.name, {
139:       lastModified: this.modified,
140:     });
141:   }

168:   for (const source of sources) {

175: export async function fromDesktopFolder(
176:   folderPath: string,
177:   options: ScanOptions = {},
178: ): Promise<Source> {
179:   if (!desktopAPI.isDesktop) throw new Error('Desktop API not available.');
180:   void desktopAPI.recordOpenedRoot?.(folderPath);
181:   const folderName = folderPath.split(/[\\/]/).at(-1) ?? folderPath;
182:   const source: Source = { title: folderName, localPath: folderPath, files: new Map(), issues: [] };
183: 
184:   if (desktopAPI.discoverLibrary) {
185:     try {
186:       const discovery = await withinTime(
187:         desktopAPI.discoverLibrary(folderPath, {
188:           signal: options.signal,
189:           onProgress: (prog) => {
190:             options.update?.({
191:               phase: 'discover',
192:               done: prog.done,
193:               total: prog.total,
194:               title: folderName,
195:             });
196:             if (prog.total > 0) {
197:               desktopAPI.setProgressBar(prog.done / prog.total);
198:             }
199:           },
200:         }),
201:         limits.ioTimeoutMs * 5,
202:         'Discovering library',
203:         options.signal,
204:       );
205: 
206:       if (discovery && discovery.titles.length > 0) {
207:         source.discoveredTitles = discovery.titles;
208:         let discovered = 0;
209:         for (const title of discovery.titles) {
210:           for (const ch of title.chapters) {
211:             for (const p of ch.pages) {
212:               alive(options.signal);
213:               const relPath =
214:                 discovery.titles.length > 1 ? `${title.title}/${p.relativePath}` : p.relativePath;
215:               assertPathLimits(relPath);
216:               assertAtMost(++discovered, limits.discoveryEntries, 'Discovered entries');
217:               assertAtMost(
218:                 source.files.size + 1,
219:                 limits.pagesPerTitle,
220:                 'Image candidates per title',
221:               );
222:               source.files.set(
223:                 relPath,
224:                 new DesktopHandle(
225:                   p.name,
226:                   p.absolutePath,
227:                   p.size,
228:                   p.modified,
229:                   p.width,
230:                   p.height,
231:                 ) as unknown as FileSystemFileHandle,
232:               );
233:             }
234:           }
235:         }
236:         options.update?.({
237:           phase: 'discover',
238:           done: source.files.size,
239:           total: source.files.size,
240:           title: folderName,
241:         });
242:         return source;
243:       }
244:     } catch (e) {
245:       if (e instanceof DOMException && e.name === 'AbortError') throw e;
246:       if (e instanceof LimitError) throw e;
247:       console.warn('Native discoverLibrary failed, falling back to listFolder:', e);
248:     }
249:   }
250: 
251:   const entries: FolderEntry[] = await withinTime(
252:     desktopAPI.listFolder(folderPath, {
253:       signal: options.signal,
254:       onProgress: (prog) => {
255:         options.update?.({
256:           phase: 'discover',
257:           done: prog.done,
258:           total: prog.total,
259:           title: folderName,
260:         });
261:         if (prog.total > 0) {
262:           desktopAPI.setProgressBar(prog.done / prog.total);
263:         }
264:       },
265:     }),
266:     limits.ioTimeoutMs * 5,
267:     'Listing folder',
268:     options.signal,
269:   );
270: 
271:   let discovered = 0;
272:   for (const entry of entries) {
273:     alive(options.signal);
274:     const relPath = normalizeRelativePath(entry.relativePath);
275:     if (ignored(relPath) || !imageFile({ name: relPath, type: '' })) continue;
276:     try {
277:       assertPathLimits(relPath);
278:       assertAtMost(++discovered, limits.discoveryEntries, 'Discovered entries');
279:       assertAtMost(source.files.size + 1, limits.pagesPerTitle, 'Image candidates per title');
280: 
281:       source.files.set(
282:         relPath,
283:         new DesktopHandle(
284:           relPath.split('/').at(-1) ?? relPath,
285:           entry.absolutePath,
286:           entry.size,
287:           entry.modified,
288:           entry.width,
289:           entry.height,
290:         ) as unknown as FileSystemFileHandle,
291:       );
292:     } catch (e) {
293:       if (e instanceof LimitError) throw e;
294:       source.issues.push({ path: relPath, reason: errorMessage(e) });
295:     }
296:     if (discovered % 256 === 0) {
297:       options.update?.({
298:         phase: 'discover',
299:         done: source.files.size,
300:         total: 0,
301:         title: folderName,
302:       });
303:     }
304:   }
305: 
306:   options.update?.({
307:     phase: 'discover',
308:     done: source.files.size,
309:     total: source.files.size,
310:     title: folderName,
311:   });
312: 
313:   return source;
314: }

331: export async function splitSource(source: Source): Promise<Source[]> {
332:   assertSourceLimits(source);
333:   if (source.discoveredTitles && source.discoveredTitles.length > 1) {
334:     const splitTitles: Source[] = [];
335:     for (const dt of source.discoveredTitles) {
336:       const s: Source = {
337:         title: dt.title,
338:         localPath: dt.rootPath,
339:         files: new Map(),
340:         issues: [],
341:       };
342:       for (const ch of dt.chapters) {
343:         for (const p of ch.pages) {
344:           s.files.set(
345:             p.relativePath,
346:             new DesktopHandle(
347:               p.name,
348:               p.absolutePath,
349:               p.size,
350:               p.modified,
351:               p.width,
352:               p.height,
353:             ) as unknown as FileSystemFileHandle,
354:           );
355:         }
356:       }
357:       splitTitles.push(s);
358:       assertAtMost(splitTitles.length, limits.titles, 'Selected titles');
359:     }
360:     return splitTitles.length > 0 ? splitTitles : [source];
361:   }
362: 
363:   const result = new Map<string, Source>();
364:   const sep = source.localPath?.includes('\\') ? '\\' : '/';
365:   for (const [path, file] of source.files) {
366:     const parts = path.split('/');
367:     const isSub = parts.length > 1;
368:     const title = isSub ? parts.shift()! : `${source.title} — root pages`;
369:     let s = result.get(title);
370:     if (!s) {
371:       const childLocalPath =
372:         source.localPath && isSub
373:           ? `${source.localPath.replace(/[\\/]+$/, '')}${sep}${title}`
374:           : source.localPath;
375:       s = {
376:         title,
377:         localPath: childLocalPath,
378:         files: new Map(),
379:         issues: [],
380:       };
381:       result.set(title, s);
382:       assertAtMost(result.size, limits.titles, 'Selected titles');
383:     }
384:     s.files.set(parts.join('/'), file);
385:   }
386:   return result.size > 0 ? [...result.values()] : [source];
387: }

389: export function chapterGroups(source: Source): Map<string, number> {
390:   const groups = new Map<string, number>();
391:   for (const path of source.files.keys()) {
392:     const parts = path.split('/');
393:     const name = parts.length === 1 ? 'Root pages' : parts.slice(0, -1).join(' / ');
394:     if (parts.length === 1 && /^(thumbnail|cover)\./i.test(path) && source.files.size > 1) continue;
395:     groups.set(name, (groups.get(name) || 0) + 1);
396:   }
397:   return groups;
398: }
399: 
400: export function chapterPreviews(source: Source): ChapterPreview[] {
401:   const groups = chapterGroups(source);
402:   return Array.from(groups.entries(), ([name, pageCount]) => ({ name, pageCount }));
403: }

405: export async function pooled<T>(
406:   items: T[],
407:   limitOrFn: number | ((item: T) => Promise<void>),
408:   fnOrSignal?: ((item: T) => Promise<void>) | AbortSignal,
409:   signal?: AbortSignal,
410: ): Promise<void> {
411:   const limit = typeof limitOrFn === 'number' ? limitOrFn : 4;
412:   const fn =
413:     typeof limitOrFn === 'function' ? limitOrFn : (fnOrSignal as (item: T) => Promise<void>);
414:   const sig = fnOrSignal instanceof AbortSignal ? fnOrSignal : signal;
415: 
416:   if (items.length === 0) return;
417:   const poolLimit = Math.max(1, Math.min(limit, items.length));
418:   let next = 0;
419: 
420:   const workers = Array.from({ length: poolLimit }, async () => {
421:     while (next < items.length) {
422:       if (sig?.aborted) throw new DOMException('Operation cancelled', 'AbortError');
423:       const idx = next++;
424:       await fn(items[idx]);
425:     }
426:   });
427: 
428:   await Promise.all(workers);
429: }

431: export async function indexSource(
432:   source: Source,
433:   options: ScanOptions = {},
434:   previous?: Manga,
435:   sort: Manga['sort'] = 'natural',
436: ) {
437:   assertSourceLimits(source);
438:   if (previous) stopBackground(previous.id);
439: 
440:   if (desktopAPI.isDesktop && desktopAPI.indexTitle && source.localPath) {
441:     alive(options.signal);
442:     const confirmedTitle = source.title?.trim() || undefined;
443:     const jobId = `index-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2)}`;
444: 
445:     let abortHandler: (() => void) | undefined;
446:     if (options.signal) {
447:       abortHandler = () => {
448:         void desktopAPI.cancelScan?.(jobId);
449:       };
450:       options.signal.addEventListener('abort', abortHandler, { once: true });
451:     }
452: 
453:     try {
454:       const dto = await desktopAPI.indexTitle(
455:         source.localPath,
456:         {
457:           sort,
458:           previous,
459:           targetId: previous?.id,
460:           title: confirmedTitle,
461:           jobId,
462:           signal: options.signal,
463:         },
464:         (progress) => {
465:           alive(options.signal);
466:           options.update?.({
467:             phase: progress.phase as 'discover' | 'index',
468:             done: progress.done,
469:             total: progress.total,
470:             title: confirmedTitle || progress.title,
471:           });
472:           if (progress.total > 0) {
473:             desktopAPI.setProgressBar(Math.min(1, Math.max(0, progress.done / progress.total)));
474:           }
475:         },
476:       );
477:       alive(options.signal);
478: 
479:       const m: Manga = {
480:         id: dto.id,
481:         title: confirmedTitle || dto.title,
482:         slug: dto.slug,
483:         localPath: dto.localPath,
484:         lastChapter: dto.lastChapter,
485:         addedAt: dto.addedAt,
486:         updatedAt: dto.updatedAt,
487:         revision: dto.revision,
488:         sort: dto.sort as 'natural' | 'name' | 'reverse',
489:         chapters: (dto.chapters || []).map((c: any) => ({
490:           ...(c.id ? { id: c.id } : {}),
491:           name: c.name,
492:           read: c.read,
493:           position: c.position,
494:           bookmarks: c.bookmarks,
495:           pages: (c.pages || []).map((p: any) => ({
496:             name: p.name,
497:             path: p.path,
498:             width: p.width,
499:             height: p.height,
500:             size: p.size,
501:             modified: p.modified,
502:             dimensionsPending: p.dimensionsPending,
503:           })),
504:         })),
505:       };
506: 
507:       alive(options.signal);
508:       invalidateSummaryCache();
509:       invalidateBookmarkCache();
510:       clearDirHandleCache(m.id);
511:       broadcastSync({ type: 'library', id: m.id });
512: 
513:       return {
514:         m,
515:         issues: [...source.issues, ...dto.issues],
516:         reused: dto.reused,
517:       };
518:     } finally {
519:       if (abortHandler && options.signal) {
520:         options.signal.removeEventListener('abort', abortHandler);
521:       }
522:     }
523:   }
524: 
525:   throw new Error('Desktop indexing requires native indexTitle and localPath');
526: }

528: export async function prepareChapter(
529:   manga: Manga,
530:   chapterIndex: number,
531:   options: PrepareChapterOptions = {},
532: ): Promise<Issue[]> {
533:   alive(options.signal);
534:   const ch = manga.chapters[chapterIndex];
535:   if (!ch) return [];
536: 
537:   const pages = ch.pages.map((p) => ({ ...p }));
538:   const unmeasured = pages.filter((p) => p.dimensionsPending || !p.width || !p.height);
539:   if (!unmeasured.length) return [];
540: 
541:   const issues: Issue[] = [];
542:   let updated = false;
543: 
544:   if (desktopAPI.prepareChapterHeaders) {
545:     const paths = unmeasured.map((p) => p.path).filter((p): p is string => Boolean(p));
546:     if (paths.length > 0) {
547:       try {
548:         alive(options.signal);
549:         const headers = await desktopAPI.prepareChapterHeaders(paths, manga.id);
550:         alive(options.signal);
551:         const headerMap = new Map<string, PageHeader>();
552:         for (const h of headers) {
553:           headerMap.set(h.path, h);
554:         }
555:         let done = 0;
556:         for (const p of unmeasured) {
557:           const found = p.path ? headerMap.get(p.path) : undefined;
558:           if (found && found.width > 0 && found.height > 0) {
559:             assertImageDimensions(found.width, found.height);
560:             p.width = found.width;
561:             p.height = found.height;
562:             p.size = found.size;
563:             p.modified = found.modified;
564:             p.dimensionsPending = false;
565:             updated = true;
566:             done++;
567:             options.update?.({
568:               phase: 'index',
569:               done,
570:               total: unmeasured.length,
571:               title: manga.title,
572:             });
573:           }
574:         }
575:       } catch (err) {
576:         if (err instanceof DOMException && err.name === 'AbortError') throw err;
577:         console.warn('Native prepareChapterHeaders failed:', err);
578:       }
579:     }
580:   }
581: 
582:   const stillPending = pages.filter((p) => p.dimensionsPending || !p.width || !p.height);
583:   if (stillPending.length > 0) {
584:     let done = unmeasured.length - stillPending.length;
585:     await pooled(
586:       stillPending,
587:       async (p) => {
588:         alive(options.signal);
589:         try {
590:           if (desktopAPI.getImageDimensions && manga.localPath && p.path) {
591:             const sep = manga.localPath.includes('\\') ? '\\' : '/';
592:             const absPath =
593:               manga.localPath.replace(/[\\/]+$/, '') + sep + p.path.split('/').join(sep);
594:             const dim = await desktopAPI.getImageDimensions(absPath);
595:             if (dim && dim.width > 0 && dim.height > 0) {
596:               assertImageDimensions(dim.width, dim.height);
597:               p.width = dim.width;
598:               p.height = dim.height;
599:               p.dimensionsPending = false;
600:               updated = true;
601:               return;
602:             }
603:           }
604:           const file = await fileFor(manga, p);
605:           alive(options.signal);
606:           if (!file.size) throw new Error('Empty file.');
607:           const size = await dimensions(file, options.signal);
608:           assertImageDimensions(size.width, size.height);
609:           p.width = size.width;
610:           p.height = size.height;
611:           p.size = file.size;
612:           p.modified = file.lastModified;
613:           p.dimensionsPending = false;
614:           updated = true;
615:         } catch (err) {
616:           alive(options.signal);
617:           issues.push({
618:             path: p.path || p.name,
619:             reason: errorMessage(err),
620:           });
621:         } finally {
622:           done++;
623:           options.update?.({
624:             phase: 'index',
625:             done,
626:             total: unmeasured.length,
627:             title: manga.title,
628:           });
629:         }
630:       },
631:       options.signal,
632:     );
633:   }
634: 
635:   if (updated) {
636:     alive(options.signal);
637:     manga.chapters[chapterIndex] = { ...ch, pages };
638:     await saveManga(manga);
639:   }
640: 
641:   return issues;
642: }
643: 
644: interface BackgroundJob {
645:   id: string;
646:   revision?: number;
647:   updatedAt?: number;
648:   controller: AbortController;
649: }
650: 
651: const backgroundJobs = new Map<string, BackgroundJob>();
652: let backgroundRunning = false;
653: 
654: export function stopBackground(id: string): void {
655:   const job = backgroundJobs.get(id);
656:   if (job) {
657:     job.controller.abort();
658:     backgroundJobs.delete(id);
659:   }
660: }
661: 
662: export function stopAllBackground(): void {
663:   for (const job of backgroundJobs.values()) {
664:     job.controller.abort();
665:   }
666:   backgroundJobs.clear();
667: }
668: 
669: onMangaDelete((id) => {
670:   if (id) stopBackground(id);
671:   else stopAllBackground();
672: });
673: 
674: export function scheduleBackground(manga: Manga): void {
675:   if (
676:     backgroundJobs.has(manga.id) ||
677:     !manga.chapters.some((c) => c.pages.some((p) => p.dimensionsPending))
678:   ) {
679:     return;
680:   }
681:   if (backgroundJobs.size >= limits.queuedJobs) return;
682:   backgroundJobs.set(manga.id, {
683:     id: manga.id,
684:     revision: manga.revision,
685:     updatedAt: manga.updatedAt,
686:     controller: new AbortController(),
687:   });
688:   void drainBackground();
689: }
690: 
691: async function runDrainPass(): Promise<void> {
692:   let remaining = backgroundJobs.size;
693:   while (backgroundJobs.size && remaining-- > 0) {
694:     const entry = backgroundJobs.entries().next().value;
695:     if (!entry) break;
696:     const [id, job] = entry;
697:     try {
698:       const initial = await getManga(id);
699:       if (
700:         !initial ||
701:         initial.updatedAt !== job.updatedAt ||
702:         (job.revision !== undefined && initial.revision !== job.revision)
703:       ) {
704:         continue;
705:       }
706:       for (let i = 0; i < initial.chapters.length; i++) {
707:         alive(job.controller.signal);
708:         if (!initial.chapters[i].pages.some((p) => p.dimensionsPending)) continue;
709:         await new Promise<void>((r) => setTimeout(r, 50));
710:         alive(job.controller.signal);
711:         if (!(await access(initial))) break;
712:         const stored = await getManga(id);
713:         if (
714:           !stored ||
715:           stored.updatedAt !== job.updatedAt ||
716:           (job.revision !== undefined && stored.revision !== job.revision)
717:         ) {
718:           break;
719:         }
720:         const issues = await prepareChapter(stored, i, { signal: job.controller.signal });
721:         job.updatedAt = stored.updatedAt;
722:         job.revision = stored.revision;
723:         if (issues.length > 0 && stored.chapters[i]?.pages.some((p) => p.dimensionsPending)) {
724:           break;
725:         }
726:       }
727:     } catch {
728:       /* Interrupted jobs remain pending in saved metadata and can resume later */
729:     } finally {
730:       if (backgroundJobs.get(id) === job) {
731:         backgroundJobs.delete(id);
732:       }
733:     }
734:   }
735: }
736: 
737: async function drainBackground(): Promise<void> {
738:   if (backgroundRunning) return;
739:   backgroundRunning = true;
740:   try {
741:     await runDrainPass();
742:   } finally {
743:     backgroundRunning = false;
744:     if (backgroundJobs.size > 0) {
745:       setTimeout(() => void drainBackground(), 100);
746:     }
747:   }
748: }
749: 
750: export async function resumeBackground(): Promise<void> {
751:   try {
752:     const list = await listLibrary();
753:     for (const item of list) {
754:       const m = await getManga(item.id);
755:       if (
756:         m &&
757:         (await access(m)) &&
758:         m.chapters.some((c) => c.pages.some((p) => p.dimensionsPending))
759:       ) {
760:         scheduleBackground(m);
761:       }
762:     }
763:   } catch (err) {
764:     console.warn('Failed to resume background jobs:', err);
765:   }
766: }

187: export async function fileFor(m: Manga, p: Page): Promise<File> {
188:   if (!p.path) throw new Error('Desktop page is missing path');
189:   assertPathLimits(p.path);
190:   if (m.localPath || desktopAPI.resolvePath) {
191:     const sep = m.localPath?.includes('\\') ? '\\' : '/';
192:     const fallbackPath = m.localPath
193:       ? `${m.localPath.replace(/[\\/]+$/, '')}${sep}${p.path.split('/').join(sep)}`
194:       : p.path;
195:     const resolvedPath = desktopAPI.resolvePath
196:       ? await desktopAPI.resolvePath(m.id, p.path, m.localPath)
197:       : fallbackPath;
198:     const buffer = await withinTime(
199:       desktopAPI.readFile(resolvedPath),
200:       limits.ioTimeoutMs,
201:       'Reading file',
202:     );
203:     if (!buffer) throw new Error(`Could not read desktop file at: ${resolvedPath}`);
204:     return new File([buffer], p.name || p.path.split('/').at(-1) || 'image.webp');
205:   }
206:   const f = sessionFiles.get(m.id)?.get(p.path);
207:   if (f) {
208:     return withinTime(
209:       f instanceof File ? Promise.resolve(f) : f.getFile(),
210:       limits.ioTimeoutMs,
211:       'Reading file',
212:     );
213:   }
214:   throw new Error('Desktop manga is missing localPath');
215: }

217: export async function access(
218:   manga: Pick<Manga, 'id' | 'localPath'>,
219:   request = false,
220: ): Promise<boolean> {
221:   if (!manga) return false;
222:   if (sessionFiles.has(manga.id)) return true;
223: 
224:   const now = Date.now();
225:   if (!request) {
226:     const cached = accessCache.get(manga.id);
227:     if (cached && now < cached.expiresAt) {
228:       return cached.granted;
229:     }
230:     const inflight = accessInFlight.get(manga.id);
231:     if (inflight) return inflight;
232:   } else {
233:     accessInFlight.delete(manga.id);
234:   }
235: 
236:   const task = (async () => {
237:     try {
238:       const granted = desktopAPI.checkAccess
239:         ? await desktopAPI.checkAccess(manga.id, manga.localPath)
240:         : Boolean(manga.localPath);
241: 
242:       const finishNow = Date.now();
243:       accessCache.set(manga.id, {
244:         expiresAt: finishNow + ACCESS_CACHE_TTL_MS,
245:         timestamp: finishNow,
246:         granted,
247:       });
248:       return granted;
249:     } catch {
250:       const finishNow = Date.now();
251:       accessCache.set(manga.id, {
252:         expiresAt: finishNow + ACCESS_CACHE_TTL_MS,
253:         timestamp: finishNow,
254:         granted: false,
255:       });
256:       return false;
257:     } finally {
258:       accessInFlight.delete(manga.id);
259:     }
260:   })();
261: 
262:   if (!request) {
263:     accessInFlight.set(manga.id, task);
264:   }
265:   return task;
266: }

1: import { limits, assertAtMost, assertPathLimits, assertImageDimensions } from '../limits';
2: import type { Manga, Chapter, Page, Position, Settings, Summary } from '../model';

7: export class ValidationError extends Error {
8:   constructor(message: string) {
9:     super(message);
10:     this.name = 'ValidationError';
11:   }
12: }
13: 
14: export function isRecord(value: unknown): value is Record<string, unknown> {
15:   return typeof value === 'object' && value !== null && !Array.isArray(value);
16: }
17: 
18: const RESERVED_DOS_NAMES = new Set([
19:   'con', 'prn', 'aux', 'nul',
20:   'com1', 'com2', 'com3', 'com4', 'com5', 'com6', 'com7', 'com8', 'com9',
21:   'lpt1', 'lpt2', 'lpt3', 'lpt4', 'lpt5', 'lpt6', 'lpt7', 'lpt8', 'lpt9',
22: ]);
23: 
24: export function validateRelativePath(path: unknown): string {
25:   if (typeof path !== 'string' || !path.trim()) {
26:     throw new ValidationError('Path must be a non-empty string.');
27:   }
28:   if (path.startsWith(' ') || path.endsWith(' ')) {
29:     throw new ValidationError(`Path cannot start or end with whitespace: "${path}"`);
30:   }
31:   const trimmed = path.trim();
32:   if (trimmed.includes(':')) {
33:     throw new ValidationError(`Path cannot contain drive prefixes or colons: "${trimmed}"`);
34:   }
35:   if (trimmed.startsWith('/') || trimmed.endsWith('/')) {
36:     throw new ValidationError(`Path cannot start or end with a slash: "${trimmed}"`);
37:   }
38:   if (trimmed.startsWith('//')) {
39:     throw new ValidationError(`Path cannot start with UNC prefix: "${trimmed}"`);
40:   }
41:   if (trimmed.includes('\\')) {
42:     throw new ValidationError(`Path must use forward slashes only: "${trimmed}"`);
43:   }
44:   if (trimmed.includes('\0')) {
45:     throw new ValidationError('Path contains invalid null byte.');
46:   }
47:   if (/[\x00-\x1f\x7f-\x9f]/.test(trimmed)) {
48:     throw new ValidationError('Path contains invalid control character.');
49:   }
50:   assertPathLimits(trimmed);
51:   const segments = trimmed.split('/');
52:   for (const seg of segments) {
53:     if (!seg || seg === '.' || seg === '..') {
54:       throw new ValidationError(`Path contains invalid traversal segment: "${trimmed}"`);
55:     }
56:     if (seg.endsWith(' ') || seg.endsWith('.')) {
57:       throw new ValidationError(`Path segment cannot end with a space or dot: "${seg}"`);
58:     }
59:     const stem = seg.split('.')[0].toLowerCase();
60:     if (RESERVED_DOS_NAMES.has(stem)) {
61:       throw new ValidationError(`Path segment uses reserved device name: "${seg}"`);
62:     }
63:   }
64:   return trimmed;
65: }
66: 
67: export function matchingPosition(
68:   saved?: Position,
69:   pages?: Page[],
70:   oldPages: Page[] = [],
71: ): Position | undefined {
72:   if (!saved || !pages || !pages.length) return undefined;
73:   const path = saved.path || oldPages[saved.page]?.path;
74:   let page = path ? pages.findIndex((p) => p.path === path) : -1;
75:   if (page < 0 && path) {
76:     const name = path.split('/').at(-1);
77:     const matches = pages.map((x, i) => (x.name === name ? i : -1)).filter((i) => i >= 0);
78:     if (matches.length === 1) page = matches[0];
79:   }
80:   if (page < 0) page = Math.min(saved.page, pages.length - 1);
81:   const validIndex = Math.max(0, page);
82:   return {
83:     page: validIndex,
84:     path: pages[validIndex].path,
85:     fraction: Math.max(0, Math.min(1, Number.isFinite(saved.fraction) ? saved.fraction : 0)),
86:     ...(saved.mangaBookmark ? { mangaBookmark: true } : {}),
87:     ...(typeof saved.timestamp === 'number' && Number.isFinite(saved.timestamp)
88:       ? { timestamp: saved.timestamp }
89:       : {}),
90:   };
91: }
92: 
93: export function parsePosition(
94:   value: unknown,
95:   pages?: Page[],
96:   fallback?: Position,
97:   oldPages: Page[] = [],
98: ): Position | undefined {
99:   if (value === undefined || value === null) return fallback;
100:   if (!isRecord(value)) throw new ValidationError('Position must be an object.');
101: 
102:   const page = value.page;
103:   if (typeof page !== 'number' || !Number.isInteger(page) || page < 0) {
104:     throw new ValidationError('Position page must be a non-negative integer.');
105:   }
106: 
107:   const fraction = value.fraction;
108:   if (typeof fraction !== 'number' || !Number.isFinite(fraction)) {
109:     throw new ValidationError('Position fraction must be a finite number.');
110:   }
111:   const clampedFraction = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, fraction));
112:   const path = value.path !== undefined ? validateRelativePath(value.path) : undefined;
113: 
114:   let mangaBookmark: boolean | undefined;
115:   if ('mangaBookmark' in value && value.mangaBookmark !== undefined) {
116:     if (typeof value.mangaBookmark !== 'boolean') {
117:       throw new ValidationError('Position mangaBookmark must be a boolean.');
118:     }
119:     if (value.mangaBookmark) {
120:       mangaBookmark = true;
121:     }
122:   }
123: 
124:   let timestamp: number | undefined;
125:   if ('timestamp' in value && value.timestamp !== undefined) {
126:     if (typeof value.timestamp !== 'number' || !Number.isFinite(value.timestamp)) {
127:       throw new ValidationError('Position timestamp must be a finite number.');
128:     }
129:     timestamp = value.timestamp;
130:   }
131: 
132:   const rawPos: Position = {
133:     page,
134:     fraction: clampedFraction,
135:     ...(path ? { path } : {}),
136:     ...(mangaBookmark ? { mangaBookmark: true } : {}),
137:     ...(timestamp !== undefined ? { timestamp } : {}),
138:   };
139: 
140:   if (pages && pages.length > 0) {
141:     return matchingPosition(rawPos, pages, oldPages);
142:   }
143:   return rawPos;
144: }
145: 
146: export function parsePage(value: unknown, seenPaths: Set<string>): Page {
147:   if (!isRecord(value)) throw new ValidationError('Page record must be an object.');
148: 
149:   if (typeof value.name !== 'string' || !value.name.trim()) {
150:     throw new ValidationError('Page name must be a non-empty string.');
151:   }
152: 
153:   const path = validateRelativePath(value.path);
154:   if (seenPaths.has(path)) {
155:     throw new ValidationError(`Duplicate page path within chapter: "${path}"`);
156:   }
157:   seenPaths.add(path);
158: 
159:   const isPending = value.dimensionsPending === true;
160:   const isWidthPending = isPending && value.width === 0;
161:   const isHeightPending = isPending && value.height === 0;
162: 
163:   if (
164:     typeof value.width !== 'number' ||
165:     !Number.isInteger(value.width) ||
166:     typeof value.height !== 'number' ||
167:     !Number.isInteger(value.height) ||
168:     (value.width <= 0 && !isWidthPending) ||
169:     (value.height <= 0 && !isHeightPending)
170:   ) {
171:     throw new ValidationError('Page dimensions must be positive integer pixels.');
172:   }
173: 
174:   if (value.width > 0 && value.height > 0) {
175:     assertImageDimensions(value.width, value.height);
176:   }
177: 
178:   let size: number | undefined;
179:   if (value.size !== undefined) {
180:     if (typeof value.size !== 'number' || !Number.isInteger(value.size) || value.size < 0) {
181:       throw new ValidationError('Page size must be a non-negative integer bytes count.');
182:     }
183:     size = value.size;
184:   }
185: 
186:   let modified: number | undefined;
187:   if (value.modified !== undefined) {
188:     if (
189:       typeof value.modified !== 'number' ||
190:       !Number.isFinite(value.modified) ||
191:       value.modified < 0
192:     ) {
193:       throw new ValidationError('Page modified timestamp must be a non-negative number.');
194:     }
195:     modified = value.modified;
196:   }
197: 
198:   const dimensionsPending =
199:     typeof value.dimensionsPending === 'boolean' ? value.dimensionsPending : undefined;
200: 
201:   return {
202:     name: value.name.trim(),
203:     path,
204:     width: value.width,
205:     height: value.height,
206:     ...(size !== undefined ? { size } : {}),
207:     ...(modified !== undefined ? { modified } : {}),
208:     ...(dimensionsPending !== undefined ? { dimensionsPending } : {}),
209:   };
210: }
211: 
212: export function parseChapter(value: unknown, chapterIndex: number, oldChapter?: Chapter): Chapter {
213:   if (!isRecord(value)) {
214:     throw new ValidationError(`Chapter ${chapterIndex + 1} must be an object.`);
215:   }
216: 
217:   if (typeof value.name !== 'string' || !value.name.trim()) {
218:     throw new ValidationError(`Chapter ${chapterIndex + 1} name must be a non-empty string.`);
219:   }
220:   const name = value.name.trim();
221: 
222:   if (!Array.isArray(value.pages) || !value.pages.length) {
223:     throw new ValidationError(`Chapter "${name}" must contain at least one page.`);
224:   }
225: 
226:   const seenPaths = new Set<string>();
227:   const pages: Page[] = value.pages.map((p, idx) => {
228:     try {
229:       return parsePage(p, seenPaths);
230:     } catch (e) {
231:       throw new ValidationError(
232:         `Chapter "${name}" page ${idx + 1} is invalid: ${e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e)}`,
233:       );
234:     }
235:   });
236: 
237:   const position = value.position
238:     ? parsePosition(value.position, pages, undefined, oldChapter?.pages)
239:     : undefined;
240: 
241:   let bookmarks: Position[] | undefined;
242:   if (value.bookmarks !== undefined) {
243:     if (!Array.isArray(value.bookmarks)) {
244:       throw new ValidationError(`Chapter "${name}" bookmarks must be an array.`);
245:     }
246:     assertAtMost(value.bookmarks.length, limits.bookmarksPerChapter, 'Bookmarks per chapter');
247:     const seenBookmarks = new Set<string>();
248:     bookmarks = [];
249:     for (const b of value.bookmarks) {
250:       const parsed = parsePosition(b, pages, undefined, oldChapter?.pages);
251:       if (parsed) {
252:         const key = `${parsed.page}:${parsed.path ?? ''}:${Boolean(parsed.mangaBookmark)}`;
253:         if (!seenBookmarks.has(key)) {
254:           seenBookmarks.add(key);
255:           bookmarks.push(parsed);
256:         }
257:       }
258:     }
259:   }
260: 
261:   const id = typeof value.id === 'string' && value.id.trim() ? value.id.trim() : undefined;
262: 
263:   return {
264:     ...(id ? { id } : {}),
265:     name,
266:     read: value.read === true,
267:     pages,
268:     position,
269:     bookmarks,
270:   };
271: }
272: 
273: export function parseManga(
274:   value: unknown,
275:   oldManga?: Manga,
276:   options: { preserveHardwarePointers?: boolean } = { preserveHardwarePointers: true },
277: ): Manga {
278:   if (!isRecord(value)) {
279:     throw new ValidationError('Manga record must be an object.');
280:   }
281: 
282:   if (typeof value.id !== 'string' || !value.id.trim()) {
283:     throw new ValidationError('Manga ID must be a non-empty string.');
284:   }
285: 
286:   if (typeof value.title !== 'string' || !value.title.trim()) {
287:     throw new ValidationError('Manga title must be a non-empty string.');
288:   }
289: 
290:   if (!Array.isArray(value.chapters) || !value.chapters.length) {
291:     throw new ValidationError(`Manga "${value.title}" must contain at least one chapter.`);
292:   }
293: 
294:   assertAtMost(value.chapters.length, limits.chaptersPerTitle, 'Chapters per title');
295: 
296:   let titlePages = 0;
297:   const chapters: Chapter[] = value.chapters.map((c, idx) => {
298:     const oldChapter = oldManga?.chapters[idx];
299:     const parsed = parseChapter(c, idx, oldChapter);
300:     titlePages += parsed.pages.length;
301:     assertAtMost(titlePages, limits.pagesPerTitle, 'Pages per title');
302:     return parsed;
303:   });
304: 
305:   const lastChapter =
306:     typeof value.lastChapter === 'number' && Number.isInteger(value.lastChapter)
307:       ? Math.max(0, Math.min(chapters.length - 1, value.lastChapter))
308:       : 0;
309: 
310:   const now = Date.now();
311:   const addedAt =
312:     typeof value.addedAt === 'number' && Number.isFinite(value.addedAt) && value.addedAt > 0
313:       ? value.addedAt
314:       : now;
315: 
316:   const updatedAt =
317:     typeof value.updatedAt === 'number' && Number.isFinite(value.updatedAt) && value.updatedAt > 0
318:       ? value.updatedAt
319:       : now;
320: 
321:   const sort =
322:     value.sort === 'name' || value.sort === 'reverse' || value.sort === 'natural'
323:       ? value.sort
324:       : undefined;
325: 
326:   const revision =
327:     typeof value.revision === 'number' && Number.isFinite(value.revision) && value.revision >= 0
328:       ? value.revision
329:       : 1;
330: 
331:   const title = value.title.trim();
332:   const slug =
333:     typeof value.slug === 'string' && value.slug.trim() ? value.slug.trim() : folderSlug(title);
334: 
335:   const localPath =
336:     options.preserveHardwarePointers &&
337:       typeof value.localPath === 'string' &&
338:       value.localPath.trim()
339:       ? value.localPath.trim()
340:       : undefined;
341: 
342:   const handle =
343:     options.preserveHardwarePointers && value.handle && typeof value.handle === 'object'
344:       ? (value.handle as Manga['handle'])
345:       : undefined;
346: 
347:   const cover =
348:     options.preserveHardwarePointers && value.cover instanceof Blob ? value.cover : undefined;
349: 
350:   return {
351:     id: value.id.trim(),
352:     title,
353:     slug,
354:     chapters,
355:     lastChapter,
356:     addedAt,
357:     updatedAt,
358:     revision,
359:     ...(sort ? { sort } : {}),
360:     ...(localPath ? { localPath } : {}),
361:     ...(handle ? { handle } : {}),
362:     ...(cover ? { cover } : {}),
363:   };
364: }

366: export function parseSummary(
367:   value: unknown,
368:   options: { preserveHardwarePointers?: boolean } = {},
369: ): Summary | undefined {

395:       Number.isInteger(value.lastChapter) &&
396:       value.lastChapter >= 0
397:       ? Math.max(0, Math.min(Math.max(0, chapterCount - 1), value.lastChapter))
398:       : 0;
399: 
400:   const lastChapterName = typeof value.lastChapterName === 'string' ? value.lastChapterName : '';
401:   const hasProgress = typeof value.hasProgress === 'boolean' ? value.hasProgress : false;
402:

404:   const indexedAt =
405:     typeof value.indexedAt === 'number' && Number.isFinite(value.indexedAt) && value.indexedAt > 0
406:       ? value.indexedAt
407:       : now;
408:

410:     typeof value.addedAt === 'number' && Number.isFinite(value.addedAt) && value.addedAt > 0

415:     typeof value.updatedAt === 'number' && Number.isFinite(value.updatedAt) && value.updatedAt > 0

420:     typeof value.revision === 'number' && Number.isFinite(value.revision) && value.revision >= 0

425:     typeof value.localPath === 'string' && value.localPath.trim()

427:       : undefined;

432:       : undefined;

435:     value.sort === 'name' || value.sort === 'reverse' || value.sort === 'natural'

437:       : undefined;

458: export function parseSettings(value: unknown): Settings {

493:     typeof s.resumeDelay === 'number' && Number.isFinite(s.resumeDelay)

524:     typeof s.confirmDelete === 'boolean' ? s.confirmDelete : (defaults.confirmDelete ?? false);

574: export function validateBackup(value: unknown): { manga: Manga[]; settings: Settings } {
575:   if (typeof value === 'string') {
576:     try {
577:       value = JSON.parse(value);
578:     } catch {
579:       throw new ValidationError('Backup must be valid JSON');
580:     }
581:   }
582:   if (!isRecord(value)) {
583:     throw new ValidationError('Backup must be an object.');
584:   }
585: 
586:   if (value.version !== 1 && value.version !== 2) {
587:     throw new ValidationError('Invalid backup version. Supported versions: 1, 2.');
588:   }
589: 
590:   if (!Array.isArray(value.manga)) {
591:     throw new ValidationError('Backup manga list must be an array.');
592:   }
593: 
594:   assertAtMost(value.manga.length, limits.titles, 'Backup titles');
595: 
596:   const seenIds = new Set<string>();
597:   let totalBackupPages = 0;
598: 
599:   const manga: Manga[] = value.manga.map((rawManga, index) => {
600:     if (!isRecord(rawManga) || typeof rawManga.id !== 'string') {
601:       throw new ValidationError(`Manga item ${index + 1} has an invalid or missing ID.`);
602:     }
603:     const id = rawManga.id.trim();
604:     if (!id) {
605:       throw new ValidationError(`Manga item ${index + 1} has an invalid or missing ID.`);
606:     }
607:     if (seenIds.has(id)) {
608:       throw new ValidationError(`Duplicate manga ID found in backup: "${id}"`);
609:     }
610:     seenIds.add(id);
611: 
612:     const parsed = parseManga(rawManga, undefined, { preserveHardwarePointers: false });
613:     for (const ch of parsed.chapters) {
614:       totalBackupPages += ch.pages.length;
615:       assertAtMost(totalBackupPages, limits.backupPages, 'Backup pages');
616:     }
617:     return parsed;
618:   });
619: 
620:   const settings = parseSettings(value.settings);
621: 
622:   return {
623:     manga,
624:     settings,
625:   };
626: }

31: function generateUUID(): string {
32:   if (typeof crypto.randomUUID === 'function') return crypto.randomUUID();
33:   const b = crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(16));
34:   b[6] = (b[6]! & 0x0f) | 0x40;
35:   b[8] = (b[8]! & 0x3f) | 0x80;
36:   const h = Array.from(b, (x) => x.toString(16).padStart(2, '0')).join('');
37:   return `${h.slice(0, 8)}-${h.slice(8, 12)}-${h.slice(12, 16)}-${h.slice(16, 20)}-${h.slice(20)}`;
38: }

40: const natural = new Intl.Collator(undefined, { numeric: true, sensitivity: 'base' });
41: const lexical = new Intl.Collator(undefined, { sensitivity: 'base' });

43: export function naturalOrder(a: string, b: string): number {
44:   const tokens = (s: string) =>
45:     s
46:       .normalize('NFC')
47:       .toLowerCase()
48:       .replace(/\b(chapter|ch)\.?\s*(?=\d)/g, 'ch ')
49:       .replace(/\b(volume|vol)\.?\s*(?=\d)/g, 'vol ')
50:       .match(/\d+(?:\.\d+)?|[^\d]+/g) || [];
51:   const category = (s: string) =>
52:     /^(prologue|opening)\b/i.test(s) ? -1 : /^(extra|bonus|special|epilogue)\b/i.test(s) ? 1 : 0;
53:   const categoryDiff = category(a) - category(b);
54:   if (categoryDiff) return categoryDiff;
55:   const aa = tokens(a),
56:     bb = tokens(b);
57:   for (let i = 0; i < Math.min(aa.length, bb.length); i++) {
58:     const x = aa[i],
59:       y = bb[i],
60:       nx = Number(x),
61:       ny = Number(y);
62:     const both = x.trim() !== '' && y.trim() !== '' && Number.isFinite(nx) && Number.isFinite(ny);
63:     const result = both ? nx - ny : natural.compare(x, y);
64:     if (result) return result;
65:   }
66:   return aa.length - bb.length || natural.compare(a, b);
67: }

70:   if (paths.length <= 1) return paths;
71:   if (desktopAPI.isDesktop && desktopAPI.naturalSortPaths) {
72:     return await desktopAPI.naturalSortPaths(paths);
73:   }
74:   return [...paths].sort(naturalOrder);
75: }

195: export function scanImage(entry: SourceFile, signal?: AbortSignal) {
196:   let task = scanned.get(entry);
197:   if (!task) {
198:     task = (async () => {
199:       alive(signal);
200:       const file = await readFile(entry);
201:       if (!file.size) throw new Error('Empty file.');
202:       const size = await dimensions(file, signal);
203:       alive(signal);
204:       return { file, ...size };
205:     })();
206:     scanned.set(entry, task);
207:     void task.catch(() => {
208:       if (scanned.get(entry) === task) scanned.delete(entry);
209:     });
210:   }
211:   return task;
212: }

227:       sort === 'name'
228:         ? lexical.compare
229:         : sort === 'reverse'
230:           ? (a: string, b: string) => naturalOrder(b, a)
231:           : naturalOrder;
232:     let chapterKeys = [...chapterGroups(source).keys()];
233:     if (desktopAPI.isDesktop && desktopAPI.naturalSortPaths && sort !== 'name') {
234:       chapterKeys = await desktopAPI.naturalSortPaths(chapterKeys);
235:       if (sort === 'reverse') chapterKeys.reverse();
236:     } else {
237:       chapterKeys.sort(compare);
238:     }
239:     const chapters = new Set(chapterKeys.slice(0, limits.warmChapters));
240:     const entries = [...source.files].filter(([path, entry]) => {
241:       if (entry instanceof DesktopHandle && entry.width && entry.height) return false;
242:       return chapters.has(
243:         path.includes('/') ? path.split('/').slice(0, -1).join(' / ') : 'Root pages',
244:       );
245:     });
246:     await pooled(
247:       entries,
248:       async ([, entry]) => {
249:         try {
250:           await scanImage(entry, options.signal);
251:         } catch (e) {
252:           alive(options.signal);
253:           if (e instanceof DOMException && e.name === 'AbortError') {
254:             scanned.delete(entry);
255:             try {
256:               await scanImage(entry, options.signal);
257:             } catch {
258:               alive(options.signal);
259:             }
260:           }
261:         }
262:       },
263:       options.signal,
264:     );
265:   }
266: }
267: 
268: function assertSourceLimits(source: Source) {
269:   assertAtMost(source.files.size, limits.pagesPerTitle, 'Image candidates per title');
270:   for (const path of source.files.keys()) assertPathLimits(path);
271:   assertAtMost(chapterGroups(source).size, limits.chaptersPerTitle, 'Chapters per title');
272: }
273: 
274: function assertSourcesLimits(sources: Source[]) {
275:   assertAtMost(sources.length, limits.titles, 'Selected titles');
276:   let total = 0;
277:   for (const source of sources) {
278:     total += source.files.size;
279:     assertAtMost(total, limits.discoveryEntries, 'Selected image candidates');
280:     assertSourceLimits(source);
281:   }
282: }
283: 
284: async function* directoryEntries(dir: Directory, signal?: AbortSignal) {
285:   const iterator = dir.values();
286:   try {
287:     for (let count = 0; count <= limits.discoveryEntries; count++) {
288:       alive(signal);
289:       const result = await withinTime(
290:         iterator.next(),
291:         limits.ioTimeoutMs,
292:         'Reading folder',
293:         signal,
294:       );
295:       if (result.done) return;
296:       assertAtMost(count + 1, limits.discoveryEntries, 'Folder entries');
297:       yield result.value;
298:     }
299:   } finally {
300:     if (iterator.return)
301:       await withinTime(iterator.return(), limits.ioTimeoutMs, 'Closing folder iterator');
302:   }
303: }
304: 
305: export async function fromHandle(handle: Directory, options: ScanOptions = {}): Promise<Source> {
306:   if (typeof __DESKTOP_BUILD__ !== 'undefined' && __DESKTOP_BUILD__) {
307:     throw new Error('fromHandle is not supported in desktop builds');
308:   }
309:   const source: Source = { title: handle.name, handle, files: new Map(), issues: [] };
310:   let count = 0;
311:   const chapterNames = new Set<string>();
312:   const queue: SourceFile[] = [];
313:   const tasks = new Set<Promise<void>>();
314:   let discovered = 0;
315:   let active = 0;
316:   const pump = () => {
317:     while (active < getDecodeConcurrency() && queue.length && !options.signal?.aborted) {
318:       const entry = queue.shift()!;
319:       active++;
320:       const task = scanImage(entry, options.signal)
321:         .then(
322:           () => { },
323:           () => { },
324:         )
325:         .finally(() => {
326:           active--;
327:           tasks.delete(task);
328:           pump();
329:         });
330:       tasks.add(task);
331:     }
332:   };
333:   const add = async (path: string, entry: SourceFile) => {
334:     assertPathLimits(path);
335:     assertAtMost(
336:       source.files.size + (source.files.has(path) ? 0 : 1),
337:       limits.pagesPerTitle,
338:       'Image candidates per title',
339:     );
340:     source.files.set(path, entry);
341:     const chapter = path.includes('/') ? path.split('/').slice(0, -1).join(' / ') : 'Root pages';
342:     if (path.includes('/') || !/^(thumbnail|cover)\./i.test(path)) chapterNames.add(chapter);
343:     assertAtMost(chapterNames.size, limits.chaptersPerTitle, 'Chapters per title');
344:     if ([...chapterNames].sort(naturalOrder).slice(0, limits.warmChapters).includes(chapter)) {
345:       while (queue.length >= limits.queuedJobs) {
346:         alive(options.signal);
347:         await Promise.race(tasks);
348:       }
349:       alive(options.signal);
350:       queue.push(entry);
351:       pump();
352:     }
353:   };
354: 
355:   interface StackFrame {
356:     dir: Directory;
357:     prefix: string;
358:     depth: number;
359:   }
360:   const stack: StackFrame[] = [{ dir: handle, prefix: '', depth: 0 }];
361:   let lastYield = performance.now();
362: 
363:   try {
364:     while (stack.length > 0) {
365:       alive(options.signal);
366:       const { dir, prefix, depth } = stack.pop()!;
367:       if (depth > limits.directoryDepth) {
368:         source.issues.push({
369:           path: prefix,
370:           reason: `Folder nesting exceeds ${limits.directoryDepth} levels.`,
371:         });
372:         continue;
373:       }
374:       try {
375:         const entries: FileSystemHandle[] = [];
376:         for await (const entry of directoryEntries(dir, options.signal)) {
377:           alive(options.signal);
378:           assertAtMost(++discovered, limits.discoveryEntries, 'Discovered entries');
379:           assertAtMost(
380:             (prefix + entry.name).length,
381:             limits.pathChars,
382:             'Relative path length (characters)',
383:           );
384:           if (!ignored(prefix + entry.name)) entries.push(entry);
385:         }
386:         entries.sort((a, b) => naturalOrder(a.name, b.name));
387: 
388:         for (let i = entries.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
389:           const entry = entries[i];
390:           const path = prefix + entry.name;
391:           if (entry.kind === 'directory') {
392:             stack.push({ dir: entry as Directory, prefix: path + '/', depth: depth + 1 });
393:           }
394:         }
395: 
396:         for (const entry of entries) {
397:           if (entry.kind === 'directory') continue;
398:           alive(options.signal);
399:           const path = prefix + entry.name;
400:           try {
401:             if (imageFile({ name: entry.name, type: '' }))
402:               await add(path, entry as FileSystemFileHandle);
403:             else {
404:               const file = await readFile(entry as FileSystemFileHandle);
405:               if (imageFile(file)) await add(path, entry as FileSystemFileHandle);
406:             }
407:           } catch (e) {
408:             alive(options.signal);
409:             if (e instanceof LimitError) throw e;
410:             source.issues.push({ path, reason: errorMessage(e) });
411:           }
412:           count++;
413:           const now = performance.now();
414:           if (count % 16 === 0 || now - lastYield >= 10) {
415:             lastYield = now;
416:             options.update?.({ phase: 'discover', done: count, total: 0, title: handle.name });
417:             await tick();
418:           }
419:         }
420:       } catch (e) {
421:         alive(options.signal);
422:         if (e instanceof LimitError) throw e;
423:         source.issues.push({ path: prefix || handle.name, reason: errorMessage(e) });
424:       }
425:     }
426: 
427:     while (active || queue.length) {
428:       alive(options.signal);
429:       await Promise.all(tasks);
430:     }
431:     await warmSources([source], options);
432:     alive(options.signal);
433:     options.update?.({ phase: 'discover', done: count, total: count, title: handle.name });
434:     return source;
435:   } catch (e) {
436:     queue.length = 0;
437:     await Promise.all(tasks);
438:     discardScan([source]);
439:     throw e;
440:   }
441: }
442: 
443: export function fromFiles(list: File[]): Source[] {
444:   if (typeof __DESKTOP_BUILD__ !== 'undefined' && __DESKTOP_BUILD__) return [];
445:   assertAtMost(list.length, limits.discoveryEntries, 'Selected files');
446:   const groups = new Map<string, Source>();
447:   for (const f of list) {
448:     const parts = (f.webkitRelativePath || f.name).split('/');
449:     const title = parts.length > 1 ? parts.shift()! : 'Selected images';
450:     const path = parts.join('/');
451:     if (ignored(path) || !imageFile(f)) continue;
452:     assertPathLimits(path);
453:     const group = groups.get(title) || { title, files: new Map(), issues: [] };
454:     group.files.set(path, f);
455:     groups.set(title, group);
456:     assertAtMost(group.files.size, limits.pagesPerTitle, 'Image candidates per title');
457:     assertAtMost(groups.size, limits.titles, 'Selected titles');
458:   }
459:   const sources = [...groups.values()];
460:   sources.forEach(assertSourceLimits);
461:   return sources;
462: }
463: 
464: export async function fromDesktopFolder(
465:   folderPath: string,
466:   options: ScanOptions = {},
467: ): Promise<Source> {
468:   if (!desktopAPI.isDesktop) throw new Error('Desktop API not available.');
469:   void desktopAPI.recordOpenedRoot?.(folderPath);
470:   const folderName = folderPath.split(/[\\/]/).at(-1) ?? folderPath;
471:   const source: Source = { title: folderName, localPath: folderPath, files: new Map(), issues: [] };
472: 
473:   if (desktopAPI.discoverLibrary) {
474:     try {
475:       const discovery = await withinTime(
476:         desktopAPI.discoverLibrary(folderPath, {
477:           signal: options.signal,
478:           onProgress: (prog) => {
479:             options.update?.({
480:               phase: 'discover',
481:               done: prog.done,
482:               total: prog.total,
483:               title: folderName,
484:             });
485:             if (prog.total > 0) {
486:               desktopAPI.setProgressBar(prog.done / prog.total);
487:             }
488:           },
489:         }),
490:         limits.ioTimeoutMs * 5,
491:         'Discovering library',
492:         options.signal,
493:       );
494: 
495:       if (discovery && discovery.titles.length > 0) {
496:         source.discoveredTitles = discovery.titles;
497:         let discovered = 0;
498:         for (const title of discovery.titles) {
499:           for (const ch of title.chapters) {
500:             for (const p of ch.pages) {
501:               alive(options.signal);
502:               const relPath =
503:                 discovery.titles.length > 1 ? `${title.title}/${p.relativePath}` : p.relativePath;
504:               assertPathLimits(relPath);
505:               assertAtMost(++discovered, limits.discoveryEntries, 'Discovered entries');
506:               assertAtMost(
507:                 source.files.size + 1,
508:                 limits.pagesPerTitle,
509:                 'Image candidates per title',
510:               );
511:               source.files.set(
512:                 relPath,
513:                 new DesktopHandle(
514:                   p.name,
515:                   p.absolutePath,
516:                   p.size,
517:                   p.modified,
518:                   p.width,
519:                   p.height,
520:                 ) as unknown as FileSystemFileHandle,
521:               );
522:             }
523:           }
524:         }
525:         options.update?.({
526:           phase: 'discover',
527:           done: source.files.size,
528:           total: source.files.size,
529:           title: folderName,
530:         });
531:         return source;
532:       }
533:     } catch (e) {
534:       if (e instanceof DOMException && e.name === 'AbortError') throw e;
535:       if (e instanceof LimitError) throw e;
536:     }
537:   }
538: 
539:   let entries: FolderEntry[];
540:   try {
541:     entries = await withinTime(
542:       desktopAPI.listFolder(folderPath, {
543:         signal: options.signal,
544:         onProgress: (prog) => {
545:           options.update?.({
546:             phase: 'discover',
547:             done: prog.done,
548:             total: prog.total,
549:             title: folderName,
550:           });
551:           if (prog.total > 0) {
552:             desktopAPI.setProgressBar(prog.done / prog.total);
553:           }
554:         },
555:       }),
556:       limits.ioTimeoutMs * 5,
557:       'Listing folder',
558:       options.signal,
559:     );
560:   } catch (e) {
561:     throw new Error(errorMessage(e));
562:   }
563:   let discovered = 0;
564:   for (const entry of entries) {
565:     alive(options.signal);
566:     const relPath = normalizeRelativePath(entry.relativePath);
567:     if (ignored(relPath) || !imageFile({ name: relPath, type: '' })) continue;
568:     try {
569:       assertPathLimits(relPath);
570:       assertAtMost(++discovered, limits.discoveryEntries, 'Discovered entries');
571:       assertAtMost(source.files.size + 1, limits.pagesPerTitle, 'Image candidates per title');
572:       const name = relPath.split('/').at(-1) ?? relPath;
573:       source.files.set(
574:         relPath,
575:         new DesktopHandle(
576:           name,
577:           entry.absolutePath,
578:           entry.size,
579:           entry.modified,
580:           entry.width,
581:           entry.height,
582:         ) as unknown as FileSystemFileHandle,
583:       );
584:     } catch (e) {
585:       if (e instanceof LimitError) throw e;
586:       source.issues.push({ path: relPath, reason: errorMessage(e) });
587:     }
588:     if (discovered % 256 === 0)
589:       options.update?.({ phase: 'discover', done: source.files.size, total: 0, title: folderName });
590:   }
591:   options.update?.({
592:     phase: 'discover',
593:     done: source.files.size,
594:     total: source.files.size,
595:     title: folderName,
596:   });
597:   await warmSources([source], options);
598:   return source;
599: }
600: 
601: interface Entry {
602:   isFile: boolean;
603:   isDirectory: boolean;
604:   name: string;
605:   file(cb: (f: File) => void, err: (e: Error) => void): void;
606:   createReader(): { readEntries(cb: (e: Entry[]) => void, err: (e: Error) => void): void };
607: }
608: 
609: export function droppedSources(
610:   items: DataTransferItem[],
611:   options: ScanOptions,
612: ): () => Promise<Source[]> {
613:   if (typeof __DESKTOP_BUILD__ !== 'undefined' && __DESKTOP_BUILD__) {
614:     return async () => [];
615:   }
616:   if (items.length > limits.discoveryEntries)
617:     return async () => {
618:       throw new LimitError(`Dropped items exceed the limit of ${limits.discoveryEntries}.`);
619:     };
620:   const entries = items
621:     .filter((i) => i.kind === 'file')
622:     .map((item) => {
623:       const typed = item as DataTransferItem & {
624:         getAsFileSystemHandle?: () => Promise<FileSystemHandle | null>;
625:       };
626:       return {
627:         entry: item.webkitGetAsEntry?.() as unknown as Entry | null,
628:         file: item.getAsFile(),
629:         handle: typed.getAsFileSystemHandle
630:           ? withinTime(
631:             typed.getAsFileSystemHandle(),
632:             limits.ioTimeoutMs,
633:             'Reading dropped handle',
634:             options.signal,
635:           ).catch(() => null)
636:           : undefined,
637:       };
638:     });
639:   return async () => {
640:     let discovered = 0;
641:     const sources: Source[] = [],
642:       loose: File[] = [];
643:     for (const item of entries) {
644:       alive(options.signal);
645:       assertAtMost(sources.length + 1, limits.titles, 'Dropped titles');
646:       const handle = await item.handle;
647:       if (handle?.kind === 'directory') {
648:         sources.push(await fromHandle(handle as Directory, options));
649:         assertSourcesLimits(sources);
650:         continue;
651:       }
652:       if (item.entry?.isDirectory) {
653:         const files = new Map<string, SourceFile>(),
654:           issues: Issue[] = [];
655:         async function walk(e: Entry, path: string, depth: number) {
656:           alive(options.signal);
657:           if (ignored(path)) return;
658:           if (!e.isFile && depth > limits.directoryDepth) {
659:             issues.push({
660:               path,
661:               reason: `Folder nesting exceeds ${limits.directoryDepth} levels.`,
662:             });
663:             return;
664:           }
665:           try {
666:             assertAtMost(path.length, limits.pathChars, 'Relative path length (characters)');
667:             if (e.isFile) {
668:               if (imageFile({ name: e.name, type: '' })) {
669:                 assertPathLimits(path);
670:                 assertAtMost(
671:                   files.size + (files.has(path) ? 0 : 1),
672:                   limits.pagesPerTitle,
673:                   'Image candidates per title',
674:                 );
675:                 files.set(
676:                   path,
677:                   await withinTime(
678:                     new Promise<File>((r, j) => e.file(r, j)),
679:                     limits.ioTimeoutMs,
680:                     'Reading dropped file',
681:                     options.signal,
682:                   ),
683:                 );
684:               }
685:               return;
686:             }
687:             const reader = e.createReader();
688:             while (true) {
689:               alive(options.signal);
690:               const list = await withinTime(
691:                 new Promise<Entry[]>((r, j) => reader.readEntries(r, j)),
692:                 limits.ioTimeoutMs,
693:                 'Reading dropped folder',
694:                 options.signal,
695:               );
696:               if (!list.length) break;
697:               discovered += list.length;
698:               assertAtMost(discovered, limits.discoveryEntries, 'Discovered entries');
699:               for (const child of list.sort((a, b) => naturalOrder(a.name, b.name)))
700:                 await walk(child, path ? path + '/' + child.name : child.name, depth + 1);
701:               await warmSources([{ title: item.entry?.name || 'Manga', files, issues }], options);
702:               options.update?.({
703:                 phase: 'discover',
704:                 done: files.size,
705:                 total: 0,
706:                 title: item.entry?.name,
707:               });
708:               await tick();
709:             }
710:           } catch (e) {
711:             alive(options.signal);
712:             if (e instanceof LimitError) throw e;
713:             issues.push({ path, reason: errorMessage(e) });
714:           }
715:         }
716:         await walk(item.entry, '', 0);
717:         sources.push({ title: item.entry.name, files, issues });
718:         assertSourcesLimits(sources);
719:       } else if (item.file) loose.push(item.file);
720:     }
721:     sources.push(...fromFiles(loose));
722:     assertSourcesLimits(sources);
723:     return sources;
724:   };
725: }
726: 
727: export async function splitSource(source: Source): Promise<Source[]> {
728:   assertSourceLimits(source);
729:   if (source.discoveredTitles && source.discoveredTitles.length > 1) {
730:     const splitTitles: Source[] = [];
731:     for (const dt of source.discoveredTitles) {
732:       const s: Source = {
733:         title: dt.title,
734:         localPath: dt.rootPath,
735:         files: new Map(),
736:         issues: [],
737:       };
738:       for (const ch of dt.chapters) {
739:         for (const p of ch.pages) {
740:           s.files.set(
741:             p.relativePath,
742:             new DesktopHandle(
743:               p.name,
744:               p.absolutePath,
745:               p.size,
746:               p.modified,
747:               p.width,
748:               p.height,
749:             ) as unknown as FileSystemFileHandle,
750:           );
751:         }
752:       }
753:       splitTitles.push(s);
754:       assertAtMost(splitTitles.length, limits.titles, 'Selected titles');
755:     }
756:     return splitTitles.length > 0 ? splitTitles : [source];
757:   }
758: 
759:   const result = new Map<string, Source>();
760:   const sep = source.localPath?.includes('\\') ? '\\' : '/';
761:   for (const [path, file] of source.files) {
762:     const parts = path.split('/');
763:     const isSub = parts.length > 1;
764:     const title = isSub ? parts.shift()! : source.title + ' — root pages';
765:     let s = result.get(title);
766:     if (!s) {
767:       const childLocalPath =
768:         source.localPath && isSub
769:           ? `${source.localPath.replace(/[\\/]+$/, '')}${sep}${title}`
770:           : source.localPath;
771:       s = { title, localPath: childLocalPath, files: new Map(), issues: [] };
772:       result.set(title, s);
773:       assertAtMost(result.size, limits.titles, 'Selected titles');
774:     }
775:     s.files.set(parts.join('/'), file);
776:   }
777:   for (const s of result.values())
778:     if (source.handle && s.title !== source.title + ' — root pages') {
779:       try {
780:         s.handle = (await withinTime(
781:           source.handle.getDirectoryHandle(s.title),
782:           limits.ioTimeoutMs,
783:           'Opening subfolder',
784:         )) as Directory;
785:       } catch (e) {
786:         s.issues.push({ path: s.title, reason: errorMessage(e) });
787:       }
788:     }
789:   return result.size > 0 ? [...result.values()] : [source];
790: }
791: 
792: export function chapterGroups(source: Source) {
793:   const groups = new Map<string, number>();
794:   for (const path of source.files.keys()) {
795:     const parts = path.split('/');
796:     const name = parts.length === 1 ? 'Root pages' : parts.slice(0, -1).join(' / ');
797:     if (parts.length === 1 && /^(thumbnail|cover)\./i.test(path) && source.files.size > 1) continue;
798:     groups.set(name, (groups.get(name) || 0) + 1);
799:   }
800:   return groups;
801: }
802: 
803: export function chapterPreviews(source: Source): ChapterPreview[] {
804:   const groups = chapterGroups(source);
805:   return Array.from(groups.entries(), ([name, pageCount]) => ({ name, pageCount }));
806: }
807: 
808: function chapterMatch(name: string, pages: Page[], old: Manga | undefined) {
809:   const exact = old?.chapters.find((c) => c.name === name);
810:   if (exact) return exact;
811:   const signature = (p: Page) => `${p.name}\0${p.size ?? ''}\0${p.modified ?? ''}`;
812:   const values = new Set(pages.map(signature));
813:   const matches = old?.chapters.filter(
814:     (c) => c.pages.length === pages.length && c.pages.every((p) => values.has(signature(p))),
815:   );
816:   return matches?.length === 1 ? matches[0] : undefined;
817: }
818: 
819: export async function pooled<T>(
820:   items: T[],
821:   work: (item: T) => Promise<void>,
822:   signal?: AbortSignal,
823: ) {
824:   let next = 0;
825:   const results = await Promise.allSettled(
826:     Array.from({ length: Math.min(getDecodeConcurrency(), items.length) }, async () => {
827:       while (next < items.length) {
828:         alive(signal);
829:         await work(items[next++]);
830:       }
831:     }),
832:   );
833:   const failure = results.find((r) => r.status === 'rejected');
834:   if (failure?.status === 'rejected') throw failure.reason;
835: }
836: 
837: export async function indexSource(
838:   source: Source,
839:   options: ScanOptions = {},
840:   previous?: Manga,
841:   sort: Manga['sort'] = 'natural',
842: ) {
843:   assertSourceLimits(source);
844:   if (previous) stopBackground(previous.id);
845: 
846:   if (desktopAPI.isDesktop && desktopAPI.indexTitle && source.localPath) {
847:     alive(options.signal);
848:     const confirmedTitle = source.title?.trim() || undefined;
849:     const jobId = `index-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2)}`;
850: 
851:     let abortHandler: (() => void) | undefined;
852:     if (options.signal) {
853:       abortHandler = () => {
854:         void desktopAPI.cancelScan?.(jobId);
855:       };
856:       options.signal.addEventListener('abort', abortHandler, { once: true });
857:     }
858: 
859:     try {
860:       const dto = await desktopAPI.indexTitle(
861:         source.localPath,
862:         {
863:           sort,
864:           previous,
865:           targetId: previous?.id,

1175: export async function prepareChapter(m: Manga, index: number, options: PrepareChapterOptions = {}) {
1176:   const chapter = m.chapters[index];
1177:   if (!chapter) return [];
1178:   const database = typeof indexedDB !== 'undefined' && !desktopAPI.isDesktop ? await db : undefined;
1179:   const cached = database ? ((await database.get('manga', m.id)) as Manga | undefined) : undefined;
1180:   alive(options.signal);
1181:   const savedChapter =
1182:     cached?.updatedAt === m.updatedAt || cached?.chapters[index]?.name === chapter.name
1183:       ? cached.chapters[index]
1184:       : undefined;
1185:   const savedMap = new Map(savedChapter?.pages.map((p) => [p.path, p]));
1186:   const pages = chapter.pages.map((p) => {
1187:     const hit = savedMap.get(p.path);
1188:     if (hit && !hit.dimensionsPending) {
1189:       sessionDimensions.set(`${m.id}:${p.path}`, { width: hit.width, height: hit.height });
1190:       return { ...hit };
1191:     }
1192:     const sessionHit = sessionDimensions.get(`${m.id}:${p.path}`);
1193:     if (sessionHit) {
1194:       return { ...p, ...sessionHit, dimensionsPending: false };
1195:     }
1196:     return { ...p };
1197:   });
1198:   const pending = pages.filter((p) => p.dimensionsPending);
1199:   if (!pending.length) {
1200:     m.chapters[index] = { ...chapter, pages };
1201:     return [];
1202:   }
1203: 
1204:   if (desktopAPI.isDesktop && desktopAPI.prepareChapterHeaders && m.localPath) {
1205:     const sep = m.localPath.includes('\\') ? '\\' : '/';
1206:     const cleanRoot = m.localPath.replace(/[\\/]+$/, '');
1207:     const pathMap = new Map<string, Page>();
1208:     const absPaths: string[] = [];
1209:     for (const page of pending) {
1210:       const absPath = cleanRoot + sep + page.path.split('/').join(sep);
1211:       pathMap.set(absPath, page);
1212:       pathMap.set(page.path, page);
1213:       absPaths.push(absPath);
1214:     }
1215:     try {
1216:       const headers = await desktopAPI.prepareChapterHeaders(absPaths, m.id);
1217:       for (const h of headers) {
1218:         const page = pathMap.get(h.path);
1219:         if (page && h.width > 0 && h.height > 0) {
1220:           assertImageDimensions(h.width, h.height);
1221:           page.width = h.width;
1222:           page.height = h.height;
1223:           page.size = h.size;
1224:           page.modified = h.modified;
1225:           page.dimensionsPending = false;
1226:           sessionDimensions.set(`${m.id}:${page.path}`, { width: h.width, height: h.height });
1227:         }
1228:       }
1229:     } catch {
1230:       // Fall through to per-page resolution
1231:     }
1232:   }
1233: 
1234:   const remainingPending = pages.filter((p) => p.dimensionsPending);
1235:   if (!remainingPending.length) {
1236:     alive(options.signal);
1237:     m.chapters[index] = { ...chapter, pages };
1238:     if (desktopAPI.isDesktop) {
1239:       await saveManga(m);
1240:     } else if (database) {
1241:       const tx = database.transaction('manga', 'readwrite');
1242:       const stored = (await tx.store.get(m.id)) as unknown as Manga | undefined;
1243:       if (
1244:         stored &&
1245:         stored.updatedAt === m.updatedAt &&
1246:         (m.revision === undefined || stored.revision === m.revision) &&
1247:         stored.chapters[index]?.name === chapter.name
1248:       ) {
1249:         stored.chapters[index].pages = pages;
1250:         await tx.store.put(stored as unknown as Omit<Manga, 'cover'>);
1251:       }
1252:       await tx.done;
1253:     }
1254:     return [];

1256: 
1257:   const issues: Issue[] = [];

1259:   let lastYield = performance.now();
1260:   await pooled(
1261:     remainingPending,
1262:     async (page) => {
1263:       try {
1264:         if (desktopAPI.isDesktop && desktopAPI.getImageDimensions && m.localPath) {
1265:           const sep = m.localPath.includes('\\') ? '\\' : '/';

1270:             Object.assign(page, dim, { dimensionsPending: false });

1272:               width: dim.width,
1273:               height: dim.height,

1281:                 done,
1282:                 total: pending.length,
1283:                 title: chapter.name,
1284:               });
1285:               await tick();
1286:             }

1288:           }

1298:         });

1300:       } catch (e) {
1301:         alive(options.signal);
1302:         issues.push({ path: page.path, reason: errorMessage(e) });
1303:       }
1304:       done++;
1305:       const now = performance.now();
1306:       if (done % 8 === 0 || done === pending.length || now - lastYield >= 10) {
1307:         lastYield = now;
1308:         options.update?.({ phase: 'index', done, total: pending.length, title: chapter.name });
1309:         await tick();

1312:     options.signal,
1313:   );
1314:   alive(options.signal);
1315:   m.chapters[index] = { ...chapter, pages };

1317:     await saveManga(m);
1318:   } else if (database) {

1321:     if (
1322:       stored &&

1324:       (m.revision === undefined || stored.revision === m.revision) &&
1325:       stored.chapters[index]?.name === chapter.name
1326:     ) {
1327:       stored.chapters[index].pages = pages;

1329:     }
1330:     await tx.done;
1331:   }
1332:   return issues;
1333: }

1335: interface BackgroundJob {
1336:   id: string;
1337:   revision?: number;
1338:   updatedAt: number;
1339:   controller: AbortController;
1340: }
1341: 
1342: const backgroundJobs = new Map<string, BackgroundJob>();
1343: let backgroundRunning = false;
1344: 
1345: export function stopBackground(id: string) {
1346:   backgroundJobs.get(id)?.controller.abort();
1347:   backgroundJobs.delete(id);
1348: }
1349: 
1350: export function stopAllBackground() {
1351:   for (const job of backgroundJobs.values()) {
1352:     job.controller.abort();
1353:   }
1354:   backgroundJobs.clear();
1355: }
1356: 
1357: onMangaDelete((id) => {
1358:   if (id) stopBackground(id);
1359:   else stopAllBackground();
1360: });
1361: 
1362: export function scheduleBackground(manga: Manga) {
1363:   if (
1364:     backgroundJobs.has(manga.id) ||
1365:     !manga.chapters.some((c) => c.pages.some((p) => p.dimensionsPending))
1366:   )
1367:     return;
1368:   if (backgroundJobs.size >= limits.queuedJobs) return;
1369:   backgroundJobs.set(manga.id, {
1370:     id: manga.id,
1371:     revision: manga.revision,
1372:     updatedAt: manga.updatedAt,
1373:     controller: new AbortController(),
1374:   });
1375:   void drainBackground();
1376: }
1377: 
1378: async function runDrainPass() {
1379:   let remaining = backgroundJobs.size;
1380:   while (backgroundJobs.size && remaining-- > 0) {
1381:     const [id, job] = backgroundJobs.entries().next().value!;
1382:     try {
1383:       const initial = await getManga(id);
1384:       if (
1385:         !initial ||
1386:         initial.updatedAt !== job.updatedAt ||
1387:         (job.revision !== undefined && initial.revision !== job.revision)
1388:       ) {
1389:         continue;
1390:       }
1391:       for (let i = 0; i < initial.chapters.length; i++) {
1392:         alive(job.controller.signal);
1393:         if (!initial.chapters[i].pages.some((p) => p.dimensionsPending)) continue;
1394:         await new Promise<void>((r) => setTimeout(r, 100));
1395:         alive(job.controller.signal);
1396:         if (!(await access(initial))) break;
1397:         const stored = await getManga(id);
1398:         if (
1399:           !stored ||
1400:           stored.updatedAt !== job.updatedAt ||
1401:           (job.revision !== undefined && stored.revision !== job.revision)
1402:         ) {
1403:           break;
1404:         }
1405:         const issues = await prepareChapter(stored, i, { signal: job.controller.signal });
1406:         job.updatedAt = stored.updatedAt;
1407:         job.revision = stored.revision;
1408:         if (issues.length > 0 && stored.chapters[i]?.pages.some((p) => p.dimensionsPending)) {
1409:           break;
1410:         }
1411:       }
1412:     } catch {
1413:       /* Interrupted jobs remain pending in saved metadata and can resume later. */
1414:     } finally {
1415:       if (backgroundJobs.get(id) === job) backgroundJobs.delete(id);
1416:     }
1417:   }
1418: }
1419: 
1420: async function drainBackground() {
1421:   if (backgroundRunning) return;
1422:   backgroundRunning = true;
1423:   try {
1424:     if (typeof navigator !== 'undefined' && 'locks' in navigator && navigator.locks?.request) {
1425:       await navigator.locks.request('manga-reader:bg-prep', { ifAvailable: true }, async (lock) => {
1426:         if (!lock) return;
1427:         await runDrainPass();
1428:       });
1429:     } else {
1430:       await runDrainPass();
1431:     }
1432:   } finally {
1433:     backgroundRunning = false;
1434:     if (backgroundJobs.size) setTimeout(() => void drainBackground(), 0);
1435:   }
1436: }
1437: 
1438: export async function resumeBackground() {
1439:   for (const item of await listLibrary()) {
1440:     const m = await getManga(item.id);
1441:     if (m && (await access(m))) scheduleBackground(m);
1442:   }
1443: }

61: export type MangaDeleteListener = (id?: string) => void;
62: const deleteListeners: MangaDeleteListener[] = [];
63: 
64: export function onMangaDelete(listener: MangaDeleteListener): () => void {
65:   deleteListeners.push(listener);
66:   return () => {
67:     const idx = deleteListeners.indexOf(listener);
68:     if (idx >= 0) deleteListeners.splice(idx, 1);
69:   };
70: }
71: 
72: export function notifyDelete(id?: string) {
73:   for (const fn of deleteListeners) {
74:     try {
75:       fn(id);
76:     } catch {
77:       // Ignore listener errors during cleanup
78:     }
79:   }
80: }

82: export interface StorageSyncMessage {
83:   type: 'library' | 'progress';
84:   id?: string;
