87: export const syncChannel =
88:   typeof BroadcastChannel !== 'undefined' ? new BroadcastChannel('manga-reader:sync') : null;
89: 
90: const localSyncListeners = new Set<(msg: StorageSyncMessage) => void>();
91: 
92: export function onStorageSync(listener: (msg: StorageSyncMessage) => void): () => void {
93:   localSyncListeners.add(listener);
94:   return () => {
95:     localSyncListeners.delete(listener);
96:   };
97: }
98: 
99: export function broadcastSync(msg: StorageSyncMessage): void {
100:   for (const listener of localSyncListeners) {
101:     try {
102:       listener(msg);
103:     } catch (e) {
104:       console.warn('Error in local storage sync listener:', e);
105:     }
106:   }
107: 
108:   if (syncChannel && typeof MessageEvent !== 'undefined') {
109:     try {
110:       syncChannel.dispatchEvent(new MessageEvent('message', { data: msg }));
111:     } catch {
112:       // Ignore dispatch failure
113:     }
114:   }
115: 
116:   try {
117:     syncChannel?.postMessage(msg);
118:   } catch {
119:     // Ignore postMessage failure
120:   }
121: }

153: export function clearDirHandleCache(mangaId?: string): void {
154:   clearAccessCache(mangaId);
155:   if (mangaId) {

157:     for (const key of dirHandleCache.keys()) {

186: export const summary = (m: Manga): Summary => {
187:   const { chapters, cover: _cover, ...rest } = m;
188: 
189:   return {
190:     ...rest,
191:     chapterCount: chapters.length,
192:     chaptersRead: chapters.filter((c: Chapter) => c.read).length,
193:     lastChapterName: chapters[m.lastChapter]?.name || '',
194:     hasProgress: chapters.some((c: Chapter) => Boolean(c.position)),
195:     indexedAt: m.updatedAt,
196:     revision: m.revision ?? 1,
197:   };
198: };

200: export async function migrateV1ToV2(tx: any, _d?: IDBPDatabase<MangaDB>): Promise<void> {
201:   let cursor = await tx.objectStore('manga').openCursor();
202:   while (cursor) {
203:     const m = cursor.value as unknown as Manga;
204:     await tx.objectStore('summaries').put(summary(m));
205:     if (m.cover) await tx.objectStore('covers').put(m.cover, m.id);
206:     for (const c of m.chapters) {
207:       await tx.objectStore('progress').put({
208:         mangaId: m.id,
209:         chapter: c.name,
210:         read: c.read,
211:         position: c.position,
212:         bookmarks: c.bookmarks,
213:       });
214:     }
215:     const { cover: _cover, ...metadata } = m;
216:     await cursor.update({
217:       ...metadata,
218:       chapters: m.chapters.map(({ position: _p, bookmarks: _b, ...c }: Chapter) => ({
219:         ...c,
220:         read: false,
221:       })),
222:     });
223:     cursor = await cursor.continue();
224:   }
225: }

229: function getWebDb(): Promise<IDBPDatabase<MangaDB>> {
230:   if (!dbInstance) {
231:     dbInstance =
232:       typeof indexedDB !== 'undefined'
233:         ? openDB<MangaDB>('manga-reader', 2, {
234:           async upgrade(
235:             d: IDBPDatabase<MangaDB>,
236:             old: number,
237:             _new: number | null,
238:             tx: IDBPTransaction<MangaDB, StoreNames<MangaDB>[], 'versionchange'>,
239:           ) {
240:             if (old < 1) {
241:               d.createObjectStore('manga', { keyPath: 'id' });
242:               d.createObjectStore('settings');
243:             }
244:             if (old < 2) {
245:               d.createObjectStore('summaries', { keyPath: 'id' });
246:               d.createObjectStore('covers');
247:               const p = d.createObjectStore('progress', { keyPath: ['mangaId', 'chapter'] });
248:               p.createIndex('mangaId', 'mangaId');
249:               try {
250:                 await migrateV1ToV2(tx, d);
251:               } catch (err) {
252:                 console.error('[db upgrade] Migration v1->v2 failed, aborting upgrade:', err);
253:                 tx.abort();
254:                 throw err;
255:               }
256:             }
257:           },
258:           blocking() {
259:             void dbInstance?.then((d: IDBPDatabase<MangaDB>) => d.close());
260:           },
261:           blocked() {
262:             if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
263:               window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('reader-storage-blocked'));
264:             }
265:           },
266:         })
267:         : (new Promise<IDBPDatabase<MangaDB>>(() => { }));
268:   }
269:   return dbInstance!;
270: }
271: 
272: type StoreNames<T> = keyof T & ('manga' | 'summaries' | 'covers' | 'progress' | 'settings');

274: export const db: Promise<IDBPDatabase<MangaDB>> = desktopAPI.isDesktop
275:   ? ({
276:     then: (onfulfilled?: any, onrejected?: any) => getWebDb().then(onfulfilled, onrejected),
277:     catch: (onrejected?: any) => getWebDb().catch(onrejected),
278:     finally: (onfinally?: any) => getWebDb().finally(onfinally),
279:   } as Promise<IDBPDatabase<MangaDB>>)
280:   : getWebDb();

282: export const listLibraryIdb = async (): Promise<Summary[]> => {
283:   if (cachedSummaries !== null) {
284:     return cachedSummaries.slice();
285:   }
286:   if (typeof indexedDB === 'undefined') {
287:     return [];
288:   }
289:   const database = await db;
290:   const rawEntries = (await database.getAll('summaries')) as unknown[];
291:   const entries: Summary[] = [];
292:   let needsSlugs = false;
293:   for (const raw of rawEntries) {
294:     const s = parseSummary(raw, { preserveHardwarePointers: true });
295:     if (s) {
296:       if (!s.slug) needsSlugs = true;
297:       entries.push(s);
298:     }
299:   }
300:   if (needsSlugs) {
301:     const tx = database.transaction(['manga', 'summaries'], 'readwrite');
302:     const used = new Set(entries.map((m) => m.slug).filter((s): s is string => Boolean(s)));
303:     for (const m of entries.sort((a, b) => a.addedAt - b.addedAt || a.id.localeCompare(b.id))) {
304:       if (!m.slug) {
305:         m.slug = uniqueSlug(m.title, used);
306:         used.add(m.slug);
307:         await tx.objectStore('summaries').put(m);
308:         const stored = await tx.objectStore('manga').get(m.id);
309:         if (stored && isRecord(stored)) {
310:           await tx.objectStore('manga').put({ ...stored, slug: m.slug });
311:         }
312:       }
313:     }
314:     await tx.done;
315:     cachedSummaries = null;
316:     invalidateBookmarkCache();
317:   }
318:   entries.sort((a, b) => b.updatedAt - a.updatedAt);
319:   cachedSummaries = entries;
320:   return entries.slice();
321: };

323: export async function getCoverIdb(id: string): Promise<Blob | undefined> {
324:   if (desktopAPI.isDesktop && desktopAPI.getThumbnail) {
325:     try {
326:       const buf = await desktopAPI.getThumbnail(id);
327:       if (buf && buf.byteLength > 0) {
328:         return new Blob([buf], { type: 'image/webp' });
329:       }
330:     } catch (err) {
331:       console.warn(`[getCover] Failed to read native thumbnail for "${id}":`, err);
332:     }
333:   }
334: 
335:   if (typeof indexedDB === 'undefined') {
336:     return undefined;
337:   }
338: 
339:   try {
340:     const d = await db;
341:     const blob = await d.get('covers', id);
342:     if (blob instanceof Blob) {
343:       if (desktopAPI.isDesktop && desktopAPI.saveThumbnail) {
344:         void blob
345:           .arrayBuffer()
346:           .then((ab) => desktopAPI.saveThumbnail?.(id, ab))
347:           .catch(() => { });
348:       }
349:       return blob;
350:     }
351:   } catch (err) {
352:     console.warn(`[getCover] Failed to read IndexedDB cover for "${id}":`, err);
353:   }
354:   return undefined;
355: }

357: export async function getMangaIdb(id: string): Promise<Manga | undefined> {
358:   const d = await db;
359:   const raw = await d.get('manga', id);
360:   if (!raw) return undefined;
361:   let m: Manga;
362:   try {
363:     m = parseManga(raw);
364:   } catch (err) {
365:     console.warn(`[getManga] Malformed metadata for title "${id}":`, err);
366:     return undefined;
367:   }
368:   const [entries, s, cover] = await Promise.all([
369:     d.getAllFromIndex('progress', 'mangaId', id),
370:     d.get('summaries', id),
371:     getCoverIdb(id),
372:   ]);
373:   const byName = new Map(
374:     (entries as Progress[])
375:       .filter((p) => p && typeof p.chapter === 'string')
376:       .map((p) => [p.chapter, p]),
377:   );
378:   return {
379:     ...m,
380:     cover: cover instanceof Blob ? cover : m.cover,
381:     lastChapter: s && Number.isInteger(s.lastChapter) ? s.lastChapter : m.lastChapter,
382:     updatedAt: m.updatedAt,
383:     chapters: m.chapters.map((c: Chapter) => {
384:       const p = byName.get(c.name);
385:       return {
386:         ...c,
387:         read: typeof p?.read === 'boolean' ? p.read : c.read,
388:         position: p?.position ? parsePosition(p.position, c.pages) : undefined,
389:         bookmarks: Array.isArray(p?.bookmarks)
390:           ? p.bookmarks
391:             .map((bm: Position) => parsePosition(bm, c.pages))
392:             .filter((bm: Position | undefined): bm is Position => Boolean(bm))
393:           : c.bookmarks || [],
394:       };
395:     }),
396:   };
397: }

399: export async function allMangaIdb(): Promise<Manga[]> {

401:     (m: Manga | undefined): m is Manga => Boolean(m),
402:   );

405: export async function saveMangaIdb(m: Manga): Promise<void> {
406:   assertMangaLimits(m);
407:   const updatedAt = Date.now();
408:   const tx = (await db).transaction(['manga', 'summaries', 'progress', 'covers'], 'readwrite');
409:   const existing = (await tx.objectStore('summaries').get(m.id)) as Summary | undefined;
410:   if (!existing && (await tx.objectStore('summaries').count()) >= limits.titles) {
411:     tx.abort();
412:     await tx.done.catch(() => { });
413:     throw new LimitError(
414:       `Library title limit is ${limits.titles}. Remove a title before importing.`,
415:     );
416:   }
417:   const revision = (existing?.revision ?? 0) + 1;
418:   const entries = (await tx.objectStore('summaries').getAll()) as Summary[];
419:   const slug =
420:     existing?.slug ||
421:     uniqueSlug(m.title, new Set(entries.filter((x) => x.id !== m.id).map((x) => x.slug || '')));
422:   const { cover, ...metadata } = m;
423:   await tx.objectStore('manga').put({
424:     ...metadata,
425:     updatedAt,
426:     revision,
427:     slug,
428:     chapters: m.chapters.map(({ position: _p, bookmarks: _b, ...c }: Chapter) => ({
429:       ...c,
430:       read: false,
431:     })),
432:   });
433:   await tx.objectStore('summaries').put(summary({ ...m, updatedAt, revision, slug }));
434:   if (cover) await tx.objectStore('covers').put(cover, m.id);
435:   const progressStore = tx.objectStore('progress');
436:   const keys = await progressStore.index('mangaId').getAllKeys(m.id);
437:   await Promise.all(keys.map((k) => progressStore.delete(k)));
438:   await Promise.all(
439:     m.chapters.map((c: Chapter) =>
440:       progressStore.put({
441:         mangaId: m.id,
442:         chapter: c.name,
443:         read: c.read,
444:         position: c.position,
445:         bookmarks: c.bookmarks,
446:       }),
447:     ),
448:   );
449:   await tx.done;
450:   m.updatedAt = updatedAt;
451:   m.revision = revision;
452:   m.slug = slug;
453:   cachedSummaries = null;
454:   invalidateBookmarkCache();
455:   clearDirHandleCache(m.id);
456: 
457:   if (cover && desktopAPI.isDesktop && desktopAPI.saveThumbnail) {
458:     try {
459:       const buffer = await cover.arrayBuffer();
460:       await desktopAPI.saveThumbnail(m.id, buffer);
461:     } catch (err) {
462:       console.warn(`[saveManga] Failed to cache thumbnail to disk for "${m.id}":`, err);
463:     }
464:   }
465: 
466:   broadcastSync({ type: 'library', id: m.id });
467: }

469: export type SaveProgressResult = 'saved' | 'stale' | 'missing';
470: 
471: export async function saveProgressIdb(
472:   m: Manga,
473:   index: number,
474:   position: Position,
475:   read: boolean,
476:   bookmarks?: Position[],
477:   updateLast = true,
478: ): Promise<SaveProgressResult> {
479:   const chapter = m.chapters[index];
480:   if (!chapter) return 'missing';
481:   if (bookmarks)
482:     assertAtMost(bookmarks.length, limits.bookmarksPerChapter, 'Bookmarks per chapter');
483:   const tx = (await db).transaction(['progress', 'summaries'], 'readwrite');
484:   const s = (await tx.objectStore('summaries').get(m.id)) as Summary | undefined;
485:   if (!s) {
486:     await tx.done;
487:     return 'missing';
488:   }
489:   if (s.indexedAt !== m.updatedAt || (m.revision && s.revision && s.revision !== m.revision)) {
490:     await tx.done;
491:     return 'stale';
492:   }
493:   const key: [string, string] = [m.id, chapter.name];
494:   const old = (await tx.objectStore('progress').get(key)) as Progress | undefined;
495:   const isRead = Boolean(old?.read || read);
496:   await tx.objectStore('progress').put({
497:     mangaId: m.id,
498:     chapter: chapter.name,
499:     read: isRead,
500:     position,
501:     bookmarks: bookmarks ?? old?.bookmarks ?? [],
502:   });
503:   await tx.objectStore('summaries').put({
504:     ...s,
505:     ...(updateLast
506:       ? {
507:         lastChapter: index,
508:         lastChapterName: chapter.name,
509:         hasProgress: true,
510:         updatedAt: Date.now(),
511:       }
512:       : {}),
513:     chaptersRead: s.chaptersRead + (!old?.read && isRead ? 1 : 0),
514:   });
515:   await tx.done;
516:   if (cachedSummaries) {
517:     const idx = cachedSummaries.findIndex((x) => x.id === m.id);
518:     if (idx >= 0) {
519:       const cur = cachedSummaries[idx];
520:       cachedSummaries[idx] = {
521:         ...cur,
522:         ...(updateLast
523:           ? {
524:             lastChapter: index,
525:             lastChapterName: chapter.name,
526:             hasProgress: true,
527:             updatedAt: Date.now(),
528:           }
529:           : {}),
530:         chaptersRead: cur.chaptersRead + (!old?.read && isRead ? 1 : 0),
531:       };
532:       cachedSummaries.sort((a, b) => b.updatedAt - a.updatedAt);
533:     } else {
534:       cachedSummaries = null;
535:     }
536:   }
537:   if (bookmarks) {
538:     invalidateBookmarkCache();
539:   }
540:   broadcastSync({ type: 'progress', id: m.id });
541:   return 'saved';
542: }
543: 
544: async function ensureBookmarkCaches(): Promise<void> {
545:   if (cachedBookmarkedMangaIds && cachedMangaBookmarkedIds) {
546:     return;
547:   }
548:   if (bookmarkCachePromise) {
549:     return bookmarkCachePromise;
550:   }
551:   const currentGen = ++bookmarkGeneration;
552:   bookmarkCachePromise = (async () => {
553:     try {
554:       if (typeof indexedDB === 'undefined') {
555:         if (bookmarkGeneration === currentGen) {
556:           cachedBookmarkedMangaIds = new Set();
557:           cachedMangaBookmarkedIds = new Set();
558:         }
559:         return;
560:       }
561:       const d = await db;
562:       const progress = await d.getAll('progress');
563:       const allBookmarked = new Set<string>();
564:       const mangaBookmarked = new Set<string>();
565: 
566:       for (const p of progress) {
567:         if (!p.bookmarks || p.bookmarks.length === 0) continue;
568:         allBookmarked.add(p.mangaId);
569:         for (const b of p.bookmarks) {
570:           if (b.mangaBookmark) {
571:             mangaBookmarked.add(p.mangaId);
572:             break;
573:           }
574:         }
575:       }
576: 
577:       if (bookmarkGeneration === currentGen) {
578:         cachedBookmarkedMangaIds = allBookmarked;
579:         cachedMangaBookmarkedIds = mangaBookmarked;
580:       }
581:     } finally {
582:       bookmarkCachePromise = null;
583:     }
584:   })();
585:   return bookmarkCachePromise;
586: }
587: 
588: export async function getBookmarkedMangaIdsIdb(): Promise<Set<string>> {
589:   await ensureBookmarkCaches();
590:   return new Set(cachedBookmarkedMangaIds ?? []);
591: }
592: 
593: export async function getMangaBookmarkedIdsIdb(): Promise<Set<string>> {
594:   await ensureBookmarkCaches();
595:   return new Set(cachedMangaBookmarkedIds ?? []);
596: }
597: 
598: export async function addBookmarkIdb(
599:   mangaId: string,
600:   chapterName: string,
601:   position: Position,
602: ): Promise<Position[]> {
603:   const database = await db;
604:   const tx = database.transaction('progress', 'readwrite');
605:   const key: [string, string] = [mangaId, chapterName];
606:   const old = (await tx.store.get(key)) as Progress | undefined;
607:   const marks = Array.isArray(old?.bookmarks) ? [...old.bookmarks] : [];
608: 
609:   const exists = marks.some((p: Position) => {
610:     if (position.mangaBookmark) {
611:       return Boolean(p.mangaBookmark);
612:     }
613:     return position.path ? p.path === position.path : p.page === position.page;
614:   });
615: 
616:   if (!exists) {
617:     assertAtMost(marks.length + 1, limits.bookmarksPerChapter, 'Bookmarks per chapter');
618:     marks.push(position);
619:     await tx.store.put({
620:       mangaId,
621:       chapter: chapterName,
622:       read: Boolean(old?.read),
623:       position: old?.position,
624:       bookmarks: marks,
625:     });
626:   }
627: 
628:   await tx.done;
629:   invalidateBookmarkCache();
630:   broadcastSync({ type: 'progress', id: mangaId });
631:   return marks;
632: }
633: 
634: export async function removeBookmarkIdb(
635:   mangaId: string,
636:   chapterName: string,
637:   position: Position,
638: ): Promise<Position[]> {
639:   const database = await db;
640:   const tx = database.transaction('progress', 'readwrite');
641:   const key: [string, string] = [mangaId, chapterName];
642:   const old = (await tx.store.get(key)) as Progress | undefined;
643:   const marks = (old?.bookmarks || []).filter((p: Position) => {
644:     if (position.mangaBookmark) {
645:       return !p.mangaBookmark;
646:     }
647:     if (p.mangaBookmark) {
648:       return true;
649:     }
650:     return position.path ? p.path !== position.path : p.page !== position.page;
651:   });
652: 
653:   if (old) {
654:     await tx.store.put({
655:       ...old,
656:       bookmarks: marks,
657:     });
658:   }
659: 
660:   await tx.done;
661:   invalidateBookmarkCache();
662:   broadcastSync({ type: 'progress', id: mangaId });
663:   return marks;
664: }
665: 
666: export async function deleteMangaIdb(id: string): Promise<void> {
667:   notifyDelete(id);
668:   const tx = (await db).transaction(['manga', 'summaries', 'covers', 'progress'], 'readwrite');
669:   const progressStore = tx.objectStore('progress');
670:   const keys = await progressStore.index('mangaId').getAllKeys(id);
671:   await Promise.all([
672:     tx.objectStore('manga').delete(id),
673:     tx.objectStore('summaries').delete(id),
674:     tx.objectStore('covers').delete(id),
675:     ...keys.map((k) => progressStore.delete(k)),
676:   ]);
677:   await tx.done;
678:   cachedSummaries = null;
679:   invalidateBookmarkCache();
680:   clearDirHandleCache(id);
681:   sessionFiles.delete(id);
682:   if (desktopAPI.isDesktop && desktopAPI.deleteThumbnail) {
683:     desktopAPI.deleteThumbnail(id).catch((err) => {
684:       console.warn(`[deleteManga] Failed to delete thumbnail from disk for "${id}":`, err);
685:     });
686:   }
687:   broadcastSync({ type: 'library', id });
688: }
689: 
690: export async function clearLibraryIdb(): Promise<void> {
691:   notifyDelete(undefined);
692:   const tx = (await db).transaction(['manga', 'summaries', 'covers', 'progress'], 'readwrite');
693:   await Promise.all(
694:     (['manga', 'summaries', 'covers', 'progress'] as const).map((name) =>
695:       tx.objectStore(name).clear(),
696:     ),
697:   );
698:   await tx.done;
699:   cachedSummaries = [];
700:   invalidateBookmarkCache();
701:   clearDirHandleCache();
702:   sessionFiles.clear();
703:   if (desktopAPI.isDesktop && desktopAPI.clearThumbnails) {
704:     desktopAPI.clearThumbnails().catch((err) => {
705:       console.warn(`[clearLibrary] Failed to clear disk thumbnails:`, err);
706:     });
707:   }
708:   broadcastSync({ type: 'library' });
709: }
710: 
711: let isRestoring = false;
712: 
713: export async function restoreBackupIdb(backup: {
714:   manga: Manga[];
715:   settings: Settings;
716: }): Promise<Settings> {
717:   if (isRestoring) {
718:     throw new Error('Another restore operation is already in progress.');
719:   }
720:   isRestoring = true;
721:   try {
722:     assertAtMost(backup.manga.length, limits.titles, 'Library titles in backup');
723:     const validatedSettings = parseSettings(backup.settings);
724: 
725:     for (const m of backup.manga) {
726:       assertMangaLimits(m);
727:     }
728: 
729:     const database = await db;
730: 
731:     const existingSummaries = await listLibraryIdb();
732:     const backupIds = new Set(backup.manga.map((m) => m.id));
733:     const usedSlugs = new Set<string>(
734:       existingSummaries
735:         .filter((s) => !backupIds.has(s.id) && Boolean(s.slug))
736:         .map((s) => s.slug!),
737:     );
738:     const existingManga = new Map<string, Manga>();
739:     for (const m of backup.manga) {
740:       const existing = await getMangaIdb(m.id);
741:       if (existing) {
742:         existingManga.set(m.id, existing);
743:       }
744:     }
745: 
746:     const now = Date.now();
747:     const preparedList: {
748:       manga: Omit<Manga, 'cover'>;
749:       summary: Summary;
750:       coverBlob?: Blob;
751:       progress: {
752:         mangaId: string;
753:         chapter: string;
754:         read: boolean;
755:         position?: Position;
756:         bookmarks?: Position[];
757:       }[];
758:     }[] = [];
759: 
760:     for (const raw of backup.manga) {
761:       const old = existingManga.get(raw.id);
762:       const handle = old?.handle;
763:       const localPath = old?.localPath;
764:       const coverBlob = old?.cover;
765: 
766:       const slug =
767:         old?.slug && !usedSlugs.has(old.slug) ? old.slug : uniqueSlug(raw.title, usedSlugs);
768:       usedSlugs.add(slug);
769: 
770:       const m: Manga = {
771:         ...raw,
772:         handle,
773:         localPath,
774:         slug,
775:         updatedAt: now,
776:       };
777: 
778:       const { cover: _c, ...metadata } = m;
779:       const mangaRecord = {
780:         ...metadata,
781:         updatedAt: now,
782:         slug,
783:         chapters: m.chapters.map(({ position: _p, bookmarks: _b, ...c }: Chapter) => ({
784:           ...c,
785:           read: false,
786:         })),
787:       };
788: 
789:       const summaryRecord = summary(m);
790: 
791:       const progressRecords = m.chapters.map((c: Chapter) => ({
792:         mangaId: m.id,
793:         chapter: c.name,
794:         read: Boolean(c.read),
795:         position: c.position,
796:         bookmarks: c.bookmarks,
797:       }));
798: 
799:       preparedList.push({
800:         manga: mangaRecord,
801:         summary: summaryRecord,
802:         coverBlob,
803:         progress: progressRecords,
804:       });
805:     }
806: 
807:     const tx = database.transaction(
808:       ['manga', 'summaries', 'covers', 'progress', 'settings'],
809:       'readwrite',
810:     );
811: 
812:     try {
813:       for (const item of preparedList) {
814:         await tx.objectStore('manga').put(item.manga);
815:         await tx.objectStore('summaries').put(item.summary);
816:         if (item.coverBlob) {
817:           await tx.objectStore('covers').put(item.coverBlob, item.manga.id);
818:         }
819:         const progressStore = tx.objectStore('progress');
820:         const oldKeys = await progressStore.index('mangaId').getAllKeys(item.manga.id);
821:         await Promise.all(oldKeys.map((k) => progressStore.delete(k)));
822:         await Promise.all(item.progress.map((p) => progressStore.put(p)));
823:       }
824: 
825:       await tx.objectStore('settings').put(validatedSettings, 'global');
826:       await tx.done;
827:       cachedSummaries = null;
828:       invalidateBookmarkCache();
829:       clearDirHandleCache();
830: 
831:       if (desktopAPI.isDesktop && desktopAPI.saveThumbnail) {
832:         for (const item of preparedList) {
833:           if (item.coverBlob) {
834:             try {
835:               const buffer = await item.coverBlob.arrayBuffer();
836:               await desktopAPI.saveThumbnail(item.manga.id, buffer);
837:             } catch (err) {
838:               console.warn(
839:                 `[restoreBackup] Failed to cache thumbnail to disk for "${item.manga.id}":`,
840:                 err,
841:               );
842:             }
843:           }
844:         }
845:       }
846:       broadcastSync({ type: 'library' });
847:     } catch (err) {
848:       try {
849:         tx.abort();
850:       } catch { }
851:       throw err;
852:     }
853: 
854:     return validatedSettings;
855:   } finally {
856:     isRestoring = false;
857:   }
858: }
859: 
860: export async function resetProgressIdb(): Promise<void> {
861:   const database = await db;
862:   const tx = database.transaction(['progress', 'summaries'], 'readwrite');
863:   try {
864:     let cursor = await tx.objectStore('progress').openCursor();
865:     while (cursor) {
866:       const p = cursor.value;
867:       if (p.read || p.position) {
868:         await cursor.update({
869:           ...p,
870:           read: false,
871:           position: undefined,
872:         });
873:       }
874:       cursor = await cursor.continue();
875:     }
876: 
877:     let sCursor = await tx.objectStore('summaries').openCursor();
878:     while (sCursor) {
879:       const s = sCursor.value;
880:       await sCursor.update({
881:         ...s,
882:         chaptersRead: 0,
883:         lastChapter: 0,
884:         hasProgress: false,
885:       });
886:       sCursor = await sCursor.continue();
887:     }
888: 
889:     await tx.done;
890:     cachedSummaries = null;
891:     invalidateBookmarkCache();
892:     broadcastSync({ type: 'progress' });
893:   } catch (err) {
894:     try {
895:       tx.abort();
896:     } catch { }
897:     throw err;
898:   }
899: }

901: export async function getStoredSettingsIdb(): Promise<Settings | undefined> {
902:   const d = await db;
903:   const raw = await d.get('settings', 'global');
904:   return raw ? parseSettings(raw) : undefined;
905: }

907: export async function saveStoredSettingsIdb(settings: Settings): Promise<Settings> {
908:   const validated = parseSettings(settings);
909:   const d = await db;
910:   await d.put('settings', validated, 'global');
911:   return validated;
912: }

914: export class IndexedDBLibraryRepo implements LibraryRepo {
915:   listLibrary(): Promise<Summary[]> {
916:     return listLibraryIdb();
917:   }
918:   getManga(id: string): Promise<Manga | undefined> {
919:     return getMangaIdb(id);
920:   }
921:   getCover(id: string): Promise<Blob | undefined> {
922:     return getCoverIdb(id);
923:   }
924:   allManga(): Promise<Manga[]> {
925:     return allMangaIdb();
926:   }
927:   saveManga(m: Manga): Promise<void> {
928:     return saveMangaIdb(m);
929:   }
930:   saveProgress(
931:     m: Manga,
932:     index: number,
933:     position: Position,
934:     read: boolean,
935:     bookmarks?: Position[],
936:     updateLast = true,
937:   ): Promise<SaveProgressResult> {
938:     return saveProgressIdb(m, index, position, read, bookmarks, updateLast);
939:   }
940:   getBookmarkedMangaIds(): Promise<Set<string>> {
941:     return getBookmarkedMangaIdsIdb();
942:   }
943:   getMangaBookmarkedIds(): Promise<Set<string>> {
944:     return getMangaBookmarkedIdsIdb();
945:   }
946:   addBookmark(mangaId: string, chapterName: string, position: Position): Promise<Position[]> {
947:     return addBookmarkIdb(mangaId, chapterName, position);
948:   }
949:   removeBookmark(mangaId: string, chapterName: string, position: Position): Promise<Position[]> {
950:     return removeBookmarkIdb(mangaId, chapterName, position);
951:   }
952:   deleteManga(id: string): Promise<void> {
953:     return deleteMangaIdb(id);
954:   }
955:   clearLibrary(): Promise<void> {
956:     return clearLibraryIdb();
957:   }
958:   resetProgress(): Promise<void> {
959:     return resetProgressIdb();
960:   }
961:   getStoredSettings(): Promise<Settings | undefined> {
962:     return getStoredSettingsIdb();
963:   }
964:   saveStoredSettings(settings: Settings): Promise<Settings> {
965:     return saveStoredSettingsIdb(settings);
966:   }
967:   async exportBackup(): Promise<string> {
968:     const list = await this.allManga();
969:     const manga = list.map(({ handle: _h, cover: _c, ...rest }: Manga) => rest);
970:     const settings = (await this.getStoredSettings()) ?? defaults;
971:     return JSON.stringify({ version: 2, manga, settings });
972:   }
973:   restoreBackup(backup: { manga: Manga[]; settings: Settings } | string): Promise<Settings> {
974:     const payload = typeof backup === 'string' ? JSON.parse(backup) : backup;
975:     return restoreBackupIdb(payload);
976:   }
977:   invalidateCaches(): void {
978:     cachedSummaries = null;
979:     bookmarkGeneration++;
980:     cachedBookmarkedMangaIds = null;
981:     cachedMangaBookmarkedIds = null;
982:     bookmarkCachePromise = null;
983:   }
984: }

1062: export function errorMessage(e: unknown): string {
1063:   const err = e as { name?: string; message?: string };
1064:   if (err?.name === 'QuotaExceededError')
1065:     return 'Browser storage is full. Export a backup and remove unused library entries before retrying.';
1066:   if (err?.name === 'NotAllowedError' || err?.name === 'SecurityError')
1067:     return 'Folder permission was denied. Re-link the folder or use standard file selection.';
1068:   if (err?.name === 'NotFoundError')
1069:     return 'A file or folder moved or disappeared. Re-link the folder to update its index.';
1070:   if (err?.name === 'InvalidStateError')
1071:     return 'Storage is unavailable or another tab is upgrading it. Close other Reader tabs and reopen.';
1072:   return err?.message || 'The operation could not complete. Please try again.';
1073: }
1074: 
1075: const readFile = (f: SourceFile): Promise<File> =>
1076:   withinTime(
1077:     f instanceof File ? Promise.resolve(f) : f.getFile(),
1078:     limits.ioTimeoutMs,
1079:     'Reading file',
1080:   );
1081: 
1082: export async function fileFor(m: Manga, p: Page): Promise<File> {
1083:   assertPathLimits(p.path);
1084:   if (desktopAPI.isDesktop && (m.localPath || desktopAPI.resolvePath)) {
1085:     const sep = m.localPath?.includes('\\') ? '\\' : '/';
1086:     const fallbackAbs = m.localPath
1087:       ? `${m.localPath.replace(/[\\/]+$/, '')}${sep}${p.path.split('/').join(sep)}`
1088:       : p.path;
1089:     const absPath = desktopAPI.resolvePath
1090:       ? await desktopAPI.resolvePath(m.id, p.path, m.localPath)
1091:       : fallbackAbs;
1092:     const buf = await withinTime(desktopAPI.readFile(absPath), limits.ioTimeoutMs, 'Reading file');
1093:     if (!buf) throw new Error('File not found on disk. The folder may have moved.');
1094:     return new File([buf], p.path.split('/').at(-1) ?? p.path);
1095:   }
1096:   if (typeof __DESKTOP_BUILD__ !== 'undefined' && __DESKTOP_BUILD__) {
1097:     throw new Error('Desktop manga is missing localPath');
1098:   }
1099:   if (m.handle) {
1100:     const parts = p.path.split('/');
1101:     const dirParts = parts.slice(0, -1);
1102:     const dirPath = dirParts.join('/');
1103:     const cacheKey = `${m.id}:${m.updatedAt}:${dirPath}`;
1104: 
1105:     let h: FileSystemDirectoryHandle | undefined =
1106:       dirParts.length === 0 ? m.handle : dirHandleCache.get(cacheKey);
1107: 
1108:     try {
1109:       if (!h) {
1110:         h = m.handle;
1111:         let subPath = '';
1112:         for (const part of dirParts) {
1113:           subPath = subPath ? `${subPath}/${part}` : part;
1114:           const subKey = `${m.id}:${m.updatedAt}:${subPath}`;
1115:           let subHandle = dirHandleCache.get(subKey);
1116:           if (!subHandle) {
1117:             subHandle = await withinTime(
1118:               h.getDirectoryHandle(part),
1119:               limits.ioTimeoutMs,
1120:               'Opening folder',
1121:             );
1122:             if (dirHandleCache.size >= MAX_CACHED_DIR_HANDLES) {
1123:               const oldest = dirHandleCache.keys().next().value;
1124:               if (oldest) dirHandleCache.delete(oldest);
1125:             }
1126:             dirHandleCache.set(subKey, subHandle);
1127:           }
1128:           h = subHandle;
1129:         }
1130:         if (dirHandleCache.size >= MAX_CACHED_DIR_HANDLES) {
1131:           const oldest = dirHandleCache.keys().next().value;
1132:           if (oldest) dirHandleCache.delete(oldest);
1133:         }
1134:         dirHandleCache.set(cacheKey, h);
1135:       }
1136:       const handle = await withinTime(
1137:         h.getFileHandle(parts.at(-1)!),
1138:         limits.ioTimeoutMs,
1139:         'Opening file',
1140:       );
1141:       return await readFile(handle);
1142:     } catch (err) {
1143:       clearDirHandleCache(m.id);
1144:       throw err;
1145:     }
1146:   }
1147:   const f = sessionFiles.get(m.id)?.get(p.path);
1148:   if (!f) throw new Error('Folder access is needed. Re-link your folder to continue.');
1149:   return readFile(f);
1150: }
1151: 
1152: export async function access(
1153:   m: Pick<Manga, 'id' | 'handle' | 'localPath'>,
1154:   request = false,
1155: ): Promise<boolean> {
1156:   if (!m) return false;
1157:   if (sessionFiles.has(m.id)) return true;
1158: 
1159:   if (desktopAPI.isDesktop) {
1160:     const now = Date.now();
1161:     if (!request) {
1162:       const cached = accessCache.get(m.id);
1163:       if (cached && now - cached.timestamp < ACCESS_CACHE_TTL_MS) {
1164:         return cached.granted;
1165:       }
1166:       const inflight = accessInFlight.get(m.id);
1167:       if (inflight) return inflight;
1168:     } else {
1169:       accessInFlight.delete(m.id);
1170:     }
1171:     const task = (async () => {
1172:       try {
1173:         const granted = desktopAPI.checkAccess
1174:           ? await desktopAPI.checkAccess(m.id, m.localPath)
1175:           : Boolean(m.localPath);
1176:         const finishNow = Date.now();
1177:         accessCache.set(m.id, {
1178:           granted,
1179:           timestamp: finishNow,
1180:           expiresAt: finishNow + ACCESS_CACHE_TTL_MS,
1181:         });
1182:         return granted;
1183:       } catch {
1184:         const finishNow = Date.now();
1185:         accessCache.set(m.id, {
1186:           granted: false,
1187:           timestamp: finishNow,
1188:           expiresAt: finishNow + ACCESS_CACHE_TTL_MS,
1189:         });
1190:         return false;
1191:       } finally {
1192:         accessInFlight.delete(m.id);
1193:       }
1194:     })();
1195:     if (!request) {
1196:       accessInFlight.set(m.id, task);
1197:     }
1198:     return task;
1199:   }
1200: 
1201:   if (typeof __DESKTOP_BUILD__ !== 'undefined' && __DESKTOP_BUILD__) {
1202:     return false;
1203:   }
1204: 
1205:   const now = Date.now();
1206:   if (!request) {
1207:     const cached = accessCache.get(m.id);
1208:     if (cached && now - cached.timestamp < ACCESS_CACHE_TTL_MS) {
1209:       return cached.granted;
1210:     }
1211:     const inFlight = accessInFlight.get(m.id);
1212:     if (inFlight) {
1213:       return inFlight;
1214:     }
1215:   }
1216: 
1217:   const queryOrRequest = async (): Promise<boolean> => {
1218:     let handle = m.handle;
1219:     if (!handle) {
1220:       if (sessionFiles.has(m.id)) return true;
1221:       const full = await getManga(m.id);
1222:       handle = full?.handle;
1223:     }
1224:     if (!handle) return sessionFiles.has(m.id);
1225:     try {
1226:       const res = await withinTime(
1227:         request
1228:           ? handle.requestPermission({ mode: 'read' })
1229:           : handle.queryPermission({ mode: 'read' }),
1230:         limits.ioTimeoutMs,
1231:         'Checking folder access',
1232:       );
1233:       return res === 'granted';
1234:     } catch {
1235:       return false;
1236:     }
1237:   };
1238: 
1239:   if (request) {
1240:     accessInFlight.delete(m.id);
1241:     const granted = await queryOrRequest();
1242:     accessCache.set(m.id, { granted, timestamp: Date.now() });
1243:     return granted;
1244:   }
1245: 
1246:   const task = queryOrRequest();
1247:   accessInFlight.set(m.id, task);
1248:   try {
1249:     const granted = await task;
1250:     accessCache.set(m.id, { granted, timestamp: Date.now() });
1251:     return granted;
1252:   } finally {
1253:     accessInFlight.delete(m.id);
1254:   }
1255: }

20: export function isRecord(value: unknown): value is Record<string, unknown> {
21:   return typeof value === 'object' && value !== null && !Array.isArray(value);

24: const RESERVED_DOS_NAMES = new Set([
25:   'con', 'prn', 'aux', 'nul',
26:   'com1', 'com2', 'com3', 'com4', 'com5', 'com6', 'com7', 'com8', 'com9',
27:   'lpt1', 'lpt2', 'lpt3', 'lpt4', 'lpt5', 'lpt6', 'lpt7', 'lpt8', 'lpt9',

30: export function validateRelativePath(path: unknown): string {
31:   if (typeof path !== 'string' || !path.trim()) {
32:     throw new ValidationError('Path must be a non-empty string.');
33:   }
34:   if (path.startsWith(' ') || path.endsWith(' ')) {
35:     throw new ValidationError(`Path cannot start or end with whitespace: "${path}"`);
36:   }
37:   const trimmed = path.trim();
38:   if (trimmed.includes(':')) {
39:     throw new ValidationError(`Path cannot contain drive prefixes or colons: "${trimmed}"`);
40:   }
41:   if (trimmed.startsWith('/') || trimmed.endsWith('/')) {
42:     throw new ValidationError(`Path cannot start or end with a slash: "${trimmed}"`);
43:   }
44:   if (trimmed.startsWith('//')) {
45:     throw new ValidationError(`Path cannot start with UNC prefix: "${trimmed}"`);
46:   }
47:   if (trimmed.includes('\\')) {
48:     throw new ValidationError(`Path must use forward slashes only: "${trimmed}"`);
49:   }
50:   if (trimmed.includes('\0')) {
51:     throw new ValidationError('Path contains invalid null byte.');
52:   }
53:   if (/[\x00-\x1f\x7f-\x9f]/.test(trimmed)) {
54:     throw new ValidationError('Path contains invalid control character.');
55:   }
56:   assertPathLimits(trimmed);
57:   const segments = trimmed.split('/');
58:   for (const seg of segments) {
59:     if (!seg || seg === '.' || seg === '..') {
60:       throw new ValidationError(`Path contains invalid traversal segment: "${trimmed}"`);
61:     }
62:     if (seg.endsWith(' ') || seg.endsWith('.')) {
63:       throw new ValidationError(`Path segment cannot end with a space or dot: "${seg}"`);
64:     }
65:     const stem = seg.split('.')[0].toLowerCase();
66:     if (RESERVED_DOS_NAMES.has(stem)) {
67:       throw new ValidationError(`Path segment uses reserved device name: "${seg}"`);
68:     }
69:   }
70:   return trimmed;
71: }
72: 
73: export function matchingPosition(
74:   p: Position | undefined,
75:   pages: Page[],
76:   oldPages: Page[] = [],
77: ): Position | undefined {
78:   if (!p || !pages.length) return;
79:   const path = p.path || oldPages[p.page]?.path;
80:   let page = path ? pages.findIndex((x) => x.path === path) : -1;
81:   if (page < 0 && path) {
82:     const name = path.split('/').at(-1);
83:     const matches = pages.map((x, i) => (x.name === name ? i : -1)).filter((i) => i >= 0);
84:     if (matches.length === 1) page = matches[0];
85:   }
86:   if (page < 0) page = Math.min(p.page, pages.length - 1);
87:   const validIndex = Math.max(0, page);
88:   return {
89:     page: validIndex,
90:     path: pages[validIndex].path,
91:     fraction: Math.max(0, Math.min(1, Number.isFinite(p.fraction) ? p.fraction : 0)),
92:     ...(p.mangaBookmark ? { mangaBookmark: true } : {}),
93:     ...(typeof p.timestamp === 'number' && Number.isFinite(p.timestamp)
94:       ? { timestamp: p.timestamp }
95:       : {}),
96:   };
97: }

99: export function parsePosition(
100:   value: unknown,
101:   pages: Page[],
102:   oldPages: Page[] = [],
103: ): Position | undefined {
104:   if (!value) return undefined;
105:   if (!isRecord(value)) {
106:     throw new ValidationError('Position must be an object.');
107:   }
108: 
109:   if (typeof value.page !== 'number' || !Number.isInteger(value.page) || value.page < 0) {
110:     throw new ValidationError('Position page must be a non-negative integer.');
111:   }
112: 
113:   if (typeof value.fraction !== 'number' || !Number.isFinite(value.fraction)) {
114:     throw new ValidationError('Position fraction must be a finite number.');
115:   }
116: 
117:   const fraction = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, value.fraction));
118:   let validatedPath: string | undefined;
119:   if (value.path !== undefined) {
120:     validatedPath = validateRelativePath(value.path);
121:   }
122: 
123:   let mangaBookmark: boolean | undefined;
124:   if ('mangaBookmark' in value && value.mangaBookmark !== undefined) {
125:     if (typeof value.mangaBookmark !== 'boolean') {
126:       throw new ValidationError('Position mangaBookmark must be a boolean.');
127:     }
128:     if (value.mangaBookmark) {
129:       mangaBookmark = true;
130:     }
131:   }
132: 
133:   let timestamp: number | undefined;
134:   if ('timestamp' in value && value.timestamp !== undefined) {
135:     if (typeof value.timestamp !== 'number' || !Number.isFinite(value.timestamp)) {
136:       throw new ValidationError('Position timestamp must be a finite number.');
137:     }
138:     timestamp = value.timestamp;
139:   }
140: 
141:   return matchingPosition(
142:     {
143:       page: value.page,
144:       path: validatedPath,
145:       fraction,
146:       ...(mangaBookmark ? { mangaBookmark: true } : {}),
147:       ...(timestamp !== undefined ? { timestamp } : {}),
148:     },
149:     pages,
150:     oldPages,
151:   );
152: }

154: export function parsePage(value: unknown, seenPaths: Set<string>): Page {
155:   if (!isRecord(value)) {
156:     throw new ValidationError('Page record must be an object.');
157:   }
158: 
159:   if (typeof value.name !== 'string' || !value.name.trim()) {
160:     throw new ValidationError('Page name must be a non-empty string.');
161:   }
162: 
163:   const path = validateRelativePath(value.path);
164:   if (seenPaths.has(path)) {
165:     throw new ValidationError(`Duplicate page path within chapter: "${path}"`);
166:   }
167:   seenPaths.add(path);
168: 
169:   const isPending = value.dimensionsPending === true;
170:   const isWidthPending = isPending && value.width === 0;
171:   const isHeightPending = isPending && value.height === 0;
172: 
173:   if (
174:     typeof value.width !== 'number' ||
175:     !Number.isInteger(value.width) ||
176:     typeof value.height !== 'number' ||
177:     !Number.isInteger(value.height) ||
178:     (value.width <= 0 && !isWidthPending) ||
179:     (value.height <= 0 && !isHeightPending)
180:   ) {
181:     throw new ValidationError('Page dimensions must be positive integer pixels.');
182:   }
183:   if (value.width > 0 && value.height > 0) {
184:     assertImageDimensions(value.width, value.height);
185:   }
186: 
187:   let size: number | undefined;
188:   if (value.size !== undefined) {
189:     if (typeof value.size !== 'number' || !Number.isInteger(value.size) || value.size < 0) {
190:       throw new ValidationError('Page size must be a non-negative integer bytes count.');
191:     }
192:     size = value.size;
193:   }
194: 
195:   let modified: number | undefined;
196:   if (value.modified !== undefined) {
197:     if (
198:       typeof value.modified !== 'number' ||
199:       !Number.isFinite(value.modified) ||
200:       value.modified < 0
201:     ) {
202:       throw new ValidationError('Page modified timestamp must be a non-negative number.');
203:     }
204:     modified = value.modified;
205:   }
206: 
207:   const dimensionsPending =
208:     typeof value.dimensionsPending === 'boolean' ? value.dimensionsPending : undefined;
209: 
210:   return {
211:     name: value.name.trim(),
212:     path,
213:     width: value.width,
214:     height: value.height,
215:     ...(size !== undefined ? { size } : {}),
216:     ...(modified !== undefined ? { modified } : {}),
217:     ...(dimensionsPending !== undefined ? { dimensionsPending } : {}),
218:   };
219: }

221: export function parseChapter(value: unknown, chapterIndex: number, oldChapter?: Chapter): Chapter {
222:   if (!isRecord(value)) {
223:     throw new ValidationError(`Chapter ${chapterIndex + 1} must be an object.`);
224:   }
225: 
226:   if (typeof value.name !== 'string' || !value.name.trim()) {
227:     throw new ValidationError(`Chapter ${chapterIndex + 1} name must be a non-empty string.`);
228:   }
229: 
230:   if (!Array.isArray(value.pages) || !value.pages.length) {
231:     throw new ValidationError(`Chapter "${value.name}" must contain at least one page.`);
232:   }
233: 
234:   const seenPaths = new Set<string>();
235:   const pages: Page[] = value.pages.map((p, idx) => {
236:     try {
237:       return parsePage(p, seenPaths);
238:     } catch (e) {
239:       throw new ValidationError(
240:         `Chapter "${value.name}" page ${idx + 1} is invalid: ${e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e)}`,
241:       );
242:     }
243:   });
244: 
245:   const position = value.position
246:     ? parsePosition(value.position, pages, oldChapter?.pages)
247:     : undefined;
248: 
249:   let bookmarks: Position[] | undefined;
250:   if (value.bookmarks !== undefined) {
251:     if (!Array.isArray(value.bookmarks)) {
252:       throw new ValidationError(`Chapter "${value.name}" bookmarks must be an array.`);
253:     }
254:     assertAtMost(value.bookmarks.length, limits.bookmarksPerChapter, 'Bookmarks per chapter');
255:     const seenBookmarks = new Set<string>();
256:     bookmarks = [];
257:     for (const b of value.bookmarks) {
258:       const parsed = parsePosition(b, pages, oldChapter?.pages);
259:       if (parsed) {
260:         const key = `${parsed.page}:${parsed.path ?? ''}:${Boolean(parsed.mangaBookmark)}`;
261:         if (!seenBookmarks.has(key)) {
262:           seenBookmarks.add(key);
263:           bookmarks.push(parsed);
264:         }
265:       }
266:     }
267:   }
268: 
269:   const id = typeof value.id === 'string' && value.id.trim() ? value.id.trim() : undefined;
270: 
271:   return {
272:     ...(id ? { id } : {}),
273:     name: value.name.trim(),
274:     read: value.read === true,
275:     pages,
276:     position,
277:     bookmarks,
278:   };
279: }

281: export function parseManga(
282:   value: unknown,
283:   oldManga?: Manga,
284:   options: { preserveHardwarePointers?: boolean } = { preserveHardwarePointers: true },
285: ): Manga {
286:   if (!isRecord(value)) {
287:     throw new ValidationError('Manga record must be an object.');
288:   }
289: 
290:   if (typeof value.id !== 'string' || !value.id.trim()) {
291:     throw new ValidationError('Manga ID must be a non-empty string.');
292:   }
293: 
294:   if (typeof value.title !== 'string' || !value.title.trim()) {
295:     throw new ValidationError('Manga title must be a non-empty string.');
296:   }
297: 
298:   if (!Array.isArray(value.chapters) || !value.chapters.length) {
299:     throw new ValidationError(`Manga "${value.title}" must contain at least one chapter.`);
300:   }
301: 
302:   assertAtMost(value.chapters.length, limits.chaptersPerTitle, 'Chapters per title');
303: 
304:   let titlePages = 0;

306:     const oldChapter = oldManga?.chapters[idx];

308:     titlePages += parsed.pages.length;
309:     assertAtMost(titlePages, limits.pagesPerTitle, 'Pages per title');
310:     return parsed;
311:   });
312: 
313:   const lastChapter =
314:     typeof value.lastChapter === 'number' && Number.isInteger(value.lastChapter)
315:       ? Math.max(0, Math.min(chapters.length - 1, value.lastChapter))
316:       : 0;
317: 
318:   const now = Date.now();
319:   const addedAt =
320:     typeof value.addedAt === 'number' && Number.isFinite(value.addedAt) && value.addedAt > 0
321:       ? value.addedAt
322:       : now;
323: 
324:   const updatedAt =
325:     typeof value.updatedAt === 'number' && Number.isFinite(value.updatedAt) && value.updatedAt > 0
326:       ? value.updatedAt
327:       : now;
328: 
329:   const sort =
330:     value.sort === 'name' || value.sort === 'reverse' || value.sort === 'natural'
331:       ? value.sort
332:       : undefined;
333: 
334:   const revision =
335:     typeof value.revision === 'number' && Number.isFinite(value.revision) && value.revision >= 0
336:       ? value.revision
337:       : 1;
338: 
339:   const title = value.title.trim();
340:   const slug =
341:     typeof value.slug === 'string' && value.slug.trim() ? value.slug.trim() : folderSlug(title);
342: 
343:   const localPath =
344:     options.preserveHardwarePointers &&
345:       typeof value.localPath === 'string' &&
346:       value.localPath.trim()
347:       ? value.localPath.trim()
348:       : undefined;
349: 
350:   const handle =
351:     options.preserveHardwarePointers && value.handle && typeof value.handle === 'object'
352:       ? (value.handle as Manga['handle'])
353:       : undefined;
354: 
355:   const cover =
356:     options.preserveHardwarePointers && value.cover instanceof Blob ? value.cover : undefined;

566: export function validateBackup(value: unknown): { manga: Manga[]; settings: Settings } {
567:   if (typeof value === 'string') {
568:     try {
569:       value = JSON.parse(value);
570:     } catch {
571:       throw new ValidationError('Backup must be valid JSON');
572:     }
573:   }
574:   if (!isRecord(value)) {
575:     throw new ValidationError('Backup must be an object.');
576:   }
577: 
578:   if (value.version !== 1 && value.version !== 2) {
579:     throw new ValidationError('Invalid backup version. Supported versions: 1, 2.');
580:   }
581: 
582:   if (!Array.isArray(value.manga)) {
583:     throw new ValidationError('Backup manga list must be an array.');
584:   }
585: 
586:   assertAtMost(value.manga.length, limits.titles, 'Backup titles');
587: 
588:   const seenIds = new Set<string>();
589:   let totalBackupPages = 0;
590: 
591:   const manga: Manga[] = value.manga.map((rawManga, index) => {
592:     if (!isRecord(rawManga) || typeof rawManga.id !== 'string') {
593:       throw new ValidationError(`Manga item ${index + 1} has an invalid or missing ID.`);
594:     }
595:     const id = rawManga.id.trim();
596:     if (!id) {
597:       throw new ValidationError(`Manga item ${index + 1} has an invalid or missing ID.`);
598:     }
599:     if (seenIds.has(id)) {
600:       throw new ValidationError(`Duplicate manga ID found in backup: "${id}"`);
601:     }
602:     seenIds.add(id);
603: 
604:     const parsed = parseManga(rawManga, undefined, { preserveHardwarePointers: false });
605:     for (const ch of parsed.chapters) {
606:       totalBackupPages += ch.pages.length;
607:       assertAtMost(totalBackupPages, limits.backupPages, 'Backup pages');
608:     }
609:     return parsed;
610:   });
611: 
612:   const settings = parseSettings(value.settings);
613: 
614:   return {
615:     manga,
616:     settings,
617:   };

128: const coverUrlPool = new Map<string, string>();

130: export function clearCoverUrlPool(mangaId?: string): void {
131:   if (mangaId) {
132:     for (const [k, existing] of coverUrlPool.entries()) {
133:       if (k === mangaId || k.startsWith(`${mangaId}:`)) {
134:         try {
135:           URL.revokeObjectURL(existing);
136:         } catch { }
137:         coverUrlPool.delete(k);
138:       }
139:     }
140:   } else {
141:     for (const u of coverUrlPool.values()) {
142:       try {
143:         URL.revokeObjectURL(u);
144:       } catch { }
145:     }
146:     coverUrlPool.clear();
147:   }
148: }

154: type CoverVisibleCallback = () => void;
155: const coverListeners = new Map<Element, CoverVisibleCallback>();
156: let sharedCoverObserver: IntersectionObserver | null = null;
157: 
158: function getSharedCoverObserver(): IntersectionObserver | null {
159:   if (typeof IntersectionObserver === 'undefined') return null;
160:   if (!sharedCoverObserver) {
161:     sharedCoverObserver = new IntersectionObserver(
162:       (entries) => {
163:         for (const entry of entries) {
164:           if (entry.isIntersecting) {
165:             const cb = coverListeners.get(entry.target);
166:             if (cb) {
167:               coverListeners.delete(entry.target);
168:               sharedCoverObserver?.unobserve(entry.target);
169:               cb();
170:             }
171:           }
172:         }
173:       },
174:       { rootMargin: '100px 0px' },
175:     );
176:   }
177:   return sharedCoverObserver;
178: }
179: 
180: function observeCoverVisibility(el: HTMLElement, callback: CoverVisibleCallback): () => void {
181:   const observer = getSharedCoverObserver();
182:   if (!observer) {
183:     callback();
184:     return () => { };
185:   }
186:   coverListeners.set(el, callback);
187:   observer.observe(el);
188:   return () => {
189:     coverListeners.delete(el);
190:     observer.unobserve(el);
191:   };
192: }

194: export const Cover = memo(function Cover({
195:   m,
196: }: {
197:   m: Pick<Summary, 'id'> & { updatedAt?: number };
198: }) {
199:   const cacheKey = `${m.id}:${m.updatedAt ?? 0}`;
200:   const [url, setUrl] = useState<string>(() => coverUrlPool.get(cacheKey) || '');
201:   const host = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
202: 
203:   useEffect(() => {
204:     let live = true;
205:     const cached = coverUrlPool.get(cacheKey);
206:     if (cached) {
207:       setUrl(cached);
208:       return;
209:     }
210: 
211:     if (!host.current) return;
212: 
213:     const unobserve = observeCoverVisibility(host.current, () => {
214:       if (desktopAPI.isDesktop && (desktopAPI.getCoverImageUrl || desktopAPI.getThumbnailUrl)) {
215:         if (desktopAPI.getCoverImageUrl) {
216:           const coverUrl = desktopAPI.getCoverImageUrl(m.id, m.updatedAt);
217:           setUrl(coverUrl);
218:           return;
219:         }
220:         desktopAPI
221:           .getThumbnailUrl!(m.id)
222:           .then((thumbUrl) => {
223:             if (!live) return;
224:             if (thumbUrl) {
225:               setUrl(thumbUrl);
226:               return;
227:             }
228:             loadCoverBlob();
229:           })
230:           .catch(() => {
231:             if (live) loadCoverBlob();
232:           });
233:         return;
234:       }
235:       loadCoverBlob();
236: 
237:       function loadCoverBlob() {
238:         void getCover(m.id)
239:           .then((blob) => {
240:             if (!live || !blob) return;
241:             let objectUrl = coverUrlPool.get(cacheKey);
242:             if (!objectUrl) {
243:               if (coverUrlPool.size >= MAX_COVER_URLS) {
244:                 const oldestKey = coverUrlPool.keys().next().value;
245:                 if (oldestKey) clearCoverUrlPool(oldestKey);
246:               }
247:               objectUrl = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
248:               coverUrlPool.set(cacheKey, objectUrl);
249:             }
250:             setUrl(objectUrl);
251:           })
252:           .catch(() => { });
253:       }
254:     });
255: 
256:     return () => {
257:       live = false;
258:       unobserve();
259:     };
260:   }, [cacheKey, m.id, m.updatedAt]);
261: 
262:   return (
263:     <div ref={host} className="cover">
264:       {url ? (
265:         <img
266:           src={url}
267:           alt=""
268:           onError={() => {
269:             if (url.includes('/cover/')) {
270:               void getCover(m.id)
271:                 .then((blob) => {
272:                   if (!blob) {
273:                     setUrl('');
274:                     return;
275:                   }
276:                   let objectUrl = coverUrlPool.get(cacheKey);
277:                   if (!objectUrl) {
278:                     if (coverUrlPool.size >= MAX_COVER_URLS) {
279:                       const oldestKey = coverUrlPool.keys().next().value;
280:                       if (oldestKey) clearCoverUrlPool(oldestKey);
281:                     }
282:                     objectUrl = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
283:                     coverUrlPool.set(cacheKey, objectUrl);
284:                   }
285:                   setUrl(objectUrl);
286:                 })
287:                 .catch(() => setUrl(''));
288:             } else {
289:               setUrl('');
290:             }
291:           }}
292:         />
293:       ) : (
294:         <BookOpen size={24} />
295:       )}
296:     </div>
297:   );
298: });

300: function CardMenu({
301:   title,
302:   needsRelink,
303:   onRelink,
304:   onMarkRead,
305:   onMarkUnread,
306:   onRemove,
307: }: {
308:   title: string;
309:   needsRelink: boolean;

311:   onMarkRead: () => void;
312:   onMarkUnread: () => void;
313:   onRemove: () => void;
314: }) {
315:   const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
316:   const [placement, setPlacement] = useState<'top' | 'bottom'>('bottom');
317:   const [popoverStyle, setPopoverStyle] = useState<CSSProperties>({});
318:   const detailsRef = useRef<HTMLDetailsElement>(null);
319:   const summaryRef = useRef<HTMLElement>(null);
320:   const popoverRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
321: 
322:   const updatePosition = useCallback(() => {
323:     const trigger = summaryRef.current || detailsRef.current;
324:     if (!trigger) return;
325:     const rect = trigger.getBoundingClientRect();
326:     const popoverEl = popoverRef.current;
327:     const popoverHeight = popoverEl?.offsetHeight || (needsRelink ? 200 : 160);
328:     const popoverWidth = popoverEl?.offsetWidth || 190;
329:     const gap = 6;
330:     const margin = 10;
331: 
332:     const spaceBelow = window.innerHeight - rect.bottom;
333:     const spaceAbove = rect.top;
334:     const placeTop = spaceBelow < popoverHeight + gap + margin && spaceAbove > spaceBelow;
335: 
336:     setPlacement(placeTop ? 'top' : 'bottom');
337: 
338:     let left = rect.right - popoverWidth;
339:     if (left < margin) {
340:       left = Math.min(rect.left, window.innerWidth - popoverWidth - margin);
341:     }
342:     left = Math.max(margin, left);
343: 
344:     if (placeTop) {
345:       setPopoverStyle({
346:         position: 'fixed',
347:         top: 'auto',
348:         bottom: `${Math.max(margin, window.innerHeight - rect.top + gap)}px`,
349:         left: `${left}px`,
350:         right: 'auto',
351:         zIndex: 9999,
352:       });
353:     } else {
354:       setPopoverStyle({
355:         position: 'fixed',
356:         top: `${Math.min(window.innerHeight - popoverHeight - margin, rect.bottom + gap)}px`,
357:         bottom: 'auto',
358:         left: `${left}px`,
359:         right: 'auto',
360:         zIndex: 9999,
361:       });
362:     }
363:   }, [needsRelink]);
364: 
365:   useLayoutEffect(() => {
366:     if (!open) return;
367:     updatePosition();
368:     const raf = requestAnimationFrame(updatePosition);
369:     return () => cancelAnimationFrame(raf);
370:   }, [open, updatePosition]);
371: 
372:   useEffect(() => {
373:     if (!open) return;
374:     function handleOutside(event: MouseEvent | PointerEvent) {
375:       const target = event.target as Node | null;
376:       if (!target) return;
377:       if (detailsRef.current?.contains(target) || popoverRef.current?.contains(target)) {
378:         return;
379:       }
380:       setOpen(false);
381:     }
382:     function handleKeyDown(event: KeyboardEvent) {
383:       if (event.key === 'Escape') {
384:         event.stopPropagation();
385:         setOpen(false);
386:         summaryRef.current?.focus();
387:       }
388:     }
389:     document.addEventListener('pointerdown', handleOutside, true);
390:     document.addEventListener('click', handleOutside, true);
391:     document.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
392:     window.addEventListener('resize', updatePosition);
393:     window.addEventListener('scroll', updatePosition, true);
394:     return () => {
395:       document.removeEventListener('pointerdown', handleOutside, true);
396:       document.removeEventListener('click', handleOutside, true);
397:       document.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
398:       window.removeEventListener('resize', updatePosition);
399:       window.removeEventListener('scroll', updatePosition, true);
400:     };
401:   }, [open, updatePosition]);
402: 
403:   const menuContent = (
404:     <div
405:       ref={popoverRef}
406:       className="menu-popover"
407:       style={popoverStyle}
408:       onClick={(event) => {
409:         if ((event.target as Element).closest('button')) {
410:           setOpen(false);
411:           summaryRef.current?.focus();
412:         }
413:       }}
414:     >
415:       {needsRelink && (
416:         <button type="button" onClick={onRelink}>
417:           <FolderSync size={16} /> Re-link / re-index
418:         </button>
419:       )}
420:       <button type="button" onClick={onMarkRead}>
421:         <Check size={16} /> Mark all read
422:       </button>
423:       <button type="button" onClick={onMarkUnread}>
424:         <BookOpen size={16} /> Mark all unread
425:       </button>
426:       <button type="button" className="danger-text" onClick={onRemove}>
427:         <Trash2 size={16} /> Remove
428:       </button>
429:     </div>
430:   );
431: 
432:   return (
433:     <details
434:       ref={detailsRef}
435:       open={open}
436:       className={`card-menu${placement === 'top' ? ' popover-top' : ''}`}
437:       onToggle={(e) => {
438:         const nextOpen = e.currentTarget.open;
439:         if (nextOpen !== open) {
440:           setOpen(nextOpen);
441:         }
442:       }}
443:     >
444:       <summary
445:         ref={summaryRef}
446:         aria-label={'Options for ' + title}
447:         aria-expanded={open}
448:         aria-haspopup="menu"
449:         onClick={(e) => {
450:           e.preventDefault();
451:           e.stopPropagation();
452:           setOpen((prev) => !prev);
453:         }}
454:       >
455:         <MoreHorizontal className="card-menu-dots" size={19} />
456:         <X className="card-menu-close" size={19} />
457:       </summary>
458:       {open &&
459:         (typeof document !== 'undefined' ? createPortal(menuContent, document.body) : menuContent)}
460:     </details>
461:   );
462: }

464: function formatAddedDate(timestamp?: number): string {
465:   if (!timestamp) return '';
466:   const d = new Date(timestamp);
467:   if (isNaN(d.getTime())) return '';
468:   const day = String(d.getDate()).padStart(2, '0');
469:   const month = String(d.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0');
470:   const year = d.getFullYear();
471:   return `${day}/${month}/${year}`;
472: }
473: 
474: function formatWholeDate(timestamp?: number): string {
475:   if (!timestamp) return '';
476:   const d = new Date(timestamp);
477:   if (isNaN(d.getTime())) return '';
478:   return d.toLocaleDateString(undefined, {
479:     weekday: 'long',
480:     year: 'numeric',
481:     month: 'long',
482:     day: 'numeric',
483:   });
484: }

535: function DateTooltip({ timestamp, children }: { timestamp?: number; children: ReactNode }) {
536:   const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
537:   const [placement, setPlacement] = useState<'top' | 'bottom'>('top');
538:   const [align, setAlign] = useState<'left' | 'right'>('left');
539:   const badgeRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
540: 
541:   const showTooltip = () => {
542:     if (badgeRef.current) {
543:       const rect = badgeRef.current.getBoundingClientRect();
544:       setPlacement(rect.top < 44 ? 'bottom' : 'top');
545:       setAlign(rect.left + 260 > window.innerWidth ? 'right' : 'left');
546:     }
547:     setOpen(true);
548:   };
549: 
550:   const hideTooltip = () => setOpen(false);
551:   const wholeDate = formatWholeDate(timestamp);
552: 
553:   return (
554:     <div
555:       ref={badgeRef}
556:       className="manga-card-date-badge"
557:       onPointerEnter={showTooltip}
558:       onPointerLeave={hideTooltip}
559:       onFocus={showTooltip}
560:       onBlur={hideTooltip}
561:       onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
562:       tabIndex={0}
563:       role="note"
564:       aria-label={wholeDate ? `Manga was added on ${wholeDate}` : undefined}
565:     >
566:       {children}
567:       {open && wholeDate && (
568:         <div className={`space-tooltip tooltip-${placement} tooltip-align-${align}`} role="tooltip">
569:           Manga was added on {wholeDate}
570:         </div>
571:       )}
572:     </div>
573:   );
574: }
575: 
576: export const shelfCollator = new Intl.Collator(undefined, { numeric: true, sensitivity: 'base' });
577: 
578: export function sortLibraryEntries(
579:   library: readonly Summary[],
580:   shelfSort: Settings['shelfSort'] = 'recent',
581: ): Summary[] {
582:   return [...library].sort((a, b) => {
583:     const byTitle = shelfCollator.compare(a.title, b.title);
584:     return (
585:       (shelfSort === 'title'
586:         ? byTitle
587:         : shelfSort === 'added'
588:           ? b.addedAt - a.addedAt
589:           : shelfSort === 'progress'
590:             ? b.chaptersRead / Math.max(1, b.chapterCount) -
591:             a.chaptersRead / Math.max(1, a.chapterCount)
592:             : b.updatedAt - a.updatedAt) ||
593:       byTitle ||
594:       shelfCollator.compare(a.id, b.id)
595:     );
596:   });
597: }

599: function Library({
600:   busy,
601:   scan,
602:   drop,
603:   cancel,
604: }: {
605:   busy: boolean;
606:   scan: ScanState;
607:   drop: (items: DataTransferItem[]) => void;
608:   cancel: () => void;
609: }) {
610:   const { library, pick, notify, refresh, openSettings, settings, setSettings } = useApp();
611:   const shelfSort = settings.shelfSort || 'recent';
612:   const sortedLibrary = useMemo(() => sortLibraryEntries(library, shelfSort), [library, shelfSort]);
613:   const [drag, setDrag] = useState(false);
614:   const [needs, setNeeds] = useState<Set<string>>(new Set());
615:   const [supported, setSupported] = useState(false);
616:   const [removeId, setRemoveId] = useState<string>();
617:   const [bookmarkedIds, setBookmarkedIds] = useState<Set<string>>(new Set());
618:   const nav = useNavigate();
619: 
620:   useEffect(() => {
621:     let live = true;
622:     function updateBookmarkedIds() {
623:       void getMangaBookmarkedIds().then((ids) => {
624:         if (live) setBookmarkedIds(ids);
625:       });
626:     }
627:     updateBookmarkedIds();

629:       if (e.data?.type === 'progress' || e.data?.type === 'library') updateBookmarkedIds();
630:     };
631:     syncChannel?.addEventListener('message', onSync);
632:     return () => {
633:       live = false;
634:       syncChannel?.removeEventListener('message', onSync);
