636:   }, [library]);
637: 
638:   async function toggleMangaBookmark(m: Summary, e: React.MouseEvent) {
639:     e.stopPropagation();
640:     e.preventDefault();
641:     const isBookmarked = bookmarkedIds.has(m.id);
642:     try {
643:       const manga = await getManga(m.id);
644:       if (!manga) return;
645:       if (isBookmarked) {
646:         for (const ch of manga.chapters) {
647:           for (const b of ch.bookmarks || []) {
648:             if (b.mangaBookmark) {
649:               await removeBookmark(m.id, ch.name, b);
650:             }
651:           }
652:         }
653:         setBookmarkedIds((prev) => {
654:           const next = new Set(prev);
655:           next.delete(m.id);
656:           return next;
657:         });
658:         await refresh();
659:         notify('Bookmark removed.');
660:       } else {
661:         const ch = manga.chapters[0];
662:         if (ch) {
663:           const pagePath = ch.pages[0]?.path;
664:           const pos: Position = {
665:             page: 0,
666:             fraction: 0,
667:             path: pagePath,
668:             mangaBookmark: true,
669:             timestamp: Date.now(),
670:           };
671:           await addBookmark(m.id, ch.name, pos);
672:           setBookmarkedIds((prev) => new Set(prev).add(m.id));
673:           await refresh();
674:           notify(`Bookmarked ${m.title}.`);
675:         }
676:       }
677:     } catch (err) {
678:       notify(errorMessage(err));
679:     }
680:   }
681:   useEffect(() => {
682:     let live = true;
683:     let revision = 0;
684:     function checkAccess() {
685:       const current = ++revision;
686:       void Promise.all(library.map(async (m) => (!(await access(m)) ? m.id : ''))).then(
687:         (ids) => live && current === revision && setNeeds(new Set(ids.filter(Boolean))),
688:       );
689:     }
690:     function onVisible() {
691:       if (document.visibilityState === 'visible') checkAccess();
692:     }
693:     checkAccess();
694:     window.addEventListener('focus', checkAccess);
695:     document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', onVisible);
696:     return () => {
697:       live = false;
698:       window.removeEventListener('focus', checkAccess);
699:       document.removeEventListener('visibilitychange', onVisible);
700:     };
701:   }, [library]);
702:   async function mark(id: string, read: boolean) {
703:     const m = await getManga(id);
704:     if (!m) return;
705:     await saveManga({
706:       ...m,
707:       chapters: m.chapters.map((c) => ({
708:         ...c,
709:         read,
710:         ...(read ? {} : { position: undefined }),
711:       })),
712:     });
713:     await refresh();
714:   }
715:   async function remove() {
716:     if (!removeId) return;
717:     await deleteManga(removeId);
718:     setRemoveId(undefined);
719:     await refresh();
720:     notify('Manga removed.');
721:   }
722:   return (
723:     <>
724:       <header className="library-header">
725:         <Link to="/" className="brand">
726:           <BookOpen size={23} strokeWidth={1.5} />
727:           <span>
728:             Reader<span className="brand-period">.</span>
729:           </span>
730:         </Link>
731:         <div className="header-actions">
732:           <Link className="icon-button" to="/bookmarks" aria-label="Bookmarks" title="Bookmarks">
733:             <BookMarked size={19} />
734:           </Link>
735:           <ThemeButton />
736:           <IconButton label="Settings" onClick={openSettings}>
737:             <Gear size={19} />
738:           </IconButton>
739:         </div>
740:       </header>
741:       <main className="library-main">
742:         <div className="page-heading">
743:           <div>
744:             <h1>Your library</h1>
745:             <DailyWelcome />
746:           </div>
747:         </div>
748:         <div
749:           aria-busy={busy}
750:           onClick={(e) => {
751:             if (!busy && !(e.target as HTMLElement).closest('button,a')) void pick();
752:           }}
753:           onDragOver={(e) => {
754:             e.preventDefault();
755:             if (!busy) setDrag(true);
756:           }}
757:           onDragLeave={() => setDrag(false)}
758:           onDrop={(e) => {
759:             e.preventDefault();
760:             setDrag(false);
761:             if (!busy && !desktopAPI.isDesktop) drop(Array.from(e.dataTransfer.items));
762:           }}
763:           className={
764:             'upload-zone group bg-white dark:bg-zinc-900 min-h-[350px] p-8 rounded w-full flex flex-col items-center justify-center relative transition-colors duration-300 cursor-pointer shadow ' +
765:             (drag ? 'dragging' : '')
766:           }
767:         >
768:           {busy ? (
769:             <>
770:               <LoaderCircle className="spin" size={26} />
771:               <strong className="index-count" aria-live="polite">
772:                 {scan.phase === 'index' ? 'Saving…' : `${scan.done} found`}
773:               </strong>
774:               <span>
775:                 {scan.phase === 'index' ? 'Adding to your library…' : 'Preparing your manga…'}
776:               </span>
777:               <span className="subtle">{scan.title}</span>
778:               <button className="secondary" onClick={cancel}>
779:                 Cancel import
780:               </button>
781:             </>
782:           ) : (
783:             <>
784:               <h2 className="upload-area-title">
785:                 {drag ? 'Drop your stories here' : 'Make room for a good story'}
786:               </h2>
787:               <p>Drop manga folders here, or choose your files.</p>
788:               <div className="upload-buttons">
789:                 <button className="browse-button" onClick={() => void pick()}>
790:                   Choose a folder <UploadIcon size={16} />
791:                 </button>
792:                 <button className="secondary support-button" onClick={() => setSupported(true)}>
793:                   <Info size={16} /> What’s supported
794:                 </button>
795:               </div>
796:             </>
797:           )}
798:         </div>
799: 
800:         {library.length > 0 && (
801:           <>
802:             <BookmarkShelf carousel />
803:             <div className="shelf-toolbar">
804:               <div className="collection-heading">
805:                 <h2>
806:                   {library.length === 1
807:                     ? '1 manga on your shelf.'
808:                     : `${library.length} manga on your shelf.`}
809:                 </h2>
810:                 <span>Saved on this device</span>
811:               </div>
812:               {library.length > 1 && (
813:                 <label className="shelf-sort">
814:                   <Dropdown
815:                     aria-label="Sort manga shelf"
816:                     value={shelfSort}
817:                     onChange={(e) =>
818:                       setSettings({
819:                         ...settings,
820:                         shelfSort: e.target.value as Settings['shelfSort'],
821:                       })
822:                     }
823:                   >
824:                     <option value="recent">Recent activity</option>
825:                     <option value="title">Title A–Z</option>
826:                     <option value="added">Newest added</option>
827:                     <option value="progress">Most completed</option>
828:                   </Dropdown>
829:                 </label>
830:               )}
831:             </div>
832:             <div className="manga-grid">
833:               {sortedLibrary.map((m) => (
834:                 <article className="manga-card" key={m.id}>
835:                   <div
836:                     className="manga-open"
837:                     role="button"
838:                     tabIndex={0}
839:                     onClick={() =>
840:                       void getManga(m.id)
841:                         .then((latest) => {
842:                           if (latest) nav(mangaPath(latest));
843:                         })
844:                         .catch((error) => notify(errorMessage(error)))
845:                     }
846:                     onKeyDown={(e) => {
847:                       if (e.target !== e.currentTarget) return;
848:                       if (e.key === 'Enter' || e.key === ' ') {
849:                         e.preventDefault();
850:                         void getManga(m.id)
851:                           .then((latest) => {
852:                             if (latest) nav(mangaPath(latest));
853:                           })
854:                           .catch((error) => notify(errorMessage(error)));
855:                       }
856:                     }}
857:                     aria-label={(m.hasProgress ? 'Continue reading ' : 'Start reading ') + m.title}
858:                   >
859:                     <div className="manga-card-left">
860:                       <Cover m={m} />
861:                     </div>
862:                     <div className="manga-card-right">
863:                       <div className="manga-card-right-top">
864:                         <div className="manga-card-title-row">
865:                           <h3 title={m.title}>{m.title}</h3>
866:                           <span className="manga-card-counter">
867:                             {m.chaptersRead} / {m.chapterCount}
868:                           </span>
869:                         </div>
870:                         <div className="reading-progress">
871:                           <span
872:                             style={{
873:                               width: (100 * m.chaptersRead) / Math.max(1, m.chapterCount) + '%',
874:                             }}
875:                           />
876:                         </div>
877:                       </div>
878:                       <div className="manga-card-right-bottom">
879:                         <DateTooltip timestamp={m.addedAt}>
880:                           <CalendarIcon className="manga-card-calendar-icon" />
881:                           <span className="manga-card-date">{formatAddedDate(m.addedAt)}</span>
882:                         </DateTooltip>
883:                         <button
884:                           type="button"
885:                           className={
886:                             'manga-bookmark-btn' + (bookmarkedIds.has(m.id) ? ' bookmarked' : '')
887:                           }
888:                           onClick={(e) => void toggleMangaBookmark(m, e)}
889:                           title={
890:                             bookmarkedIds.has(m.id) ? 'Remove bookmark' : 'Bookmark this title'
891:                           }
892:                           aria-label={
893:                             bookmarkedIds.has(m.id) ? 'Remove bookmark' : 'Bookmark this title'
894:                           }
895:                           aria-pressed={bookmarkedIds.has(m.id)}
896:                         >
897:                           <Bookmark
898:                             size={16}
899:                             fill={bookmarkedIds.has(m.id) ? 'currentColor' : 'none'}
900:                           />
901:                         </button>
902:                       </div>
903:                     </div>
904:                   </div>
905:                   <div className="manga-card-bottom">
906:                     {needs.has(m.id) ? (
907:                       <button
908:                         type="button"
909:                         className="relink-needed-btn"
910:                         onClick={async (e) => {
911:                           e.stopPropagation();
912:                           try {
913:                             const allowed = await access(m, true);
914:                             if (allowed) {
915:                               setNeeds((prev) => {
916:                                 const next = new Set(prev);
917:                                 next.delete(m.id);
918:                                 return next;
919:                               });
920:                               await refresh();
921:                               return;
922:                             }
923:                           } catch {
924:                             // Fall through to folder picker
925:                           }
926:                           void pick(m);
927:                         }}
928:                         title="Grant access or re-link folder"
929:                       >
930:                         Re-link needed
931:                       </button>
932:                     ) : (
933:                       <Link
934:                         className="manga-info-btn"
935:                         to={mangaDetailsPath(m)}
936:                         aria-label={'Info for ' + m.title}
937:                       >
938:                         <svg
939:                           xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg"
940:                           width="18"
941:                           height="18"
942:                           fill="none"
943:                           stroke="currentColor"
944:                           strokeLinecap="round"
945:                           strokeLinejoin="round"
946:                           strokeWidth="2"
947:                           color="currentColor"
948:                           viewBox="0 0 24 24"
949:                         >
950:                           <circle cx="12" cy="12" r="10" />
951:                           <path d="M12 16v-4m.125-3.75H12m.25 0a.25.25 0 1 0-.5 0 .25.25 0 0 0 .5 0" />
952:                         </svg>
953:                         <span>Info</span>
954:                       </Link>
955:                     )}
956:                     <CardMenu
957:                       title={m.title}
958:                       needsRelink={needs.has(m.id)}
959:                       onRelink={() => void pick(m)}
960:                       onMarkRead={() => void mark(m.id, true).catch((e) => notify(errorMessage(e)))}
961:                       onMarkUnread={() =>
962:                         void mark(m.id, false).catch((e) => notify(errorMessage(e)))
963:                       }
964:                       onRemove={() => {
965:                         if (settings.confirmDelete) {
966:                           setRemoveId(m.id);
967:                         } else {
968:                           void deleteManga(m.id)
969:                             .then(refresh)
970:                             .then(() => notify('Manga removed.'))
971:                             .catch((e) => notify(errorMessage(e)));
972:                         }
973:                       }}
974:                     />
975:                   </div>
976:                 </article>
977:               ))}
978:             </div>
979:           </>
980:         )}
981: 
982:         <footer className="library-footer">
983:           <span>By Vivek Kr. Poddar · v{APP_VERSION}</span>
984:           <span>A quieter way to read.</span>
985:         </footer>
986:       </main>
987:       <SupportedDialog open={supported} onClose={() => setSupported(false)} />
988:       <Modal
989:         open={!!removeId}
990:         onClose={() => setRemoveId(undefined)}
991:         title="Remove this title?"
992:         className="confirm-modal"
993:         description="Only this title’s saved metadata, cover, bookmarks, and reading progress will be removed. Your original files stay untouched."
994:       >
995:         <div className="button-row">
996:           <button
997:             className="secondary danger-text"
998:             onClick={() => void remove().catch((e) => notify(errorMessage(e)))}
999:           >
1000:             Remove title
1001:           </button>
1002:           <button className="secondary" onClick={() => setRemoveId(undefined)}>
1003:             Cancel
1004:           </button>
1005:         </div>
1006:       </Modal>
1007:     </>
1008:   );
1009: }
1010: export default function App() {
1011:   const [settings, setSettingsState] = useState<Settings>(defaults);
1012:   const [library, setLibrary] = useState<Summary[]>([]);
1013:   const [ready, setReady] = useState(false);
1014:   const [settingsOpen, setSettingsOpen] = useState(false);
1015:   const [notice, notify] = useState('');
1016:   const [updateReady, setUpdateReady] = useState(hasUpdate());
1017:   const [updating, setUpdating] = useState(false);
1018:   const [updateError, setUpdateError] = useState('');
1019:   const fileInput = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
1020:   const imageInput = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
1021:   const pending = useRef<Summary | Manga | undefined>(undefined);
1022:   const standardOnly = useRef(false);
1023:   useInterfaceMotion(settings.reduced);
1024:   const refresh = async () => setLibrary(await listLibrary());
1025: 
1026:   const {
1027:     state: importState,
1028:     isBusy: busy,
1029:     scanState: scan,
1030:     discover,
1031:     runImport,
1032:     cancel,
1033:     dismiss: dismissImport,
1034:   } = useImport({
1035:     onRefresh: refresh,
1036:     onNotify: notify,
1037:   });
1038:   useEffect(() => {
1039:     const blocked = () =>
1040:       notify(
1041:         'Close other Reader tabs to finish upgrading local storage. Your data will be preserved.',
1042:       );
1043:     window.addEventListener('reader-storage-blocked', blocked);
1044:     const onSync = () => {
1045:       void refresh();
1046:     };
1047:     syncChannel?.addEventListener('message', onSync);
1048: 
1049:     const init = async () => {
1050:       try {
1051:         if (desktopAPI.isDesktop) {
1052:           await ensureLibraryMigrated();
1053:         }
1054:         await Promise.all([
1055:           refresh(),
1056:           getStoredSettings().then((s) => {
1057:             if (s) {
1058:               setSettingsState(s);
1059:             }
1060:           }),
1061:         ]);
1062:         void resumeBackground().catch(() => { });
1063:       } catch (e) {
1064:         notify(errorMessage(e));
1065:       } finally {
1066:         setReady(true);
1067:       }
1068:     };
1069:     void init();
1070: 
1071:     return () => {
1072:       window.removeEventListener('reader-storage-blocked', blocked);
1073:       syncChannel?.removeEventListener('message', onSync);
1074:     };
1075:   }, []);
1076:   useEffect(() => {
1077:     const update = () => setUpdateReady(hasUpdate());
1078:     window.addEventListener('reader-update-ready', update);
1079:     update();
1080:     return () => window.removeEventListener('reader-update-ready', update);
1081:   }, []);
1082:   function setSettings(s: Settings) {
1083:     const validated = parseSettings(s);
1084:     setSettingsState(validated);
1085:     void saveStoredSettings(validated).catch((e) => notify(errorMessage(e)));
1086:   }
1087:   useEffect(() => {
1088:     const media = matchMedia('(prefers-color-scheme: dark)');
1089:     const update = () => {
1090:       document.documentElement.classList.toggle(
1091:         'dark',
1092:         settings.theme === 'dark' || (settings.theme === 'system' && media.matches),
1093:       );
1094:       document.documentElement.classList.toggle('reduced', settings.reduced);
1095:       document.documentElement.style.setProperty(
1096:         '--font-size-ui',
1097:         `${settings.fontSizeUi ?? 15}px`,
1098:       );
1099:       document.documentElement.style.setProperty(
1100:         '--font-size-title',
1101:         `${settings.fontSizeTitle ?? 15}px`,
1102:       );
1103:       document.documentElement.style.setProperty(
1104:         '--font-size-button',
1105:         `${settings.fontSizeButton ?? 15}px`,
1106:       );
1107:     };
1108:     update();
1109:     media.addEventListener('change', update);
1110:     return () => media.removeEventListener('change', update);
1111:   }, [settings]);
1112:   useEffect(() => {
1113:     if (!notice) return;
1114:     const timer = setTimeout(() => notify(''), 3000);
1115:     return () => clearTimeout(timer);
1116:   }, [notice]);
1117:   async function pick(m?: Summary | Manga) {
1118:     if (busy) return;
1119:     pending.current = m;
1120:     if (desktopAPI.isDesktop) {
1121:       let folderPath: string | null = null;
1122:       try {
1123:         folderPath = await desktopAPI.selectFolder();
1124:       } catch {
1125:         // dialog error
1126:       }
1127:       if (!folderPath) return;
1128:       await discover(async (opts) => [await fromDesktopFolder(folderPath!, opts)], m);
1129:       return;
1130:     }
1131:     const picker = (window as unknown as { showDirectoryPicker?: () => Promise<Directory> })
1132:       .showDirectoryPicker;
1133:     const standard = () => {
1134:       if (fileInput.current && 'webkitdirectory' in fileInput.current) fileInput.current.click();
1135:       else imageInput.current?.click();
1136:     };
1137:     if (!picker || window.self !== window.top || standardOnly.current) {
1138:       standard();
1139:       return;
1140:     }
1141:     try {
1142:       const handle = await picker.call(window);
1143:       await discover(async (options) => [await fromHandle(handle, options)], m);
1144:     } catch (e) {
1145:       if (e instanceof DOMException && e.name === 'AbortError') return;
1146:       standardOnly.current = true;
1147:       if (navigator.userActivation?.isActive) standard();
1148:       else notify('Direct folder selection is unavailable. Click again to use standard selection.');
1149:     }
1150:   }
1151: 
1152:   const drop = (items: DataTransferItem[]) => {
1153:     if (busy) return;
1154:     void discover(async (options) => {
1155:       const read = droppedSources(items, options);
1156:       return await read();
1157:     });
1158:   };
1159: 
1160:   useEffect(() => {
1161:     if (!desktopAPI.isDesktop || !desktopAPI.onDragDrop) return;
1162:     return desktopAPI.onDragDrop((paths) => {
1163:       if (busy || !paths.length) return;
1164:       void discover(async (options) => {
1165:         const sources = [];
1166:         for (const p of paths) {
1167:           try {
1168:             sources.push(await fromDesktopFolder(p, options));
1169:           } catch (e) {
1170:             console.warn('Failed to import dropped folder:', p, e);
1171:           }
1172:         }
1173:         return sources;
1174:       });
1175:     });
1176:   }, [busy, discover]);
1177:   useEffect(() => {
1178:     const context = (
1179:       document as unknown as {
1180:         modelContext?: { registerTool: (tool: unknown, options: unknown) => Promise<void> };
1181:       }
1182:     ).modelContext;
1183:     if (!context) return;
1184:     const lifecycle = new AbortController();
1185:     void Promise.resolve(
1186:       context.registerTool(
1187:         {
1188:           name: 'list_local_manga',
1189:           description: 'Read local library titles and chapter progress.',
1190:           inputSchema: { type: 'object', properties: {}, additionalProperties: false },
1191:           annotations: { readOnlyHint: true, untrustedContentHint: true },
1192:           execute: async (input: unknown) => {
1193:             if (!input || typeof input !== 'object' || Object.keys(input).length)
1194:               throw new Error('Expected an empty object.');
1195:             return (await listLibrary()).map((m) => ({
1196:               id: m.id,
1197:               title: m.title,
1198:               chapters: m.chapterCount,
1199:               read: m.chaptersRead,
1200:             }));
1201:           },
1202:         },
1203:         { signal: lifecycle.signal },
1204:       ),
1205:     ).catch(() => { });
1206:     return () => lifecycle.abort();
1207:   }, []);
1208:   function exportReport() {
1209:     if (importState.status !== 'complete') return;
1210:     const url = URL.createObjectURL(
1211:       new Blob([importState.report.issues.map((i) => `${i.path}: ${i.reason}`).join('\n')], {
1212:         type: 'text/plain',
1213:       }),
1214:     );
1215:     const a = document.createElement('a');
1216:     a.href = url;
1217:     a.download = 'manga-report.txt';
1218:     a.click();
1219:     setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(url), 1000);
1220:   }
1221:   return (
1222:     <AppContext.Provider
1223:       value={{
1224:         settings,
1225:         setSettings,
1226:         library,
1227:         refresh,
1228:         notify,
1229:         pick,
1230:         openSettings: () => setSettingsOpen(true),
1231:       }}
1232:     >
1233:       <input
1234:         hidden
1235:         ref={fileInput}
1236:         type="file"
1237:         multiple
1238:         {...{ webkitdirectory: '' }}
1239:         onChange={(e) => {
1240:           const files = Array.from(e.target.files || []);
1241:           if (files.length) void discover(async () => fromFiles(files), pending.current);
1242:           e.target.value = '';
1243:         }}
1244:       />
1245:       <input
1246:         hidden
1247:         ref={imageInput}
1248:         type="file"
1249:         multiple
1250:         accept="image/*,.heic,.heif,.tif,.tiff"
1251:         onChange={(e) => {
1252:           const files = Array.from(e.target.files || []);
1253:           if (files.length) void discover(async () => fromFiles(files), pending.current);
1254:           e.target.value = '';
1255:         }}
1256:       />
1257:       {ready ? (
1258:         <Suspense
1259:           fallback={
1260:             <div className="loading-screen">
1261:               <LoaderCircle className="spin" /> Opening…
1262:             </div>
1263:           }
1264:         >
1265:           <Routes>
1266:             <Route path="/bookmarks" element={<BookmarksPage />} />
1267:             <Route
1268:               path="/"
1269:               element={<Library busy={busy} scan={scan} drop={drop} cancel={cancel} />}
1270:             />
1271:             <Route
1272:               path="/index.html"
1273:               element={<Library busy={busy} scan={scan} drop={drop} cancel={cancel} />}
1274:             />
1275:             <Route
1276:               path="/read/:mangaId"
1277:               element={
1278:                 <Reader
1279:                   indexing={busy}
1280:                   indexingProgress={[scan.done, scan.total]}
1281:                   cancelImport={cancel}
1282:                 />
1283:               }
1284:             />
1285:             <Route path="/:slug" element={<MangaDetails />} />
1286:             <Route
1287:               path="/:slug/:chapterSegment"
1288:               element={
1289:                 <Reader
1290:                   indexing={busy}
1291:                   indexingProgress={[scan.done, scan.total]}
1292:                   cancelImport={cancel}
1293:                 />
1294:               }
1295:             />
1296:             <Route
1297:               path="*"
1298:               element={<Library busy={busy} scan={scan} drop={drop} cancel={cancel} />}
1299:             />
1300:           </Routes>
1301:         </Suspense>
1302:       ) : (
1303:         <div className="loading-screen">
1304:           <LoaderCircle className="spin" /> Opening your library…
1305:         </div>
1306:       )}
1307:       {settingsOpen && (
1308:         <Suspense fallback={null}>
1309:           <SettingsPanel open={settingsOpen} onClose={() => setSettingsOpen(false)} />
1310:         </Suspense>
1311:       )}
1312:       {importState.status === 'preview' && (
1313:         <ImportDialog
1314:           sources={importState.preview.sources}

266:   function getSortedMarks(items: Mark[], sort: BookmarkSort): Mark[] {
267:     const list = [...items];
268:     switch (sort) {
269:       case 'newest':
270:         return list.sort((a, b) => {
271:           const timeA = a.position.timestamp || a.manga.updatedAt || a.manga.addedAt || 0;
272:           const timeB = b.position.timestamp || b.manga.updatedAt || b.manga.addedAt || 0;
273:           return timeB - timeA;
274:         });
275:       case 'oldest':
276:         return list.sort((a, b) => {
277:           const timeA = a.position.timestamp || a.manga.addedAt || a.manga.updatedAt || 0;
278:           const timeB = b.position.timestamp || b.manga.addedAt || b.manga.updatedAt || 0;
279:           return timeA - timeB;
280:         });
281:       case 'title-asc':
282:         return list.sort((a, b) => {
283:           const cmp = bookmarkCollator.compare(a.manga.title, b.manga.title);
284:           if (cmp !== 0) return cmp;
285:           return a.chapter - b.chapter || a.position.page - b.position.page;
286:         });
287:       case 'title-desc':
288:         return list.sort((a, b) => {
289:           const cmp = bookmarkCollator.compare(b.manga.title, a.manga.title);
290:           if (cmp !== 0) return cmp;
291:           return a.chapter - b.chapter || a.position.page - b.position.page;
292:         });
293:       case 'chapter':
294:         return list.sort((a, b) => {
295:           const cmp = bookmarkCollator.compare(a.manga.title, b.manga.title);
296:           if (cmp !== 0) return cmp;
297:           return a.chapter - b.chapter || a.position.page - b.position.page;
298:         });
299:       default:
300:         return list;
301:     }
302:   }

24: export function welcomeForDate(date: Date) {
25:   const day = Math.floor(Date.UTC(date.getFullYear(), date.getMonth(), date.getDate()) / 86400000);
26:   return welcomeMessages[
27:     ((day % welcomeMessages.length) + welcomeMessages.length) % welcomeMessages.length
28:   ];
29: }

31:   const [message, setMessage] = useState(() => welcomeForDate(new Date()));
32:   useEffect(() => {
33:     let timer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout>;
34:     const update = () => {
35:       clearTimeout(timer);
36:       const now = new Date();
37:       setMessage(welcomeForDate(now));
38:       const midnight = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth(), now.getDate() + 1);
39:       timer = setTimeout(
40:         update,
41:         Math.max(1000, Math.min(midnight.getTime() - now.getTime() + 50, 60000)),
42:       );
43:     };
44:     update();
45:     document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', update);
46:     window.addEventListener('focus', update);
47:     return () => {
48:       clearTimeout(timer);
49:       document.removeEventListener('visibilitychange', update);
50:       window.removeEventListener('focus', update);
51:     };
52:   }, []);

=== FILE: C:\Users\VKPs\Documents\manga-reader\src\diagnostics.ts ===
1: export type DiagnosticLevel = 'info' | 'warn' | 'error';
2: 
3: export interface DiagnosticRecord {
4:   id: string;
5:   timestamp: number;
6:   operation: 'import' | 'cover' | 'background' | 'storage' | 'update' | 'reader';
7:   level: DiagnosticLevel;
8:   message: string;
9:   code?: string;
10:   safeIdentifier?: string;
11:   retryable?: boolean;
12:   durationMs?: number;
13: }
14: 
15: const MAX_DIAGNOSTICS = 50;
16: const ringBuffer: DiagnosticRecord[] = [];
17: const subscribers = new Set<() => void>();
18: 
19: let nextId = 1;
20: 
21: export function sanitizeIdentifier(raw?: string): string | undefined {
22:   if (!raw) return undefined;
23:   const normalized = raw.replace(/\\/g, '/');
24:   const parts = normalized.split('/').filter(Boolean);
25:   return parts.slice(-2).join('/');
26: }
27: 
28: export function recordDiagnostic(
29:   entry: Omit<DiagnosticRecord, 'id' | 'timestamp'>,
30: ): DiagnosticRecord | null {
31:   if (
32:     entry.code === 'AbortError' ||
33:     entry.message.includes('Import cancelled') ||
34:     entry.message.includes('aborted')
35:   ) {
36:     return null;
37:   }
38: 
39:   const now = Date.now();
40:   const recent = ringBuffer[ringBuffer.length - 1];
41:   if (
42:     recent &&
43:     recent.operation === entry.operation &&
44:     recent.level === entry.level &&
45:     recent.message === entry.message &&
46:     now - recent.timestamp < 2000
47:   ) {
48:     return recent;
49:   }
50: 
51:   const record: DiagnosticRecord = {
52:     id: `diag-${nextId++}`,
53:     timestamp: now,
54:     ...entry,
55:     safeIdentifier: sanitizeIdentifier(entry.safeIdentifier),
56:   };
57: 
58:   ringBuffer.push(record);
59:   if (ringBuffer.length > MAX_DIAGNOSTICS) {
60:     ringBuffer.shift();
61:   }
62: 
63:   for (const listener of subscribers) {
64:     try {
65:       listener();
66:     } catch { }
67:   }
68: 
69:   return record;
70: }
71: 
72: export function getDiagnostics(): DiagnosticRecord[] {
73:   return [...ringBuffer];
74: }
75: 
76: export function clearDiagnostics(): void {
77:   ringBuffer.length = 0;
78:   for (const listener of subscribers) {
79:     try {
80:       listener();
81:     } catch { }
82:   }
83: }
84: 
85: export function subscribeDiagnostics(listener: () => void): () => void {
86:   subscribers.add(listener);
87:   return () => {
88:     subscribers.delete(listener);
89:   };
90: }

5: type Props = Omit<

=== FILE: C:\Users\VKPs\Documents\manga-reader\src\imageDimensions.ts ===
1: import { limits, assertImageDimensions, withinTime } from './limits';
2: 
3: const dimensionCache = new Map<string, { width: number; height: number }>();
4: const MAX_DIMENSION_CACHE = 1000;
5: 
6: export function clearDimensionCache(): void {
7:   dimensionCache.clear();
8: }
9: 
10: export async function headerDimensions(
11:   file: File,
12: ): Promise<{ width: number; height: number } | undefined> {
13:   const cacheKey = `${file.name}:${file.size}:${file.lastModified}`;
14:   const cached = dimensionCache.get(cacheKey);
15:   if (cached) return cached;
16: 
17:   const b = new Uint8Array(
18:     await withinTime(
19:       file.slice(0, limits.headerBytes).arrayBuffer(),
20:       limits.ioTimeoutMs,
21:       'Reading image header',
22:     ),
23:   );
24:   const v = new DataView(b.buffer);
25:   let width = 0,
26:     height = 0;
27: 
28:   if (b.length >= 24 && v.getUint32(0) === 0x89504e47 && v.getUint32(4) === 0x0d0a1a0a) {
29:     width = v.getUint32(16);
30:     height = v.getUint32(20);
31:   } else if (b.length >= 10 && String.fromCharCode(...b.slice(0, 6)).match(/^GIF8[79]a$/)) {
32:     width = v.getUint16(6, true);
33:     height = v.getUint16(8, true);
34:   } else if (
35:     b.length >= 25 &&
36:     v.getUint32(0) === 0x52494646 &&
37:     v.getUint32(8) === 0x57454250
38:   ) {
39:     const chunkType = v.getUint32(12);
40:     if (
41:       b.length >= 30 &&
42:       chunkType === 0x56503820 &&
43:       b[23] === 0x9d &&
44:       b[24] === 0x01 &&
45:       b[25] === 0x2a
46:     ) {
47:       width = v.getUint16(26, true) & 0x3fff;
48:       height = v.getUint16(28, true) & 0x3fff;
49:     } else if (chunkType === 0x5650384c && b[20] === 0x2f) {
50:       const bits = v.getUint32(21, true);
51:       width = (bits & 0x3fff) + 1;
52:       height = ((bits >> 14) & 0x3fff) + 1;
53:     } else if (b.length >= 30 && chunkType === 0x56503858) {

55:       height = 1 + (b[27] | (b[28] << 8) | (b[29] << 16));
56:     }
57:   } else if (b.length > 4 && b[0] === 255 && b[1] === 216) {

61:       if (b[i++] !== 255) break;
62:       while (i < b.length && b[i] === 255) i++;
63:       const marker = b[i++];
64:       if (marker === 0xda || marker === 0xd9) break;
65:       if (marker === 1 || (marker >= 0xd0 && marker <= 0xd7)) continue;
66:       if (i + 2 > b.length) break;

68:       if (length < 2 || i + length > b.length) break;

92:                   if (orientation >= 5 && orientation <= 8) {
93:                     swapForExif = true;
94:                   }
95:                   break;
96:                 }
97:               }
98:             }
99:           }
100:         } catch { }
101:       }

103:       if (

105:           marker,
106:         ) &&
107:         length >= 7

110:         width = v.getUint16(i + 5);
111:         if (swapForExif) {
112:           const tmp = width;
113:           width = height;
114:           height = tmp;
115:         }
116:         break;
117:       }
118:       i += length;
119:     }
120:   }

123:     assertImageDimensions(width, height);
124:     const result = { width, height };
125:     if (dimensionCache.size >= MAX_DIMENSION_CACHE) {
126:       const oldest = dimensionCache.keys().next().value;
127:       if (oldest) dimensionCache.delete(oldest);
128:     }
129:     dimensionCache.set(cacheKey, result);
130:     return result;
131:   }
132:   return undefined;

87: export class ImageQueue {
88:   private queue: QueuedItem[] = [];
89:   private waiting = new Map<string, QueuedItem>();
90:   private activeCount = 0;
91:   private inFlight = new Map<string, QueuedItem>();
92:   private queueDirty = false;
93:   private nextSubscriberId = 0;
94: 
95:   get pendingCount(): number {
96:     return this.queue.length;
97:   }
98: 
99:   get runningCount(): number {
100:     return this.activeCount;
101:   }
102: 
103:   private getMaxConcurrency(): number {
104:     if (getIsMobile()) {
105:       return limits.mobilePageConcurrency;
106:     }
107:     return getHardwareConcurrencyLimit();
108:   }
109: 
110:   reprioritize(key: string, priority: number): void {
111:     const item = this.waiting.get(key);
112:     if (item && item.priority !== priority) {
113:       item.priority = priority;
114:       this.queueDirty = true;
115:     }
116:   }
117: 
118:   enqueue(options: ImageTaskOptions): CancelHandle | null {
119:     const { key, priority = 0, run, onComplete, onError } = options;
120:     const subId = ++this.nextSubscriberId;
121:     const sub: Subscriber = { onComplete, onError };
122: 
123:     const existingWaiting = this.waiting.get(key);
124:     if (existingWaiting) {
125:       existingWaiting.subscribers.set(subId, sub);
126:       if (priority > existingWaiting.priority) {
127:         existingWaiting.priority = priority;
128:         this.queueDirty = true;
129:       }
130:       return () => this.cancelSubscriber(key, subId);
131:     }
132: 
133:     const existingInFlight = this.inFlight.get(key);
134:     if (existingInFlight && !existingInFlight.canceled) {
135:       existingInFlight.subscribers.set(subId, sub);
136:       return () => this.cancelSubscriber(key, subId);
137:     }
138: 
139:     if (this.queue.length >= limits.queuedJobs) {
140:       return null;
141:     }
142: 
143:     const item: QueuedItem = {
144:       key,
145:       priority,
146:       primaryRun: run,
147:       subscribers: new Map([[subId, sub]]),
148:       controller: new AbortController(),
149:       canceled: false,
150:     };
151: 
152:     this.queue.push(item);
153:     this.waiting.set(key, item);
154:     this.queueDirty = true;
155:     this.pump();
156: 
157:     return () => this.cancelSubscriber(key, subId);
158:   }
159: 
160:   private cancelSubscriber(key: string, subId: number): void {
161:     const waitingItem = this.waiting.get(key);
162:     if (waitingItem) {
163:       waitingItem.subscribers.delete(subId);
164:       if (waitingItem.subscribers.size === 0) {
165:         this.waiting.delete(key);
166:         waitingItem.canceled = true;
167:         waitingItem.controller.abort();
168:         const index = this.queue.indexOf(waitingItem);
169:         if (index >= 0) {
170:           this.queue.splice(index, 1);
171:         }
172:       }
173:       return;
174:     }
175: 
176:     const running = this.inFlight.get(key);
177:     if (running) {
178:       running.subscribers.delete(subId);
179:       if (running.subscribers.size === 0) {
180:         running.canceled = true;
181:         running.controller.abort();
182:       }
183:     }
184:   }
185: 
186:   cancel(key: string): void {
187:     const waitingItem = this.waiting.get(key);
188:     if (waitingItem) {
189:       this.waiting.delete(key);
190:       waitingItem.canceled = true;
191:       waitingItem.controller.abort();
192:       const index = this.queue.indexOf(waitingItem);
193:       if (index >= 0) {
194:         this.queue.splice(index, 1);
195:       }
196:       return;
197:     }
198: 
199:     const running = this.inFlight.get(key);
200:     if (running) {
201:       running.canceled = true;
202:       running.controller.abort();
203:     }
204:   }
205: 
206:   clear(): void {
207:     for (const item of this.queue) {
208:       item.canceled = true;
209:       item.controller.abort();
210:     }
211:     this.queue.length = 0;
212:     this.waiting.clear();
213:     this.queueDirty = false;
214: 
215:     for (const running of this.inFlight.values()) {
216:       running.canceled = true;
217:       running.controller.abort();
218:     }
219:   }
220: 
221:   private ensureSorted(): void {
222:     if (!this.queueDirty) return;
223:     for (let i = 1; i < this.queue.length; i++) {
224:       const item = this.queue[i];
225:       let j = i - 1;
226:       while (j >= 0 && this.queue[j].priority < item.priority) {
227:         this.queue[j + 1] = this.queue[j];
228:         j--;
229:       }
230:       this.queue[j + 1] = item;
231:     }
232:     this.queueDirty = false;
233:   }
234: 
235:   private pump(): void {
236:     const maxConcurrency = this.getMaxConcurrency();
237:     this.ensureSorted();
238: 
239:     while (this.activeCount < maxConcurrency && this.queue.length > 0) {
240:       const item = this.queue.shift();
241:       if (!item) break;
242: 
243:       this.waiting.delete(item.key);
244: 
245:       if (item.canceled || item.subscribers.size === 0) {
246:         continue;
247:       }
248: 
249:       this.activeCount++;
250:       this.inFlight.set(item.key, item);
251: 
252:       void (async () => {
253:         try {
254:           if (!item.canceled && !item.controller.signal.aborted) {
255:             await item.primaryRun(item.controller.signal);
256: 
257:             if (!item.canceled && !item.controller.signal.aborted) {
258:               for (const sub of Array.from(item.subscribers.values())) {
259:                 try {
260:                   sub.onComplete?.();
261:                 } catch (e) {
262:                   console.error(`[imageQueue] Task ${item.key} onComplete error:`, e);
263:                 }
264:               }
265:             }
266:           }
267:         } catch (err) {
268:           if (!item.controller.signal.aborted) {
269:             console.warn(`[imageQueue] Task ${item.key} failed:`, err);
270:             for (const sub of Array.from(item.subscribers.values())) {
271:               try {
272:                 sub.onError?.(err);
273:               } catch (e) {
274:                 console.error(`[imageQueue] Task ${item.key} onError error:`, e);
275:               }
276:             }
277:           }
278:         } finally {
279:           this.activeCount = Math.max(0, this.activeCount - 1);
280:           if (this.inFlight.get(item.key) === item) {
281:             this.inFlight.delete(item.key);
282:           }
283:           this.pump();
284:         }
285:       })();
286:     }
287:   }
288: }

57:   names,
58:   value,
59:   onChange,
60:   id,
61: }: {
62:   names: string[];
63:   value: number;
64:   onChange: (i: number) => void;
65:   id: string;
66: }) {
67:   return (
68:     <div className="settings-tabs example-tabs" role="tablist" aria-label={id}>
69:       {names.map((n, i) => (
70:         <button
71:           key={n}
72:           type="button"
73:           role="tab"
74:           id={id + '-' + i}
75:           aria-selected={value === i}
76:           aria-controls={id + '-panel'}
77:           tabIndex={value === i ? 0 : -1}
78:           onClick={() => onChange(i)}
79:           onKeyDown={(e) => {
80:             const next =
81:               e.key === 'ArrowRight'
82:                 ? (i + 1) % names.length
83:                 : e.key === 'ArrowLeft'
84:                   ? (i + names.length - 1) % names.length
85:                   : e.key === 'Home'
86:                     ? 0
87:                     : e.key === 'End'
88:                       ? names.length - 1
89:                       : -1;
90:             if (next < 0) return;
91:             e.preventDefault();
92:             onChange(next);

251: export function ImportDialog({
252:   sources,
253:   target,
254:   library,
255:   onClose,
256:   onImport,
257: }: {
258:   sources: Source[];
259:   target?: Manga;
260:   library: Summary[];
261:   onClose: () => void;
262:   onImport: (plans: ImportPlan[]) => void;
263: }) {
264:   const [modes, setModes] = useState<boolean[]>(sources.map(() => false));
265:   const [plans, setPlans] = useState<ImportPlan[]>([]);
266:   const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
267:   const [confirmed, setConfirmed] = useState(false);
268:   const [contentMismatch, setContentMismatch] = useState(false);
269:   const [targetTitles, setTargetTitles] = useState<Map<string, string>>(new Map());
270:   const [error, setError] = useState('');
271:   const [checking, setChecking] = useState(false);
272: 
273:   useEffect(() => {
274:     let live = true;
275:     setLoading(true);
276:     setError('');
277:     void (async () => {
278:       const list: ImportPlan[] = [];
279:       for (let i = 0; i < sources.length; i++) {
280:         const children = modes[i] ? await splitSource(sources[i]) : [sources[i]];
281:         for (const source of children) {
282:           let duplicate: Summary | undefined;
283:           for (const candidate of library) {
284:             if (source.handle && candidate.handle) {
285:               try {
286:                 if (await source.handle.isSameEntry(candidate.handle)) {
287:                   duplicate = candidate;
288:                   break;
289:                 }
290:               } catch { }
291:             } else if (source.localPath && candidate.localPath) {
292:               try {
293:                 const same = desktopAPI.isSamePath
294:                   ? await desktopAPI.isSamePath(source.localPath, candidate.localPath)
295:                   : source.localPath.toLowerCase().replace(/[\\/]+$/, '') ===
296:                   candidate.localPath.toLowerCase().replace(/[\\/]+$/, '');
297:                 if (same) {
298:                   duplicate = candidate;
299:                   break;
300:                 }
301:               } catch { }
302:             }
303:           }
304:           list.push({
305:             source,
306:             targetId: target?.id || duplicate?.id,
307:             sort: target?.sort || 'natural',
308:           });
309:         }
310:       }
311:       if (live) {
312:         setPlans(list);
313:         setLoading(false);
314:       }
315:     })().catch(() => {
316:       if (live) {
317:         setError('Could not prepare the import preview. Re-select the folders.');
318:         setLoading(false);
319:       }
320:     });
321:     return () => {
322:       live = false;
323:     };
324:   }, [sources, modes, library, target]);
325: 
326:   const structuralKey = useMemo(
327:     () => plans.map((p, idx) => `${idx}:${p.targetId ?? 'new'}:${p.sort ?? 'natural'}`).join('|'),
328:     [plans],
329:   );
330: 
331:   useEffect(() => {
332:     if (loading) return;
333:     let live = true;
334:     const controller = new AbortController();
335:     setConfirmed(false);
336:     setContentMismatch(false);
337:     setChecking(true);
338:     void (async () => {
339:       const titlesMap = new Map<string, string>();
340:       let hasPageMismatch = false;
341:       for (const plan of plans) {
342:         await warmSources([plan.source], { signal: controller.signal }, plan.sort);
343:         if (!plan.targetId) continue;
344:         const previous = target?.id === plan.targetId ? target : await getManga(plan.targetId);
345:         if (!previous) continue;
346:         titlesMap.set(plan.targetId, previous.title);
347:         const oldPages = previous.chapters.flatMap((c) => c.pages);
348:         const sample = oldPages
349:           .filter((_, i) => i % Math.max(1, Math.floor(oldPages.length / 24)) === 0)
350:           .slice(0, 24);
351:         let matched = 0;
352:         for (const page of sample) {
353:           const candidate = plan.source.files.get(page.path);
354:           if (!candidate) continue;
355:           try {
356:             const cand = candidate as any;
357:             const candidateSize =
358:               typeof cand.size === 'number'
359:                 ? cand.size
360:                 : candidate instanceof File
361:                   ? candidate.size
362:                   : undefined;
363:             const candidateModified =
364:               typeof cand.modified === 'number'
365:                 ? cand.modified
366:                 : typeof cand.lastModified === 'number'
367:                   ? cand.lastModified
368:                   : candidate instanceof File
369:                     ? candidate.lastModified
370:                     : undefined;
371: 
372:             if (candidateSize !== undefined && candidateModified !== undefined) {
373:               if (
374:                 page.size !== undefined &&
375:                 page.size === candidateSize &&
376:                 page.modified === candidateModified
377:               ) {
378:                 matched++;
379:               }
380:             } else {
381:               const file = candidate instanceof File ? candidate : await candidate.getFile();
382:               if (
383:                 page.size !== undefined &&
384:                 page.size === file.size &&
385:                 page.modified === file.lastModified
386:               ) {
387:                 matched++;
388:               }
389:             }
390:           } catch { }
391:         }
392:         if (matched < sample.length * 0.5 || !sample.length) {
393:           hasPageMismatch = true;
394:         }
395:       }
396:       if (live) {
397:         setTargetTitles(titlesMap);
398:         setContentMismatch(hasPageMismatch);
399:       }
400:     })()
401:       .catch(() => {
402:         if (live) setContentMismatch(true);
403:       })
404:       .finally(() => {
405:         if (live) setChecking(false);
406:       });
407:     return () => {
408:       live = false;
409:       controller.abort();
410:     };
411:   }, [structuralKey, loading]);
412: 
413:   const titleMismatch = plans.some((p) => {
414:     if (!p.targetId) return false;
415:     const savedTitle =
416:       targetTitles.get(p.targetId) ?? library.find((m) => m.id === p.targetId)?.title;
417:     return savedTitle !== undefined && savedTitle !== p.source.title;
418:   });
419: 
420:   const mismatch = contentMismatch || titleMismatch;
421: 
422:   const duplicateTargets = plans.some(
423:     (p, i) => p.targetId && plans.some((q, j) => j < i && q.targetId === p.targetId),
424:   );
425:   return (
426:     <Modal
427:       open
428:       onClose={onClose}
429:       title="Review your import"
430:       description="Check the grouping and sorting before saving. Existing titles are changed only when you choose to update them."
431:     >
432:       {!target &&
433:         sources.map((source, i) => (
434:           <label className="import-choice" key={i}>
435:             <span>{source.title}</span>
436:             <Dropdown
437:               aria-label={'Folder grouping for ' + source.title}
438:               value={modes[i] ? 'collection' : 'single'}
439:               onChange={(e) =>
440:                 setModes((m) => m.map((v, j) => (j === i ? e.target.value === 'collection' : v)))

442:             >
443:               <option value="single">One title</option>
444:               <option value="collection">Multiple titles (one per subfolder)</option>
445:             </Dropdown>
446:           </label>
447:         ))}
448:       {loading ? (

450:       ) : error ? (
451:         <p role="alert">{error}</p>
452:       ) : (

455:           return (
456:             <section className="import-preview" key={i}>
457:               <label>
458:                 Title
459:                 <input
460:                   value={plan.source.title}
461:                   onChange={(e) =>
462:                     setPlans((p) =>
463:                       p.map((v, j) =>
464:                         j === i ? { ...v, source: { ...v.source, title: e.target.value } } : v,
465:                       ),
466:                     )
467:                   }
468:                 />
469:               </label>

471:                 <label>
472:                   Save as
473:                   <Dropdown
474:                     aria-label="Save as"
475:                     value={plan.targetId || 'new'}
476:                     onChange={(e) =>
477:                       setPlans((p) =>
478:                         p.map((v, j) =>
479:                           j === i
480:                             ? {
481:                               ...v,
482:                               targetId: e.target.value === 'new' ? undefined : e.target.value,
483:                             }
484:                             : v,
485:                         ),
486:                       )

27: export class LimitError extends Error {
28:   constructor(message: string) {
29:     super(message);
30:     this.name = 'LimitError';
31:   }
32: }

34: export function assertAtMost(value: number, maximum: number, label: string): void {
35:   if (!Number.isSafeInteger(value) || value < 0 || value > maximum) {
36:     throw new LimitError(`${label} exceeds the limit of ${maximum}. Use a smaller selection.`);
37:   }
38: }
39: 
40: export function assertPathLimits(path: string): void {
41:   assertAtMost(path.length, limits.pathChars, 'Relative path length (characters)');
42:   assertAtMost(path.split('/').length - 1, limits.directoryDepth, 'Folder depth');
43: }
44: 
45: export function assertImageDimensions(width: number, height: number): void {
46:   if (!Number.isSafeInteger(width) || !Number.isSafeInteger(height) || width < 1 || height < 1) {
47:     throw new LimitError('Image dimensions must be positive integer pixels.');
48:   }
49:   if (!Number.isSafeInteger(width * height)) {
50:     throw new LimitError('Image area cannot be represented safely.');
51:   }
52: }
53: 
54: export function assertMangaLimits(manga: Manga): number {
55:   assertAtMost(manga.chapters.length, limits.chaptersPerTitle, 'Chapters per title');
56:   let pages = 0;
57:   for (const chapter of manga.chapters) {
58:     pages += chapter.pages.length;
59:     assertAtMost(pages, limits.pagesPerTitle, 'Pages per title');
60:     assertAtMost(
61:       chapter.bookmarks?.length ?? 0,
62:       limits.bookmarksPerChapter,
63:       'Bookmarks per chapter',
64:     );
65:     for (const page of chapter.pages) {
66:       assertPathLimits(page.path);
67:       if (!(page.dimensionsPending && page.width === 0 && page.height === 0)) {
68:         assertImageDimensions(page.width, page.height);
69:       }
70:     }
71:   }
72:   return pages;
73: }

75: export function withinTime<T>(
76:   task: PromiseLike<T>,
77:   milliseconds: number,
78:   operation: string,
79:   signal?: AbortSignal,
80: ): Promise<T> {
81:   return new Promise<T>((resolve, reject) => {
82:     let settled = false;
83:     const finish = (success: boolean, value: unknown) => {
84:       if (settled) return;
85:       settled = true;
86:       clearTimeout(timer);
87:       signal?.removeEventListener('abort', abort);
88:       if (success) resolve(value as T);
89:       else reject(value);
90:     };
91:     const abort = () =>
92:       finish(false, signal?.reason ?? new DOMException('Cancelled', 'AbortError'));
93:     const timer = setTimeout(
94:       () => finish(false, new Error(`${operation} timed out. Please retry.`)),
95:       milliseconds,
96:     );
97:     signal?.addEventListener('abort', abort, { once: true });
98:     Promise.resolve(task).then(
99:       (value) => finish(true, value),
100:       (error) => finish(false, error),
101:     );
102:     if (signal?.aborted) abort();
103:   });
104: }

=== FILE: C:\Users\VKPs\Documents\manga-reader\src\objectUrlPool.ts ===
1: interface PoolEntry {
2:   url: string;
3:   refCount: number;
4:   inCache: boolean;
5: }
6: 
7: export class ObjectUrlPool {
8:   private entries = new Map<string, PoolEntry>();
9:   private readonly maxEntries: number;
10: 
11:   constructor(maxEntries = 32) {
12:     this.maxEntries = Math.max(1, maxEntries);
13:   }
14: 
15:   get size(): number {
16:     let count = 0;
17:     for (const entry of this.entries.values()) {
18:       if (entry.inCache) count++;
19:     }
20:     return count;
21:   }
22: 
23:   get totalAllocated(): number {
24:     return this.entries.size;
25:   }
26: 
27:   get(key: string): string | undefined {
28:     const entry = this.entries.get(key);
29:     if (!entry || !entry.inCache) return undefined;
30:     this.entries.delete(key);
31:     this.entries.set(key, entry);
32:     return entry.url;
33:   }
34: 
35:   retain(key: string): () => void {
36:     const entry = this.entries.get(key);
37:     if (!entry) return () => { };
38:     entry.refCount++;
39:     let done = false;
40:     return () => {
41:       if (done) return;
42:       done = true;
43:       entry.refCount = Math.max(0, entry.refCount - 1);
44:       if (!entry.inCache && entry.refCount <= 0) {
45:         if (this.entries.get(key) === entry) this.entries.delete(key);
46:         try {
47:           URL.revokeObjectURL(entry.url);
48:         } catch { }
49:       }
50:     };
51:   }
52: 
53:   release(key: string): void {
54:     const entry = this.entries.get(key);
55:     if (!entry) return;
56:     entry.refCount = Math.max(0, entry.refCount - 1);
57:     if (!entry.inCache && entry.refCount <= 0) {
58:       if (this.entries.get(key) === entry) this.entries.delete(key);
59:       try {
60:         URL.revokeObjectURL(entry.url);
61:       } catch { }
62:     }
63:   }
64: 
65:   set(key: string, url: string): void {
66:     const existing = this.entries.get(key);
67:     if (existing) {
68:       this.entries.delete(key);
69:       if (existing.url !== url) {
70:         existing.inCache = false;
71:         if (existing.refCount <= 0) {
72:           try {
73:             URL.revokeObjectURL(existing.url);
74:           } catch { }
75:         }
76:       } else {
77:         existing.inCache = true;
78:         this.entries.set(key, existing);
79:         return;
80:       }
81:     } else if (this.size >= this.maxEntries) {
82:       for (const [oldKey, oldEntry] of this.entries.entries()) {
83:         if (oldEntry.inCache) {
84:           oldEntry.inCache = false;
85:           if (oldEntry.refCount <= 0) {
86:             this.entries.delete(oldKey);
87:             try {
88:               URL.revokeObjectURL(oldEntry.url);
89:             } catch { }
90:           }
91:           break;
92:         }
93:       }
94:     }
95: 
96:     this.entries.set(key, {
97:       url,
98:       refCount: 0,
99:       inCache: true,
100:     });
101:   }
102: 
103:   has(key: string): boolean {
104:     const entry = this.entries.get(key);
105:     return Boolean(entry && entry.inCache);
106:   }
107: 
108:   revoke(key: string): void {
109:     const entry = this.entries.get(key);
110:     if (entry) {
111:       entry.inCache = false;
112:       if (entry.refCount <= 0) {
113:         this.entries.delete(key);
114:         try {
115:           URL.revokeObjectURL(entry.url);
116:         } catch { }
117:       }
118:     }
119:   }
120: 
121:   clear(): void {
122:     for (const [key, entry] of Array.from(this.entries.entries())) {
123:       entry.inCache = false;
124:       if (entry.refCount <= 0) {
125:         this.entries.delete(key);
126:         try {
127:           URL.revokeObjectURL(entry.url);
128:         } catch { }
129:       }
130:     }
131:   }
132: }
133: 
134: export function getAdaptivePoolSize(): number {
135:   if (typeof navigator === 'undefined') return 32;
136:   const nav = navigator as Navigator & { deviceMemory?: number; hardwareConcurrency?: number };
137:   const mem = nav.deviceMemory ?? 4;
138:   const cores = nav.hardwareConcurrency ?? 4;
139:   if (mem <= 2 || cores <= 2) return 12;
140:   if (mem <= 4 || cores <= 4) return 24;
141:   if (mem >= 8 && cores >= 8) return 64;
142:   return 40;
143: }
144: 
145: export const defaultObjectUrlPool = new ObjectUrlPool(getAdaptivePoolSize());
146: 
147: export function getObjectUrl(key: string): string | undefined {
148:   return defaultObjectUrlPool.get(key);
149: }
150: 
151: export function storeObjectUrl(key: string, url: string): void {
152:   defaultObjectUrlPool.set(key, url);
153: }
154: 
155: export function retainObjectUrl(key: string): () => void {
156:   return defaultObjectUrlPool.retain(key);
157: }
158: 
159: export function releaseObjectUrl(key: string): void {
160:   defaultObjectUrlPool.release(key);
161: }
162: 
163: export function clearObjectUrlPool(): void {
164:   defaultObjectUrlPool.clear();
165: }

10: let observer: IntersectionObserver | undefined;
11: 
12: function observe(element: Element, callback: (visible: boolean) => void) {
13:   observer ??= new IntersectionObserver(
14:     (entries) => {
15:       for (const entry of entries) watched.get(entry.target)?.(entry.isIntersecting);
16:     },
17:     { rootMargin: '180% 0px' },
18:   );
19:   watched.set(element, callback);
20:   observer.observe(element);
21:   return () => {
22:     watched.delete(element);
23:     observer?.unobserve(element);
24:     if (!watched.size) {
25:       observer?.disconnect();
26:       observer = undefined;
27:     }
28:   };

1: export function normalizePath(path: string): string {
2:   if (!path) return '';
3:   const isUnc = path.startsWith('\\\\') || path.startsWith('//');
4:   const converted = path.replace(/\\/g, '/');
5:   if (isUnc) {
6:     const withoutUncPrefix = converted.replace(/^\/\/+/, '');
7:     const cleaned = withoutUncPrefix.replace(/\/+/g, '/').replace(/\/+$/, '');
8:     return '//' + cleaned;
9:   }
10:   return converted.replace(/\/+/g, '/').replace(/\/+$/, '');
11: }
12: 
13: export function normalizeRelativePath(path: string): string {
14:   if (!path) return '';
15:   return path.replace(/\\/g, '/').replace(/^\/+/, '').replace(/\/+/g, '/').replace(/\/+$/, '');
16: }
17: 
18: export function folderSlug(name: string) {
19:   return (
20:     name
21:       .normalize('NFKD')
22:       .replace(/[0-9#*]\uFE0F?\u20E3/g, '')
23:       .replace(
24:         /[\p{Extended_Pictographic}\p{Regional_Indicator}\p{Emoji_Modifier}\uFE0F\u200D\u{E0020}-\u{E007F}]/gu,
25:         '',
26:       )
27:       .replace(/([A-Za-z])\p{M}+/gu, '$1')
28:       .toLowerCase()
29:       .replace(/[^\p{L}\p{N}\p{M}]+/gu, '-')
30:       .replace(/^-+|-+$/g, '')
31:       .slice(0, 100)
32:       .replace(/-+$/, '')
33:       .normalize('NFC') || 'manga'
34:   );
35: }
36: export function uniqueSlug(name: string, used: Set<string>) {
37:   const base = folderSlug(name);
38:   const reserved = ['read', 'assets', 'api', 'bookmarks', 'index.html'];
39:   for (let attempt = 0; attempt <= used.size + reserved.length; attempt++) {
40:     const slug = attempt === 0 ? base : base + '-' + (attempt + 1);
41:     if (!used.has(slug) && !reserved.includes(slug)) return slug;
42:   }
43:   throw new Error('Could not allocate a unique title URL.');
44: }
45: export function mangaPath(
46:   m: { slug?: string; title: string; lastChapter: number },
47:   index = m.lastChapter,
48: ) {
49:   return '/' + encodeURIComponent(m.slug || folderSlug(m.title)) + '/ch' + (index + 1);
50: }
51: export function chapterIndex(segment: string | undefined, count: number, fallback: number) {
52:   const match = segment?.match(/^ch([1-9]\d*)$/);
53:   const n = match ? Number(match[1]) - 1 : fallback;
54:   return Math.max(0, Math.min(count - 1, Number.isFinite(n) ? Math.floor(n) : 0));
55: }
56: 
57: export function mangaDetailsPath(m: { slug?: string; title: string }) {
58:   return '/' + encodeURIComponent(m.slug || folderSlug(m.title)) + '/';
59: }

66: export interface PageStore {
67:   getPage: () => number;
68:   setPage: (page: number) => void;
69:   subscribe: (listener: () => void) => () => void;

72: export function createPageStore(initialPage = 0): PageStore {
73:   let current = initialPage;
74:   const listeners = new Set<() => void>();
75:   return {
76:     getPage: () => current,
77:     setPage: (page: number) => {
78:       if (current === page) return;
79:       current = page;
80:       for (const listener of listeners) {
81:         listener();
82:       }
83:     },
84:     subscribe: (listener: () => void) => {
85:       listeners.add(listener);
86:       return () => listeners.delete(listener);
87:     },
88:   };
89: }
