267:     if (location.pathname !== canonical || location.search) navigate(canonical, { replace: true });
268:   }, [manga.slug, ci, location.pathname, location.search, navigate]);
269:   const [allowed, setAllowed] = useState<boolean | null>(null),
270:     [bars, setBars] = useState({ top: true, bottom: true }),
271:     [singlePage, setSinglePage] = useState(0),
272:     [ready, setReady] = useState(false),
273:     [zoom, setZoom] = useState(1),
274:     [wide, setWide] = useState(matchMedia('(min-width:700px)').matches),
275:     [shortcuts, setShortcuts] = useState(false),
276:     [bookmarkRevision, setBookmarkRevision] = useState(0),
277:     [failure, setFailure] = useState(''),
278:     [readRevision, setReadRevision] = useState(0);
279:   const pageStoreRef = useRef<PageStore | null>(null);
280:   if (!pageStoreRef.current) {
281:     pageStoreRef.current = createPageStore(0);
282:   }
283:   const pageStore = pageStoreRef.current;
284:   const [prepared, setPrepared] = useState(
285:     chapter.pages.some((p) => p.dimensionsPending) ? -1 : ci,
286:   );
287:   const [preparation, setPreparation] = useState({ done: 0, total: 0 });
288:   const [preparationAttempt, setPreparationAttempt] = useState(0);
289:   const retryPreparation = () => setPreparationAttempt((attempt) => attempt + 1);
290:   useEffect(() => {
291:     stopBackground(manga.id);
292:     if (!allowed) return;
293:     if (!manga.chapters[ci].pages.some((p) => p.dimensionsPending)) {
294:       setPrepared(ci);
295:       return;
296:     }
297:     const controller = new AbortController();
298:     setFailure('');
299:     setPrepared(-1);
300:     setReady(false);
301:     setPreparation({
302:       done: 0,
303:       total: manga.chapters[ci].pages.filter((p) => p.dimensionsPending).length,
304:     });
305:     void prepareChapter(manga, ci, {
306:       signal: controller.signal,
307:       update: (state) => setPreparation({ done: state.done, total: state.total }),
308:     })
309:       .then((issues) => {
310:         if (!controller.signal.aborted) {
311:           if (issues.length)
312:             setFailure(
313:               `${issues.length} page(s) could not be prepared. ` +
314:               issues
315:                 .slice(0, 3)
316:                 .map((issue) => `${issue.path}: ${issue.reason}`)
317:                 .join(' ') +
318:               (issues.length > 3 ? ' More affected pages are marked below.' : ''),
319:             );
320:           setPrepared(ci);
321:         }
322:       })
323:       .catch((e) => {
324:         if (!controller.signal.aborted) {
325:           setFailure(errorMessage(e));
326:           setPrepared(ci);
327:         }
328:       });
329:     return () => controller.abort();
330:   }, [manga, ci, allowed, preparationAttempt]);
331:   const edgeProgress = useRef<HTMLSpanElement>(null);
332:   const content = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
333:   const offsets = useRef<Offset[]>([]);
334:   const measuredHeights = useRef<Map<number, number>>(new Map());
335:   const lastWindowScrollY = useRef(0);
336:   const [windowRange, setWindowRange] = useState<WindowRange>(() =>
337:     getWindowRange(offsets.current, 0, typeof window !== 'undefined' ? window.innerHeight : 800),
338:   );
339:   const position = useRef<Position>({ page: 0, fraction: 0 });
340:   const pendingRestore = useRef<Position | undefined>(undefined);
341:   const pointer = useRef(-1);
342:   const held = useRef(new Set<number>());
343:   const restoring = useRef(false);
344:   const chapterRef = useRef(ci);
345:   const touch = useRef<{ x: number; y: number; multi: boolean } | null>(null);
346:   const metadata = useRef(manga);
347:   const latest = useRef({ s, ci, ready, allowed, page: 0, zoom });
348:   latest.current = {
349:     s,
350:     ci,
351:     ready: ready && prepared === ci,
352:     allowed,
353:     page: s.mode === 'single' ? singlePage : pageStore.getPage(),
354:     zoom,
355:   };
356:   const { groups, pageToGroup } = useMemo(() => {
357:     const result: number[][] = [];
358:     const p2g = new Int32Array(chapter.pages.length);
359:     for (let i = 0; i < chapter.pages.length;) {
360:       const groupIdx = result.length;
361:       if (
362:         !s.spread ||
363:         !wide ||
364:         (s.singleCover && i === 0) ||
365:         chapter.pages[i].width > chapter.pages[i].height ||
366:         i === chapter.pages.length - 1 ||
367:         chapter.pages[i + 1].width > chapter.pages[i + 1].height
368:       ) {
369:         p2g[i] = groupIdx;
370:         result.push([i++]);
371:       } else {
372:         p2g[i] = groupIdx;
373:         p2g[i + 1] = groupIdx;
374:         result.push([i, i + 1]);
375:         i += 2;
376:       }
377:     }
378:     return { groups: result, pageToGroup: p2g };
379:   }, [chapter, s.spread, s.singleCover, wide]);
380:   const pageToGroupRef = useRef(pageToGroup);
381:   pageToGroupRef.current = pageToGroup;
382:   const groupIndex =
383:     s.mode === 'single'
384:       ? Math.max(0, Math.min(groups.length - 1, pageToGroup[singlePage] ?? 0))
385:       : 0;
386:   const visible = s.mode === 'single' ? groups[groupIndex] || [] : [];
387:   const groupsRef = useRef(groups);
388:   groupsRef.current = groups;
389:   useEffect(() => {
390:     let live = true;
391:     void access(manga).then((v) => live && setAllowed(v));
392:     return () => {
393:       live = false;
394:     };
395:   }, [manga]);
396:   const { dirty, lastSave, snapshotRevision, flush, capture } = useProgressSave({
397:     metadata,
398:     chapterRef,
399:     position,
400:     latest,
401:     offsets,
402:     pageToGroupRef,
403:     groupsRef,
404:     edgeProgress,
405:     restoring,
406:     singlePage,
407:     pageStoreRef,
408:     setReadRevision,
409:     notify,
410:   });
411:   function updateWindow(force = false, targetPage?: number) {
412:     if (
413:       latest.current.s.mode !== 'continuous' ||
414:       chapter.pages.length <= VIRTUALIZATION_THRESHOLD
415:     ) {
416:       setWindowRange((prev) => {
417:         if (prev.startIndex === 0 && prev.endIndex === Math.max(0, chapter.pages.length - 1)) {
418:           return prev;
419:         }
420:         return {
421:           startIndex: 0,
422:           endIndex: Math.max(0, chapter.pages.length - 1),
423:           topSpacer: 0,
424:           bottomSpacer: 0,
425:         };
426:       });
427:       return;
428:     }
429:     const y =
430:       targetPage !== undefined && offsets.current[targetPage]
431:         ? offsets.current[targetPage].top
432:         : window.scrollY;
433:     const h = window.innerHeight;
434:     if (!force && Math.abs(y - lastWindowScrollY.current) < h * 1.0) {
435:       return;
436:     }
437:     lastWindowScrollY.current = y;
438:     const next = getWindowRange(offsets.current, y, h, 3);
439:     setWindowRange((prev) => {
440:       if (
441:         prev.startIndex === next.startIndex &&
442:         prev.endIndex === next.endIndex &&
443:         Math.abs(prev.topSpacer - next.topSpacer) < 2 &&
444:         Math.abs(prev.bottomSpacer - next.bottomSpacer) < 2
445:       ) {
446:         return prev;
447:       }
448:       return next;
449:     });
450:   }
451:   function measure(recomputeOnly = false) {
452:     if (!recomputeOnly) {
453:       const nodes = content.current?.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>('[data-page]');
454:       if (nodes) {
455:         for (const el of nodes) {
456:           const p = Number(el.dataset.page);
457:           const rect = el.getBoundingClientRect();
458:           if (rect.height > 0) {
459:             measuredHeights.current.set(p, rect.height);
460:           }
461:         }
462:       }
463:     }
464:     const containerWidth = content.current?.clientWidth || window.innerWidth;
465:     const viewportHeight = window.innerHeight;
466:     offsets.current = computeOffsets(
467:       chapter.pages,
468:       {
469:         containerWidth,
470:         viewportHeight,
471:         layout: latest.current.s.layout,
472:         maxWidth: latest.current.s.maxWidth,
473:         zoom: latest.current.zoom,
474:       },
475:       measuredHeights.current,
476:     );
477:   }
478:   function restore(anchor: Position) {
479:     const index = Math.max(0, Math.min(offsets.current.length - 1, anchor.page));
480:     const o = offsets.current[index];
481:     if (o) window.scrollTo({ top: o.top + o.height * anchor.fraction, behavior: 'instant' });
482:     position.current = { ...anchor };
483:     playbackRevision.current++;
484:     restoring.current = false;
485:     updateWindow(true, anchor.page);
486:   }
487:   async function goChapter(index: number, reset = false) {
488:     if (index < 0 || index >= manga.chapters.length) return;
489:     capture();
490:     if (index === ci + 1) {
491:       metadata.current.chapters[ci].read = true;
492:       dirty.current = true;
493:       snapshotRevision.current++;
494:       setReadRevision((v) => v + 1);
495:     }
496:     await flush().catch((e) => notify(errorMessage(e)));
497:     restoring.current = true;
498:     playbackRevision.current++;
499:     if (reset)
500:       metadata.current.chapters[index].position = {
501:         page: 0,
502:         path: metadata.current.chapters[index].pages[0]?.path,
503:         fraction: 0,
504:       };
505:     clearImageQueue();
506:     clearObjectUrlPool();
507:     if (index === ci) {
508:       pendingRestore.current = metadata.current.chapters[index].position || {
509:         page: 0,
510:         fraction: 0,
511:       };
512:       setSinglePage(0);
513:       pageStore.setPage(0);
514:       setZoom(1);
515:       measuredHeights.current.clear();
516:       requestAnimationFrame(() => {
517:         measure();
518:         updateWindow(true, 0);
519:         restore(pendingRestore.current!);
520:         capture();
521:       });
522:     } else navigate(mangaPath(manga, index));
523:     endHandled.current = false;
524:     setEnded(false);
525:   }
526:   function goPage(n: number, user = true) {
527:     const count = chapter.pages.length;
528:     n = Math.max(0, Math.min(count - 1, Math.floor(n) || 0));
529:     if (user) pause();
530:     setEnded(false);
531:     endHandled.current = false;
532:     if (s.mode === 'single') {
533:       const gIndex = pageToGroupRef.current[n] ?? 0;
534:       const group = groupsRef.current[gIndex] || [n];
535:       n = group[0];
536:       position.current = { page: n, path: chapter.pages[n].path, fraction: 0 };
537:       metadata.current.chapters[ci].position = { ...position.current };
538:       pendingRestore.current = { ...position.current };
539:       setSinglePage(n);
540:       pageStore.setPage(n);
541:       setZoom(1);
542:       dirty.current = true;
543:       snapshotRevision.current++;
544:       if (edgeProgress.current) {
545:         const progress = (Math.max(...group) + 1) / chapter.pages.length;
546:         edgeProgress.current.style.transform = `scaleX(${Math.min(1, Math.max(0, progress))})`;
547:       }
548:       if (performance.now() - lastSave.current >= 1500) {
549:         void flush().catch((e) => notify(errorMessage(e)));
550:       }
551:     } else {
552:       const anchor = { page: n, path: chapter.pages[n].path, fraction: 0 };
553:       pendingRestore.current = anchor;
554:       pageStore.setPage(n);
555:       measure();
556:       updateWindow(true, n);
557:       restore(anchor);
558:       capture();
559:     }
560:   }
561:   function step(direction: number) {
562:     const list = groupsRef.current;
563:     const index = Math.max(
564:       0,
565:       Math.min(list.length - 1, pageToGroupRef.current[latest.current.page] ?? 0),
566:     );
567:     const next = list[index + direction];
568:     if (next) goPage(next[0]);
569:   }
570:   const actions = useRef({
571:     capture,
572:     flush,
573:     goChapter,
574:     goPage,
575:     step,
576:     pause: () => { },
577:     updateWindow,
578:     measure,
579:   });
580:   actions.current = {
581:     capture,
582:     flush,
583:     goChapter,
584:     goPage,
585:     step,
586:     pause: () => { },
587:     updateWindow,
588:     measure,
589:   };
590: 
591:   const {
592:     keysHeld,
593:     onKeyDown: key,
594:     onKeyUp: keyup,
595:   } = useReaderKeys({
596:     latest,
597:     actions,
598:     setPlaying: (v) => setPlaying(v),
599:     setBars,
600:     toggleBookmark: () => toggleBookmark(),
601:   });
602: 
603:   const { playing, setPlaying, ended, setEnded, endHandled, playbackRevision, pause } =
604:     useAutoplay({
605:       allowed,
606:       ready,
607:       prepared,
608:       ci,
609:       manga,
610:       settings: s,
611:       offsets,
612:       groupsRef,
613:       pageToGroupRef,
614:       chapterRef,
615:       restoring,
616:       held,
617:       keysHeld,
618:       latest,
619:       actions,
620:       setBars,
621:       notify,
622:     });
623:   actions.current.pause = pause;
624:   useLayoutEffect(() => {
625:     if (!allowed || prepared !== ci) {
626:       setReady(false);
627:       return;
628:     }
629:     setReady(false);
630:     restoring.current = true;
631:     chapterRef.current = ci;
632:     const c = metadata.current.chapters[ci];
633:     const anchor = matchingPosition(c.position, c.pages) || {
634:       page: 0,
635:       path: c.pages[0].path,
636:       fraction: 0,
637:     };
638:     position.current = anchor;
639:     pendingRestore.current = anchor;
640:     setSinglePage(anchor.page);
641:     pageStore.setPage(anchor.page);
642:     setZoom(1);
643:     setEnded(false);
644:     endHandled.current = false;
645:     measuredHeights.current.clear();
646:     const frame = requestAnimationFrame(() => {
647:       measure();
648:       updateWindow(true, anchor.page);
649:       restore(pendingRestore.current || anchor);
650:       setReady(true);
651:     });
652:     return () => cancelAnimationFrame(frame);
653:   }, [ci, allowed, prepared]);
654:   const prevLayoutProps = useRef({
655:     mode: s.mode,
656:     layout: s.layout,
657:     maxWidth: s.maxWidth,
658:     spread: s.spread,
659:     singleCover: s.singleCover,
660:     direction: s.direction,
661:     zoom,
662:     wide,
663:   });
664:   useLayoutEffect(() => {
665:     if (!allowed || !ready) return;
666:     const prev = prevLayoutProps.current;
667:     const layoutUnchanged =
668:       prev.mode === s.mode &&
669:       prev.layout === s.layout &&
670:       prev.spread === s.spread &&
671:       prev.singleCover === s.singleCover &&
672:       prev.direction === s.direction &&
673:       prev.wide === wide;
674: 
675:     if (layoutUnchanged) {
676:       if (prev.zoom !== zoom || prev.maxWidth !== s.maxWidth) {
677:         if (prev.zoom !== zoom && prev.zoom > 0) {
678:           const scale = zoom / prev.zoom;
679:           for (const [k, v] of measuredHeights.current) {
680:             measuredHeights.current.set(k, Math.max(1, Math.round(v * scale)));
681:           }
682:         }
683:         measure(true);
684:         const anchor = pendingRestore.current || position.current;
685:         updateWindow(true, anchor.page);
686:         if (latest.current.s.mode === 'continuous') {
687:           restore(anchor);
688:         }
689:         pendingRestore.current = undefined;
690:         capture();
691:       }
692:       prevLayoutProps.current = {
693:         mode: s.mode,
694:         layout: s.layout,
695:         maxWidth: s.maxWidth,
696:         spread: s.spread,
697:         singleCover: s.singleCover,
698:         direction: s.direction,
699:         zoom,
700:         wide,
701:       };
702:       return;
703:     }
704: 
705:     prevLayoutProps.current = {
706:       mode: s.mode,
707:       layout: s.layout,
708:       maxWidth: s.maxWidth,
709:       spread: s.spread,
710:       singleCover: s.singleCover,
711:       direction: s.direction,
712:       zoom,
713:       wide,
714:     };
715: 
716:     restoring.current = true;
717:     measuredHeights.current.clear();
718:     const frame = requestAnimationFrame(() => {
719:       const anchor = pendingRestore.current || position.current;
720:       measure();
721:       updateWindow(true, anchor.page);
722:       restore(anchor);
723:       pendingRestore.current = undefined;
724:       capture();
725:     });
726:     return () => cancelAnimationFrame(frame);
727:   }, [
728:     s.mode,
729:     s.layout,
730:     s.maxWidth,
731:     s.spread,
732:     s.singleCover,
733:     s.direction,
734:     zoom,
735:     wide,
736:     s.mode === 'single' ? singlePage : 0,
737:     ready,
738:   ]);
739:   useEffect(() => {
740:     if (!ready || !content.current) return;
741:     let frame = 0;
742:     let width = innerWidth,
743:       height = innerHeight;
744:     const resize = () => {
745:       if (window.visualViewport && window.visualViewport.scale > 1.01) return;
746:       const changed = width !== innerWidth || height !== innerHeight;
747:       width = innerWidth;
748:       height = innerHeight;
749:       if (!changed) return;
750:       const anchor = { ...position.current };
751:       restoring.current = true;
752:       setWide(innerWidth >= 700);
753:       measuredHeights.current.clear();
754:       cancelAnimationFrame(frame);
755:       frame = requestAnimationFrame(() => {
756:         measure();
757:         updateWindow(true, anchor.page);
758:         restore(anchor);
759:         actions.current.capture();
760:       });
761:     };
762:     window.addEventListener('resize', resize);
763:     return () => {
764:       window.removeEventListener('resize', resize);
765:       cancelAnimationFrame(frame);
766:     };
767:   }, [ready]);
768:   useEffect(() => {
769:     if (!ready) return;
770:     let frame = 0;
771:     const scroll = () => {
772:       if (restoring.current) return;
773:       if (!frame)
774:         frame = requestAnimationFrame(() => {
775:           frame = 0;
776:           if (latest.current.s.autoHideBars) {
777:             const y = pointer.current;
778:             setBars((old) => {
779:               const top = y >= 0 && y < 60,
780:                 bottom = y > innerHeight - 60;
781:               return old.top === top && old.bottom === bottom ? old : { top, bottom };
782:             });
783:           }
784:           actions.current.capture();
785:           actions.current.updateWindow();
786:         });
787:     };
788:     const move = (e: PointerEvent) => {
789:       pointer.current = e.clientY;
790:       if (held.current.size) actions.current.pause();
791:       if (e.clientY < 60 || e.clientY > innerHeight - 60)
792:         setBars({ top: e.clientY < 60, bottom: e.clientY > innerHeight - 60 });
793:     };
794:     const down = (e: PointerEvent) => {
795:       held.current.add(e.pointerId);
796:       actions.current.pause();
797:     };
798:     const up = (e: PointerEvent) => {
799:       held.current.delete(e.pointerId);
800:       actions.current.pause();
801:     };
802:     const wheel = () => actions.current.pause();
803:     const blur = () => {
804:       held.current.clear();
805:       keysHeld.current.clear();
806:       void actions.current.flush().catch((e) => notify(errorMessage(e)));
807:     };
808:     const leave = () => {
809:       pointer.current = -1;
810:     };
811:     const save = () => {
812:       actions.current.capture();
813:       void actions.current.flush().catch((e) => notify(errorMessage(e)));
814:     };
815:     const update = (e: Event) => {
816:       actions.current.capture();
817:       (e as CustomEvent<Promise<unknown>[]>).detail.push(actions.current.flush());
818:     };
819:     const interval = setInterval(() => {
820:       if (dirty.current) void actions.current.flush().catch((e) => notify(errorMessage(e)));
821:     }, 1500);
822:     window.addEventListener('scroll', scroll, { passive: true });
823:     window.addEventListener('pointermove', move, { passive: true });
824:     window.addEventListener('pointerdown', down, { passive: true });
825:     window.addEventListener('pointerup', up, { passive: true });
826:     window.addEventListener('pointercancel', up, { passive: true });
827:     window.addEventListener('wheel', wheel, { passive: true });
828:     window.addEventListener('keydown', key);
829:     window.addEventListener('keyup', keyup);
830:     window.addEventListener('blur', blur);
831:     document.addEventListener('pointerleave', leave);
832:     document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', save);
833:     window.addEventListener('pagehide', save);
834:     window.addEventListener('reader-before-update', update);
835:     requestAnimationFrame(() => actions.current.capture());
836:     return () => {
837:       clearInterval(interval);
838:       cancelAnimationFrame(frame);
839:       window.removeEventListener('scroll', scroll);
840:       window.removeEventListener('pointermove', move);
841:       window.removeEventListener('pointerdown', down);
842:       window.removeEventListener('pointerup', up);
843:       window.removeEventListener('pointercancel', up);
844:       window.removeEventListener('wheel', wheel);
845:       window.removeEventListener('keydown', key);
846:       window.removeEventListener('keyup', keyup);
847:       window.removeEventListener('blur', blur);
848:       document.removeEventListener('pointerleave', leave);
849:       document.removeEventListener('visibilitychange', save);
850:       window.removeEventListener('pagehide', save);
851:       window.removeEventListener('reader-before-update', update);
852:     };
853:   }, [ready]);
854:   useEffect(
855:     () => () => {
856:       void actions.current
857:         .flush()
858:         .then((res) => {
859:           if (res === 'saved') {
860:             refresh();
861:           }
862:           scheduleBackground(metadata.current);
863:         })
864:         .catch((e) => {
865:           console.warn('Failed to flush progress on unmount:', e);
866:         });
867:       clearImageQueue();
868:       clearObjectUrlPool();
869:     },
870:     [],
871:   );
872:   function markComplete() {
873:     capture();
874:     metadata.current.chapters[ci].read = true;
875:     dirty.current = true;
876:     snapshotRevision.current++;
877:     setReadRevision((v) => v + 1);
878:     void flush().catch((e) => notify(errorMessage(e)));
879:   }
880:   function changeZoom(value: number) {
881:     capture();
882:     pendingRestore.current = { ...position.current };
883:     setZoom(Math.min(3, Math.max(0.5, value)));
884:   }
885:   useEffect(() => {
886:     const close = (e: PointerEvent) => {
887:       if ((e.target as HTMLElement).closest('[data-reader-dropdown]')) return;
888:       for (const panel of document.querySelectorAll<HTMLDetailsElement>(
889:         '.reader-bar details[open]',
890:       ))
891:         if (!panel.contains(e.target as Node)) panel.open = false;
892:     };
893:     const escape = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
894:       if (e.key === 'Escape')
895:         for (const panel of document.querySelectorAll<HTMLDetailsElement>(
896:           '.reader-bar details[open]',
897:         ))
898:           panel.open = false;
899:     };
900:     document.addEventListener('pointerdown', close);
901:     document.addEventListener('keydown', escape);
902:     return () => {
903:       document.removeEventListener('pointerdown', close);
904:       document.removeEventListener('keydown', escape);
905:     };
906:   }, []);
907:   function toggleBookmark() {
908:     actions.current.capture();
909:     const c = metadata.current.chapters[chapterRef.current];
910:     if (!c) return;

25: export function calculatePageDimensions(
26:   page: Pick<Page, 'width' | 'height'>,
27:   options: GeometryOptions,
28: ): { width: number; height: number } {
29:   const { containerWidth, viewportHeight, layout, maxWidth, zoom } = options;
30:   const safeZoom = Math.max(0.1, zoom || 1);
31:   const nativeWidth = Math.max(1, page.width) * safeZoom;
32:   const pageAspect = Math.max(1, page.width) / Math.max(1, page.height);
33: 
34:   let targetWidth: number;
35:   switch (layout) {
36:     case 'width':
37:       targetWidth = Math.min(containerWidth, nativeWidth);
38:       break;
39:     case 'height': {
40:       const byHeight = Math.max(1, viewportHeight) * pageAspect * safeZoom;
41:       targetWidth = Math.min(containerWidth, byHeight, nativeWidth);
42:       break;
43:     }
44:     case 'auto':
45:       targetWidth = Math.min(containerWidth, 920 * safeZoom, nativeWidth);
46:       break;
47:     case 'max':
48:     default: {
49:       const readingWidth = maxWidth > 0 ? maxWidth * safeZoom : containerWidth;
50:       targetWidth = Math.min(containerWidth, readingWidth, nativeWidth);
51:       break;
52:     }
53:   }
54: 
55:   targetWidth = Math.max(1, Math.round(targetWidth));
56:   const targetHeight = Math.max(1, Math.round(targetWidth / pageAspect));
57:   return { width: targetWidth, height: targetHeight };
58: }
59: 
60: export function computeOffsets(
61:   pages: Page[],
62:   options: GeometryOptions,
63:   measuredHeights?: Map<number, number>,
64: ): PageOffset[] {
65:   const offsets: PageOffset[] = [];
66:   let currentTop = 0;
67: 
68:   for (let i = 0; i < pages.length; i++) {
69:     const page = pages[i];
70:     let height = measuredHeights?.get(i);
71:     if (height === undefined || height <= 0 || !Number.isFinite(height)) {
72:       height = calculatePageDimensions(page, options).height;
73:     }
74:     const bottom = currentTop + height;
75:     offsets.push({
76:       page: i,
77:       top: currentTop,
78:       height,
79:       bottom,
80:     });
81:     currentTop = bottom;
82:   }
83: 
84:   return offsets;
85: }
86: 
87: export const VIRTUALIZATION_THRESHOLD = 8;
88: 
89: export function getWindowRange(
90:   offsets: PageOffset[],
91:   scrollY: number,
92:   viewportHeight: number,
93:   bufferScreenCount: number = 3,
94:   threshold: number = VIRTUALIZATION_THRESHOLD,
95: ): WindowRange {
96:   const count = offsets.length;
97:   if (count <= threshold) {
98:     return {
99:       startIndex: 0,
100:       endIndex: Math.max(0, count - 1),
101:       topSpacer: 0,
102:       bottomSpacer: 0,
103:     };
104:   }
105: 
106:   const safeViewportHeight = Math.max(1, viewportHeight || 800);
107:   const buffer = safeViewportHeight * Math.max(1, bufferScreenCount);
108:   const minY = Math.max(0, scrollY - buffer);
109:   const maxY = scrollY + safeViewportHeight + buffer;
110: 
111:   let lo = 0;
112:   let hi = count - 1;
113:   while (lo < hi) {
114:     const mid = Math.floor((lo + hi) / 2);
115:     if (offsets[mid].bottom >= minY) {
116:       hi = mid;
117:     } else {
118:       lo = mid + 1;
119:     }
120:   }
121:   const startIndex = Math.max(0, lo);
122: 
123:   lo = startIndex;
124:   hi = count - 1;
125:   while (lo < hi) {
126:     const mid = Math.ceil((lo + hi) / 2);
127:     if (offsets[mid].top <= maxY) {
128:       lo = mid;
129:     } else {
130:       hi = mid - 1;
131:     }
132:   }
133:   const endIndex = Math.min(count - 1, Math.max(startIndex, lo));
134: 
135:   const topSpacer = offsets[startIndex].top;
136:   const bottomSpacer = offsets[count - 1].bottom - offsets[endIndex].bottom;
137: 
138:   return {
139:     startIndex,
140:     endIndex,
141:     topSpacer: Math.max(0, topSpacer),
142:     bottomSpacer: Math.max(0, bottomSpacer),
143:   };
144: }

368: export type LegacyReadResult =
369:   | { status: 'unavailable' }
370:   | { status: 'empty' }
371:   | { status: 'failed'; error: Error }
372:   | { status: 'data'; titles: Manga[]; settings?: Settings };
373: 
374: export async function readLegacyIndexedDB(): Promise<LegacyReadResult> {
375:   if (typeof window === 'undefined' || typeof window.indexedDB === 'undefined') {
376:     return { status: 'unavailable' };
377:   }
378: 
379:   return new Promise<LegacyReadResult>((resolve) => {
380:     let existed = true;
381:     let req: IDBOpenDBRequest;
382:     try {
383:       req = window.indexedDB.open('manga-reader');
384:     } catch (err) {
385:       return resolve({
386:         status: 'failed',
387:         error: err instanceof Error ? err : new Error(String(err)),
388:       });
389:     }
390: 
391:     req.onupgradeneeded = (event) => {
392:       if (event.oldVersion === 0) {
393:         existed = false;
394:         try {
395:           req.transaction?.abort();
396:         } catch { }
397:       }
398:     };
399: 
400:     req.onerror = () => {
401:       if (!existed) {
402:         return resolve({ status: 'empty' });
403:       }
404:       return resolve({
405:         status: 'failed',
406:         error: req.error ?? new Error('IndexedDB open error'),
407:       });
408:     };
409: 
410:     req.onsuccess = () => {
411:       const db = req.result;
412:       try {
413:         if (!db.objectStoreNames.contains('manga')) {
414:           db.close();
415:           return resolve({ status: 'empty' });
416:         }
417: 
418:         const storeNames = ['manga'];
419:         if (db.objectStoreNames.contains('settings')) {
420:           storeNames.push('settings');
421:         }
422:         if (db.objectStoreNames.contains('progress')) {
423:           storeNames.push('progress');
424:         }
425: 
426:         const tx = db.transaction(storeNames, 'readonly');
427:         const mangaReq = tx.objectStore('manga').getAll();
428:         const settingsReq = db.objectStoreNames.contains('settings')
429:           ? tx.objectStore('settings').get('global')
430:           : null;
431:         const progressReq = db.objectStoreNames.contains('progress')
432:           ? tx.objectStore('progress').getAll()
433:           : null;
434: 
435:         tx.oncomplete = () => {
436:           db.close();
437:           try {
438:             const rawManga = (mangaReq.result || []) as Manga[];
439:             let settings: Settings | undefined = undefined;
440:             if (settingsReq?.result) {
441:               try {
442:                 settings = parseSettings(settingsReq.result);
443:               } catch { }
444:             }
445: 
446:             const rawProgress = (progressReq?.result || []) as any[];
447:             const progressByManga = new Map<string, Map<string, any>>();
448:             for (const p of rawProgress) {
449:               if (p && p.mangaId && p.chapter) {
450:                 let m = progressByManga.get(p.mangaId);
451:                 if (!m) {
452:                   m = new Map();
453:                   progressByManga.set(p.mangaId, m);
454:                 }
455:                 m.set(p.chapter, p);
456:               }
457:             }
458: 
459:             const titles: Manga[] = [];
460:             for (const item of rawManga) {
461:               if (!item || typeof item !== 'object') continue;
462:               const mangaProgress = progressByManga.get(item.id);
463:               if (mangaProgress && Array.isArray(item.chapters)) {
464:                 for (const ch of item.chapters) {
465:                   const prog = mangaProgress.get(ch.name);
466:                   if (prog) {
467:                     if (prog.read !== undefined) ch.read = prog.read;
468:                     if (prog.position) ch.position = prog.position;
469:                     if (prog.bookmarks) ch.bookmarks = prog.bookmarks;
470:                   }
471:                 }
472:               }
473:               titles.push(item);
474:             }
475: 
476:             if (titles.length === 0 && !settings) {
477:               return resolve({ status: 'empty' });
478:             }
479:             return resolve({ status: 'data', titles, settings });
480:           } catch (processErr) {
481:             return resolve({
482:               status: 'failed',
483:               error: processErr instanceof Error ? processErr : new Error(String(processErr)),
484:             });
485:           }
486:         };
487: 
488:         tx.onerror = () => {
489:           db.close();
490:           return resolve({
491:             status: 'failed',
492:             error: tx.error ?? new Error('IndexedDB transaction error'),
493:           });
494:         };
495:       } catch (err) {
496:         try {
497:           db.close();
498:         } catch { }
499:         return resolve({
500:           status: 'failed',
501:           error: err instanceof Error ? err : new Error(String(err)),
502:         });
503:       }
504:     };
505:   });
506: }
507: 
508: export async function ensureLibraryMigrated(idbRepo?: LibraryRepo): Promise<boolean> {
509:   if (!desktopAPI.isDesktop || !desktopAPI.isLibraryMigrated) {
510:     return false;
511:   }
512:   const alreadyMigrated = await desktopAPI.isLibraryMigrated();
513:   if (alreadyMigrated) {
514:     return true;
515:   }
516: 
517:   let titles: Manga[] = [];
518:   let settings: Settings | undefined;
519: 
520:   if (idbRepo) {
521:     try {
522:       titles = await idbRepo.allManga();
523:       settings = await idbRepo.getStoredSettings();
524:     } catch (err) {
525:       throw new Error(
526:         `Failed to read from provided migration repo: ${err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err)}`,
527:       );
528:     }
529:   } else {
530:     const legacyResult = await readLegacyIndexedDB();
531:     if (legacyResult.status === 'failed') {
532:       throw new Error(`Legacy IndexedDB migration read failed: ${legacyResult.error.message}`);
533:     }
534:     if (legacyResult.status === 'unavailable') {
535:       console.warn(
536:         '[ensureLibraryMigrated] IndexedDB unavailable in environment; skipping legacy migration',
537:       );
538:       return false;
539:     }
540:     if (legacyResult.status === 'data') {

163: export interface PageImageResourceOptions {
164:   mangaId: string;
165:   pagePath: string;
166:   version?: string | number;
167:   transform?: 'full' | 'raw' | 'low' | 'preview' | 'thumb' | string;
168:   localPath?: string;
169: }
170: 
171: export function getPageImageUrl(options: PageImageResourceOptions): string {
172:   const { mangaId, pagePath, version = 1, transform = 'full', localPath } = options;
173:   const encodedMangaId = encodeURIComponent(mangaId);
174:   const encodedVersion = encodeURIComponent(String(version));
175:   const encodedTransform = encodeURIComponent(transform);
176:   const normalizedPath = pagePath.replace(/\\/g, '/').replace(/^\/+/, '');
177:   const segments = normalizedPath.split('/').filter(Boolean).map(encodeURIComponent);
178:   const encodedPagePath = segments.join('/');
179: 
180:   let url = `http://mangaimg.localhost/page/${encodedMangaId}/${encodedVersion}/${encodedTransform}/${encodedPagePath}`;
181:   if (localPath) {
182:     url += `?root=${encodeURIComponent(localPath)}`;
183:   }
184:   return url;
185: }
186: 
187: export function getCoverImageUrl(mangaId: string, version?: string | number): string {
188:   const encodedMangaId = encodeURIComponent(mangaId);
189:   const ver =
190:     version !== undefined && version !== null ? `/${encodeURIComponent(String(version))}` : '';
191:   return `http://mangaimg.localhost/cover/${encodedMangaId}${ver}`;
192: }

200: export interface FsErrorPayload {
201:   code: 'NotFound' | 'TooLarge' | 'ForbiddenExt' | 'PathNotAllowed' | 'Cancelled' | 'Io';
202:   message?: string;
203: }
204: 
205: export interface DbErrorPayload {
206:   code:
207:   | 'Sqlite'
208:   | 'Migration'
209:   | 'Lock'
210:   | 'Validation'
211:   | 'NotFound'
212:   | 'Conflict'
213:   | 'Task'
214:   | 'Io'
215:   | 'Serialization';
216:   message?: string;
217: }
218: 
219: export type TauriErrorPayload =
220:   | FsErrorPayload
221:   | DbErrorPayload
222:   | { code: 'Unknown'; message: string };
223: 
224: export function parseFsError(err: unknown): FsErrorPayload | null {
225:   if (err && typeof err === 'object') {
226:     if ('code' in err && typeof (err as any).code === 'string') {
227:       return err as FsErrorPayload;
228:     }
229:   }
230:   if (typeof err === 'string') {
231:     try {
232:       const parsed = JSON.parse(err);
233:       if (parsed && typeof parsed === 'object' && 'code' in parsed) {
234:         return parsed as FsErrorPayload;
235:       }
236:     } catch {
237:       // not JSON
238:     }
239:   }
240:   return null;
241: }
242: 
243: export function parseTauriError(err: unknown): TauriErrorPayload {
244:   const fsErr = parseFsError(err);
245:   if (fsErr) return fsErr;
246:   if (err && typeof err === 'object' && 'code' in err && typeof (err as any).code === 'string') {
247:     return err as DbErrorPayload;
248:   }
249:   if (typeof err === 'string') {
250:     try {
251:       const parsed = JSON.parse(err);
252:       if (parsed && typeof parsed === 'object' && 'code' in parsed) {
253:         return parsed as DbErrorPayload;
254:       }
255:     } catch {
256:       // not JSON
257:     }
258:     return { code: 'Unknown', message: err };
259:   }
260:   if (err instanceof Error) {
261:     return { code: 'Unknown', message: err.message };
262:   }
263:   return { code: 'Unknown', message: String(err) };
264: }

378: export async function invokeCommand<K extends keyof TauriCommands>(
379:   cmd: K,
380:   args?: TauriCommands[K]['args'],
381:   options?: any,
382: ): Promise<TauriCommands[K]['return']> {
383:   try {
384:     return await invoke<TauriCommands[K]['return']>(cmd, args as any, options);
385:   } catch (err) {
386:     const parsed = parseTauriError(err);
387:     console.error(`[invokeCommand] ${cmd} failed:`, parsed);
388:     throw parsed;
389:   }
390: }

455:   }): Promise<Position[]>;
456:   sqliteGetBookmarkedMangaIds?(): Promise<{
457:     allBookmarkedIds: string[];
458:     mangaBookmarkedIds: string[];
459:   }>;
460:   getBookmarkCards?(query: BookmarkCardsQuery): Promise<BookmarkCardsResult>;
461:   sqliteDeleteManga?(id: string): Promise<void>;
462:   sqliteClearLibrary?(): Promise<void>;
463:   sqliteResetProgress?(): Promise<void>;
464:   sqliteGetSettings?(): Promise<Settings | undefined>;
465:   sqliteSaveSettings?(settings: Settings): Promise<Settings>;
466:   exportBackup?(): Promise<string>;
467:   restoreBackup?(backupJson: string): Promise<Settings>;
468:   validateBackup?(backupJson: string): Promise<unknown>;
469:   indexTitle?(
470:     root: string,
471:     options?: IndexTitleOptions,
472:     onProgress?: (progress: IndexScanProgress) => void,
473:   ): Promise<IndexTitleDto>;
474:   convertFileSrc?(filePath: string, protocol?: string): string;
475:   getPageImageUrl?(options: PageImageResourceOptions): string;
476:   getCoverImageUrl?(mangaId: string, version?: string | number): string;
477:   onDragDrop?(callback: (paths: string[]) => void): () => void;
478:   checkForUpdate?(): Promise<DesktopUpdateInfo | null>;
479:   installUpdate?(): Promise<void>;
480:   getStorageStats?(): Promise<StorageStats>;
481:   saveFileDialog?(defaultName: string, content: string): Promise<boolean>;
482: }

491: // Detect Tauri runtime presence
492: export const isTauri =
493:   typeof window !== 'undefined' &&
494:   (Boolean((window as unknown as { __TAURI_INTERNALS__?: unknown }).__TAURI_INTERNALS__) ||
495:     Boolean((window as unknown as { __TAURI__?: unknown }).__TAURI__));
496: 
497: export const isDesktop =
498:   (typeof __DESKTOP_BUILD__ !== 'undefined' && __DESKTOP_BUILD__) ||
499:   (typeof import.meta !== 'undefined' && import.meta.env?.VITE_TARGET === 'desktop') ||
500:   isTauri;
501: 
502: export class TauriDesktopBridge implements DesktopAPI {
503:   readonly isDesktop = true;
504:   readonly isTauri = true;
505: 
506:   async selectFolder(): Promise<string | null> {
507:     try {
508:       const selected = await invokeCommand('select_folder');
509:       return selected ?? null;
510:     } catch (err) {
511:       console.error('Tauri selectFolder failed:', parseFsError(err) ?? err);
512:       return null;
513:     }
514:   }
515: 
516:   async listFolder(folderPath: string, options?: ListFolderOptions): Promise<FolderEntry[]> {
517:     let unlisten: (() => void) | undefined;
518:     const scanId =
519:       options?.scanId ??
520:       (options?.signal || options?.onProgress
521:         ? `scan-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2)}`
522:         : undefined);
523: 
524:     let abortHandler: (() => void) | undefined;
525:     if (options?.signal && scanId) {
526:       if (options.signal.aborted) {
527:         throw new DOMException('The operation was aborted', 'AbortError');
528:       }
529:       abortHandler = () => {
530:         void this.cancelScan(scanId);
531:       };
532:       options.signal.addEventListener('abort', abortHandler, { once: true });
533:     }
534: 
535:     try {
536:       if (options?.onProgress && isTauri) {
537:         try {
538:           unlisten = await listen<ScanProgress>('scan-progress', (event) => {
539:             if (!scanId || event.payload.scanId === scanId) {
540:               options.onProgress?.(event.payload);
541:             }
542:           });
543:         } catch (err) {
544:           console.warn('Tauri scan-progress listen failed:', err);
545:         }
546:       }
547: 
548:       return await invokeCommand('list_folder', { folderPath, scanId });
549:     } catch (err) {
550:       const fsErr = parseFsError(err);
551:       if (fsErr?.code === 'Cancelled') {
552:         throw new DOMException('The operation was aborted', 'AbortError');
553:       }
554:       console.error('Tauri listFolder failed:', fsErr ?? err);
555:       return [];
556:     } finally {
557:       if (abortHandler && options?.signal) {
558:         options.signal.removeEventListener('abort', abortHandler);
559:       }
560:       unlisten?.();
561:     }
562:   }
563: 
564:   async discoverLibrary(
565:     folderPath: string,
566:     options?: ListFolderOptions,
567:   ): Promise<LibraryDiscovery | null> {
568:     let unlisten: (() => void) | undefined;
569:     const scanId =
570:       options?.scanId ??
571:       (options?.signal || options?.onProgress
572:         ? `scan-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2)}`
573:         : undefined);
574: 
575:     let abortHandler: (() => void) | undefined;
576:     if (options?.signal && scanId) {
577:       if (options.signal.aborted) {
578:         throw new DOMException('The operation was aborted', 'AbortError');
579:       }
580:       abortHandler = () => {
581:         void this.cancelScan(scanId);
582:       };
583:       options.signal.addEventListener('abort', abortHandler, { once: true });
584:     }
585: 
586:     try {
587:       if (options?.onProgress && isTauri) {
588:         try {
589:           unlisten = await listen<ScanProgress>('scan-progress', (event) => {
590:             if (!scanId || event.payload.scanId === scanId) {
591:               options.onProgress?.(event.payload);
592:             }
593:           });
594:         } catch (err) {
595:           console.warn('Tauri scan-progress listen failed:', err);
596:         }
597:       }
598: 
599:       return await invokeCommand('discover_library', { folderPath, scanId });
600:     } catch (err) {
601:       const fsErr = parseFsError(err);
602:       if (fsErr?.code === 'Cancelled') {
603:         throw new DOMException('The operation was aborted', 'AbortError');
604:       }
605:       console.error('Tauri discoverLibrary failed:', fsErr ?? err);
606:       return null;
607:     } finally {
608:       if (abortHandler && options?.signal) {
609:         options.signal.removeEventListener('abort', abortHandler);
610:       }
611:       unlisten?.();
612:     }
613:   }

674:     try {
675:       const response = await invokeCommand('read_file', {
676:         absolutePath,
677:       });
678:       if (response instanceof ArrayBuffer) {
679:         return response;
680:       }
681:       if (response instanceof Uint8Array) {
682:         return response.buffer.slice(
683:           response.byteOffset,
684:           response.byteOffset + response.byteLength,
685:         ) as ArrayBuffer;
686:       }
687:       return null;
688:     } catch (err) {
689:       console.error('Tauri readFile failed for', absolutePath, parseFsError(err) ?? err);
690:       return null;
691:     }
692:   }

703:       }

785:     try {
786:       const response = await invokeCommand('read_thumbnail', { id });
787:       if (response instanceof ArrayBuffer) {
788:         return response.byteLength > 0 ? response : null;
789:       }
790:       if (response instanceof Uint8Array) {
791:         return response.byteLength > 0
792:           ? (response.buffer.slice(
793:             response.byteOffset,
794:             response.byteOffset + response.byteLength,
795:           ) as ArrayBuffer)
796:           : null;
797:       }
798:       return null;
799:     } catch (err) {
800:       console.warn('Tauri getThumbnail failed for', id, parseFsError(err) ?? err);
801:       return null;
802:     }
803:   }

930:     mangaId: string;

934:     bookmarks?: Position[];
935:     updateLast: boolean;

940:       mangaId: params.mangaId,

942:       position: params.position,

944:       bookmarks: params.bookmarks,
945:       updateLast: params.updateLast,
946:       expectedRevision: params.expectedRevision,
947:       expectedUpdatedAt: params.expectedUpdatedAt,

950:   }

1040:   async isSamePath(pathA: string, pathB: string): Promise<boolean> {

1042:       return await invokeCommand('is_same_path', { pathA, pathB });
1043:     } catch {
1044:       return (
1045:         pathA.trim().replace(/\\/g, '/').replace(/\/+$/, '').toLowerCase() ===
1046:         pathB.trim().replace(/\\/g, '/').replace(/\/+$/, '').toLowerCase()
1047:       );

1050: 
1051:   async indexTitle(
1052:     root: string,
1053:     options?: IndexTitleOptions,
1054:     onProgress?: (progress: IndexScanProgress) => void,
1055:   ): Promise<IndexTitleDto> {
1056:     const channel = new Channel<IndexScanProgress>();
1057:     const jobId =
1058:       options?.jobId ??
1059:       (options?.signal ? `index-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2)}` : undefined);
1060: 
1061:     let abortHandler: (() => void) | undefined;
1062:     if (options?.signal && jobId) {
1063:       if (options.signal.aborted) {
1064:         throw new DOMException('The operation was aborted', 'AbortError');
1065:       }
1066:       abortHandler = () => {
1067:         void this.cancelScan?.(jobId);
1068:       };
1069:       options.signal.addEventListener('abort', abortHandler, { once: true });
1070:     }
1071: 
1072:     if (onProgress) {
1073:       channel.onmessage = (progress) => {
1074:         if (options?.signal?.aborted) {
1075:           if (jobId) void this.cancelScan?.(jobId);
1076:           return;
1077:         }
1078:         onProgress(progress);
1079:       };
1080:     }
1081: 
1082:     try {
1083:       return await invokeCommand('index_title', {
1084:         root,
1085:         options: options
1086:           ? {
1087:             sort: options.sort,
1088:             warmChapters: options.warmChapters,
1089:             jobId,
1090:             title: options.title,
1091:             targetId: options.targetId ?? options.previous?.id,
1092:             previous: options.previous
1093:               ? {
1094:                 id: options.previous.id,
1095:                 title: options.previous.title,
1096:                 slug: options.previous.slug,
1097:                 lastChapter: options.previous.lastChapter,
1098:                 addedAt: options.previous.addedAt,
1099:                 revision: options.previous.revision,
1100:                 chapters: options.previous.chapters.map((c) => ({
1101:                   name: c.name,
1102:                   read: c.read,
1103:                   position: c.position,
1104:                   bookmarks: c.bookmarks,
1105:                   pages: c.pages.map((p) => ({
1106:                     name: p.name,
1107:                     path: p.path,
1108:                     width: p.width,
1109:                     height: p.height,
1110:                     size: p.size,

49: export function useAutoplay({
50:   allowed,
51:   ready,
52:   prepared,
53:   ci,
54:   manga,
55:   settings,
56:   offsets,
57:   groupsRef,
58:   pageToGroupRef,
59:   chapterRef,
60:   restoring,
61:   held,
62:   keysHeld,
63:   latest,
64:   actions,
65:   setBars,
66:   notify,
67: }: UseAutoplayOptions) {
68:   const [playing, setPlaying] = useState(false);
69:   const [ended, setEnded] = useState(false);
70:   const endHandled = useRef(false);
71:   const pauseUntil = useRef(0);
72:   const playbackRevision = useRef(0);
73: 
74:   function pause() {
75:     playbackRevision.current++;
76:     pauseUntil.current = performance.now() + latest.current.s.resumeDelay * 1000;
77:   }
78: 
79:   useEffect(() => {
80:     if (!playing || !allowed || !ready || prepared !== ci) return;
81:     // Each start owns a fresh clock and uses the viewport, never a previous play target.
82:     actions.current.measure();
83:     actions.current.capture();
84:     let frame = 0,
85:       last = 0,
86:       elapsed = 0,
87:       scrollRemainder = 0,
88:       revision = playbackRevision.current;
89: 
90:     function end() {
91:       if (endHandled.current) return;
92:       endHandled.current = true;
93:       actions.current.capture();
94:       void actions.current.flush().catch((e) => notify(errorMessage(e)));
95: 
96:       const handleTransition = (promise: Promise<void>) => {
97:         promise.catch((err) => {
98:           setPlaying(false);
99:           setEnded(true);
100:           setBars({ top: true, bottom: true });
101:           notify(errorMessage(err));
102:         });
103:       };
104: 
105:       const action = latest.current.s.endAction;
106:       if (action === 'next' && ci < manga.chapters.length - 1)
107:         handleTransition(actions.current.goChapter(ci + 1, true));
108:       else if (action === 'replay') handleTransition(actions.current.goChapter(ci, true));
109:       else {
110:         setPlaying(false);
111:         setEnded(true);
112:         setBars({ top: true, bottom: true });
113:       }
114:     }
115: 
116:     const animate = (now: number) => {
117:       const reset = revision !== playbackRevision.current;
118:       if (reset) {
119:         revision = playbackRevision.current;
120:         elapsed = 0;
121:         scrollRemainder = 0;
122:       }
123:       const dt = last && !reset ? Math.min(now - last, 80) / 1000 : 0;
124:       last = now;
125:       if (
126:         !document.hidden &&
127:         latest.current.ready &&
128:         chapterRef.current === ci &&
129:         !restoring.current &&
130:         !held.current.size &&
131:         !keysHeld.current.size &&
132:         now > pauseUntil.current &&
133:         !document.querySelector(
134:           '[role="dialog"],[data-reader-dropdown],.reader-bar details[open]',
135:         ) &&
136:         (window.visualViewport?.scale || 1) <= 1.01
137:       ) {
138:         const state = latest.current;
139:         if (state.s.mode === 'single') {
140:           elapsed += dt;
141:           if (elapsed >= state.s.seconds) {
142:             elapsed = 0;
143:             const list = groupsRef.current;
144:             const i = pageToGroupRef.current[state.page] ?? 0;
145:             if (i < list.length - 1) actions.current.goPage(list[i + 1][0], false);
146:             else end();
147:           }
148:         } else {
149:           // One visible screen per interval, independent of image height or zoom.
150:           // Read each frame so mobile browser chrome and rotation adapt immediately.
151:           const viewportHeight = window.visualViewport?.height || innerHeight;
152:           const speed = viewportHeight / state.s.seconds;
153:           const y = scrollY + speed * dt + scrollRemainder;
154:           window.scrollTo({ top: y, behavior: 'instant' });
155:           // Preserve subpixel movement at slow speeds / high refresh rates.
156:           // Discard clamped movement at document boundaries.
157:           scrollRemainder = Math.abs(y - scrollY) < 1 ? y - scrollY : 0;
158:           const final = offsets.current.at(-1);
159:           if (final && scrollY + innerHeight >= final.bottom - 1) end();
160:         }
161:       } else {
162:         elapsed = 0;
163:         scrollRemainder = 0;
164:       }
165:       frame = requestAnimationFrame(animate);
166:     };
167: 
168:     frame = requestAnimationFrame(animate);
169:     return () => cancelAnimationFrame(frame);
170:   }, [playing, allowed, ready, prepared, ci, settings.mode, settings.seconds]);
171: 
172:   return {
173:     playing,
174:     setPlaying,
175:     ended,
176:     setEnded,
177:     endHandled,
178:     pauseUntil,
179:     playbackRevision,
180:     pause,
181:   };
182: }

56:

75:       if (controller.current) {
76:         controller.current.abort();
77:       }
78:       cleanupStaged();
79:       desktopAPI.setProgressBar(-1);
80:     };

83:   const startOptions = useCallback((opId: number, phase: 'discover' | 'index'): ScanOptions => {

86:     desktopAPI.setProgressBar(2);
87: 
88:     return {
89:       signal: controller.current.signal,
90:       update: (s: ScanState) => {
91:         if (activeOp.current !== opId) return;
92:         const now = performance.now();
93:         if (now - last > 80 || s.done === s.total) {
94:           last = now;
95:           setState((prev) => {
96:             if (phase === 'discover' && prev.status === 'discovering') {
97:               return { status: 'discovering', scan: s };
98:             }
99:             if (phase === 'index' && prev.status === 'indexing') {
100:               return { status: 'indexing', scan: s };
101:             }
102:             return prev;
103:           });
104:           if (s.total > 0) {
105:             desktopAPI.setProgressBar(Math.min(1, Math.max(0, s.done / s.total)));
106:           } else {
107:             desktopAPI.setProgressBar(2);
108:           }
109:         }
110:       },
111:     };
112:   }, []);
113: 
114:   const discover = useCallback(
115:     async (read: (options: ScanOptions) => Promise<Source[]>, target?: Summary | Manga) => {
116:       const opId = ++activeOp.current;
117:       cleanupStaged();
118:       setState({
119:         status: 'discovering',
120:         scan: { phase: 'discover', done: 0, total: 0, title: target?.title ?? '' },
121:       });
122: 
123:       const options = startOptions(opId, 'discover');
124:       let sources: Source[] = [];
125:       const startTime = performance.now();
126:       let outcome: 'preview' | 'failed' | 'cancelled' | 'idle' = 'idle';
127: 
128:       try {
129:         sources = await read(options);
130:         if (activeOp.current !== opId) return;
131:         stagedSources.current = sources;
132:         await warmSources(sources, options);
133:         if (activeOp.current !== opId) return;
134:         options.signal?.throwIfAborted();
135: 
136:         if (!sources.length) {
137:           throw new Error(
138:             'No supported image candidates found. Select folders with images, or extract archives first.',
139:           );
140:         }
141: 
142:         if (activeOp.current === opId) {
143:           const targetManga = target ? await getManga(target.id) : undefined;
144:           outcome = 'preview';
145:           setState({
146:             status: 'preview',
147:             preview: { sources, target: targetManga },
148:           });
149:         }
150:       } catch (e) {
151:         cleanupStaged();
152:         if (activeOp.current === opId) {
153:           const isAbort = e instanceof DOMException && e.name === 'AbortError';
154:           outcome = isAbort ? 'cancelled' : 'failed';
155:           const msg = isAbort ? 'Import cancelled. Existing data is unchanged.' : errorMessage(e);
156:           setState(isAbort ? { status: 'idle' } : { status: 'failed', message: msg });
157:           onNotify(msg);
158:           if (!isAbort) {
159:             recordDiagnostic({
160:               operation: 'import',
161:               level: 'warn',
162:               code: (e as any)?.name,
163:               message: msg,
164:               safeIdentifier: target?.title,
165:               durationMs: Math.round(performance.now() - startTime),
166:               retryable: true,
167:             });
168:           }
169:         }
170:       } finally {
171:         if (activeOp.current === opId && outcome !== 'preview') {
172:           desktopAPI.setProgressBar(-1);
173:         }
174:       }
175:     },
176:     [cleanupStaged, onNotify, startOptions],
177:   );
178: 
179:   const runImport = useCallback(
180:     async (plans: ImportPlan[]) => {
181:       const opId = ++activeOp.current;
182:       setState({
183:         status: 'indexing',
184:         scan: { phase: 'index', done: 0, total: 0, title: plans[0]?.source.title ?? '' },
185:       });
186: 
187:       const options = startOptions(opId, 'index');
188:       const issues: Issue[] = [];
189:       const retry: ImportPlan[] = [];
190:       const titles: Manga[] = [];
191:       let count = 0;
192:       let reused = 0;
193:       const startTime = performance.now();
194: 
195:       try {
196:         for (const plan of plans) {
197:           if (activeOp.current !== opId) break;
198:           options.signal?.throwIfAborted();
199:           try {
200:             const previous = plan.targetId ? await getManga(plan.targetId) : undefined;
201:             const result = await indexSource(plan.source, options, previous, plan.sort);
202:             if (activeOp.current !== opId || options.signal?.aborted) {
203:               break;
204:             }
205:             if (!desktopAPI.isDesktop) {
206:               await saveManga(result.m);
207:             }
208:             count++;
209:             titles.push(result.m);
210:             scheduleBackground(result.m);
211:             reused += result.reused;
212:             issues.push(
213:               ...result.issues.map((i) => ({ ...i, path: plan.source.title + '/' + i.path })),
214:             );
215:             if (result.issues.length) {
216:               retry.push({ ...plan, targetId: result.m.id });
217:             }
218:           } catch (e) {
219:             if (activeOp.current !== opId || options.signal?.aborted) {
220:               break;
221:             }
222:             options.signal?.throwIfAborted();
223:             const msg = errorMessage(e);
224:             issues.push({ path: plan.source.title, reason: msg });
225:             retry.push(plan);
226:             recordDiagnostic({
227:               operation: 'import',
228:               level: 'error',
229:               code: (e as any)?.name,
230:               message: msg,
231:               safeIdentifier: plan.source.title,
232:               retryable: true,
233:             });
234:           }
235:         }
236: 
237:         await onRefresh();
238:         if (activeOp.current === opId) {
239:           setState({
240:             status: 'complete',
241:             report: { titles, count, reused, issues, retry },
242:           });
243:         }
244:       } catch (e) {
245:         await onRefresh();
246:         if (activeOp.current === opId) {
247:           const isAbort = e instanceof DOMException && e.name === 'AbortError';
248:           const msg = isAbort
249:             ? `Import cancelled. ${count} completed titles saved; the unfinished title was not changed.`
250:             : errorMessage(e);
251:           setState(isAbort ? { status: 'idle' } : { status: 'failed', message: msg });
252:           onNotify(msg);
253:           if (!isAbort) {
254:             recordDiagnostic({
255:               operation: 'import',
256:               level: 'error',
257:               code: (e as any)?.name,
258:               message: msg,
259:               durationMs: Math.round(performance.now() - startTime),
260:               retryable: true,
261:             });
262:           }
263:         }
264:       } finally {
265:         discardScan(plans.map((p) => p.source));
266:         if (activeOp.current === opId) {
267:           finish();
268:         }
269:       }
270:     },
271:     [finish, onNotify, onRefresh, startOptions],
272:   );
273: 
274:   const dismiss = useCallback(() => {
275:     cleanupStaged();
276:     setState({ status: 'idle' });
277:   }, [cleanupStaged]);
278: 
279:   return {
280:     state,
281:     isBusy: state.status === 'discovering' || state.status === 'indexing',
282:     scanState:
283:       state.status === 'discovering' || state.status === 'indexing'

1: import { useEffect } from 'react';
2: // One delegated listener; no timers, observers or animation loop.
3: export default function useInterfaceMotion(reduced: boolean) {
4:   useEffect(() => {
5:     if (reduced) return;
6:     const media = matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)');
7:     const press = (event: MouseEvent) => {
8:       if (media.matches || !(event.target instanceof Element)) return;
9:       const control = event.target.closest<HTMLElement>(
10:         '.icon-button,.close-btn,.primary,.secondary,.browse-button',
11:       );
12:       if (!control || control.matches(':disabled') || !control.animate) return;
13:       for (const animation of control.getAnimations()) animation.cancel();
14:       control.animate([{ scale: '1' }, { scale: '.96' }, { scale: '1' }], {
15:         duration: 160,
16:         easing: 'ease-out',
17:       });
18:     };
19:     document.addEventListener('click', press);
20:     return () => document.removeEventListener('click', press);
21:   }, [reduced]);
22: }

1: import {
2:   useRef,
3:   type MutableRefObject,
4:   type Dispatch,
5:   type SetStateAction,
6:   type RefObject,
7: } from 'react';
8: import type { Manga, Position, Settings } from './model';
9: import type { PageOffset } from './readerGeometry';
10: import type { PageStore } from './Reader';
11: import { saveProgress, errorMessage, type SaveProgressResult } from './data';
12: 
13: export interface UseProgressSaveOptions {
14:   metadata: MutableRefObject<Manga>;
15:   chapterRef: MutableRefObject<number>;
16:   position: MutableRefObject<Position>;
17:   latest: MutableRefObject<{
18:     s: Settings;
19:     ci: number;
20:     ready: boolean;
21:     allowed: boolean | null;
22:     page: number;
23:     zoom: number;
24:   }>;
25:   offsets: MutableRefObject<PageOffset[]>;
26:   pageToGroupRef: MutableRefObject<Int32Array>;
27:   groupsRef: MutableRefObject<number[][]>;
28:   edgeProgress: RefObject<HTMLSpanElement | null>;
29:   restoring: MutableRefObject<boolean>;
30:   singlePage: number;
31:   pageStoreRef: MutableRefObject<PageStore | null>;
32:   setReadRevision: Dispatch<SetStateAction<number>>;
33:   notify: (msg: string) => void;
34: }
35: 
36: export function useProgressSave({
37:   metadata,
38:   chapterRef,
39:   position,
40:   latest,
41:   offsets,
42:   pageToGroupRef,
43:   groupsRef,
44:   edgeProgress,
45:   restoring,
46:   singlePage,
47:   pageStoreRef,
48:   setReadRevision,
49:   notify,
50: }: UseProgressSaveOptions) {
51:   const dirty = useRef(false);
52:   const lastSave = useRef(0);
53:   const inFlight = useRef<Promise<SaveProgressResult> | null>(null);
54:   const queuedFlush = useRef<Promise<SaveProgressResult> | null>(null);
55:   const snapshotRevision = useRef(0);
56:   const committedRevision = useRef(0);
57: 
58:   function flush(): Promise<SaveProgressResult> {
59:     if (!latest.current.ready || !latest.current.allowed) {
60:       return Promise.resolve('missing');
61:     }
62:     if (inFlight.current) {
63:       if (!queuedFlush.current) {
64:         queuedFlush.current = inFlight.current
65:           .catch(() => {
66:             // Absorb in-flight rejection so the queued retry attempt can proceed
67:           })
68:           .then(() => {
69:             queuedFlush.current = null;
70:             if (dirty.current) {
71:               return flush();
72:             }
73:             return 'saved';
74:           })
75:           .catch((err) => {
76:             queuedFlush.current = null;
77:             throw err;
78:           });
79:       }
80:       return queuedFlush.current;
81:     }
82:     if (!dirty.current) {
83:       return Promise.resolve('saved');
84:     }
85:     const index = chapterRef.current;
86:     const c = metadata.current.chapters[index];
87:     if (!c) {
88:       dirty.current = false;
89:       return Promise.resolve('missing');
90:     }
91:     const targetRevision = snapshotRevision.current;
92:     const p = { ...position.current };
93:     const isRead = Boolean(c.read);
94:     const bookmarks = c.bookmarks ? [...c.bookmarks] : undefined;
95: 
96:     dirty.current = false;
97:     lastSave.current = performance.now();
98: 
99:     const savePromise = (async (): Promise<SaveProgressResult> => {
100:       try {
101:         const result = await saveProgress(metadata.current, index, p, isRead, bookmarks);
102:         if (result === 'saved') {
103:           committedRevision.current = Math.max(committedRevision.current, targetRevision);
104:         } else {
105:           if (committedRevision.current < targetRevision) {
106:             dirty.current = true;
107:           }
108:         }
109:         return result;
110:       } catch (err) {
111:         if (committedRevision.current < targetRevision) {
112:           dirty.current = true;
113:         }
114:         throw err;
115:       }
116:     })();
117: 
118:     inFlight.current = savePromise;
119:     // Handled cleanup chain prevents unhandled rejection on the cleanup promise
120:     savePromise.then(
121:       () => {
122:         if (inFlight.current === savePromise) {
123:           inFlight.current = null;
124:         }
125:       },
126:       () => {
127:         if (inFlight.current === savePromise) {
128:           inFlight.current = null;
129:         }
130:       },
131:     );
132:     return savePromise;
133:   }
134: 
135:   function capture() {
136:     if (restoring.current || !latest.current.ready || !offsets.current.length) return;
137:     const y = window.scrollY;
138:     const list = offsets.current;
139:     let index = 0;
140:     if (latest.current.s.mode === 'continuous') {
141:       let lo = 0,
142:         hi = list.length - 1;
143:       while (lo < hi) {
144:         const mid = Math.ceil((lo + hi) / 2);
145:         if (list[mid].top <= y + 1) lo = mid;
146:         else hi = mid - 1;
147:       }
148:       index = lo;
149:     } else {
150:       index = Math.max(0, Math.min(list.length - 1, latest.current.page));
151:     }
152:     const o = list[index];
153:     position.current = {
154:       page: o.page,
155:       path: metadata.current.chapters[chapterRef.current].pages[o.page]?.path,
156:       fraction: Math.min(1, Math.max(0, (y - o.top) / o.height)),
157:     };
158:     latest.current.page = o.page;
159:     if (latest.current.s.mode === 'continuous' && pageStoreRef.current) {
160:       pageStoreRef.current.setPage(o.page);
161:     }
162:     const c = metadata.current.chapters[chapterRef.current];
163:     const final = list[c.pages.length - 1] || list.at(-1);
164:     if (final && y + innerHeight >= final.bottom - 2 && !c.read) {
165:       c.read = true;
166:       setReadRevision((v) => v + 1);
167:     }
168:     if (edgeProgress.current) {
169:       const currentPage =
170:         latest.current.s.mode === 'single'
171:           ? singlePage
172:           : pageStoreRef.current
173:             ? pageStoreRef.current.getPage()
174:             : 0;
175:       const gIndex = pageToGroupRef.current[currentPage] ?? 0;
176:       const currentGroup = groupsRef.current[gIndex] || [currentPage];
177:       const progress =
178:         latest.current.s.mode === 'single'
179:           ? (Math.max(...currentGroup) + 1) / c.pages.length
180:           : final && y + innerHeight >= final.bottom - 2
181:             ? 1
182:             : (position.current.page + position.current.fraction) / c.pages.length;
183:       edgeProgress.current.style.transform = `scaleX(${Math.min(1, Math.max(0, progress))})`;
184:     }
185:     c.position = { ...position.current };
186:     dirty.current = true;
187:     snapshotRevision.current++;
188:     if (performance.now() - lastSave.current >= 1500) {
189:       void flush().catch((e) => notify(errorMessage(e)));
190:     }
191:   }
192: 
193:   return {
194:     dirty,
195:     lastSave,
196:     snapshotRevision,
197:     committedRevision,
198:     flush,
199:     capture,
200:   };
201: }

1: import { useRef, type Dispatch, type SetStateAction, type MutableRefObject } from 'react';
2: import type { Settings } from './model';
3: 
4: export interface UseReaderKeysOptions {
5:   latest: MutableRefObject<{
6:     s: Settings;
7:     ci: number;
8:     ready: boolean;
9:     allowed: boolean | null;
10:     page: number;
11:     zoom: number;
12:   }>;
13:   actions: MutableRefObject<{
14:     pause: () => void;
15:     step: (direction: number) => void;
16:     goChapter: (index: number, reset?: boolean) => Promise<void>;
17:   }>;
18:   setPlaying: Dispatch<SetStateAction<boolean>>;
19:   setBars: Dispatch<SetStateAction<{ top: boolean; bottom: boolean }>>;
20:   toggleBookmark: () => void;
21: }
22: 
23: export function useReaderKeys({
24:   latest,
25:   actions,
26:   setPlaying,
27:   setBars,
28:   toggleBookmark,
29: }: UseReaderKeysOptions) {
30:   const keysHeld = useRef(new Set<string>());
31:   const navigatingRef = useRef(false);
32: 
33:   const onKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
34:     keysHeld.current.add(e.code);
35:     actions.current.pause();
36:     if (
37:       e.defaultPrevented ||
38:       document.querySelector('[role="dialog"],[data-reader-dropdown]') ||
39:       (e.target as HTMLElement).closest('input,select,button,a,[contenteditable="true"]')
40:     )
41:       return;
42:     if (e.code === 'Space') {
43:       e.preventDefault();
44:       if (!e.repeat) setPlaying((v) => !v);
45:     }
46:     if (e.key === 'ArrowRight' || e.key === 'ArrowLeft') {
47:       e.preventDefault();
48:       const delta = e.key === 'ArrowRight' ? 1 : -1;
49:       if (latest.current.s.mode === 'single') {
50:         actions.current.step(delta * (latest.current.s.direction === 'rtl' ? -1 : 1));
51:       } else {
52:         // Serialize navigation: ignore repeated keystrokes while chapter transition is active
53:         if (navigatingRef.current) return;
54:         navigatingRef.current = true;
55:         actions.current
56:           .goChapter(latest.current.ci + delta)
57:           .catch((err) => {
58:             console.warn('Chapter navigation failed:', err);
59:           })
60:           .finally(() => {
61:             navigatingRef.current = false;
62:           });
63:       }
64:     }
65:     if (e.key === 'Escape') {
66:       setPlaying(false);
67:       setBars({ top: true, bottom: true });
68:     }
69:     if (e.key.toLowerCase() === 'b' && !e.ctrlKey && !e.metaKey) {
70:       e.preventDefault();
71:       toggleBookmark();
72:     }
73:   };
74: 
75:   const onKeyUp = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
76:     keysHeld.current.delete(e.code);
77:     actions.current.pause();
78:   };
79: 
80:   return {
81:     keysHeld,
82:     onKeyDown,
83:     onKeyUp,
84:   };
85: }

6: const MAX_THUMBNAIL_BYTES: usize = 10 * 1024 * 1024; // 10 MiB safety cap
7: 
8: pub fn percent_decode(input: &str) -> String {
9:     let mut bytes = Vec::with_capacity(input.len());
10:     let mut chars = input.bytes();
11:     while let Some(b) = chars.next() {
12:         if b == b'%' {
13:             let h1 = chars.next();
14:             let h2 = chars.next();
15:             match (h1, h2) {
16:                 (Some(c1), Some(c2)) => {
17:                     let hex_str = [c1, c2];
18:                     if let Ok(s) = std::str::from_utf8(&hex_str) {
19:                         if let Ok(val) = u8::from_str_radix(s, 16) {
20:                             bytes.push(val);
21:                             continue;
22:                         }
23:                     }
24:                     bytes.push(b'%');
25:                     bytes.push(c1);
26:                     bytes.push(c2);
27:                 }
28:                 (Some(c1), None) => {
29:                     bytes.push(b'%');
30:                     bytes.push(c1);
31:                 }
32:                 _ => {
33:                     bytes.push(b'%');
34:                 }
35:             }
36:         } else {
37:             bytes.push(b);
38:         }
39:     }
40:     String::from_utf8(bytes).unwrap_or_else(|_| input.to_string())
41: }
42: 
43: /// Sanitizes thumbnail ID to prevent path traversal attacks, namespace collisions,
44: /// and Windows reserved device name collisions (e.g. CON, PRN, AUX, NUL).
45: /// Always hashes with an explicit "thumb_v1:" domain separator and "t_" prefix.
46: pub fn sanitize_id(id: &str) -> String {
47:     if id.is_empty() {
48:         return String::new();
49:     }
50:     let mut hasher = Sha256::new();
51:     hasher.update(b"thumb_v1:");
52:     hasher.update(id.as_bytes());
53:     format!("t_{:x}", hasher.finalize())
54: }
55: 
56: /// Generates a collision-resistant SHA-256 hash for variant cache keys with an explicit namespace.
57: pub fn hash_key(key: &str) -> String {
58:     let mut hasher = Sha256::new();
59:     hasher.update(b"variant_v1:");
60:     hasher.update(key.as_bytes());
61:     format!("v_{:x}", hasher.finalize())
62: }
63: 
64: pub fn get_thumbnail_path(app: &AppHandle, id: &str) -> Result<PathBuf, String> {
65:     if id.is_empty() {
66:         return Err("Thumbnail ID cannot be empty".into());
67:     }
68: 
69:     let safe_id = sanitize_id(id);
70:     let cache_dir = app
71:         .path()
72:         .app_cache_dir()
73:         .map_err(|e| format!("Failed to resolve cache dir: {e}"))?;
74: 
75:     let thumb_dir = cache_dir.join("thumbnails");
76:     if !thumb_dir.exists() {
77:         let _ = std::fs::create_dir_all(&thumb_dir);
78:     }
79: 
80:     Ok(thumb_dir.join(format!("{}.webp", safe_id)))
81: }

83: pub fn get_variant_path(
84:     app: &AppHandle,
85:     key: &str,
86:     width: u32,
87:     height: u32,
88: ) -> Result<PathBuf, String> {
89:     let hash = hash_key(key);
90:     let cache_dir = app
91:         .path()
92:         .app_cache_dir()
93:         .map_err(|e| format!("Failed to resolve cache dir: {e}"))?;
94: 
95:     let var_dir = cache_dir.join("variants");
96:     if !var_dir.exists() {
97:         let _ = std::fs::create_dir_all(&var_dir);
98:     }
99: 
100:     Ok(var_dir.join(format!("{}_{}x{}.webp", hash, width, height)))
101: }

103: #[tauri::command]
104: pub async fn save_thumbnail(
105:     app: AppHandle,
106:     request: tauri::ipc::Request<'_>,
107: ) -> Result<(), String> {
108:     let (id, bytes) = match request.body() {
109:         tauri::ipc::InvokeBody::Raw(raw_bytes) => {
110:             let id_header = request
111:                 .headers()
112:                 .get("x-id")
113:                 .and_then(|v| v.to_str().ok())
114:                 .ok_or_else(|| "missing x-id header".to_string())?;
115:             (percent_decode(id_header), raw_bytes.to_vec())
116:         }
117:         tauri::ipc::InvokeBody::Json(json) => {
118:             let id = json
119:                 .get("id")
120:                 .and_then(|v| v.as_str())
121:                 .unwrap_or("")
122:                 .to_string();
123:             let bytes = if let Some(arr) = json.get("bytes").and_then(|v| v.as_array()) {
124:                 let mut byte_vec = Vec::with_capacity(arr.len());
125:                 for (idx, item) in arr.iter().enumerate() {
126:                     let val = item.as_u64().ok_or_else(|| {
127:                         format!("Invalid byte entry at index {idx}: expected unsigned integer")
128:                     })?;
129:                     if val > 255 {
130:                         return Err(format!(
131:                             "Byte value {val} at index {idx} exceeds 255 (valid byte range 0..=255)"
132:                         ));
133:                     }
134:                     byte_vec.push(val as u8);
135:                 }
136:                 byte_vec
137:             } else {
138:                 return Err("Expected raw body or JSON 'bytes' array".into());
139:             };
140:             (id, bytes)
141:         }
142:     };
143: 
144:     if id.is_empty() {
145:         return Err("Thumbnail ID cannot be empty".into());
146:     }
147: 
148:     if bytes.len() > MAX_THUMBNAIL_BYTES {
149:         return Err(format!(
150:             "Thumbnail exceeds maximum size of {} bytes",
151:             MAX_THUMBNAIL_BYTES
152:         ));
153:     }
154: 
155:     let path = get_thumbnail_path(&app, &id)?;
156:     tauri::async_runtime::spawn_blocking(move || -> Result<(), String> {
157:         crate::commands::fs::write_atomic(&path, &bytes).map_err(|e| format!("{e}"))?;
158:         if !path.exists() {
159:             return Err("Failed to verify thumbnail file existence on disk".to_string());
160:         }
161:         Ok(())
162:     })
163:     .await
164:     .map_err(|e| format!("Failed to spawn blocking write: {e}"))?
165:     .map_err(|e| format!("Failed to write thumbnail to disk: {e}"))?;
166: 
167:     Ok(())
168: }

170: #[tauri::command]
171: pub async fn read_thumbnail(app: AppHandle, id: String) -> Result<tauri::ipc::Response, String> {
172:     let path = get_thumbnail_path(&app, &id)?;
173:     if !path.exists() || !path.is_file() {
174:         return Ok(tauri::ipc::Response::new(Vec::new()));
175:     }
176: 
177:     let bytes = tokio::fs::read(&path)
178:         .await
179:         .map_err(|e| format!("Failed to read thumbnail: {e}"))?;
180: 
181:     Ok(tauri::ipc::Response::new(bytes))
182: }
