194: /// Prunes image variants cache directory if size exceeds 500 MiB or 1,000 files.
195: pub fn prune_cache(app: &AppHandle) -> Result<(), String> {
196:     let cache_dir = app
197:         .path()
198:         .app_cache_dir()
199:         .map_err(|e| format!("Failed to resolve cache dir: {e}"))?;
200:     let var_dir = cache_dir.join("variants");
201:     if !var_dir.exists() {
202:         return Ok(());
203:     }
204: 
205:     if let Ok(entries) = std::fs::read_dir(&var_dir) {
206:         let mut files: Vec<(PathBuf, std::time::SystemTime, u64)> = Vec::new();
207:         for entry in entries.flatten() {
208:             if let Ok(meta) = entry.metadata() {
209:                 if meta.is_file() {
210:                     let mtime = meta.modified().unwrap_or(std::time::UNIX_EPOCH);
211:                     files.push((entry.path(), mtime, meta.len()));
212:                 }
213:             }
214:         }
215: 
216:         let mut total_size: u64 = files.iter().map(|f| f.2).sum();
217:         let mut remaining = files.len();
218:         if total_size > 500 * 1024 * 1024 || remaining > 1000 {
219:             files.sort_by_key(|f| f.1); // oldest first
220:             for (p, _, len) in files {
221:                 if total_size <= 400 * 1024 * 1024 && remaining <= 800 {
222:                     break;
223:                 }
224:                 if std::fs::remove_file(p).is_ok() {
225:                     total_size = total_size.saturating_sub(len);
226:                     remaining = remaining.saturating_sub(1);
227:                 }
228:             }
229:         }
230:     }
231: 
232:     Ok(())
233: }
234: 
235: #[tauri::command]
236: pub async fn delete_thumbnail(app: AppHandle, id: String) -> Result<(), String> {
237:     let path = get_thumbnail_path(&app, &id)?;
238:     if path.exists() {
239:         let _ = tokio::fs::remove_file(&path).await;
240:     }
241:     let app_clone = app.clone();
242:     let _ = tauri::async_runtime::spawn_blocking(move || {
243:         let _ = prune_cache(&app_clone);
244:     })
245:     .await;
246:     Ok(())
247: }

249: #[tauri::command]
250: pub async fn clear_thumbnails(app: AppHandle) -> Result<(), String> {
251:     let cache_dir = app
252:         .path()
253:         .app_cache_dir()
254:         .map_err(|e| format!("Failed to resolve cache dir: {e}"))?;
255:

257:     if thumb_dir.exists() {
258:         let _ = tokio::fs::remove_dir_all(&thumb_dir).await;
259:     }
260:     let var_dir = cache_dir.join("variants");
261:     if var_dir.exists() {
262:         let _ = tokio::fs::remove_dir_all(&var_dir).await;
263:     }
264:     Ok(())
265: }

267: #[derive(Debug, Clone, serde::Serialize, serde::Deserialize)]
268: pub struct StorageStats {
269:     pub db_bytes: u64,
270:     pub cache_bytes: u64,
271:     pub total_bytes: u64,
272: }
273: 
274: fn dir_size(path: &Path) -> u64 {
275:     let mut total = 0;
276:     if let Ok(entries) = std::fs::read_dir(path) {
277:         for entry in entries.flatten() {
278:             if let Ok(metadata) = entry.metadata() {
279:                 if metadata.is_dir() {
280:                     total += dir_size(&entry.path());
281:                 } else {
282:                     total += metadata.len();
283:                 }
284:             }
285:         }
286:     }
287:     total
288: }

290: #[tauri::command]
291: pub async fn get_storage_stats(app: AppHandle) -> Result<StorageStats, String> {
292:     tauri::async_runtime::spawn_blocking(move || {
293:         let mut db_bytes = 0u64;
294:         if let Ok(data_dir) = app.path().app_data_dir() {
295:             for file_name in &["library.db", "library.db-wal", "library.db-shm"] {
296:                 let p = data_dir.join(file_name);
297:                 if let Ok(meta) = std::fs::metadata(&p) {
298:                     db_bytes += meta.len();
299:                 }
300:             }
301:         }
302: 
303:         let mut cache_bytes = 0u64;
304:         if let Ok(cache_dir) = app.path().app_cache_dir() {
305:             cache_bytes += dir_size(&cache_dir.join("thumbnails"));
306:             cache_bytes += dir_size(&cache_dir.join("variants"));
307:         }
308: 
309:         let total_bytes = db_bytes + cache_bytes;
310:         Ok(StorageStats {
311:             db_bytes,
312:             cache_bytes,
313:             total_bytes,
314:         })
315:     })
316:     .await
317:     .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?
318: }

320: #[tauri::command]
321: pub async fn save_file_dialog(
322:     app: AppHandle,
323:     default_name: String,
324:     content: String,
325: ) -> Result<bool, String> {
326:     let safe_default = Path::new(&default_name)
327:         .file_name()
328:         .and_then(|n| n.to_str())
329:         .unwrap_or("export.txt")
330:         .to_string();
331: 
332:     let app_clone = app.clone();
333:     let path_opt = tokio::task::spawn_blocking(move || {
334:         let mut builder = app_clone.dialog().file();
335:         if !safe_default.is_empty() {
336:             builder = builder.set_file_name(&safe_default);
337:         }
338:         builder.blocking_save_file()
339:     })
340:     .await
341:     .map_err(|e| format!("Dialog task join error: {e}"))?;
342: 
343:     if let Some(file_path) = path_opt {
344:         let pb = file_path
345:             .into_path()
346:             .map_err(|e| format!("Invalid selected file path: {e}"))?;
347:         let ext = pb
348:             .extension()
349:             .and_then(|e| e.to_str())
350:             .map(|e| e.to_ascii_lowercase())
351:             .unwrap_or_default();
352:         if ext != "json" && ext != "txt" {
353:             return Err("Only .json and .txt exports are permitted".to_string());
354:         }
355:         let bytes = content.into_bytes();
356:         tokio::task::spawn_blocking(move || crate::commands::fs::write_atomic(&pb, &bytes))
357:             .await
358:             .map_err(|e| format!("Write task join error: {e}"))?
359:             .map_err(|e| format!("Failed to write file: {e}"))?;
360:         Ok(true)
361:     } else {
362:         Ok(false)
363:     }
364: }

366: #[cfg(test)]
367: mod tests {
368:     use super::*;
369: 
370:     #[test]
371:     fn test_percent_decode() {
372:         assert_eq!(percent_decode("hello"), "hello");
373:         assert_eq!(percent_decode("hello%20world"), "hello world");
374:         assert_eq!(percent_decode("%2Fpath%2Fto%2Ffile"), "/path/to/file");
375:         assert_eq!(percent_decode("special%3A%2B%3D"), "special:+=");
376:         assert_eq!(percent_decode("incomplete%2"), "incomplete%2");
377:     }
378: 
379:     #[test]
380:     fn test_sanitize_id() {
381:         let safe1 = sanitize_id("manga-123_abc");
382:         assert!(safe1.starts_with("t_"));
383:         assert_eq!(safe1.len(), 66);
384: 
385:         // Deterministic hashing
386:         assert_eq!(safe1, sanitize_id("manga-123_abc"));
387: 
388:         // Windows reserved device names cannot form raw filename
389:         let con_safe = sanitize_id("con");
390:         assert!(con_safe.starts_with("t_"));
391:         assert_ne!(con_safe, "con");
392:         assert_eq!(con_safe.len(), 66);
393: 
394:         let aux_safe = sanitize_id("aux");
395:         assert!(aux_safe.starts_with("t_"));
396:         assert_ne!(aux_safe, "aux");
397: 
398:         // Traversal attempts cannot escape or contain slashes
399:         let traversal = sanitize_id("../../etc/passwd");
400:         assert!(!traversal.contains(".."));
401:         assert!(!traversal.contains('/'));
402:         assert!(!traversal.contains('\\'));
403:         assert_eq!(traversal.len(), 66);
404: 
405:         // Different IDs produce different hashes
406:         assert_ne!(sanitize_id("manga-1"), sanitize_id("manga-2"));
407: 
408:         // Empty string returns empty
409:         assert_eq!(sanitize_id(""), "");
410:     }
411: 
412:     #[test]
413:     fn test_hash_key() {
414:         let hash1 = hash_key("path/to/image.png");
415:         let hash2 = hash_key("path/to/image.png");
416:         assert_eq!(
417:             hash1, hash2,
418:             "Identical inputs must produce identical hashes"
419:         );
420:         assert!(hash1.starts_with("v_"));
421: 
422:         let hash3 = hash_key("path/to/image2.png");
423:         assert_ne!(
424:             hash1, hash3,
425:             "Different inputs must produce different hashes"
426:         );
427: 
428:         assert_eq!(
429:             hash1.len(),
430:             66,
431:             "Hash key must be 66-char prefixed SHA-256 string"
432:         );
433:         assert!(hash1[2..].chars().all(|c| c.is_ascii_hexdigit()));
434:     }
435: }

20: pub struct AppConfig {
21:     pub last_opened_roots: Vec<String>,
22:     pub window_preferences: WindowPreferences,
23:     pub custom_preferences: HashMap<String, serde_json::Value>,
24: }

26: pub struct AppConfigState(pub RwLock<AppConfig>);
27: 
28: impl AppConfigState {
29:     pub fn new(app: &AppHandle) -> Self {
30:         let config = load_persisted_config(app);
31:         Self(RwLock::new(config))
32:     }
33: 
34:     pub fn get(&self) -> AppConfig {
35:         self.0.read().unwrap().clone()
36:     }
37: 
38:     pub fn update<F, R>(&self, app: &AppHandle, f: F) -> Result<R, String>
39:     where
40:         F: FnOnce(&mut AppConfig) -> R,
41:     {
42:         let mut lock = self.0.write().unwrap();
43:         let mut cloned = lock.clone();
44:         let res = f(&mut cloned);
45:         save_persisted_config(app, &cloned)?;
46:         *lock = cloned;
47:         Ok(res)
48:     }
49: }

51: fn get_config_path(app: &AppHandle) -> Result<PathBuf, String> {
52:     let app_dir = app
53:         .path()
54:         .app_data_dir()
55:         .map_err(|e| format!("Failed to resolve app data dir: {e}"))?;
56:     let _ = fs::create_dir_all(&app_dir);
57:     Ok(app_dir.join("app_config.json"))
58: }
59: 
60: pub fn load_persisted_config(app: &AppHandle) -> AppConfig {
61:     if let Ok(path) = get_config_path(app) {
62:         if path.exists() {
63:             if let Ok(content) = fs::read_to_string(&path) {
64:                 if let Ok(config) = serde_json::from_str::<AppConfig>(&content) {
65:                     return config;
66:                 }
67:             }
68:         }
69:         // Fallback recovery: check if a valid .tmp file exists from an interrupted rename
70:         let tmp = path.with_extension("tmp");
71:         if tmp.exists() {
72:             if let Ok(content) = fs::read_to_string(&tmp) {
73:                 if let Ok(config) = serde_json::from_str::<AppConfig>(&content) {
74:                     let _ = fs::rename(&tmp, &path);
75:                     return config;
76:                 }
77:             }
78:         }
79:     }
80:     AppConfig::default()
81: }
82: 
83: pub fn save_persisted_config(app: &AppHandle, config: &AppConfig) -> Result<(), String> {
84:     let path = get_config_path(app)?;
85:     let json = serde_json::to_string_pretty(config)
86:         .map_err(|e| format!("Failed to serialize config: {e}"))?;
87:     write_atomic(&path, json.as_bytes())
88:         .map_err(|e| format!("Failed to write config atomically: {e}"))?;
89:     Ok(())
90: }

142: #[tauri::command(async)]
143: pub fn record_opened_root(app: AppHandle, root_path: String) -> Result<Vec<String>, String> {
144:     let trimmed = root_path.trim().to_string();
145:     if trimmed.is_empty() {
146:         return Ok(Vec::new());
147:     }
148: 
149:     let raw_path = std::path::Path::new(&trimmed);
150:     let canonical = dunce::canonicalize(raw_path)
151:         .map_err(|e| format!("Cannot record unresolvable path: {e}"))?;
152: 
153:     let allowed = app.state::<super::fs::AllowedRoots>();
154:     if !allowed.is_allowed(&canonical) {
155:         return Err(format!(
156:             "Path '{}' is not under an authorized root",
157:             canonical.display()
158:         ));
159:     }
160: 
161:     let canonical_str = canonical.to_string_lossy().to_string();
162:     let state = app.state::<AppConfigState>();
163:     state.update(&app, |cfg| {
164:         // Remove duplicate if already present, push to front
165:         cfg.last_opened_roots
166:             .retain(|r| r != &canonical_str && r != &trimmed);
167:         cfg.last_opened_roots.insert(0, canonical_str);
168:         // Cap at 25 entries
169:         if cfg.last_opened_roots.len() > 25 {
170:             cfg.last_opened_roots.truncate(25);
171:         }
172:         cfg.last_opened_roots.clone()
173:     })
174: }
175: 
176: #[tauri::command(async)]
177: pub fn get_last_opened_roots(app: AppHandle) -> Result<Vec<String>, String> {
178:     let state = app.state::<AppConfigState>();
179:     Ok(state.get().last_opened_roots)
180: }

113: #[derive(Debug, Clone)]

116:     pub created_at: std::time::Instant,
117:     pub candidates: Vec<DiscoveredPage>,

120: #[derive(Default)]
121: pub struct DiscoveryState {
122:     snapshots: std::sync::Mutex<std::collections::HashMap<PathBuf, DiscoverySnapshot>>,
123: }
124: 
125: impl DiscoveryState {
126:     pub fn store(&self, root_path: PathBuf, candidates: Vec<DiscoveredPage>) {
127:         if let Ok(mut map) = self.snapshots.lock() {
128:             let now = std::time::Instant::now();
129:             map.retain(|_, v| now.duration_since(v.created_at).as_secs() < 900);
130:             if map.len() >= 8 {
131:                 if let Some(oldest) = map
132:                     .iter()
133:                     .min_by_key(|(_, v)| v.created_at)
134:                     .map(|(k, _)| k.clone())
135:                 {
136:                     map.remove(&oldest);
137:                 }
138:             }
139:             map.insert(
140:                 root_path.clone(),
141:                 DiscoverySnapshot {
142:                     root_path,
143:                     created_at: now,
144:                     candidates,
145:                 },
146:             );
147:         }
148:     }
149: 
150:     pub fn get_for_path(&self, path: &Path) -> Option<DiscoverySnapshot> {
151:         if let Ok(map) = self.snapshots.lock() {
152:             if let Some(snap) = map.get(path) {
153:                 return Some(snap.clone());
154:             }
155:             for (root, snap) in map.iter() {
156:                 if path.starts_with(root) {
157:                     return Some(snap.clone());
158:                 }
159:             }
160:         }
161:         None
162:     }
163: }
164: 
165: pub fn natural_order(a: &str, b: &str) -> std::cmp::Ordering {
166:     fn has_word_prefix(s: &str, prefix: &str) -> bool {
167:         if let Some(rest) = s.strip_prefix(prefix) {
168:             rest.is_empty() || !rest.chars().next().unwrap().is_alphanumeric()
169:         } else {
170:             false
171:         }
172:     }
173: 
174:     fn category(s: &str) -> i32 {
175:         let trimmed = s.trim_start();
176:         let lower = trimmed.to_lowercase();
177:         if has_word_prefix(&lower, "prologue") || has_word_prefix(&lower, "opening") {
178:             -1
179:         } else if has_word_prefix(&lower, "extra")
180:             || has_word_prefix(&lower, "bonus")
181:             || has_word_prefix(&lower, "special")
182:             || has_word_prefix(&lower, "epilogue")
183:         {
184:             1
185:         } else {
186:             0
187:         }
188:     }
189: 
190:     let cat_a = category(a);
191:     let cat_b = category(b);
192:     if cat_a != cat_b {
193:         return cat_a.cmp(&cat_b);
194:     }
195: 
196:     #[derive(Debug, PartialEq)]
197:     enum Token<'a> {
198:         Num(u128, usize),
199:         BigNum(&'a str),
200:         Str(&'a str),
201:     }
202: 
203:     fn extract_tokens(s: &str) -> Vec<Token<'_>> {
204:         let mut tokens = Vec::new();
205:         let mut start = 0;
206:         let mut in_num = false;
207:         let bytes = s.as_bytes();
208:         let len = bytes.len();
209: 
210:         let mut i = 0;
211:         while i < len {
212:             let c = bytes[i];
213:             let is_digit = c.is_ascii_digit();
214:             if is_digit != in_num {
215:                 if i > start {
216:                     let slice = &s[start..i];
217:                     if in_num {
218:                         if let Ok(n) = slice.parse::<u128>() {
219:                             tokens.push(Token::Num(n, slice.len()));
220:                         } else {
221:                             tokens.push(Token::BigNum(slice));
222:                         }
223:                     } else {
224:                         tokens.push(Token::Str(slice));
225:                     }
226:                 }
227:                 start = i;
228:                 in_num = is_digit;
229:             }
230:             i += 1;
231:         }
232: 
233:         if start < len {
234:             let slice = &s[start..len];
235:             if in_num {
236:                 if let Ok(n) = slice.parse::<u128>() {
237:                     tokens.push(Token::Num(n, slice.len()));
238:                 } else {
239:                     tokens.push(Token::BigNum(slice));
240:                 }
241:             } else {
242:                 tokens.push(Token::Str(slice));
243:             }
244:         }
245: 
246:         tokens
247:     }
248: 
249:     let lower_a = a.to_lowercase();
250:     let lower_b = b.to_lowercase();
251: 
252:     let tokens_a = extract_tokens(&lower_a);
253:     let tokens_b = extract_tokens(&lower_b);
254: 
255:     for (x, y) in tokens_a.iter().zip(tokens_b.iter()) {
256:         match (x, y) {
257:             (Token::Num(nx, lx), Token::Num(ny, ly)) => {
258:                 let ord = nx.cmp(ny);
259:                 if ord != std::cmp::Ordering::Equal {
260:                     return ord;
261:                 }
262:                 if lx != ly {
263:                     return lx.cmp(ly);
264:                 }
265:             }
266:             (Token::BigNum(bx), Token::BigNum(by)) => {
267:                 let tx = bx.trim_start_matches('0');
268:                 let ty = by.trim_start_matches('0');
269:                 let ord = tx.len().cmp(&ty.len()).then_with(|| tx.cmp(ty));
270:                 if ord != std::cmp::Ordering::Equal {
271:                     return ord;
272:                 }
273:                 if bx.len() != by.len() {
274:                     return bx.len().cmp(&by.len());
275:                 }
276:             }
277:             (Token::Num(nx, _), Token::BigNum(by)) => {
278:                 let ty = by.trim_start_matches('0');
279:                 if ty.len() > 38 {
280:                     return std::cmp::Ordering::Less;
281:                 }
282:                 if let Ok(ny) = ty.parse::<u128>() {
283:                     let ord = nx.cmp(&ny);
284:                     if ord != std::cmp::Ordering::Equal {
285:                         return ord;
286:                     }
287:                 }
288:                 return std::cmp::Ordering::Less;
289:             }
290:             (Token::BigNum(bx), Token::Num(ny, _)) => {
291:                 let tx = bx.trim_start_matches('0');
292:                 if tx.len() > 38 {
293:                     return std::cmp::Ordering::Greater;
294:                 }
295:                 if let Ok(nx) = tx.parse::<u128>() {
296:                     let ord = nx.cmp(ny);
297:                     if ord != std::cmp::Ordering::Equal {
298:                         return ord;
299:                     }
300:                 }
301:                 return std::cmp::Ordering::Greater;
302:             }
303:             (Token::Str(sx), Token::Str(sy)) => {
304:                 let ord = sx.chars().cmp(sy.chars());
305:                 if ord != std::cmp::Ordering::Equal {
306:                     return ord;
307:                 }
308:             }
309:             (Token::Num(_, _) | Token::BigNum(_), Token::Str(_)) => {
310:                 return std::cmp::Ordering::Less;
311:             }
312:             (Token::Str(_), Token::Num(_, _) | Token::BigNum(_)) => {
313:                 return std::cmp::Ordering::Greater;
314:             }
315:         }
316:     }
317: 
318:     tokens_a.len().cmp(&tokens_b.len()).then_with(|| a.cmp(b))
319: }
320: 
321: #[tauri::command(async)]
322: pub fn natural_sort_paths(mut paths: Vec<String>) -> Vec<String> {
323:     paths.sort_by(|a, b| natural_order(a, b));
324:     paths
325: }
326: 
327: pub fn resolve_relative_path(base_dir: &Path, rel_path: &str) -> Result<PathBuf, FsError> {
328:     let trimmed = rel_path.trim().replace('\\', "/");
329:     if trimmed.is_empty() {
330:         return Err(FsError::NotFound("Empty relative path".to_string()));
331:     }
332:     let rel = Path::new(&trimmed);
333: 
334:     // Prevent parent directory traversal
335:     if rel
336:         .components()
337:         .any(|c| matches!(c, std::path::Component::ParentDir))
338:     {
339:         return Err(FsError::PathNotAllowed(format!(
340:             "Path contains illegal parent directory traversal: '{rel_path}'"
341:         )));
342:     }
343: 
344:     // Prevent absolute paths from escaping base_dir
345:     #[cfg(windows)]
346:     if trimmed.starts_with('/') || (trimmed.len() >= 2 && trimmed.as_bytes()[1] == b':') {
347:         return Err(FsError::PathNotAllowed(format!(
348:             "Path must be relative to root: '{rel_path}'"
349:         )));
350:     }
351:     #[cfg(not(windows))]
352:     if rel.is_absolute() {
353:         return Err(FsError::PathNotAllowed(format!(
354:             "Path must be relative to root: '{rel_path}'"
355:         )));
356:     }
357: 
358:     let combined = base_dir.join(rel);
359:     let canonical = dunce::canonicalize(&combined).map_err(|e| {
360:         FsError::NotFound(format!(
361:             "Failed to resolve path '{}' under '{}': {e}",
362:             rel_path,
363:             base_dir.display()
364:         ))
365:     })?;
366: 
367:     let canonical_base = dunce::canonicalize(base_dir).unwrap_or_else(|_| base_dir.to_path_buf());
368:     if !canonical.starts_with(&canonical_base) {
369:         return Err(FsError::PathNotAllowed(format!(
370:             "Path '{}' escapes base directory '{}'",
371:             rel_path,
372:             base_dir.display()
373:         )));
374:     }
375: 
376:     Ok(canonical)
377: }
378: 
379: #[tauri::command]
380: pub async fn resolve_manga_path(
381:     app: AppHandle,
382:     manga_id: Option<String>,
383:     relative_path: String,
384:     local_path: Option<String>,
385: ) -> Result<String, FsError> {
386:     let base_str = if let Some(ref lp) = local_path {
387:         if !lp.trim().is_empty() {
388:             Some(lp.trim().to_string())
389:         } else {
390:             None
391:         }
392:     } else {
393:         None
394:     };
395: 
396:     let base_str = match base_str {
397:         Some(s) => s,
398:         None => {
399:             if let Some(ref id) = manga_id {
400:                 if let Some(db) = app.try_state::<crate::db::DbState>() {
401:                     db.get_local_path(id).await.ok_or_else(|| {
402:                         FsError::NotFound(format!("Manga '{id}' has no localPath recorded"))
403:                     })?
404:                 } else {
405:                     return Err(FsError::NotFound(
406:                         "Database state not initialized".to_string(),
407:                     ));
408:                 }
409:             } else {
410:                 return Err(FsError::NotFound(
411:                     "Neither manga_id nor local_path provided".to_string(),
412:                 ));
413:             }
414:         }
415:     };
416: 
417:     let base_dir = PathBuf::from(base_str);
418:     let resolved = resolve_relative_path(&base_dir, &relative_path)?;
419: 
420:     let allowed = app.state::<AllowedRoots>();
421:     if !allowed.is_allowed(&resolved) {
422:         return Err(FsError::PathNotAllowed(format!(
423:             "Resolved path '{}' is not under an allowed root",
424:             resolved.display()
425:         )));
426:     }
427: 
428:     Ok(resolved.to_string_lossy().to_string())
429: }

431: #[tauri::command]
432: pub async fn check_access(
433:     app: AppHandle,
434:     manga_id: Option<String>,
435:     local_path: Option<String>,
436: ) -> Result<bool, FsError> {
437:     let target_path = if let Some(ref lp) = local_path {
438:         if !lp.trim().is_empty() {
439:             Some(lp.trim().to_string())
440:         } else {
441:             None
442:         }
443:     } else {
444:         None
445:     };
446: 
447:     let target_path = match target_path {
448:         Some(p) => p,
449:         None => {
450:             if let Some(ref id) = manga_id {
451:                 if let Some(db) = app.try_state::<crate::db::DbState>() {
452:                     match db.get_local_path(id).await {
453:                         Some(p) => p,
454:                         None => return Ok(false),
455:                     }
456:                 } else {
457:                     return Ok(false);
458:                 }
459:             } else {
460:                 return Ok(false);
461:             }
462:         }
463:     };
464: 
465:     let trimmed = target_path.trim();
466:     if trimmed.is_empty() {
467:         return Ok(false);
468:     }
469: 
470:     let pb = Path::new(trimmed);
471:     let canonical = match dunce::canonicalize(pb) {
472:         Ok(c) => c,
473:         Err(_) => return Ok(false),
474:     };
475: 
476:     if !canonical.is_dir() {
477:         return Ok(false);
478:     }
479: 
480:     let allowed = app.state::<AllowedRoots>();
481:     Ok(allowed.is_allowed(&canonical))
482: }

484: #[tauri::command]
485: pub async fn prepare_chapter_headers(
486:     app: AppHandle,
487:     paths: Vec<String>,
488:     manga_id: Option<String>,
489: ) -> Result<Vec<PageHeader>, FsError> {
490:     let allowed_set = {
491:         let allowed = app.state::<AllowedRoots>();
492:         allowed.get_active_roots()
493:     };
494: 
495:     let base_dir: Option<PathBuf> = if let Some(ref id) = manga_id {
496:         if let Some(db) = app.try_state::<crate::db::DbState>() {
497:             db.get_local_path(id).await.map(PathBuf::from)
498:         } else {
499:             None
500:         }
501:     } else {
502:         None
503:     };
504: 
505:     // Parallel extraction with Rayon off main thread
506:     let results: Vec<PageHeader> = tauri::async_runtime::spawn_blocking(move || {
507:         paths
508:             .into_par_iter()
509:             .filter_map(|p_str| {
510:                 let trimmed = p_str.trim();
511:                 let path = Path::new(trimmed);
512: 
513:                 let resolved = if path.is_relative() {
514:                     if let Some(ref base) = base_dir {
515:                         resolve_relative_path(base, trimmed).ok()?
516:                     } else {
517:                         dunce::canonicalize(path).ok()?
518:                     }
519:                 } else {
520:                     dunce::canonicalize(path).ok()?
521:                 };
522: 
523:                 if allowed_set.is_empty() || !allowed_set.iter().any(|r| resolved.starts_with(r)) {
524:                     return None;
525:                 }
526:                 let ext = resolved.extension()?.to_str()?.to_lowercase();
527:                 if !ALLOWED_IMAGE_EXTENSIONS.contains(&ext.as_str()) {
528:                     return None;
529:                 }
530:                 let metadata = std::fs::metadata(&resolved).ok()?;
531:                 if !metadata.is_file() || metadata.len() > MAX_FILE_READ_BYTES {
532:                     return None;
533:                 }
534:                 let (width, height) = match parse_image_dimensions(&resolved) {
535:                     (Some(w), Some(h)) => (w, h),
536:                     _ => return None,
537:                 };
538: 
539:                 let modified = metadata
540:                     .modified()
541:                     .ok()
542:                     .and_then(|t| t.duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH).ok())
543:                     .map(|d| d.as_millis() as f64)
544:                     .unwrap_or(0.0);
545: 
546:                 // Lightweight checksum: FNV-1a of size + modified + first 512 bytes
547:                 let mut hash: u64 = 0xcbf29ce484222325;
548:                 let size = metadata.len();
549:                 for b in size.to_le_bytes() {
550:                     hash = (hash ^ (b as u64)).wrapping_mul(0x100000001b3);
551:                 }
552:                 for b in (modified as u64).to_le_bytes() {
553:                     hash = (hash ^ (b as u64)).wrapping_mul(0x100000001b3);
554:                 }
555:                 if let Ok(mut f) = File::open(&resolved) {
556:                     let mut sample = [0u8; 512];
557:                     if let Ok(n) = f.read(&mut sample) {
558:                         for &b in &sample[..n] {
559:                             hash = (hash ^ (b as u64)).wrapping_mul(0x100000001b3);
560:                         }
561:                     }
562:                 }
563:                 let checksum = Some(format!("{:016x}", hash));
564: 
565:                 Some(PageHeader {
566:                     path: p_str,
567:                     width,
568:                     height,
569:                     size,
570:                     modified,
571:                     checksum,
572:                 })
573:             })
574:             .collect()
575:     })
576:     .await
577:     .map_err(|e| FsError::Io(e.to_string()))?;
578: 
579:     Ok(results)
580: }
581: 
582: #[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
583: #[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
584: pub struct ScanProgressPayload {
585:     pub scan_id: Option<String>,
586:     pub done: usize,
587:     pub total: usize,
588: }
589: 
590: #[derive(Clone)]
591: struct CandidateEntry {
592:     rel_path: String,
593:     abs_path: PathBuf,
594:     size: u64,
595:     modified: f64,
596: }
597: 
598: #[derive(Default)]
599: pub struct AllowedRoots {
600:     pub active: RwLock<HashSet<PathBuf>>,
601:     pub unavailable: RwLock<HashSet<PathBuf>>,
602: }
603: 
604: pub fn resolve_canonical_path(path: &Path) -> Option<PathBuf> {
605:     if let Ok(c) = dunce::canonicalize(path) {
606:         return Some(c);
607:     }
608:     // If the leaf does not exist on disk, canonicalize its closest existing ancestor
609:     let mut remaining = Vec::new();
610:     let mut current = path;
611:     while let Some(parent) = current.parent() {
612:         if let Some(file_name) = current.file_name() {
613:             remaining.push(file_name);
614:         }
615:         if let Ok(c) = dunce::canonicalize(parent) {
616:             let mut resolved = c;
617:             for comp in remaining.into_iter().rev() {
618:                 resolved.push(comp);
619:             }
620:             return Some(resolved);
621:         }
622:         current = parent;
623:     }
624:     None
625: }
626: 
627: impl AllowedRoots {
628:     pub fn new(active: HashSet<PathBuf>, unavailable: HashSet<PathBuf>) -> Self {
629:         Self {
630:             active: RwLock::new(active),
631:             unavailable: RwLock::new(unavailable),
632:         }
633:     }
634: 
635:     pub fn is_allowed(&self, path: &Path) -> bool {
636:         // Fail closed if path cannot be canonicalized to an existing ancestor
637:         let canonical = match resolve_canonical_path(path) {
638:             Some(c) => c,
639:             None => return false,
640:         };
641:         if canonical
642:             .components()
643:             .any(|c| matches!(c, std::path::Component::ParentDir))
644:         {
645:             return false;
646:         }
647: 
648:         // 1. Check currently active canonical roots
649:         {
650:             let active = self.active.read().unwrap();
651:             if !active.is_empty() && active.iter().any(|root| canonical.starts_with(root)) {
652:                 return true;
653:             }
654:         }
655: 
656:         // 2. Check if an unavailable root (e.g. re-attached drive) has become reachable
657:         let mut promoted = false;
658:         {
659:             let mut unavail = self.unavailable.write().unwrap();
660:             let mut to_promote = Vec::new();
661:             for unavail_root in unavail.iter() {
662:                 if let Ok(c_root) = dunce::canonicalize(unavail_root) {
663:                     if canonical.starts_with(&c_root) {
664:                         to_promote.push((unavail_root.clone(), c_root));
665:                     }
666:                 }
667:             }
668:             for (u, c) in to_promote {
669:                 unavail.remove(&u);
670:                 self.active.write().unwrap().insert(c);
671:                 promoted = true;
672:             }
673:         }
674:         promoted
675:     }
676: 
677:     pub fn add_root(&self, path: PathBuf) {
678:         if let Ok(canonical) = dunce::canonicalize(&path) {
679:             self.active.write().unwrap().insert(canonical);
680:         } else {
681:             // Fail closed for active authorization; preserve separately for relinking
682:             self.unavailable.write().unwrap().insert(path);
683:         }
684:     }
685: 
686:     pub fn revoke_root(&self, path: &Path) -> bool {
687:         let canonical = dunce::canonicalize(path).unwrap_or_else(|_| path.to_path_buf());
688:         let mut active = self.active.write().unwrap();
689:         let mut unavail = self.unavailable.write().unwrap();
690: 
691:         let removed_active = active.remove(&canonical);
692:         let removed_unavail = unavail.remove(path) || unavail.remove(&canonical);
693: 
694:         let to_remove_active: Vec<PathBuf> = active
695:             .iter()
696:             .filter(|r| **r == canonical || r.starts_with(&canonical))
697:             .cloned()
698:             .collect();
699:         for r in to_remove_active {
700:             active.remove(&r);
701:         }
702: 
703:         let to_remove_unavail: Vec<PathBuf> = unavail
704:             .iter()
705:             .filter(|r| **r == canonical || r.starts_with(&canonical))
706:             .cloned()
707:             .collect();
708:         for r in to_remove_unavail {
709:             unavail.remove(&r);
710:         }
711: 
712:         removed_active || removed_unavail
713:     }
714: 
715:     pub fn get_roots(&self) -> Vec<PathBuf> {
716:         let mut all: Vec<PathBuf> = self.active.read().unwrap().iter().cloned().collect();
717:         for unavail in self.unavailable.read().unwrap().iter() {
718:             if !all.contains(unavail) {
719:                 all.push(unavail.clone());
720:             }
721:         }
722:         all
723:     }
724: 
725:     pub fn get_active_roots(&self) -> HashSet<PathBuf> {
726:         self.active.read().unwrap().clone()
727:     }
728: }
729: 
730: pub fn is_image_file(path: &Path) -> bool {
731:     path.extension()
732:         .and_then(|s| s.to_str())
733:         .map(|s| s.to_lowercase())
734:         .map(|ext| ALLOWED_IMAGE_EXTENSIONS.contains(&ext.as_str()))
735:         .unwrap_or(false)
736: }
737: 
738: pub fn is_ignored_directory(path: &Path) -> bool {
739:     let name = path
740:         .file_name()
741:         .and_then(|n| n.to_str())
742:         .unwrap_or("")
743:         .to_lowercase();
744:     name.starts_with('.') || IGNORED_FOLDER_NAMES.contains(&name.as_str())
745: }
746: 
747: use std::collections::HashMap;
748: use std::sync::atomic::{AtomicU64, Ordering};
749: use std::sync::{Arc, LazyLock, Mutex};
750: 
751: static ATOMIC_WRITE_COUNTER: AtomicU64 = AtomicU64::new(1);
752: static DESTINATION_LOCKS: LazyLock<Mutex<HashMap<PathBuf, Arc<Mutex<()>>>>> =
753:     LazyLock::new(|| Mutex::new(HashMap::new()));
754: 
755: fn get_destination_lock(path: &Path) -> Arc<Mutex<()>> {
756:     let key = dunce::canonicalize(path).unwrap_or_else(|_| path.to_path_buf());
757:     let mut locks = DESTINATION_LOCKS.lock().unwrap();
758:     if locks.len() > 1024 {
759:         locks.retain(|_, arc| Arc::strong_count(arc) > 1);
760:     }
761:     locks
762:         .entry(key)
763:         .or_insert_with(|| Arc::new(Mutex::new(())))
764:         .clone()
765: }
766: 
767: /// Atomically writes data to path using a unique same-directory temporary file
768: /// serialized per destination, syncing to disk before renaming over the target.
769: pub fn write_atomic(path: &Path, data: &[u8]) -> std::io::Result<()> {
770:     let dest_lock = get_destination_lock(path);
771:     let _guard = dest_lock.lock().unwrap();
772: 
773:     let parent = path.parent().unwrap_or_else(|| Path::new("."));
774:     std::fs::create_dir_all(parent)?;
775: 
776:     let file_stem = path
777:         .file_name()
778:         .and_then(|n| n.to_str())
779:         .unwrap_or("atomic");
780:     let pid = std::process::id();
781:     let counter = ATOMIC_WRITE_COUNTER.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
782:     let tmp = parent.join(format!(".{file_stem}_{pid}_{counter}.tmp"));
783: 
784:     let res = (|| -> std::io::Result<()> {
785:         use std::io::Write;
786:         let mut file = std::fs::File::create(&tmp)?;
787:         file.write_all(data)?;
788:         file.sync_all()?;
789:         drop(file);
790: 
791:         #[cfg(target_os = "windows")]
792:         {
793:             if path.exists() {
794:                 let mut attempts = 0;
795:                 loop {
796:                     match std::fs::rename(&tmp, path) {
797:                         Ok(()) => return Ok(()),
798:                         Err(_e) if attempts < 3 => {
799:                             attempts += 1;
800:                             std::thread::sleep(std::time::Duration::from_millis(15));
801:                         }
802:                         Err(e) => return Err(e),
803:                     }
804:                 }
805:             } else {
806:                 std::fs::rename(&tmp, path)?;
807:             }
808:         }
809: 
810:         #[cfg(not(target_os = "windows"))]
811:         {
812:             std::fs::rename(&tmp, path)?;
813:         }
814: 
815:         Ok(())
816:     })();
817: 
818:     if res.is_err() {
819:         let _ = std::fs::remove_file(&tmp);
820:     }
821:     res
822: }
823: 
824: pub fn load_persisted_roots(app: &AppHandle) -> (HashSet<PathBuf>, HashSet<PathBuf>) {
825:     let mut active = HashSet::new();
826:     let mut unavailable = HashSet::new();
827:     if let Ok(app_dir) = app.path().app_data_dir() {
828:         let file_path = app_dir.join("allowed_roots.json");
829:         let candidates = [file_path.clone(), file_path.with_extension("tmp")];
830:         for candidate in &candidates {
831:             if candidate.exists() {
832:                 if let Ok(data) = std::fs::read_to_string(candidate) {
833:                     if let Ok(paths) = serde_json::from_str::<Vec<String>>(&data) {
834:                         for p in paths {
835:                             let pb = PathBuf::from(p);
836:                             if let Ok(canonical) = dunce::canonicalize(&pb) {
837:                                 active.insert(canonical);
838:                             } else {
839:                                 // Unavailable at startup (e.g. disconnected drive), preserve separately
840:                                 unavailable.insert(pb);
841:                             }
842:                         }
843:                         if !active.is_empty() || !unavailable.is_empty() {
844:                             if candidate != &file_path {
845:                                 let _ = std::fs::rename(candidate, &file_path);
846:                             }
847:                             break;
848:                         }
849:                     }
850:                 }
851:             }
852:         }
853:     }
854:     (active, unavailable)
855: }
856: 
857: pub fn save_persisted_roots(app: &AppHandle, allowed: &AllowedRoots) {
858:     if let Ok(app_dir) = app.path().app_data_dir() {
859:         let _ = std::fs::create_dir_all(&app_dir);
860:         let file_path = app_dir.join("allowed_roots.json");
861:         let list: Vec<String> = allowed
862:             .get_roots()
863:             .iter()
864:             .map(|p| p.to_string_lossy().to_string())
865:             .collect();
866:         if let Ok(data) = serde_json::to_string(&list) {
867:             let _ = write_atomic(&file_path, data.as_bytes());
868:         }
869:     }
870: }
871: 
872: pub fn init_allowed_roots(app: &AppHandle) -> AllowedRoots {
873:     let (active, unavailable) = load_persisted_roots(app);
874:     AllowedRoots::new(active, unavailable)
875: }
876: 
877: pub struct ScanHandle {
878:     pub receiver: tokio::sync::watch::Receiver<bool>,
879:     pub flag: std::sync::Arc<std::sync::atomic::AtomicBool>,
880: }
881: 
882: type ActiveScanEntry = (
883:     tokio::sync::watch::Sender<bool>,
884:     std::sync::Arc<std::sync::atomic::AtomicBool>,
885: );
886: type ActiveScanMap = std::collections::HashMap<String, ActiveScanEntry>;
887: 
888: #[derive(Default)]
889: pub struct ScanManager {
890:     active_scans: std::sync::Mutex<ActiveScanMap>,
891: }
892: 
893: impl ScanManager {
894:     pub fn register(&self, id: &str) -> ScanHandle {
895:         let (tx, rx) = tokio::sync::watch::channel(false);
896:         let flag = std::sync::Arc::new(std::sync::atomic::AtomicBool::new(false));
897:         self.active_scans
898:             .lock()
899:             .unwrap()
900:             .insert(id.to_string(), (tx, std::sync::Arc::clone(&flag)));
901:         ScanHandle { receiver: rx, flag }
902:     }
903: 
904:     pub fn unregister(&self, id: &str) {
905:         self.active_scans.lock().unwrap().remove(id);
906:     }
907: 
908:     pub fn cancel(&self, id: &str) -> bool {
909:         if let Some((tx, flag)) = self.active_scans.lock().unwrap().get(id) {
910:             flag.store(true, std::sync::atomic::Ordering::Relaxed);
911:             let _ = tx.send(true);
912:             true
913:         } else {
914:             false
915:         }
916:     }
917: 
918:     pub fn is_cancelled(&self, id: &str) -> bool {
919:         if let Some((_, flag)) = self.active_scans.lock().unwrap().get(id) {
920:             flag.load(std::sync::atomic::Ordering::Relaxed)
921:         } else {
922:             false
923:         }
924:     }
925: }
926: 
927: /// Parses image dimensions (width, height) without decoding pixel buffers.
928: /// First reads up to 32 KB to parse headers via blob_size; falls back to size(path)
929: /// for large EXIF/TIFF headers.
930: pub fn parse_image_dimensions(path: &Path) -> (Option<u32>, Option<u32>) {
931:     // 1. Fast header parse: read first 32 KB
932:     if let Ok(mut file) = File::open(path) {
933:         let mut buf = vec![0u8; HEADER_READ_LIMIT];
934:         let mut total = 0;
935:         while total < HEADER_READ_LIMIT {
936:             match file.read(&mut buf[total..]) {
937:                 Ok(0) => break,
938:                 Ok(n) => total += n,
939:                 Err(_) => break,
940:             }
941:         }
942:         buf.truncate(total);
943: 
944:         if !buf.is_empty() {
945:             if let Ok(dim) = imagesize::blob_size(&buf) {
946:                 if dim.width > 0 && dim.height > 0 {
947:                     let w = dim.width as u64;
948:                     let h = dim.height as u64;
949:                     if w <= u32::MAX as u64
950:                         && h <= u32::MAX as u64
951:                         && w.saturating_mul(h) < (1u64 << 40)
952:                     {
953:                         return (Some(dim.width as u32), Some(dim.height as u32));
954:                     }
955:                 }
956:             }
957:         }
958:     }
959: 
960:     // 2. Fallback: stream through reader if metadata chunk is located past 32 KB
961:     if let Ok(dim) = imagesize::size(path) {
962:         if dim.width > 0 && dim.height > 0 {
963:             let w = dim.width as u64;
964:             let h = dim.height as u64;
965:             if w <= u32::MAX as u64 && h <= u32::MAX as u64 && w.saturating_mul(h) < (1u64 << 40) {
966:                 return (Some(dim.width as u32), Some(dim.height as u32));
967:             }
968:         }
969:     }
970: 
971:     (None, None)
972: }
973: 
974: #[tauri::command]
975: pub async fn select_folder(app: AppHandle) -> Result<Option<String>, FsError> {
976:     let app_clone = app.clone();
977:     let folder = tokio::task::spawn_blocking(move || app_clone.dialog().file().blocking_pick_folder())
978:         .await
979:         .map_err(|e| FsError::Io(format!("Dialog task join error: {e}")))?;
980:     if let Some(ref path) = folder {
981:         let pb = PathBuf::from(path.to_string());
982:         let allowed = app.state::<AllowedRoots>();
983:         allowed.add_root(pb);
984:         save_persisted_roots(&app, &allowed);
985:     }
986:     Ok(folder.map(|p| p.to_string()))
987: }

989: #[tauri::command(async)]
990: pub fn cancel_scan(app: AppHandle, scan_id: String) -> Result<bool, FsError> {
991:     let scan_mgr = app.state::<ScanManager>();
992:     Ok(scan_mgr.cancel(&scan_id))
993: }

995: #[tauri::command(async)]
996: pub fn is_same_path(path_a: String, path_b: String) -> bool {
997:     let pa = Path::new(path_a.trim());
998:     let pb = Path::new(path_b.trim());
999:     if let (Ok(ca), Ok(cb)) = (dunce::canonicalize(pa), dunce::canonicalize(pb)) {
1000:         return ca == cb;
1001:     }
1002:     let norm_a = path_a
1003:         .trim()
1004:         .replace('\\', "/")
1005:         .trim_end_matches('/')
1006:         .to_lowercase();
1007:     let norm_b = path_b
1008:         .trim()
1009:         .replace('\\', "/")
1010:         .trim_end_matches('/')
1011:         .to_lowercase();
1012:     norm_a == norm_b
1013: }
1014: 
1015: #[tauri::command(async)]
1016: pub fn revoke_root(app: AppHandle, root_path: String) -> Result<bool, FsError> {
1017:     let trimmed = root_path.trim();
1018:     if trimmed.is_empty() {
1019:         return Ok(false);
1020:     }
1021:     let pb = PathBuf::from(trimmed);
1022:     let allowed = app.state::<AllowedRoots>();
1023:     let removed = allowed.revoke_root(&pb);
1024:     if removed {
1025:         save_persisted_roots(&app, &allowed);
1026:         // Also remove from last_opened_roots in AppConfig if present
1027:         if let Some(config_state) = app.try_state::<crate::commands::config::AppConfigState>() {
1028:             let _ = config_state.update(&app, |cfg| {
1029:                 cfg.last_opened_roots.retain(|r| {
1030:                     if let (Ok(cr), Ok(cp)) =
1031:                         (dunce::canonicalize(Path::new(r)), dunce::canonicalize(&pb))
1032:                     {
1033:                         cr != cp
1034:                     } else {
1035:                         r != trimmed
1036:                     }
1037:                 });
1038:             });
1039:         }
1040:     }
1041:     Ok(removed)
1042: }
1043: 
1044: #[tauri::command(async)]
1045: pub fn get_authorized_roots(app: AppHandle) -> Result<Vec<String>, FsError> {
1046:     let allowed = app.state::<AllowedRoots>();
1047:     let roots = allowed.get_roots();
1048:     Ok(roots
1049:         .into_iter()
1050:         .map(|p| p.to_string_lossy().to_string())
1051:         .collect())
1052: }
1053: 
1054: #[tauri::command]
1055: pub fn get_image_dimensions(
1056:     app: AppHandle,
1057:     absolute_path: String,
1058: ) -> Result<Option<(u32, u32)>, FsError> {
1059:     let path = Path::new(&absolute_path);
1060:     if let Ok(resolved) = dunce::canonicalize(path) {
1061:         let allowed = app.state::<AllowedRoots>();
1062:         if !allowed.is_allowed(&resolved) {
1063:             return Err(FsError::PathNotAllowed(format!(
1064:                 "Path '{}' is not under an allowed library root",
1065:                 resolved.display()
1066:             )));
1067:         }
1068:         let (w, h) = parse_image_dimensions(&resolved);
1069:         match (w, h) {
1070:             (Some(width), Some(height)) => Ok(Some((width, height))),
1071:             _ => Ok(None),
1072:         }
1073:     } else {
1074:         Ok(None)
1075:     }
1076: }
1077: 
1078: #[tauri::command]
1079: pub async fn list_folder(
1080:     app: AppHandle,
1081:     folder_path: String,
1082:     scan_id: Option<String>,
1083: ) -> Result<Vec<FolderEntry>, FsError> {
1084:     let raw_path = std::path::Path::new(folder_path.trim());
1085:     if !raw_path.exists() || !raw_path.is_dir() {
1086:         return Ok(Vec::new());
1087:     }
1088: 
1089:     let root = dunce::canonicalize(raw_path).unwrap_or_else(|_| raw_path.to_path_buf());
1090: 
1091:     // Verify root is under an allowed library root (added via select_folder or drag-and-drop)
1092:     let allowed = app.state::<AllowedRoots>();
1093:     if !allowed.is_allowed(&root) {
1094:         return Err(FsError::PathNotAllowed(format!(
1095:             "Path '{}' is not under an allowed library root",
1096:             root.display()
1097:         )));
1098:     }
1099: 
1100:     // Setup cancellation receiver if scan_id provided
1101:     let scan_mgr = app.state::<ScanManager>();
1102:     let handle = scan_id.as_deref().map(|id| scan_mgr.register(id));
1103:     let cancel_flag = handle.as_ref().map(|h| std::sync::Arc::clone(&h.flag));
1104: 
1105:     struct ScanGuard<'a> {
1106:         mgr: &'a ScanManager,
1107:         id: Option<&'a str>,
1108:     }
1109:     impl<'a> Drop for ScanGuard<'a> {
1110:         fn drop(&mut self) {
1111:             if let Some(id) = self.id {
1112:                 self.mgr.unregister(id);
1113:             }
1114:         }
1115:     }
1116:     let _guard = ScanGuard {
1117:         mgr: &scan_mgr,
1118:         id: scan_id.as_deref(),
1119:     };
1120: 
1121:     let check_cancelled = |handle: Option<&ScanHandle>| -> Result<(), FsError> {
1122:         if let Some(h) = handle {
1123:             if h.flag.load(std::sync::atomic::Ordering::Relaxed) || *h.receiver.borrow() {
1124:                 return Err(FsError::Cancelled);
1125:             }
1126:         }
1127:         Ok(())
1128:     };
1129: 
1130:     check_cancelled(handle.as_ref())?;
1131: 
1132:     // Collect candidates off the main thread
1133:     let root_clone = root.clone();
1134:     let candidates = tauri::async_runtime::spawn_blocking(move || {
1135:         let mut list = Vec::new();
1136:         let walker = walkdir::WalkDir::new(&root_clone)
1137:             .max_depth(MAX_DEPTH)
1138:             .follow_links(false)
1139:             .into_iter()
1140:             .filter_entry(|entry| {
1141:                 if entry.depth() == 0 {
1142:                     return true;
1143:                 }
1144:                 let name = entry.file_name().to_string_lossy().to_lowercase();
1145:                 if entry.file_type().is_dir() {
1146:                     if name.starts_with('.') || IGNORED_FOLDER_NAMES.contains(&name.as_str()) {
1147:                         return false;
1148:                     }
1149:                 }
1150:                 true
1151:             });
1152: 
1153:         for entry_res in walker {
1154:             if let Some(ref cf) = cancel_flag {
1155:                 if cf.load(std::sync::atomic::Ordering::Relaxed) {
1156:                     break;
1157:                 }
1158:             }
1159:             if list.len() >= MAX_SCAN_FILES {
1160:                 break;
1161:             }
1162:             let entry = match entry_res {
1163:                 Ok(e) => e,
1164:                 Err(_) => continue,
1165:             };
1166: 
1167:             if !entry.file_type().is_file() {
1168:                 continue;
1169:             }
1170: 
1171:             let file_path = entry.path();
1172:             let ext = match file_path.extension() {
1173:                 Some(e) => e.to_string_lossy().to_lowercase(),
1174:                 None => continue,
1175:             };
1176: 
1177:             if !ALLOWED_IMAGE_EXTENSIONS.contains(&ext.as_str()) {
1178:                 continue;
1179:             }
1180: 
1181:             let metadata = match entry.metadata() {
1182:                 Ok(m) => m,
1183:                 Err(_) => continue,
1184:             };
1185: 
1186:             let rel_path = match file_path.strip_prefix(&root_clone) {
1187:                 Ok(p) => p.to_string_lossy().replace('\\', "/"),
1188:                 Err(_) => continue,
1189:             };
1190: 
1191:             let modified = metadata
1192:                 .modified()
1193:                 .ok()
1194:                 .and_then(|t| t.duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH).ok())
1195:                 .map(|d| d.as_millis() as f64)
1196:                 .unwrap_or(0.0);
1197: 
1198:             list.push(CandidateEntry {
1199:                 rel_path,
1200:                 abs_path: file_path.to_path_buf(),
1201:                 size: metadata.len(),
1202:                 modified,
1203:             });
1204:         }
1205:         list
1206:     })
1207:     .await
1208:     .map_err(|e| FsError::Io(e.to_string()))?;
1209: 
1210:     check_cancelled(handle.as_ref())?;
1211: 
1212:     let total = candidates.len();
1213:     let _ = app.emit(
1214:         "scan-progress",
1215:         ScanProgressPayload {
1216:             scan_id: scan_id.clone(),
1217:             done: 0,
1218:             total,
1219:         },
1220:     );
1221: 
1222:     if total == 0 {
1223:         return Ok(Vec::new());
1224:     }
1225: 
1226:     // Process dimensions in chunks with Rayon parallelism and progress emission
1227:     let mut results = Vec::with_capacity(total);
1228:     let mut done = 0;
1229: 
1230:     for chunk in candidates.chunks(SCAN_BATCH_SIZE) {
1231:         check_cancelled(handle.as_ref())?;
1232: 
1233:         let chunk_vec: Vec<CandidateEntry> = chunk.to_vec();
1234:         let batch_results: Vec<FolderEntry> = tauri::async_runtime::spawn_blocking(move || {
1235:             chunk_vec
1236:                 .into_par_iter()
1237:                 .map(|entry| {
1238:                     let (width, height) = parse_image_dimensions(&entry.abs_path);
1239:                     FolderEntry {
1240:                         relative_path: entry.rel_path,
1241:                         absolute_path: entry.abs_path.to_string_lossy().to_string(),
1242:                         size: entry.size,
1243:                         modified: entry.modified,
1244:                         width,
1245:                         height,
1246:                     }
1247:                 })
1248:                 .collect()
1249:         })
1250:         .await
1251:         .map_err(|e| FsError::Io(e.to_string()))?;
1252: 
1253:         done += batch_results.len();
1254:         results.extend(batch_results);
1255: 
1256:         let _ = app.emit(
1257:             "scan-progress",
1258:             ScanProgressPayload {
1259:                 scan_id: scan_id.clone(),
1260:                 done,
1261:                 total,
1262:             },
1263:         );
1264: 
1265:         tokio::task::yield_now().await;
1266:     }
1267: 
1268:     check_cancelled(handle.as_ref())?;
1269: 
1270:     Ok(results)
1271: }
1272: 
1273: #[tauri::command]
1274: pub async fn discover_library(
1275:     app: AppHandle,
1276:     folder_path: String,
1277:     scan_id: Option<String>,
1278: ) -> Result<LibraryDiscovery, FsError> {
1279:     let raw_path = std::path::Path::new(folder_path.trim());
1280:     if !raw_path.exists() || !raw_path.is_dir() {
1281:         return Ok(LibraryDiscovery {
1282:             root_path: folder_path,
1283:             titles: Vec::new(),
1284:             total_files: 0,
1285:         });
1286:     }
1287: 
1288:     let root = dunce::canonicalize(raw_path).unwrap_or_else(|_| raw_path.to_path_buf());
1289: 
1290:     // Verify root is under an allowed library root (added via select_folder or drag-and-drop)
1291:     let allowed = app.state::<AllowedRoots>();
1292:     if !allowed.is_allowed(&root) {
1293:         return Err(FsError::PathNotAllowed(format!(
1294:             "Path '{}' is not under an allowed library root",
1295:             root.display()
1296:         )));
1297:     }
1298: 
1299:     let scan_mgr = app.state::<ScanManager>();
1300:     let handle = scan_id.as_deref().map(|id| scan_mgr.register(id));
1301:     let cancel_flag = handle.as_ref().map(|h| std::sync::Arc::clone(&h.flag));
1302: 
1303:     struct ScanGuard<'a> {
1304:         mgr: &'a ScanManager,
1305:         id: Option<&'a str>,
1306:     }
1307:     impl<'a> Drop for ScanGuard<'a> {
1308:         fn drop(&mut self) {
1309:             if let Some(id) = self.id {
1310:                 self.mgr.unregister(id);
1311:             }
1312:         }
1313:     }
1314:     let _guard = ScanGuard {
1315:         mgr: &scan_mgr,
1316:         id: scan_id.as_deref(),
1317:     };
1318: 
1319:     let check_cancelled = |handle: Option<&ScanHandle>| -> Result<(), FsError> {
1320:         if let Some(h) = handle {
1321:             if h.flag.load(std::sync::atomic::Ordering::Relaxed) || *h.receiver.borrow() {
1322:                 return Err(FsError::Cancelled);
1323:             }
1324:         }
1325:         Ok(())
1326:     };
1327: 
1328:     check_cancelled(handle.as_ref())?;
1329: 
1330:     // Collect candidates off the main thread
1331:     let root_clone = root.clone();
1332:     let candidates = tauri::async_runtime::spawn_blocking(move || {
1333:         let mut list = Vec::new();
1334:         let walker = walkdir::WalkDir::new(&root_clone)
1335:             .max_depth(MAX_DEPTH)
1336:             .follow_links(false)
1337:             .into_iter()
1338:             .filter_entry(|entry| {
1339:                 if entry.depth() == 0 {
1340:                     return true;
1341:                 }
1342:                 let name = entry.file_name().to_string_lossy().to_lowercase();
1343:                 if entry.file_type().is_dir() {
1344:                     if name.starts_with('.') || IGNORED_FOLDER_NAMES.contains(&name.as_str()) {
1345:                         return false;
1346:                     }
1347:                 }
1348:                 true
1349:             });
1350: 
1351:         for entry_res in walker {
1352:             if let Some(ref cf) = cancel_flag {
1353:                 if cf.load(std::sync::atomic::Ordering::Relaxed) {
1354:                     break;
1355:                 }
1356:             }
1357:             if list.len() >= MAX_SCAN_FILES {
1358:                 break;
1359:             }
1360:             let entry = match entry_res {
1361:                 Ok(e) => e,
1362:                 Err(_) => continue,
1363:             };
1364: 
1365:             if !entry.file_type().is_file() {
1366:                 continue;
1367:             }
1368: 
1369:             let file_path = entry.path();
1370:             let ext = match file_path.extension() {
1371:                 Some(e) => e.to_string_lossy().to_lowercase(),
1372:                 None => continue,
1373:             };
1374: 
1375:             if !ALLOWED_IMAGE_EXTENSIONS.contains(&ext.as_str()) {
1376:                 continue;
1377:             }
1378: 
1379:             let metadata = match entry.metadata() {
1380:                 Ok(m) => m,
1381:                 Err(_) => continue,
1382:             };
1383: 
1384:             let rel_path = match file_path.strip_prefix(&root_clone) {
1385:                 Ok(p) => p.to_string_lossy().replace('\\', "/"),
1386:                 Err(_) => continue,
1387:             };
1388: 
1389:             let modified = metadata
1390:                 .modified()
1391:                 .ok()
1392:                 .and_then(|t| t.duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH).ok())
1393:                 .map(|d| d.as_millis() as f64)
1394:                 .unwrap_or(0.0);
1395: 
1396:             list.push(CandidateEntry {
1397:                 rel_path,
1398:                 abs_path: file_path.to_path_buf(),
1399:                 size: metadata.len(),
1400:                 modified,
1401:             });
1402:         }
1403:         list
1404:     })
1405:     .await
1406:     .map_err(|e| FsError::Io(e.to_string()))?;
1407: 
1408:     check_cancelled(handle.as_ref())?;
1409: 
1410:     let total_files = candidates.len();
1411:     let _ = app.emit(
1412:         "scan-progress",
1413:         ScanProgressPayload {
1414:             scan_id: scan_id.clone(),
1415:             done: 0,
1416:             total: total_files,
1417:         },
1418:     );
1419: 
1420:     if total_files == 0 {
1421:         return Ok(LibraryDiscovery {
1422:             root_path: root.to_string_lossy().to_string(),
1423:             titles: Vec::new(),
1424:             total_files: 0,
1425:         });
1426:     }
1427: 
1428:     let has_nested_titles = false;
1429:     let root_folder_name = root
1430:         .file_name()
1431:         .map(|n| n.to_string_lossy().to_string())
1432:         .unwrap_or_else(|| "Library".to_string());
1433: 
1434:     let mut measured_candidates: Vec<(CandidateEntry, Option<u32>, Option<u32>)> =
1435:         Vec::with_capacity(total_files);
1436:     let mut done = 0;
1437: 
1438:     for chunk in candidates.chunks(SCAN_BATCH_SIZE) {
1439:         check_cancelled(handle.as_ref())?;
1440: 
1441:         let chunk_vec: Vec<CandidateEntry> = chunk.to_vec();
1442:         let batch: Vec<(CandidateEntry, Option<u32>, Option<u32>)> =
1443:             tauri::async_runtime::spawn_blocking(move || {
1444:                 chunk_vec
1445:                     .into_par_iter()
1446:                     .map(|entry| {
1447:                         let (w, h) = parse_image_dimensions(&entry.abs_path);
1448:                         (entry, w, h)
1449:                     })
1450:                     .collect()
1451:             })
1452:             .await
1453:             .map_err(|e| FsError::Io(e.to_string()))?;
1454: 
1455:         done += batch.len();
1456:         measured_candidates.extend(batch);
1457: 
1458:         let _ = app.emit(
1459:             "scan-progress",
1460:             ScanProgressPayload {
1461:                 scan_id: scan_id.clone(),
1462:                 done,
1463:                 total: total_files,
1464:             },
1465:         );
1466: 
1467:         tokio::task::yield_now().await;
1468:     }
1469: 
1470:     check_cancelled(handle.as_ref())?;
1471: 
1472:     use std::collections::BTreeMap;
1473:     let mut title_map: BTreeMap<String, BTreeMap<String, Vec<DiscoveredPage>>> = BTreeMap::new();
1474:     let mut title_covers: BTreeMap<String, Option<String>> = BTreeMap::new();
1475: 
1476:     for (entry, width, height) in measured_candidates {
1477:         let parts: Vec<&str> = entry.rel_path.split('/').collect();
1478:         let (title_name, rel_to_title) = if has_nested_titles {
1479:             if parts.len() > 1 {
1480:                 (parts[0].to_string(), parts[1..].join("/"))
1481:             } else {
1482:                 (
1483:                     format!("{} — root pages", root_folder_name),
1484:                     parts[0].to_string(),
1485:                 )
1486:             }
1487:         } else {
1488:             (root_folder_name.clone(), entry.rel_path.clone())
1489:         };
1490: 
1491:         let title_parts: Vec<&str> = rel_to_title.split('/').collect();
1492:         let page_file_name = title_parts
1493:             .last()
1494:             .copied()
1495:             .unwrap_or(&rel_to_title)
1496:             .to_string();
1497: 
1498:         let is_root_file = title_parts.len() == 1;
1499:         let is_cover_name = is_root_file
1500:             && (page_file_name.to_lowercase().starts_with("cover.")
1501:                 || page_file_name.to_lowercase().starts_with("thumbnail."));
1502: 
1503:         if is_cover_name {
1504:             title_covers.insert(
1505:                 title_name.clone(),
1506:                 Some(entry.abs_path.to_string_lossy().to_string()),
1507:             );
1508:         }
1509: 
1510:         let chapter_name = if is_root_file {
1511:             "Root pages".to_string()
1512:         } else {
1513:             title_parts[..title_parts.len() - 1].join(" / ")
1514:         };
1515: 
1516:         let page = DiscoveredPage {
1517:             name: page_file_name,
1518:             relative_path: rel_to_title,
1519:             absolute_path: entry.abs_path.to_string_lossy().to_string(),
1520:             width,
1521:             height,
1522:             size: entry.size,
1523:             modified: entry.modified,
1524:         };
1525: 
1526:         title_map
1527:             .entry(title_name.clone())
1528:             .or_default()
1529:             .entry(chapter_name)
1530:             .or_default()
1531:             .push(page);
1532:     }
1533: 
1534:     let mut titles = Vec::new();
1535:     for (title_name, chapters_map) in title_map {
1536:         let mut chapters = Vec::new();
1537:         let mut title_images = 0;
1538: 
1539:         for (chapter_name, mut pages) in chapters_map {
1540:             pages.sort_by(|a, b| natural_order(&a.name, &b.name));
1541:             title_images += pages.len();
1542:             chapters.push(DiscoveredChapter {
1543:                 name: chapter_name,
1544:                 pages,
1545:             });
1546:         }
1547: 
1548:         chapters.sort_by(|a, b| natural_order(&a.name, &b.name));
1549: 
1550:         let cover_path = title_covers
1551:             .get(&title_name)
1552:             .cloned()
1553:             .flatten()
1554:             .or_else(|| {
1555:                 chapters
1556:                     .first()
1557:                     .and_then(|c| c.pages.first())
1558:                     .map(|p| p.absolute_path.clone())
1559:             });
1560: 
1561:         let root_pages_label = format!("{} — root pages", root_folder_name);
1562:         let title_root = if has_nested_titles && title_name != root_pages_label {
1563:             root.join(&title_name).to_string_lossy().to_string()
1564:         } else {
1565:             root.to_string_lossy().to_string()
1566:         };
1567: 
1568:         titles.push(DiscoveredTitle {
1569:             title: title_name,
1570:             root_path: title_root,
1571:             chapters,
1572:             total_images: title_images,
1573:             cover_path,
1574:         });
1575:     }
1576: 
1577:     titles.sort_by(|a, b| natural_order(&a.title, &b.title));
1578: 
1579:     let all_pages: Vec<DiscoveredPage> = titles
1580:         .iter()
1581:         .flat_map(|t| t.chapters.iter().flat_map(|c| c.pages.clone()))
1582:         .collect();
1583: 
1584:     if let Some(state) = app.try_state::<DiscoveryState>() {
1585:         state.store(root.clone(), all_pages);
1586:     }
1587: 
1588:     Ok(LibraryDiscovery {
1589:         root_path: root.to_string_lossy().to_string(),
1590:         titles,
1591:         total_files,
1592:     })
1593: }
1594: 
1595: #[tauri::command]
1596: pub async fn read_file(
1597:     app: AppHandle,
1598:     absolute_path: String,
1599: ) -> Result<tauri::ipc::Response, FsError> {
1600:     let trimmed = absolute_path.trim();
1601:     if trimmed.is_empty() {
1602:         return Err(FsError::NotFound("Path cannot be empty".into()));
1603:     }
1604: 
1605:     let path = std::path::Path::new(trimmed);
1606:     let resolved = dunce::canonicalize(path)
1607:         .map_err(|e| FsError::NotFound(format!("Failed to resolve path: {e}")))?;
1608: 
1609:     let allowed = app.state::<AllowedRoots>();
1610:     if !allowed.is_allowed(&resolved) {
1611:         return Err(FsError::PathNotAllowed(format!(
1612:             "Path '{}' is not under an allowed library root",
1613:             resolved.display()
1614:         )));
1615:     }
1616: 
1617:     let ext = resolved
1618:         .extension()
1619:         .and_then(|s| s.to_str())
1620:         .map(|s| s.to_lowercase())
1621:         .ok_or_else(|| FsError::ForbiddenExt("Missing file extension".to_string()))?;
1622: 
1623:     if !ALLOWED_IMAGE_EXTENSIONS.contains(&ext.as_str()) {
1624:         return Err(FsError::ForbiddenExt(format!(
1625:             "File extension '.{ext}' is not an allowed image format"
1626:         )));
1627:     }
1628: 
1629:     // Open file directly - holding the descriptor prevents TOCTOU junction / link substitution
1630:     let mut file = tokio::fs::File::open(&resolved)
1631:         .await
1632:         .map_err(|e| FsError::Io(format!("Failed to open file: {e}")))?;
1633: 
1634:     let metadata = file
1635:         .metadata()
1636:         .await
1637:         .map_err(|e| FsError::Io(format!("Failed to read metadata: {e}")))?;
1638: 
1639:     if !metadata.is_file() {
1640:         return Err(FsError::NotFound(
1641:             "Target path is not a regular file".into(),
1642:         ));
1643:     }
1644: 
1645:     if metadata.len() > MAX_FILE_READ_BYTES {
1646:         return Err(FsError::TooLarge(format!(
1647:             "File size ({} bytes) exceeds safety cap of {} bytes",
1648:             metadata.len(),
1649:             MAX_FILE_READ_BYTES
1650:         )));
1651:     }
1652: 
1653:     let mut bytes = Vec::with_capacity(metadata.len() as usize);
1654:     tokio::io::AsyncReadExt::read_to_end(&mut file, &mut bytes)
1655:         .await
1656:         .map_err(|e| FsError::Io(format!("Failed to read file bytes: {e}")))?;
1657: 
1658:     Ok(tauri::ipc::Response::new(bytes))
1659: }
1660: 
1661: #[cfg(test)]
1662: mod tests {
1663:     use super::*;
1664:     use std::cmp::Ordering;
1665: 
1666:     #[test]
1667:     fn test_natural_order_chapters() {
1668:         assert_eq!(natural_order("Chapter 1", "Chapter 2"), Ordering::Less);
1669:         assert_eq!(natural_order("Chapter 2", "Chapter 10"), Ordering::Less);
1670:         assert_eq!(natural_order("Chapter 10", "Chapter 2"), Ordering::Greater);
1671:         assert_eq!(natural_order("Chapter 1.5", "Chapter 1"), Ordering::Greater);
1672:         assert_eq!(natural_order("Chapter 1.5", "Chapter 2"), Ordering::Less);
1673:         assert_eq!(natural_order("001.png", "2.png"), Ordering::Less);
1674:         assert_eq!(natural_order("10.jpg", "2.jpg"), Ordering::Greater);
1675:         assert_eq!(natural_order("same", "same"), Ordering::Equal);
1676: 
1677:         // Long digit string precision (> 18 digits)
1678:         assert_eq!(
1679:             natural_order(
1680:                 "item_100000000000000000000001",
1681:                 "item_100000000000000000000002"
1682:             ),
1683:             Ordering::Less
1684:         );
1685:         assert_eq!(
1686:             natural_order(
1687:                 "item_100000000000000000000002",
1688:                 "item_100000000000000000000001"
1689:             ),
1690:             Ordering::Greater
1691:         );
1692:     }
1693: 
1694:     #[test]
1695:     fn test_natural_order_categories() {
1696:         assert_eq!(natural_order("Prologue", "Chapter 1"), Ordering::Less);
1697:         assert_eq!(natural_order("Opening", "Chapter 1"), Ordering::Less);
1698:         assert_eq!(natural_order("Chapter 50", "Epilogue"), Ordering::Less);
1699:         assert_eq!(natural_order("Chapter 50", "Extra 1"), Ordering::Less);
1700:         assert_eq!(natural_order("Chapter 50", "Bonus 1"), Ordering::Less);
1701:         assert_eq!(natural_order("Chapter 50", "Special"), Ordering::Less);
1702:     }
1703: 
1704:     #[test]
1705:     fn test_is_image_file() {
1706:         for ext in ALLOWED_IMAGE_EXTENSIONS {
1707:             let filename = format!("cover.{}", ext);
1708:             assert!(
1709:                 is_image_file(Path::new(&filename)),
1710:                 "Extension .{} should be allowed",
1711:                 ext
1712:             );
1713:             let uppercase = format!("cover.{}", ext.to_uppercase());
1714:             assert!(
1715:                 is_image_file(Path::new(&uppercase)),
1716:                 "Uppercase .{} should be allowed",
1717:                 ext
1718:             );
1719:         }
1720: 
1721:         assert!(!is_image_file(Path::new("document.pdf")));
1722:         assert!(!is_image_file(Path::new("script.js")));
1723:         assert!(!is_image_file(Path::new("archive.zip")));
1724:         assert!(!is_image_file(Path::new(".gitignore")));
1725:         assert!(!is_image_file(Path::new("noextension")));
1726:     }
1727: 
1728:     #[test]
1729:     fn test_is_ignored_directory() {
1730:         assert!(is_ignored_directory(Path::new(".git")));
1731:         assert!(is_ignored_directory(Path::new(".svn")));
1732:         assert!(is_ignored_directory(Path::new(".cache")));
1733:         assert!(is_ignored_directory(Path::new("node_modules")));

127: pub fn get_format_capabilities(ext: &str) -> ImageFormatCapabilities {
128:     let clean = ext.trim_start_matches('.').to_ascii_lowercase();
129:     FORMAT_MATRIX
130:         .iter()
131:         .find(|f| f.extension == clean)
132:         .copied()
133:         .unwrap_or(ImageFormatCapabilities {
134:             extension: "unknown",
135:             recognized_extension: false,
136:             header_readable: false,
137:             webview_displayable: false,
138:             rust_decodable: false,
139:         })
140: }
141: 
142: pub fn calculate_fitted_dimensions(src_w: u32, src_h: u32, max_w: u32, max_h: u32) -> (u32, u32) {
143:     if src_w == 0 || src_h == 0 || max_w == 0 || max_h == 0 {
144:         return (1, 1);
145:     }
146:     let scale = (max_w as f64 / src_w as f64)
147:         .min(max_h as f64 / src_h as f64)
148:         .min(1.0);
149:     let target_w = (src_w as f64 * scale).round().max(1.0) as u32;
150:     let target_h = (src_h as f64 * scale).round().max(1.0) as u32;
151:     (target_w, target_h)
152: }
