154: pub static IMAGE_RESIZE_POOL: LazyLock<rayon::ThreadPool> = LazyLock::new(|| {
155:     let threads = std::thread::available_parallelism()
156:         .map(|n| n.get())
157:         .unwrap_or(4)
158:         .clamp(2, 6);
159:     rayon::ThreadPoolBuilder::new()
160:         .num_threads(threads)
161:         .thread_name(|idx| format!("image-resize-{}", idx))
162:         .build()
163:         .expect("Failed to initialize bounded image thread pool")
164: });
165: 
166: pub fn resize_image_fir(
167:     src_img: DynamicImage,
168:     target_width: u32,
169:     target_height: u32,
170: ) -> Result<Vec<u8>, String> {
171:     if target_width == 0 || target_height == 0 {
172:         return Err("Target dimensions must be greater than zero".into());
173:     }
174:     if target_width > MAX_IMAGE_SIDE || target_height > MAX_IMAGE_SIDE {
175:         return Err("Target dimensions exceed maximum allowed image side".into());
176:     }
177:     let target_area = (target_width as u64) * (target_height as u64);
178:     if target_area > MAX_IMAGE_PIXELS {
179:         return Err("Target pixel area exceeds limit".into());
180:     }
181:     if target_area * 4 > MAX_IMAGE_JOB_MEMORY_BYTES {
182:         return Err("Target buffer exceeds job memory budget".into());
183:     }
184: 
185:     let rgba = src_img.to_rgba8();
186:     let (src_w, src_h) = (rgba.width(), rgba.height());
187: 
188:     let src = Image::from_vec_u8(src_w, src_h, rgba.into_raw(), PixelType::U8x4)
189:         .map_err(|e| format!("Failed to create source image buffer: {e}"))?;
190: 
191:     let mut dst = Image::new(target_width, target_height, PixelType::U8x4);
192:     let mut resizer = fr::Resizer::new();
193:     resizer
194:         .resize(&src, &mut dst, None)
195:         .map_err(|e| format!("Image resize failed: {e}"))?;
196: 
197:     let dst_rgba = RgbaImage::from_raw(target_width, target_height, dst.into_vec())
198:         .ok_or_else(|| "Failed to construct destination RgbaImage".to_string())?;
199: 
200:     let out = webp::Encoder::from_rgba(dst_rgba.as_raw(), target_width, target_height)
201:         .encode(80.0)
202:         .to_vec();
203: 
204:     Ok(out)
205: }
206: 
207: /// Creates a neutral placeholder image in WebP format when requested.
208: /// Strictly validates width, height, pixel area, and memory budget before allocation.
209: pub fn create_placeholder_webp(width: u32, height: u32) -> Result<Vec<u8>, String> {
210:     if width == 0 || height == 0 {
211:         return Err("Placeholder dimensions must be greater than zero".into());
212:     }
213:     if width > MAX_IMAGE_SIDE || height > MAX_IMAGE_SIDE {
214:         return Err(format!(
215:             "Placeholder dimensions ({width}x{height}) exceed maximum allowed side of {MAX_IMAGE_SIDE}"
216:         ));
217:     }
218:     let pixel_area = (width as u64) * (height as u64);
219:     if pixel_area > MAX_IMAGE_PIXELS {
220:         return Err(format!(
221:             "Placeholder pixel area ({pixel_area}) exceeds limit of {MAX_IMAGE_PIXELS}"
222:         ));
223:     }
224:     let estimated_bytes = pixel_area
225:         .checked_mul(4)
226:         .ok_or_else(|| "Estimated bytes overflow".to_string())?;
227:     if estimated_bytes > MAX_IMAGE_JOB_MEMORY_BYTES {
228:         return Err(format!(
229:             "Placeholder estimated memory ({estimated_bytes} bytes) exceeds job budget of {MAX_IMAGE_JOB_MEMORY_BYTES} bytes"
230:         ));
231:     }
232: 
233:     let raw = [39u8, 39, 42, 255].repeat((width as usize) * (height as usize));
234:     let out = webp::Encoder::from_rgba(&raw, width, height)
235:         .encode(80.0)
236:         .to_vec();
237:     Ok(out)
238: }
239: 
240: /// Safely decodes an image with total job memory budget enforcement and catch_unwind
241: /// protection against decoder panics.
242: pub fn safe_decode_image(path: &Path) -> Result<DynamicImage, String> {
243:     let ext = path.extension().and_then(|s| s.to_str()).unwrap_or("");
244:     let caps = get_format_capabilities(ext);
245:     if !caps.rust_decodable {
246:         return Err(format!(
247:             "Format '.{}' is recognized by library allowlist but not decodable in native Rust pipeline (WebView displayable: {}, header readable: {})",
248:             ext, caps.webview_displayable, caps.header_readable
249:         ));
250:     }
251: 
252:     // Check header dimensions before reading file body to enforce job budget
253:     if let (Some(w), Some(h)) = crate::commands::fs::parse_image_dimensions(path) {
254:         if w == 0 || h == 0 || w > MAX_IMAGE_SIDE || h > MAX_IMAGE_SIDE {
255:             return Err(format!("Image dimensions ({w}x{h}) exceed limits"));
256:         }
257:         let pixel_area = (w as u64) * (h as u64);
258:         if pixel_area > MAX_IMAGE_PIXELS {
259:             return Err(format!(
260:                 "Image pixel area ({pixel_area}) exceeds limit of {MAX_IMAGE_PIXELS}"
261:             ));
262:         }
263:         let raw_bytes = pixel_area * 4;
264:         if raw_bytes > MAX_IMAGE_JOB_MEMORY_BYTES {
265:             return Err(format!(
266:                 "Estimated uncompressed RGBA image memory ({raw_bytes} bytes) exceeds total job budget of {MAX_IMAGE_JOB_MEMORY_BYTES} bytes"
267:             ));
268:         }
269:     }
270: 
271:     let mut limits = image::Limits::default();
272:     limits.max_alloc = Some(MAX_IMAGE_JOB_MEMORY_BYTES);
273:     limits.max_image_width = Some(MAX_IMAGE_SIDE);
274:     limits.max_image_height = Some(MAX_IMAGE_SIDE);
275: 
276:     let mut reader = image::ImageReader::open(path)
277:         .map_err(|e| format!("Failed to open image file: {e}"))?
278:         .with_guessed_format()
279:         .map_err(|e| format!("Failed to determine image format: {e}"))?;
280: 
281:     reader.limits(limits);
282: 
283:     let decode_result = std::panic::catch_unwind(std::panic::AssertUnwindSafe(|| reader.decode()));
284: 
285:     match decode_result {
286:         Ok(Ok(img)) => {
287:             let (w, h) = (img.width(), img.height());
288:             let pixel_area = (w as u64) * (h as u64);
289:             if w > MAX_IMAGE_SIDE || h > MAX_IMAGE_SIDE || pixel_area > MAX_IMAGE_PIXELS {
290:                 return Err("Decoded image exceeds size or pixel limits".into());
291:             }
292:             if pixel_area * 4 > MAX_IMAGE_JOB_MEMORY_BYTES {
293:                 return Err("Decoded image RGBA size exceeds job memory budget".into());
294:             }
295:             Ok(img)
296:         }
297:         Ok(Err(e)) => Err(format!("Failed to decode image: {e}")),
298:         Err(_) => Err("Image decoder panicked during decoding".into()),
299:     }
300: }
301: 
302: #[derive(Debug, Clone, serde::Serialize, serde::Deserialize)]
303: #[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
304: pub struct CachedImageResult {
305:     pub path: String,
306:     pub url: String,
307:     #[serde(default)]
308:     pub is_placeholder: bool,
309:     #[serde(default)]
310:     pub status: String,
311:     #[serde(default)]
312:     pub error: Option<String>,
313: }
314: 
315: #[tauri::command]
316: pub async fn generate_thumbnail(
317:     app: AppHandle,
318:     file_path: String,
319:     id: String,
320: ) -> Result<CachedImageResult, String> {
321:     let trimmed = file_path.trim().to_string();
322:     if trimmed.is_empty() {
323:         return Err("File path cannot be empty".into());
324:     }
325: 
326:     let thumb_path = crate::commands::cache::get_thumbnail_path(&app, &id)?;
327:     let thumb_path_clone = thumb_path.clone();
328:     let app_clone = app.clone();
329: 
330:     let status = tauri::async_runtime::spawn_blocking(move || -> Result<&'static str, String> {
331:         let resolved = dunce::canonicalize(&trimmed)
332:             .map_err(|e| format!("Failed to resolve image path '{trimmed}': {e}"))?;
333: 
334:         let allowed = app_clone.state::<crate::commands::fs::AllowedRoots>();
335:         if !allowed.is_allowed(&resolved) {
336:             return Err(format!(
337:                 "Path '{}' is not under an allowed library root",
338:                 resolved.display()
339:             ));
340:         }
341: 
342:         // Check if cached thumbnail exists and is still fresh compared to source file
343:         let should_regenerate = if thumb_path_clone.exists() {
344:             match (
345:                 std::fs::metadata(&resolved),
346:                 std::fs::metadata(&thumb_path_clone),
347:             ) {
348:                 (Ok(src_meta), Ok(thumb_meta)) => {
349:                     match (src_meta.modified(), thumb_meta.modified()) {
350:                         (Ok(src_mtime), Ok(thumb_mtime)) => src_mtime > thumb_mtime,
351:                         _ => false,
352:                     }
353:                 }
354:                 _ => true,
355:             }
356:         } else {
357:             true
358:         };
359: 
360:         if !should_regenerate {
361:             return Ok("cached");
362:         }
363: 
364:         IMAGE_RESIZE_POOL.install(|| -> Result<(), String> {
365:             let decoded = safe_decode_image(&resolved)?;
366:             let (src_w, src_h) = (decoded.width(), decoded.height());
367:             let (target_w, target_h) = calculate_fitted_dimensions(
368:                 src_w,
369:                 src_h,
370:                 THUMBNAIL_MAX_WIDTH,
371:                 THUMBNAIL_MAX_HEIGHT,
372:             );
373:             let bytes = resize_image_fir(decoded, target_w, target_h)?;
374: 
375:             if let Some(parent) = thumb_path_clone.parent() {
376:                 let _ = std::fs::create_dir_all(parent);
377:             }
378:             crate::commands::fs::write_atomic(&thumb_path_clone, &bytes)
379:                 .map_err(|e| format!("Failed to write thumbnail: {e}"))?;
380: 
381:             if !thumb_path_clone.exists() {
382:                 return Err("Failed to verify thumbnail file on disk".into());
383:             }
384: 
385:             Ok(())
386:         })?;
387: 
388:         Ok("generated")
389:     })
390:     .await
391:     .map_err(|e| format!("Task join error: {e}"))??;
392: 
393:     Ok(CachedImageResult {
394:         path: thumb_path.to_string_lossy().to_string(),
395:         url: crate::protocol::path_to_mangaimg_url(&thumb_path),
396:         is_placeholder: false,
397:         status: status.into(),
398:         error: None,
399:     })
400: }
401: 
402: pub fn generate_thumbnail_direct(
403:     app: &AppHandle,
404:     resolved: &Path,
405:     id: &str,
406: ) -> Result<PathBuf, String> {
407:     let thumb_path = crate::commands::cache::get_thumbnail_path(app, id)?;
408: 
409:     let should_regenerate = if thumb_path.exists() {
410:         match (std::fs::metadata(resolved), std::fs::metadata(&thumb_path)) {
411:             (Ok(src_meta), Ok(thumb_meta)) => match (src_meta.modified(), thumb_meta.modified()) {
412:                 (Ok(src_mtime), Ok(thumb_mtime)) => src_mtime > thumb_mtime,
413:                 _ => false,
414:             },
415:             _ => true,
416:         }
417:     } else {
418:         true
419:     };
420: 
421:     if !should_regenerate {
422:         return Ok(thumb_path);
423:     }
424: 
425:     IMAGE_RESIZE_POOL.install(|| -> Result<(), String> {
426:         let decoded = safe_decode_image(resolved)?;
427:         let (src_w, src_h) = (decoded.width(), decoded.height());
428:         let (target_w, target_h) =
429:             calculate_fitted_dimensions(src_w, src_h, THUMBNAIL_MAX_WIDTH, THUMBNAIL_MAX_HEIGHT);
430:         let bytes = resize_image_fir(decoded, target_w, target_h)?;
431: 
432:         if let Some(parent) = thumb_path.parent() {
433:             let _ = std::fs::create_dir_all(parent);
434:         }
435:         crate::commands::fs::write_atomic(&thumb_path, &bytes)
436:             .map_err(|e| format!("Failed to write direct thumbnail: {e}"))?;
437: 
438:         if !thumb_path.exists() {
439:             return Err("Failed to verify direct thumbnail on disk".into());
440:         }
441: 
442:         Ok(())
443:     })?;
444: 
445:     Ok(thumb_path)
446: }
447: 
448: pub fn generate_image_variant_direct(
449:     app: &AppHandle,
450:     resolved: &Path,
451:     max_width: u32,
452:     max_height: u32,
453: ) -> Result<PathBuf, String> {
454:     // 1. Validate output bounds upfront before cache lookup or decode
455:     if max_width == 0 || max_height == 0 {
456:         return Err("Target dimensions must be greater than zero".into());
457:     }
458:     if max_width > MAX_IMAGE_SIDE || max_height > MAX_IMAGE_SIDE {
459:         return Err(format!(
460:             "Target dimensions ({max_width}x{max_height}) exceed maximum allowed side of {MAX_IMAGE_SIDE}"
461:         ));
462:     }
463:     let target_area = (max_width as u64) * (max_height as u64);
464:     if target_area > MAX_IMAGE_PIXELS {
465:         return Err(format!(
466:             "Target pixel area ({target_area}) exceeds limit of {MAX_IMAGE_PIXELS}"
467:         ));
468:     }
469:     if target_area * 4 > MAX_IMAGE_JOB_MEMORY_BYTES {
470:         return Err("Target dimensions exceed job memory budget".into());
471:     }
472: 
473:     // 2. Include source identity, size, modification timestamp, transform parameters, and encoder version in cache key
474:     let metadata = std::fs::metadata(resolved)
475:         .map_err(|e| format!("Failed to read metadata for '{}': {e}", resolved.display()))?;
476:     let file_size = metadata.len();
477:     let mtime_millis = metadata
478:         .modified()
479:         .ok()
480:         .and_then(|t| t.duration_since(std::time::UNIX_EPOCH).ok())
481:         .map(|d| d.as_millis())
482:         .unwrap_or(0);
483: 
484:     let variant_cache_key = format!(
485:         "{}:{}:{}:fit_{}x{}:{}",
486:         resolved.to_string_lossy(),
487:         file_size,
488:         mtime_millis,
489:         max_width,
490:         max_height,
491:         IMAGE_ENCODER_VERSION
492:     );
493: 
494:     let variant_cache_path =
495:         crate::commands::cache::get_variant_path(app, &variant_cache_key, max_width, max_height)?;
496: 
497:     if variant_cache_path.exists() {
498:         return Ok(variant_cache_path);
499:     }
500: 
501:     let variant_cache_path_clone = variant_cache_path.clone();
502:     let resolved_clone = resolved.to_path_buf();
503:     IMAGE_RESIZE_POOL.install(|| -> Result<(), String> {
504:         let decoded = safe_decode_image(&resolved_clone)?;
505:         let (src_w, src_h) = (decoded.width(), decoded.height());
506:         let (target_w, target_h) = calculate_fitted_dimensions(src_w, src_h, max_width, max_height);
507:         let bytes = resize_image_fir(decoded, target_w, target_h)?;
508: 
509:         if let Some(parent) = variant_cache_path_clone.parent() {
510:             let _ = std::fs::create_dir_all(parent);
511:         }
512:         crate::commands::fs::write_atomic(&variant_cache_path_clone, &bytes)
513:             .map_err(|e| format!("Failed to write image variant: {e}"))?;
514: 
515:         if !variant_cache_path_clone.exists() {
516:             return Err("Failed to verify image variant file on disk".into());
517:         }
518: 
519:         Ok(())
520:     })?;
521: 
522:     Ok(variant_cache_path)
523: }
524: 
525: #[tauri::command]
526: pub async fn generate_image_variant(
527:     app: AppHandle,
528:     file_path: String,
529:     max_width: u32,
530:     max_height: u32,
531: ) -> Result<CachedImageResult, String> {
532:     let trimmed = file_path.trim().to_string();
533:     if trimmed.is_empty() {
534:         return Err("File path cannot be empty".into());
535:     }
536: 
537:     let app_clone = app.clone();
538:     let variant_cache_path =
539:         tauri::async_runtime::spawn_blocking(move || -> Result<PathBuf, String> {
540:             let resolved = dunce::canonicalize(&trimmed)
541:                 .map_err(|e| format!("Failed to resolve image path '{trimmed}': {e}"))?;
542: 
543:             let allowed = app_clone.state::<crate::commands::fs::AllowedRoots>();
544:             if !allowed.is_allowed(&resolved) {
545:                 return Err(format!(
546:                     "Path '{}' is not under an allowed library root",
547:                     resolved.display()
548:                 ));
549:             }
550: 
551:             generate_image_variant_direct(&app_clone, &resolved, max_width, max_height)
552:         })
553:         .await
554:         .map_err(|e| format!("Task join error: {e}"))??;
555: 
556:     Ok(CachedImageResult {
557:         path: variant_cache_path.to_string_lossy().to_string(),
558:         url: crate::protocol::path_to_mangaimg_url(&variant_cache_path),
559:         is_placeholder: false,
560:         status: "generated".into(),
561:         error: None,
562:     })
563: }

565: #[cfg(test)]
566: mod tests {
567:     use super::*;
568: 
569:     #[test]
570:     fn test_calculate_fitted_dimensions() {
571:         // Image smaller than target bounds is NOT upscaled (min 1.0 scale)
572:         let (w, h) = calculate_fitted_dimensions(100, 150, 200, 300);
573:         assert_eq!((w, h), (100, 150));
574: 
575:         // Aspect ratio preserved when scaled down
576:         let (w, h) = calculate_fitted_dimensions(800, 1600, 160, 320);
577:         assert_eq!((w, h), (160, 320));
578: 
579:         // Wide image bounded by width
580:         let (w, h) = calculate_fitted_dimensions(1000, 500, 200, 200);
581:         assert_eq!((w, h), (200, 100));
582: 
583:         // Tall image bounded by height
584:         let (w, h) = calculate_fitted_dimensions(500, 1000, 200, 200);
585:         assert_eq!((w, h), (100, 200));
586: 
587:         // Extreme bounds or 0 input
588:         let (w, h) = calculate_fitted_dimensions(0, 0, 100, 100);
589:         assert_eq!((w, h), (1, 1));
590:     }
591: 
592:     #[test]
593:     fn test_resize_image_fir() {
594:         let img = DynamicImage::ImageRgba8(RgbaImage::from_fn(200, 200, |_, _| {
595:             image::Rgba([255, 0, 0, 255])
596:         }));
597: 
598:         let resized_bytes = resize_image_fir(img, 100, 100).expect("Image resize must succeed");
599:         assert!(
600:             !resized_bytes.is_empty(),
601:             "Resized image bytes must not be empty"
602:         );
603: 
604:         // Verify the decoded output has the target dimensions
605:         let decoded = image::load_from_memory(&resized_bytes)
606:             .expect("Output bytes must be valid image format");
607:         assert_eq!(decoded.width(), 100);
608:         assert_eq!(decoded.height(), 100);
609:     }
610: 
611:     #[test]
612:     fn test_create_placeholder_webp_limits() {
613:         // Valid placeholder within limits succeeds
614:         let bytes =
615:             create_placeholder_webp(160, 320).expect("Placeholder WebP must encode cleanly");
616:         assert!(!bytes.is_empty());
617:         let decoded = image::load_from_memory(&bytes).expect("Placeholder must be decodable");
618:         assert_eq!(decoded.width(), 160);
619:         assert_eq!(decoded.height(), 320);
620: 
621:         // Zero dimensions rejected
622:         assert!(create_placeholder_webp(0, 100).is_err());
623:         assert!(create_placeholder_webp(100, 0).is_err());
624: 
625:         // Extreme dimensions exceeding MAX_IMAGE_SIDE rejected
626:         assert!(create_placeholder_webp(32_769, 100).is_err());
627: 
628:         // Excessive pixel area exceeding memory budget rejected
629:         assert!(create_placeholder_webp(10_000, 10_000).is_err());
630:     }
631: 
632:     #[test]
633:     fn test_safe_decode_image_on_corrupt_or_missing() {
634:         let nonexistent = Path::new("nonexistent-image-path-12345.jpg");
635:         let result = safe_decode_image(nonexistent);
636:         assert!(result.is_err());
637:     }
638: 
639:     #[test]
640:     fn test_format_matrix_capabilities() {
641:         // Rust decodable raster formats
642:         for ext in &["jpg", "jpeg", "png", "webp", "gif", "bmp"] {
643:             let caps = get_format_capabilities(ext);
644:             assert!(caps.recognized_extension);
645:             assert!(caps.header_readable);
646:             assert!(caps.webview_displayable);
647:             assert!(
648:                 caps.rust_decodable,
649:                 "Format {} should be rust decodable",
650:                 ext
651:             );
652:         }
653: 
654:         // AVIF: recognized, header readable, webview displayable, but NOT rust decodable
655:         let avif = get_format_capabilities("avif");
656:         assert!(avif.recognized_extension);
657:         assert!(avif.header_readable);
658:         assert!(avif.webview_displayable);
659:         assert!(!avif.rust_decodable);
660: 
661:         // SVG: recognized, vector (no fixed raster header), webview displayable, not rust decodable
662:         let svg = get_format_capabilities("svg");
663:         assert!(svg.recognized_extension);
664:         assert!(!svg.header_readable);
665:         assert!(svg.webview_displayable);
666:         assert!(!svg.rust_decodable);
667: 
668:         // HEIC / TIFF: recognized, header readable, but NOT natively webview displayable and NOT rust decodable
669:         let heic = get_format_capabilities("heic");
670:         assert!(heic.recognized_extension);
671:         assert!(heic.header_readable);
672:         assert!(!heic.webview_displayable);
673:         assert!(!heic.rust_decodable);
674: 
675:         let tiff = get_format_capabilities("tiff");
676:         assert!(tiff.recognized_extension);
677:         assert!(tiff.header_readable);
678:         assert!(!tiff.webview_displayable);
679:         assert!(!tiff.rust_decodable);
680: 
681:         // Unrecognized format
682:         let unknown = get_format_capabilities("xyz");
683:         assert!(!unknown.recognized_extension);
684:         assert!(!unknown.rust_decodable);
685:     }
686: }

119:     pub local_path: String,
120:     pub last_chapter: usize,
121:     pub added_at: f64,
122:     pub updated_at: f64,
123:     pub revision: u64,
124:     pub sort: String,
125:     #[serde(default)]
126:     pub chapter_count: usize,
127:     #[serde(default)]
128:     pub page_count: usize,
129:     #[serde(default)]
130:     pub has_pending_dimensions: bool,
131:     pub chapters: Vec<ChapterDto>,
132:     pub cover_path: Option<String>,
133:     pub issues: Vec<ScanIssue>,
134:     pub reused: usize,
135: }
136: 
137: fn is_combining_mark(c: char) -> bool {
138:     matches!(
139:         c as u32,
140:         0x0300..=0x036F
141:             | 0x0483..=0x0489
142:             | 0x0591..=0x05BD
143:             | 0x05BF
144:             | 0x05C1..=0x05C2
145:             | 0x05C4..=0x05C5
146:             | 0x05C7
147:             | 0x0610..=0x061A
148:             | 0x064B..=0x065F
149:             | 0x0670
150:             | 0x06D6..=0x06ED
151:             | 0x0730..=0x074A
152:             | 0x07A6..=0x07B0
153:             | 0x07EB..=0x07F3
154:             | 0x0816..=0x082D
155:             | 0x0859..=0x085B
156:             | 0x08D3..=0x0903
157:             | 0x093A..=0x094F
158:             | 0x0951..=0x0957
159:             | 0x0962..=0x0963
160:             | 0x0981..=0x0983
161:             | 0x09BC..=0x09CD
162:             | 0x09D7
163:             | 0x09E2..=0x09E3
164:             | 0x09FE
165:             | 0x0A01..=0x0A51
166:             | 0x0A70..=0x0A75
167:             | 0x0A81..=0x0AE3
168:             | 0x0AFA..=0x0AFF
169:             | 0x0B01..=0x0B63
170:             | 0x0B82..=0x0BD7
171:             | 0x0C00..=0x0C63
172:             | 0x0C81..=0x0CE3
173:             | 0x0D00..=0x0D63
174:             | 0x0D81..=0x0DDF
175:             | 0x0E31..=0x0E3A
176:             | 0x0E47..=0x0E4E
177:             | 0x0EB1..=0x0EBC
178:             | 0x0EC8..=0x0ECD
179:             | 0x0F18..=0x0F19
180:             | 0x0F35
181:             | 0x0F37
182:             | 0x0F39
183:             | 0x0F3E..=0x0F3F
184:             | 0x0F71..=0x0F84
185:             | 0x0F86..=0x0F87
186:             | 0x0F8D..=0x0FBC
187:             | 0x102B..=0x103E
188:             | 0x1056..=0x1059
189:             | 0x105E..=0x1060
190:             | 0x1062..=0x1064
191:             | 0x1067..=0x106D
192:             | 0x1071..=0x1074
193:             | 0x1082..=0x108D
194:             | 0x108F
195:             | 0x109A..=0x109D
196:             | 0x135D..=0x135F
197:             | 0x1712..=0x1715
198:             | 0x1732..=0x1734
199:             | 0x1752..=0x1753
200:             | 0x1772..=0x1773
201:             | 0x17B4..=0x17D3
202:             | 0x17DD
203:             | 0x180B..=0x180F
204:             | 0x1885..=0x1886
205:             | 0x18A9
206:             | 0x1920..=0x193B
207:             | 0x1A17..=0x1A1B
208:             | 0x1A55..=0x1A7F
209:             | 0x1AB0..=0x1AFF
210:             | 0x1B00..=0x1B04
211:             | 0x1B34..=0x1B44
212:             | 0x1B6B..=0x1B73
213:             | 0x1B80..=0x1B82
214:             | 0x1BA1..=0x1BAD
215:             | 0x1BE6..=0x1BF3
216:             | 0x1C24..=0x1C37
217:             | 0x1CD0..=0x1CF9
218:             | 0x1DC0..=0x1DFF
219:             | 0x20D0..=0x20FF
220:             | 0x302A..=0x302F
221:             | 0x3099..=0x309A
222:             | 0xA66F..=0xA67D
223:             | 0xA69E..=0xA69F
224:             | 0xA6F0..=0xA6F1
225:             | 0xA802
226:             | 0xA806
227:             | 0xA80B
228:             | 0xA823..=0xA827
229:             | 0xA880..=0xA881
230:             | 0xA8B4..=0xA8C5
231:             | 0xA8E0..=0xA8F1
232:             | 0xA8FF..=0xA953
233:             | 0xA980..=0xA9C0
234:             | 0xA9E5
235:             | 0xAA29..=0xAA36
236:             | 0xAA43
237:             | 0xAA4C..=0xAA4D
238:             | 0xAA7B..=0xAA7D
239:             | 0xAAB0..=0xAABE
240:             | 0xAAEB..=0xAAEF
241:             | 0xAAF5..=0xAAF6
242:             | 0xABE3..=0xABEA
243:             | 0xABEC..=0xABED
244:             | 0xFB1E
245:             | 0xFE00..=0xFE0F
246:             | 0xFE20..=0xFE2F
247:     )
248: }
249: 
250: /// Generates a clean URL slug from a title string.
251: pub fn generate_slug(name: &str) -> String {
252:     let mut slug = String::new();
253:     let mut prev_dash = false;
254:     for c in name.chars() {
255:         if c.is_alphanumeric() || is_combining_mark(c) {
256:             for lc in c.to_lowercase() {
257:                 slug.push(lc);
258:             }
259:             prev_dash = false;
260:         } else if !prev_dash && !slug.is_empty() {
261:             slug.push('-');
262:             prev_dash = true;
263:         }
264:         if slug.len() >= 100 {
265:             break;
266:         }
267:     }
268:     let trimmed = slug.trim_matches('-');
269:     if trimmed.is_empty()
270:         || matches!(
271:             trimmed,
272:             "read" | "assets" | "api" | "bookmarks" | "index-html" | "index.html"
273:         )
274:     {
275:         "manga".to_string()
276:     } else {
277:         trimmed.to_string()
278:     }
279: }
280: 
281: /// Resolves a reading position against a chapter's newly indexed pages list.
282: pub fn matching_position(
283:     p: &PositionDto,
284:     pages: &[PageDto],
285:     old_pages: &[PreviousPageDto],
286: ) -> Option<PositionDto> {
287:     if pages.is_empty() {
288:         return None;
289:     }
290: 
291:     let target_path = p
292:         .path
293:         .clone()
294:         .or_else(|| old_pages.get(p.page).map(|pg| pg.path.clone()));
295: 
296:     let mut page_idx = target_path
297:         .as_ref()
298:         .and_then(|tp| pages.iter().position(|x| &x.path == tp));
299: 
300:     // If path not found, try matching by leaf file name
301:     if page_idx.is_none() {
302:         if let Some(tp) = target_path.as_ref() {
303:             let leaf = tp.rsplit('/').next().unwrap_or(tp);
304:             let matches: Vec<usize> = pages
305:                 .iter()
306:                 .enumerate()
307:                 .filter(|(_, x)| x.name == leaf)
308:                 .map(|(i, _)| i)
309:                 .collect();
310:             if matches.len() == 1 {
311:                 page_idx = Some(matches[0]);
312:             }
313:         }
314:     }
315: 
316:     // Fall back to clamping by index
317:     let valid_idx = page_idx.unwrap_or_else(|| p.page.min(pages.len().saturating_sub(1)));
318:     let fraction = p
319:         .fraction
320:         .map(|f| if f.is_nan() { 0.0 } else { f.clamp(0.0, 1.0) });
321: 
322:     Some(PositionDto {
323:         page: valid_idx,
324:         path: Some(pages[valid_idx].path.clone()),
325:         fraction,
326:         manga_bookmark: p.manga_bookmark,
327:         timestamp: p.timestamp,
328:     })
329: }
330: 
331: /// Matches a newly indexed chapter against an existing manga record.
332: pub fn chapter_match<'a>(
333:     name: &str,
334:     pages: &[PageDto],
335:     previous: Option<&'a PreviousMangaDto>,
336: ) -> Option<&'a PreviousChapterDto> {
337:     let prev = previous?;
338: 
339:     // 1. Exact name match
340:     if let Some(exact) = prev.chapters.iter().find(|c| c.name == name) {
341:         return Some(exact);
342:     }
343: 
344:     // 2. Renamed chapter matching: same page count and all page signatures match
345:     fn sig(name: &str, size: Option<u64>, modified: Option<f64>) -> String {
346:         format!(
347:             "{}\0{}\0{}",
348:             name,
349:             size.unwrap_or(0),
350:             modified.map(|m| m.round() as i64).unwrap_or(0)
351:         )
352:     }
353: 
354:     let current_signatures: HashSet<String> = pages
355:         .iter()
356:         .map(|p| sig(&p.name, p.size, p.modified))
357:         .collect();
358: 
359:     let candidate_matches: Vec<&'a PreviousChapterDto> = prev
360:         .chapters
361:         .iter()
362:         .filter(|c| {
363:             if c.pages.len() != pages.len() {
364:                 return false;
365:             }
366:             c.pages
367:                 .iter()
368:                 .all(|p| current_signatures.contains(&sig(&p.name, p.size, p.modified)))
369:         })
370:         .collect();
371: 
372:     if candidate_matches.len() == 1 {
373:         Some(candidate_matches[0])
374:     } else {
375:         None
376:     }
377: }
378: 
379: pub fn manga_record_to_previous_dto(rec: crate::db::MangaRecord) -> PreviousMangaDto {
380:     PreviousMangaDto {
381:         id: rec.id,
382:         title: rec.title,
383:         slug: rec.slug,
384:         last_chapter: Some(rec.last_chapter),
385:         added_at: Some(rec.added_at),
386:         revision: rec.revision,
387:         chapters: rec
388:             .chapters
389:             .into_iter()
390:             .map(|c| PreviousChapterDto {
391:                 name: c.name,
392:                 read: Some(c.read),
393:                 position: c.position.map(|p| PositionDto {
394:                     page: p.page as usize,
395:                     path: p.path,
396:                     fraction: Some(p.fraction),
397:                     manga_bookmark: p.manga_bookmark,
398:                     timestamp: p.timestamp,
399:                 }),
400:                 bookmarks: c.bookmarks.map(|bms| {
401:                     bms.into_iter()
402:                         .map(|p| PositionDto {
403:                             page: p.page as usize,
404:                             path: p.path,
405:                             fraction: Some(p.fraction),
406:                             manga_bookmark: p.manga_bookmark,
407:                             timestamp: p.timestamp,
408:                         })
409:                         .collect()
410:                 }),
411:                 pages: c
412:                     .pages
413:                     .into_iter()
414:                     .map(|p| PreviousPageDto {
415:                         name: p.name,
416:                         path: p.path,
417:                         width: Some(p.width),
418:                         height: Some(p.height),
419:                         size: p.size,
420:                         modified: p.modified,
421:                         dimensions_pending: p.dimensions_pending,
422:                     })
423:                     .collect(),
424:             })
425:             .collect(),
426:     }
427: }
428: 
429: #[derive(Debug, Clone)]
430: struct ScannedFile {
431:     rel_path: String,
432:     abs_path: PathBuf,
433:     name: String,
434:     size: u64,
435:     modified: f64,
436:     cached_width: Option<u32>,
437:     cached_height: Option<u32>,
438: }
439: 
440: #[tauri::command]
441: pub async fn index_title(
442:     app: AppHandle,
443:     root: String,
444:     options: Option<IndexOptions>,
445:     on_progress: tauri::ipc::Channel<ScanProgress>,
446: ) -> Result<TitleDto, String> {
447:     let raw_path = Path::new(root.trim());
448:     if !raw_path.exists() || !raw_path.is_dir() {
449:         return Err(format!(
450:             "Target path '{}' is not an existing directory",
451:             root
452:         ));
453:     }
454: 
455:     let root_path = dunce::canonicalize(raw_path).unwrap_or_else(|_| raw_path.to_path_buf());
456: 
457:     // Security check: must be under allowed library roots
458:     let allowed = app.state::<AllowedRoots>();
459:     if !allowed.is_allowed(&root_path) {
460:         return Err(format!(
461:             "Path '{}' is not under an allowed library root",
462:             root_path.display()
463:         ));
464:     }
465: 
466:     let opts = options.unwrap_or_default();
467:     let sort_mode = opts.sort.unwrap_or_else(|| "natural".to_string());
468:     let warm_count = opts.warm_chapters.unwrap_or(5);
469: 
470:     let scan_mgr = app.state::<crate::commands::fs::ScanManager>();
471:     let handle = opts.job_id.as_deref().map(|id| scan_mgr.register(id));
472:     let cancel_flag = handle.as_ref().map(|h| std::sync::Arc::clone(&h.flag));
473: 
474:     struct JobGuard<'a> {
475:         mgr: &'a crate::commands::fs::ScanManager,
476:         id: Option<&'a str>,
477:     }
478:     impl<'a> Drop for JobGuard<'a> {
479:         fn drop(&mut self) {
480:             if let Some(id) = self.id {
481:                 self.mgr.unregister(id);
482:             }
483:         }
484:     }
485:     let _guard = JobGuard {
486:         mgr: &scan_mgr,
487:         id: opts.job_id.as_deref(),
488:     };
489: 
490:     let check_cancelled = |handle: Option<&crate::commands::fs::ScanHandle>| -> Result<(), String> {
491:         if let Some(h) = handle {
492:             if h.flag.load(std::sync::atomic::Ordering::Relaxed) || *h.receiver.borrow() {
493:                 return Err("Indexing cancelled".to_string());
494:             }
495:         }
496:         Ok(())
497:     };
498: 
499:     check_cancelled(handle.as_ref())?;
500: 
501:     let folder_name = root_path
502:         .file_name()
503:         .map(|n| n.to_string_lossy().to_string())
504:         .unwrap_or_else(|| "Manga".to_string());
505: 
506:     let root_clone = root_path.clone();
507: 
508:     // 0. Load previous manga from SQLite off the async executor thread if not provided in options
509:     let mut previous_manga = opts.previous;
510:     if previous_manga.is_none() {
511:         if let Some(db_state) = app.try_state::<crate::db::DbState>() {
512:             let db_clone = db_state.inner().clone();
513:             let target_id = opts.target_id.clone();
514:             let root_str = root_path.to_string_lossy().to_string();
515:             if let Ok(Some(prev_rec)) = tauri::async_runtime::spawn_blocking(move || {
516:                 db_clone.get_previous_manga_for_indexing_sync(
517:                     target_id.as_deref(),
518:                     Some(&root_str),
519:                 )
520:             })
521:             .await
522:             {
523:                 previous_manga = Some(manga_record_to_previous_dto(prev_rec));
524:             }
525:         }
526:     }
527: 
528:     // 1. Check for retained discovery snapshot to eliminate duplicate walkdir and dimension parsing
529:     let (mut scanned_files, mut traversal_issues) =
530:         if let Some(discovery_state) = app.try_state::<crate::commands::fs::DiscoveryState>() {
531:             if let Some(snapshot) = discovery_state.get_for_path(&root_path) {
532:                 let relevant: Vec<_> = snapshot
533:                     .candidates
534:                     .into_iter()
535:                     .filter(|c| {
536:                         let p = Path::new(&c.absolute_path);
537:                         p.starts_with(&root_path)
538:                     })
539:                     .collect();
540: 
541:                 if !relevant.is_empty() {
542:                     let root_clone2 = root_path.clone();
543:                     let cancel_clone = cancel_flag.clone();
544:                     tauri::async_runtime::spawn_blocking(move || {
545:                         let mut files = Vec::new();
546:                         let mut issues = Vec::new();
547:                         for c in relevant {
548:                             if let Some(ref cf) = cancel_clone {
549:                                 if cf.load(std::sync::atomic::Ordering::Relaxed) {
550:                                     break;
551:                                 }
552:                             }
553:                             let abs = PathBuf::from(&c.absolute_path);
554:                             let meta = match std::fs::metadata(&abs) {
555:                                 Ok(m) => m,
556:                                 Err(e) => {
557:                                     issues.push(ScanIssue {
558:                                         path: c.absolute_path.clone(),
559:                                         reason: format!("File missing on commit: {e}"),
560:                                     });
561:                                     continue;
562:                                 }
563:                             };
564:                             if !meta.is_file() {
565:                                 continue;
566:                             }
567:                             let mtime = meta
568:                                 .modified()
569:                                 .ok()
570:                                 .and_then(|t| t.duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH).ok())
571:                                 .map(|d| d.as_millis() as f64)
572:                                 .unwrap_or(0.0);
573:                             let size = meta.len();
574: 
575:                             let rel = match abs.strip_prefix(&root_clone2) {
576:                                 Ok(p) => p.to_string_lossy().replace('\\', "/"),
577:                                 Err(_) => c.relative_path.clone(),
578:                             };
579: 
580:                             let name = abs
581:                                 .file_name()
582:                                 .map(|n| n.to_string_lossy().to_string())
583:                                 .unwrap_or_else(|| rel.clone());
584: 
585:                             let (w, h) = if size == c.size
586:                                 && (mtime - c.modified).abs() < 1000.0
587:                                 && c.width.is_some()
588:                                 && c.height.is_some()
589:                             {
590:                                 (c.width, c.height)
591:                             } else {
592:                                 parse_image_dimensions(&abs)
593:                             };
594: 
595:                             files.push(ScannedFile {
596:                                 rel_path: rel,
597:                                 abs_path: abs,
598:                                 name,
599:                                 size,
600:                                 modified: mtime,
601:                                 cached_width: w,
602:                                 cached_height: h,
603:                             });
604:                         }
605:                         (files, issues)
606:                     })
607:                     .await
608:                     .unwrap_or_else(|_| (Vec::new(), Vec::new()))
609:                 } else {
610:                     (Vec::new(), Vec::new())
611:                 }
612:             } else {
613:                 (Vec::new(), Vec::new())
614:             }
615:         } else {
616:             (Vec::new(), Vec::new())
617:         };
618: 
619:     // If no retained snapshot or snapshot had no valid files, run standard recursive walkdir scan
620:     if scanned_files.is_empty() {
621:         let cancel_clone = cancel_flag.clone();
622:         let (files, issues) = tauri::async_runtime::spawn_blocking(move || {
623:             let mut files = Vec::new();
624:             let mut issues = Vec::new();
625:             let walker = walkdir::WalkDir::new(&root_clone)
626:                 .max_depth(MAX_DEPTH)
627:                 .follow_links(false)
628:                 .into_iter()
629:                 .filter_entry(|entry| {
630:                     if entry.depth() == 0 {
631:                         return true;
632:                     }
633:                     let name = entry.file_name().to_string_lossy().to_lowercase();
634:                     if entry.file_type().is_dir() {
635:                         if name.starts_with('.') || IGNORED_FOLDER_NAMES.contains(&name.as_str()) {
636:                             return false;
637:                         }
638:                     }
639:                     true
640:                 });
641: 
642:             for entry_res in walker {
643:                 if let Some(ref cf) = cancel_clone {
644:                     if cf.load(std::sync::atomic::Ordering::Relaxed) {
645:                         break;
646:                     }
647:                 }
648:                 if files.len() >= MAX_SCAN_FILES {
649:                     issues.push(ScanIssue {
650:                         path: root_clone.to_string_lossy().to_string(),
651:                         reason: format!(
652:                             "Reached maximum scan limit of {MAX_SCAN_FILES} files; scan results may be incomplete"
653:                         ),
654:                     });
655:                     break;
656:                 }
657:                 let entry = match entry_res {
658:                     Ok(e) => e,
659:                     Err(err) => {
660:                         let p = err
661:                             .path()
662:                             .map(|p| p.to_string_lossy().to_string())
663:                             .unwrap_or_else(|| "unknown".to_string());
664:                         issues.push(ScanIssue {
665:                             path: p,
666:                             reason: err.to_string(),
667:                         });
668:                         continue;
669:                     }
670:                 };
671: 
672:                 if !entry.file_type().is_file() {
673:                     continue;
674:                 }
675: 
676:                 let file_path = entry.path();
677:                 let ext = match file_path.extension() {
678:                     Some(e) => e.to_string_lossy().to_lowercase(),
679:                     None => continue,
680:                 };
681: 
682:                 if !ALLOWED_IMAGE_EXTENSIONS.contains(&ext.as_str()) {
683:                     continue;
684:                 }
685: 
686:                 let metadata = match entry.metadata() {
687:                     Ok(m) => m,
688:                     Err(err) => {
689:                         issues.push(ScanIssue {
690:                             path: file_path.to_string_lossy().to_string(),
691:                             reason: format!("Failed to read metadata: {err}"),
692:                         });
693:                         continue;
694:                     }
695:                 };
696: 
697:                 let rel_path = match file_path.strip_prefix(&root_clone) {
698:                     Ok(p) => p.to_string_lossy().replace('\\', "/"),
699:                     Err(_) => continue,
700:                 };
701: 
702:                 let file_name = file_path
703:                     .file_name()
704:                     .map(|n| n.to_string_lossy().to_string())
705:                     .unwrap_or_else(|| rel_path.clone());
706: 
707:                 let modified = metadata
708:                     .modified()
709:                     .ok()
710:                     .and_then(|t| t.duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH).ok())
711:                     .map(|d| d.as_millis() as f64)
712:                     .unwrap_or(0.0);
713: 
714:                 files.push(ScannedFile {
715:                     rel_path,
716:                     abs_path: file_path.to_path_buf(),
717:                     name: file_name,
718:                     size: metadata.len(),
719:                     modified,
720:                     cached_width: None,
721:                     cached_height: None,
722:                 });
723:             }
724:             (files, issues)
725:         })
726:         .await
727:         .map_err(|e| format!("Scan error: {e}"))?;
728: 
729:         scanned_files = files;
730:         traversal_issues.extend(issues);
731:     }
732: 
733:     check_cancelled(handle.as_ref())?;
734: 
735:     let total_scanned = scanned_files.len();
736:     if total_scanned == 0 {
737:         return Err("No image pages found in this selection.".to_string());
738:     }
739: 
740:     if on_progress
741:         .send(ScanProgress {
742:             phase: "discover".to_string(),
743:             done: total_scanned,
744:             total: total_scanned,
745:             title: folder_name.clone(),
746:         })
747:         .is_err()
748:     {
749:         if let Some(ref h) = handle {
750:             h.flag.store(true, std::sync::atomic::Ordering::Relaxed);
751:         }
752:         return Err("Indexing cancelled: progress channel disconnected".to_string());
753:     }
754: 
755:     // 2. Build lookup for previous dimensions reuse
756:     let mut old_pages_map: HashMap<String, PreviousPageDto> = HashMap::new();
757:     if let Some(ref prev) = previous_manga {
758:         for ch in &prev.chapters {
759:             for pg in &ch.pages {
760:                 old_pages_map.insert(pg.path.clone(), pg.clone());
761:             }
762:         }
763:     }
764: 
765:     // 3. Chapter grouping and cover detection
766:     let mut cover_candidate: Option<PathBuf> = None;
767:     let mut grouped_pages: HashMap<String, Vec<PageDto>> = HashMap::new();
768:     let mut reused_count = 0;
769: 
770:     let has_multiple_files = total_scanned > 1;
771: 
772:     for file in &scanned_files {
773:         let parts: Vec<&str> = file.rel_path.split('/').collect();
774:         let is_root = parts.len() == 1;
775: 
776:         if is_root && has_multiple_files {
777:             let lower = file.name.to_lowercase();
778:             if lower.starts_with("cover.") || lower.starts_with("thumbnail.") {
779:                 if cover_candidate.is_none() {
780:                     cover_candidate = Some(file.abs_path.clone());
781:                 }
782:                 continue;
783:             }
784:         }
785: 
786:         let chapter_name = if is_root {
787:             "Root pages".to_string()
788:         } else {
789:             parts[..parts.len() - 1].join(" / ")
790:         };
791: 
792:         let mut width = 800;
793:         let mut height = 1200;
794:         let mut dimensions_pending = true;
795: 
796:         if let (Some(w), Some(h)) = (file.cached_width, file.cached_height) {
797:             if w > 0 && h > 0 {
798:                 width = w;
799:                 height = h;
800:                 dimensions_pending = false;
801:                 reused_count += 1;
802:             }
803:         } else if let Some(cached) = old_pages_map.get(&file.rel_path) {
804:             let not_pending = !cached.dimensions_pending.unwrap_or(false);
805:             let size_matches = cached.size == Some(file.size);
806:             let mtime_matches = cached
807:                 .modified
808:                 .map(|m| (m - file.modified).abs() < 1000.0)
809:                 .unwrap_or(false);
810: 
811:             if not_pending && size_matches && mtime_matches {
812:                 if let (Some(w), Some(h)) = (cached.width, cached.height) {
813:                     if w > 0 && h > 0 {
814:                         width = w;
815:                         height = h;
816:                         dimensions_pending = false;
817:                         reused_count += 1;
818:                     }
819:                 }
820:             }
821:         }
822: 
823:         grouped_pages
824:             .entry(chapter_name)
825:             .or_default()
826:             .push(PageDto {
827:                 name: file.name.clone(),
828:                 path: file.rel_path.clone(),
829:                 width,
830:                 height,
831:                 size: Some(file.size),
832:                 modified: Some(file.modified),
833:                 dimensions_pending,
834:             });
835:     }
836: 
837:     if grouped_pages.is_empty() {
838:         return Err("No valid chapters or readable pages found in selection.".to_string());
839:     }
840: 
841:     // 4. Sort chapter names according to sort option
842:     let sort_cmp = |a: &str, b: &str| -> std::cmp::Ordering {
843:         match sort_mode.as_str() {
844:             "name" => a.cmp(b),
845:             "reverse" => natural_order(b, a),
846:             _ => natural_order(a, b),
847:         }
848:     };
849: 
850:     let mut chapter_names: Vec<String> = grouped_pages.keys().cloned().collect();
851:     chapter_names.sort_by(|a, b| sort_cmp(a, b));
852: 
853:     // Sort pages within each chapter
854:     for pages in grouped_pages.values_mut() {
855:         pages.sort_by(|a, b| sort_cmp(&a.name, &b.name));
856:     }
857: 
858:     // 5. Dimension extraction for warm chapters (first N chapters, default 5)
859:     let warm_names: Vec<String> = chapter_names.iter().take(warm_count).cloned().collect();
860: 
861:     // Collect pending pages from warm chapters
862:     let mut pending_warm: Vec<(String, PathBuf)> = Vec::new();
863:     for name in &warm_names {
864:         if let Some(pages) = grouped_pages.get(name) {
865:             for p in pages {
866:                 if p.dimensions_pending {
867:                     let abs = root_path.join(p.path.replace('/', std::path::MAIN_SEPARATOR_STR));
868:                     pending_warm.push((p.path.clone(), abs));
869:                 }
870:             }
871:         }
872:     }
873: 
874:     let total_pending = pending_warm.len();
875:     if total_pending > 0 {
876:         if on_progress
877:             .send(ScanProgress {
878:                 phase: "index".to_string(),
879:                 done: 0,
880:                 total: total_pending,
881:                 title: format!("{folder_name} · first {warm_count} chapters"),
882:             })
883:             .is_err()
884:         {
885:             if let Some(ref h) = handle {
886:                 h.flag.store(true, std::sync::atomic::Ordering::Relaxed);
887:             }
888:             return Err("Indexing cancelled: progress channel disconnected".to_string());
889:         }
890: 
891:         let mut done = 0;
892:         let mut parsed_results: HashMap<String, (u32, u32)> = HashMap::with_capacity(total_pending);
893: 
894:         for chunk in pending_warm.chunks(SCAN_BATCH_SIZE) {
895:             check_cancelled(handle.as_ref())?;
896: 
897:             let chunk_vec = chunk.to_vec();
898:             let parsed_batch: Vec<(String, Option<u32>, Option<u32>)> =
899:                 tauri::async_runtime::spawn_blocking(move || {
900:                     chunk_vec
901:                         .into_par_iter()
902:                         .map(|(rel, abs)| {
903:                             let (w, h) = parse_image_dimensions(&abs);
904:                             (rel, w, h)
905:                         })
906:                         .collect()
907:                 })
908:                 .await
909:                 .map_err(|e| format!("Parse join error: {e}"))?;
910: 
911:             done += parsed_batch.len();
912:             for (rel, w, h) in parsed_batch {
913:                 if let (Some(width), Some(height)) = (w, h) {
914:                     if width > 0 && height > 0 {
915:                         parsed_results.insert(rel, (width, height));
916:                     } else {
917:                         traversal_issues.push(ScanIssue {
918:                             path: rel,
919:                             reason: "Zero or invalid image dimensions in header".to_string(),
920:                         });
921:                     }
922:                 } else {
923:                     traversal_issues.push(ScanIssue {
924:                         path: rel,
925:                         reason: "Could not parse image dimensions from header".to_string(),
926:                     });
927:                 }
928:             }
929: 
930:             if on_progress
931:                 .send(ScanProgress {
932:                     phase: "index".to_string(),
933:                     done,
934:                     total: total_pending,
935:                     title: format!("{folder_name} · first {warm_count} chapters"),
936:                 })
937:                 .is_err()
938:             {
939:                 if let Some(ref h) = handle {
940:                     h.flag.store(true, std::sync::atomic::Ordering::Relaxed);
941:                 }
942:                 return Err("Indexing cancelled: progress channel disconnected".to_string());
943:             }
944:             tokio::task::yield_now().await;
945:         }
946: 
947:         // Apply parsed dimensions back to warm chapters
948:         for name in &warm_names {
949:             if let Some(pages) = grouped_pages.get_mut(name) {
950:                 pages.retain_mut(|p| {
951:                     if !p.dimensions_pending {
952:                         return true;
953:                     }
954:                     if let Some(&(w, h)) = parsed_results.get(&p.path) {
955:                         p.width = w;
956:                         p.height = h;
957:                         p.dimensions_pending = false;
958:                         true
959:                     } else {
960:                         false
961:                     }
962:                 });
963:             }
964:         }
965:     }
966: 
967:     grouped_pages.retain(|_, pages| !pages.is_empty());
968:     chapter_names.retain(|name| grouped_pages.contains_key(name));
969: 
970:     if chapter_names.is_empty() {
971:         return Err(if let Some(first_issue) = traversal_issues.first() {
972:             format!(
973:                 "No readable pages. {}: {}",
974:                 first_issue.path, first_issue.reason
975:             )
976:         } else {
977:             "No image pages found in this selection.".to_string()
978:         });
979:     }
980: 
981:     check_cancelled(handle.as_ref())?;
982: 
983:     // 6. Re-link matching and chapters construction
984:     let mut final_chapters = Vec::with_capacity(chapter_names.len());
985: 
986:     for name in chapter_names {
987:         let pages = grouped_pages.remove(&name).unwrap_or_default();
988:         let old_match = chapter_match(&name, &pages, previous_manga.as_ref());
989: 
990:         let (read, position, bookmarks) = if let Some(old) = old_match {
991:             let old_names: HashSet<&str> = old.pages.iter().map(|p| p.name.as_str()).collect();
992:             let read = old.read.unwrap_or(false)
993:                 && pages.iter().all(|p| old_names.contains(p.name.as_str()));
994: 
995:             let position = old
996:                 .position
997:                 .as_ref()
998:                 .and_then(|pos| matching_position(pos, &pages, &old.pages));
999: 
1000:             let bookmarks = old
1001:                 .bookmarks
1002:                 .as_ref()
1003:                 .map(|bms| {
1004:                     bms.iter()
1005:                         .filter_map(|bm| matching_position(bm, &pages, &old.pages))
1006:                         .collect()
1007:                 })
1008:                 .unwrap_or_default();
1009: 
1010:             (read, position, bookmarks)
1011:         } else {
1012:             (false, None, Vec::new())
1013:         };
1014: 
1015:         final_chapters.push(ChapterDto {
1016:             name,
1017:             pages,
1018:             read,
1019:             position,
1020:             bookmarks,
1021:         });
1022:     }
1023: 
1024:     // 7. Cover identification, thumbnail generation, and SQLite commit off the async thread
1025:     let first_page_abs = final_chapters
1026:         .first()
1027:         .and_then(|c| c.pages.first())
1028:         .map(|p| root_path.join(p.path.replace('/', std::path::MAIN_SEPARATOR_STR)));
1029: 
1030:     let cover_abs = cover_candidate.or(first_page_abs);
1031: 
1032:     let title_name = opts
1033:         .title
1034:         .as_ref()
1035:         .map(|t| t.trim().to_string())
1036:         .filter(|t| !t.is_empty())
1037:         .or_else(|| previous_manga.as_ref().map(|p| p.title.clone()))
1038:         .unwrap_or_else(|| folder_name.clone());
1039: 
1040:     let manga_id = previous_manga
1041:         .as_ref()
1042:         .map(|p| p.id.clone())
1043:         .unwrap_or_else(|| uuid::Uuid::new_v4().to_string());
1044: 
1045:     let base_slug = generate_slug(&title_name);
1046:     let preserve_prev_slug = previous_manga
1047:         .as_ref()
1048:         .and_then(|prev| {
1049:             if opts.title.as_deref().map(|t| t.trim()) == Some(prev.title.as_str())
1050:                 || opts.title.is_none()
1051:             {
1052:                 prev.slug.clone()
1053:             } else {
1054:                 None
1055:             }
1056:         });
1057: 
1058:     let now = SystemTime::now()
1059:         .duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH)
1060:         .unwrap_or_default()
1061:         .as_millis() as f64;
1062: 
1063:     let added_at = previous_manga
1064:         .as_ref()
1065:         .and_then(|p| p.added_at)
1066:         .unwrap_or(now);
1067: 
1068:     let revision = previous_manga
1069:         .as_ref()
1070:         .and_then(|p| p.revision)
1071:         .map(|r| r + 1)
1072:         .unwrap_or(1);
1073: 
1074:     let last_chapter = previous_manga
1075:         .as_ref()
1076:         .and_then(|p| p.last_chapter)
1077:         .unwrap_or(0)
1078:         .min(final_chapters.len().saturating_sub(1));
1079: 
1080:     let final_cover_path = cover_abs
1081:         .as_ref()
1082:         .map(|p| p.to_string_lossy().to_string());
1083: 
1084:     let chapter_count = final_chapters.len();
1085:     let page_count = final_chapters.iter().map(|c| c.pages.len()).sum();
1086:     let has_pending = final_chapters
1087:         .iter()
1088:         .any(|c| c.pages.iter().any(|p| p.dimensions_pending));
1089: 
1090:     let app_for_commit = app.clone();
1091:     let manga_id_for_commit = manga_id.clone();
1092:     let title_for_commit = title_name.clone();
1093:     let root_str_for_commit = root_path.to_string_lossy().to_string();
1094:     let sort_for_commit = sort_mode.clone();
1095:     let chapters_for_commit = final_chapters.clone();
1096: 
1097:     let slug = tauri::async_runtime::spawn_blocking(move || -> Result<String, String> {
1098:         let resolved_slug = if let Some(existing_slug) = preserve_prev_slug {
1099:             existing_slug
1100:         } else if let Some(db_state) = app_for_commit.try_state::<crate::db::DbState>() {
1101:             db_state.allocate_unique_slug_sync(&base_slug, &manga_id_for_commit)
1102:         } else {
1103:             base_slug
1104:         };
1105: 
1106:         if let Some(ref cover_file) = cover_abs {
1107:             let _ = crate::commands::image::generate_thumbnail_direct(
1108:                 &app_for_commit,
1109:                 cover_file,
1110:                 &manga_id_for_commit,
1111:             );
1112:         }
1113: 
1114:         if let Some(db_state) = app_for_commit.try_state::<crate::db::DbState>() {
1115:             let rec = crate::db::MangaRecord {
1116:                 id: manga_id_for_commit,
1117:                 slug: Some(resolved_slug.clone()),
1118:                 title: title_for_commit,
1119:                 local_path: Some(root_str_for_commit),
1120:                 last_chapter,
1121:                 added_at,
1122:                 updated_at: now,
1123:                 revision: Some(revision),
1124:                 sort: Some(sort_for_commit),
1125:                 chapters: chapters_for_commit
1126:                     .iter()
1127:                     .map(|c| crate::db::ChapterRecord {
1128:                         id: None,
1129:                         name: c.name.clone(),
1130:                         pages: c
1131:                             .pages
1132:                             .iter()
1133:                             .map(|p| crate::db::PageRecord {
1134:                                 name: p.name.clone(),
1135:                                 path: p.path.clone(),
1136:                                 width: p.width,
1137:                                 height: p.height,
1138:                                 size: p.size,
1139:                                 modified: p.modified,
1140:                                 dimensions_pending: Some(p.dimensions_pending),
1141:                             })
1142:                             .collect(),
1143:                         read: c.read,
1144:                         position: c.position.as_ref().map(|p| crate::db::PositionRecord {
1145:                             page: p.page as u32,
1146:                             path: p.path.clone(),
1147:                             fraction: p.fraction.unwrap_or(0.0),
1148:                             manga_bookmark: p.manga_bookmark,
1149:                             timestamp: p.timestamp,
1150:                         }),
1151:                         bookmarks: Some(
1152:                             c.bookmarks
1153:                                 .iter()
1154:                                 .map(|b| crate::db::PositionRecord {
1155:                                     page: b.page as u32,
1156:                                     path: b.path.clone(),
1157:                                     fraction: b.fraction.unwrap_or(0.0),
1158:                                     manga_bookmark: b.manga_bookmark,
1159:                                     timestamp: b.timestamp,
1160:                                 })
1161:                                 .collect(),
1162:                         ),
1163:                     })
1164:                     .collect(),
1165:             };
1166:             db_state
1167:                 .save_manga_sync(&rec)
1168:                 .map_err(|e| format!("Failed to save indexed manga to database: {e}"))?;
1169:         }
1170: 
1171:         Ok(resolved_slug)
1172:     })
1173:     .await
1174:     .map_err(|e| format!("Commit task join error: {e}"))??;
1175: 
1176:     Ok(TitleDto {
1177:         id: manga_id,
1178:         title: title_name,
1179:         slug,
1180:         local_path: root_path.to_string_lossy().to_string(),
1181:         last_chapter,
1182:         added_at,
1183:         updated_at: now,
1184:         revision,
1185:         sort: sort_mode,
1186:         chapter_count,
1187:         page_count,
1188:         has_pending_dimensions: has_pending,
1189:         chapters: final_chapters,
1190:         cover_path: final_cover_path,
1191:         issues: traversal_issues,
1192:         reused: reused_count,
1193:     })
1194: }
1195: 
1196: #[cfg(test)]
1197: mod tests {
1198:     use super::*;
1199: 
1200:     #[test]
1201:     fn test_generate_slug() {
1202:         assert_eq!(generate_slug("One Piece (Digital)"), "one-piece-digital");
1203:         assert_eq!(
1204:             generate_slug("Attack on Titan: Vol 1"),
1205:             "attack-on-titan-vol-1"
1206:         );
1207:         assert_eq!(generate_slug("   "), "manga");
1208:         assert_eq!(generate_slug("---"), "manga");
1209:         assert_eq!(generate_slug("bookmarks"), "manga");
1210:         assert_eq!(generate_slug("index.html"), "manga");
1211:         // Verify combining marks (e.g. Devanagari vowel signs) are preserved without hyphen splitting
1212:         assert_eq!(generate_slug("हिंदी मंगा"), "हिंदी-मंगा");
1213:     }
1214: 
1215:     #[test]
1216:     fn test_matching_position_exact_path() {
1217:         let pages = vec![
1218:             PageDto {
1219:                 name: "01.jpg".into(),
1220:                 path: "ch1/01.jpg".into(),
1221:                 width: 800,
1222:                 height: 1200,
1223:                 size: Some(100),
1224:                 modified: Some(1000.0),
1225:                 dimensions_pending: false,
1226:             },
1227:             PageDto {
1228:                 name: "02.jpg".into(),
1229:                 path: "ch1/02.jpg".into(),
1230:                 width: 800,
1231:                 height: 1200,
1232:                 size: Some(100),
1233:                 modified: Some(1000.0),
1234:                 dimensions_pending: false,
1235:             },
1236:         ];
1237: 
1238:         let pos = PositionDto {
1239:             page: 0,
1240:             path: Some("ch1/02.jpg".into()),
1241:             fraction: Some(0.4),
1242:             manga_bookmark: Some(true),
1243:             timestamp: Some(12345.0),
1244:         };
1245: 
1246:         let matched = matching_position(&pos, &pages, &[]).expect("should match");
1247:         assert_eq!(matched.page, 1);
1248:         assert_eq!(matched.path, Some("ch1/02.jpg".into()));
1249:         assert_eq!(matched.fraction, Some(0.4));
1250:         assert_eq!(matched.manga_bookmark, Some(true));
1251:         assert_eq!(matched.timestamp, Some(12345.0));
1252:     }
1253: 
1254:     #[test]
1255:     fn test_matching_position_leaf_fallback() {
1256:         let pages = vec![PageDto {
1257:             name: "02.jpg".into(),
1258:             path: "new_folder/02.jpg".into(),
1259:             width: 800,
1260:             height: 1200,
1261:             size: Some(100),
1262:             modified: Some(1000.0),
1263:             dimensions_pending: false,
1264:         }];
1265: 
1266:         let pos = PositionDto {
1267:             page: 5,
1268:             path: Some("old_folder/02.jpg".into()),
1269:             fraction: Some(0.5),
1270:             manga_bookmark: None,
1271:             timestamp: None,
1272:         };
1273: 
1274:         let matched = matching_position(&pos, &pages, &[]).expect("should match");
1275:         assert_eq!(matched.page, 0);
1276:         assert_eq!(matched.path, Some("new_folder/02.jpg".into()));
1277:         assert_eq!(matched.fraction, Some(0.5));
1278:     }
1279: 
1280:     #[test]
1281:     fn test_chapter_match_exact_and_renamed() {
1282:         let pages = vec![
1283:             PageDto {
1284:                 name: "01.jpg".into(),
1285:                 path: "new_ch1/01.jpg".into(),
1286:                 width: 800,
1287:                 height: 1200,
1288:                 size: Some(100),
1289:                 modified: Some(1000.0),
1290:                 dimensions_pending: false,
1291:             },
1292:             PageDto {
1293:                 name: "02.jpg".into(),
1294:                 path: "new_ch1/02.jpg".into(),
1295:                 width: 800,
1296:                 height: 1200,
1297:                 size: Some(200),
1298:                 modified: Some(2000.0),
1299:                 dimensions_pending: false,
1300:             },
1301:         ];
1302: 
1303:         let prev = PreviousMangaDto {
1304:             id: "m-1".into(),
1305:             title: "Title".into(),
1306:             slug: Some("title".into()),
1307:             last_chapter: Some(0),
1308:             added_at: Some(1000.0),
1309:             revision: Some(2),
1310:             chapters: vec![
1311:                 PreviousChapterDto {
1312:                     name: "Old Ch 1".into(),
1313:                     read: Some(true),
1314:                     position: None,
1315:                     bookmarks: None,
1316:                     pages: vec![
1317:                         PreviousPageDto {
1318:                             name: "01.jpg".into(),
1319:                             path: "old_ch1/01.jpg".into(),
1320:                             width: Some(800),
1321:                             height: Some(1200),
1322:                             size: Some(100),
1323:                             modified: Some(1000.0),
1324:                             dimensions_pending: Some(false),
1325:                         },
1326:                         PreviousPageDto {
1327:                             name: "02.jpg".into(),
1328:                             path: "old_ch1/02.jpg".into(),
1329:                             width: Some(800),
1330:                             height: Some(1200),
1331:                             size: Some(200),
1332:                             modified: Some(2000.0),
1333:                             dimensions_pending: Some(false),
1334:                         },
1335:                     ],
1336:                 },
1337:                 PreviousChapterDto {
1338:                     name: "Exact Chapter".into(),
1339:                     read: Some(false),
1340:                     position: None,
1341:                     bookmarks: None,
1342:                     pages: vec![],
1343:                 },
1344:             ],
1345:         };
1346: 
1347:         // 1. Exact match
1348:         let exact = chapter_match("Exact Chapter", &[], Some(&prev));
1349:         assert!(exact.is_some());
1350:         assert_eq!(exact.unwrap().name, "Exact Chapter");
1351: 
1352:         // 2. Renamed match
1353:         let renamed = chapter_match("Different Name", &pages, Some(&prev));
1354:         assert!(renamed.is_some());
1355:         assert_eq!(renamed.unwrap().name, "Old Ch 1");
1356:     }

1358:     #[test]
1359:     fn test_indexing_grouping_logic() {
1360:         fn determine_chapter(rel_path: &str, is_cover: bool) -> Option<String> {
1361:             if is_cover {
1362:                 return None;
1363:             }
1364:             if let Some(idx) = rel_path.rfind('/') {
1365:                 let dir_part = &rel_path[..idx];
1366:                 let chapter_name = dir_part.replace('/', " / ");
1367:                 Some(chapter_name)
1368:             } else {
1369:                 Some("Root pages".to_string())
1370:             }
1371:         }
1372: 
1373:         // Subdirectory becomes chapter name with slash normalization
1374:         assert_eq!(
1375:             determine_chapter("Chapter 1/01.jpg", false),
1376:             Some("Chapter 1".into())
1377:         );
1378:         assert_eq!(
1379:             determine_chapter("Volume 1/Chapter 1/01.jpg", false),
1380:             Some("Volume 1 / Chapter 1".into())
1381:         );
1382: 
1383:         // Root file becomes "Root pages"
1384:         assert_eq!(
1385:             determine_chapter("01.jpg", false),
1386:             Some("Root pages".into())
1387:         );
1388: 
1389:         // Cover file excluded from regular chapters
1390:         assert_eq!(determine_chapter("cover.jpg", true), None);
1391:         assert_eq!(determine_chapter("thumbnail.png", true), None);
1392:     }

16: #[tauri::command(async)]
17: pub fn set_progress_bar(window: WebviewWindow, progress: f64) -> Result<(), String> {
18:     let state = if progress < 0.0 {
19:         ProgressBarState {
20:             progress: None,
21:             status: Some(ProgressBarStatus::None),
22:         }
23:     } else if progress > 1.0 {
24:         ProgressBarState {
25:             progress: None,
26:             status: Some(ProgressBarStatus::Indeterminate),
27:         }
28:     } else {
29:         let percent = (progress * 100.0).round().clamp(0.0, 100.0) as u64;
30:         ProgressBarState {
31:             progress: Some(percent),
32:             status: Some(ProgressBarStatus::Normal),
33:         }
34:     };
35: 
36:     let _ = window.set_progress_bar(state);
37:     Ok(())
38: }

6: #[derive(Debug, thiserror::Error, Serialize, Deserialize, PartialEq, Clone)]
7: #[serde(tag = "code", content = "message")]
8: pub enum DbError {
9:     #[error("Database error: {0}")]
10:     Sqlite(String),
11: 
12:     #[error("Migration error: {0}")]
13:     Migration(String),
14: 
15:     #[error("Lock error: {0}")]
16:     Lock(String),
17: 
18:     #[error("Validation error: {0}")]
19:     Validation(String),
20: 
21:     #[error("Not found: {0}")]
22:     NotFound(String),
23: 
24:     #[error("Stale revision conflict: {0}")]
25:     Conflict(String),
26: 
27:     #[error("Task execution error: {0}")]
28:     Task(String),
29: 
30:     #[error("IO error: {0}")]
31:     Io(String),
32: 
33:     #[error("Serialization error: {0}")]
34:     Serialization(String),
35: }
36: 
37: impl From<rusqlite::Error> for DbError {
38:     fn from(err: rusqlite::Error) -> Self {
39:         DbError::Sqlite(err.to_string())
40:     }
41: }
42: 
43: impl From<serde_json::Error> for DbError {
44:     fn from(err: serde_json::Error) -> Self {
45:         DbError::Serialization(err.to_string())
46:     }
47: }

49: pub type DbResult<T> = std::result::Result<T, DbError>;
50: 
51: #[derive(Clone)]
52: pub struct DbState {
53:     pub reader: Arc<Mutex<Connection>>,
54:     pub writer: Arc<Mutex<Connection>>,
55: }
56: 
57: impl DbState {
58:     pub fn new(reader: Connection, writer: Connection) -> Self {
59:         Self {
60:             reader: Arc::new(Mutex::new(reader)),
61:             writer: Arc::new(Mutex::new(writer)),
62:         }
63:     }
64: 
65:     pub async fn read<F, R>(&self, f: F) -> DbResult<R>
66:     where
67:         F: FnOnce(&Connection) -> DbResult<R> + Send + 'static,
68:         R: Send + 'static,
69:     {
70:         let reader = Arc::clone(&self.reader);
71:         tauri::async_runtime::spawn_blocking(move || {
72:             let conn = reader.lock().map_err(|e| DbError::Lock(e.to_string()))?;
73:             f(&conn)
74:         })
75:         .await
76:         .map_err(|e| DbError::Task(format!("Database read task failed: {e}")))?
77:     }
78: 
79:     pub async fn write<F, R>(&self, f: F) -> DbResult<R>
80:     where
81:         F: FnOnce(&mut Connection) -> DbResult<R> + Send + 'static,
82:         R: Send + 'static,
83:     {
84:         let writer = Arc::clone(&self.writer);
85:         tauri::async_runtime::spawn_blocking(move || {
86:             let mut conn = writer.lock().map_err(|e| DbError::Lock(e.to_string()))?;
87:             f(&mut conn)
88:         })
89:         .await
90:         .map_err(|e| DbError::Task(format!("Database write task failed: {e}")))?
91:     }
92: 
93:     pub async fn get_local_path(&self, manga_id: &str) -> Option<String> {
94:         let id = manga_id.to_string();
95:         self.read(move |conn| {
96:             let mut stmt = conn
97:                 .prepare_cached("SELECT local_path FROM manga WHERE id = ?1")
98:                 .map_err(|e| DbError::Sqlite(e.to_string()))?;
99:             let res = stmt
100:                 .query_row([&id], |row| row.get::<_, Option<String>>(0))
101:                 .optional()
102:                 .map_err(|e| DbError::Sqlite(e.to_string()))?
103:                 .flatten();
104:             Ok(res)
105:         })
106:         .await
107:         .ok()
108:         .flatten()
109:     }
110: 
111:     pub fn get_local_path_sync(&self, manga_id: &str) -> Option<String> {
112:         let conn = self.reader.lock().ok()?;
113:         let mut stmt = conn
114:             .prepare_cached("SELECT local_path FROM manga WHERE id = ?1")
115:             .ok()?;
116:         let res: Option<String> = stmt
117:             .query_row([manga_id], |row| row.get(0))
118:             .optional()
119:             .ok()
120:             .flatten();
121:         res
122:     }
123: 
124:     pub fn get_previous_manga_for_indexing_sync(
125:         &self,
126:         target_id: Option<&str>,
127:         local_path: Option<&str>,
128:     ) -> Option<MangaRecord> {
129:         let conn = self.reader.lock().ok()?;
130:         let id_found: Option<String> = if let Some(tid) = target_id {
131:             let mut stmt = conn
132:                 .prepare_cached("SELECT id FROM manga WHERE id = ?1")
133:                 .ok()?;
134:             stmt.query_row([tid], |row| row.get(0))
135:                 .optional()
136:                 .ok()
137:                 .flatten()
138:         } else if let Some(lp) = local_path {
139:             let mut stmt = conn
140:                 .prepare_cached("SELECT id FROM manga WHERE local_path = ?1")
141:                 .ok()?;
142:             stmt.query_row([lp], |row| row.get(0))
143:                 .optional()
144:                 .ok()
145:                 .flatten()
146:         } else {
147:             None
148:         };
149: 
150:         if let Some(id) = id_found {
151:             get_manga(&conn, &id).ok().flatten()
152:         } else {
153:             None
154:         }
155:     }
156: 
157:     pub fn save_manga_sync(&self, manga: &MangaRecord) -> DbResult<()> {
158:         let mut conn = self
159:             .writer
160:             .lock()
161:             .map_err(|e| DbError::Lock(e.to_string()))?;
162:         save_manga(&mut conn, manga)
163:     }
164:     
165:     pub fn allocate_unique_slug_sync(&self, base_slug: &str, manga_id: &str) -> String {
166:         let reserved = ["read", "assets", "api"];
167:         let used: std::collections::HashSet<String> = self
168:             .reader
169:             .lock()
170:             .ok()
171:             .and_then(|conn| {
172:                 let mut stmt = conn
173:                     .prepare_cached("SELECT slug FROM manga WHERE id != ?1")
174:                     .ok()?;
175:                 let rows = stmt
176:                     .query_map([manga_id], |row| row.get::<_, String>(0))
177:                     .ok()?;
178:                 Some(rows.flatten().collect())
179:             })
180:             .unwrap_or_default();
181: 
182:         for attempt in 0..=(used.len() + reserved.len()) {
183:             let candidate = if attempt == 0 {
184:                 base_slug.to_string()
185:             } else {
186:                 format!("{}-{}", base_slug, attempt + 1)
187:             };
188:             if !used.contains(&candidate) && !reserved.contains(&candidate.as_str()) {
189:                 return candidate;
190:             }
191:         }
192:         format!("{}-{}", base_slug, uuid::Uuid::new_v4())
193:     }
194: }
