20: /// Restricts CORS headers to trusted application origins instead of wildcard *.
21: pub fn get_cors_allow_origin(req: &Request<Vec<u8>>) -> Option<&'static str> {
22:     if let Some(origin_val) = req.headers().get("origin").and_then(|v| v.to_str().ok()) {
23:         let trimmed = origin_val.trim();
24:         for &trusted in TRUSTED_ORIGINS {
25:             if trimmed.eq_ignore_ascii_case(trusted) {
26:                 return Some(trusted);
27:             }
28:         }
29:     }
30:     None
31: }
32: 
33: fn apply_cors(
34:     builder: tauri::http::response::Builder,
35:     req: &Request<Vec<u8>>,
36: ) -> tauri::http::response::Builder {
37:     if let Some(origin) = get_cors_allow_origin(req) {
38:         builder
39:             .header("Access-Control-Allow-Origin", origin)
40:             .header("Vary", "Origin")
41:     } else {
42:         builder
43:     }
44: }

46: pub fn matches_if_none_match(header_val: &str, current_etag: &str) -> bool {
47:     let clean_current = current_etag
48:         .trim()
49:         .trim_start_matches("W/")
50:         .trim()
51:         .trim_matches('"');
52:     for tag in header_val.split(',') {
53:         let tag = tag.trim();
54:         if tag == "*" {
55:             return true;
56:         }
57:         let clean_tag = tag.trim_start_matches("W/").trim().trim_matches('"');
58:         if clean_tag == clean_current {
59:             return true;
60:         }
61:     }
62:     false
63: }

65: pub fn decode_request_path(path_str: &str) -> String {
66:     let decoded = crate::commands::cache::percent_decode(path_str);
67:     #[cfg(windows)]
68:     {
69:         let trimmed = decoded.trim_start_matches('/');
70:         if trimmed.len() >= 2
71:             && trimmed.as_bytes()[1] == b':'
72:             && trimmed.as_bytes()[0].is_ascii_alphabetic()
73:         {
74:             return trimmed.to_string();
75:         }
76:     }
77:     decoded
78: }

80: /// Encodes a single path segment per RFC 3986, preserving unreserved characters
81: /// (alphanumeric, -, _, ., ~) and percent-encoding all others including spaces, #, %, and UTF-8 bytes.
82: pub fn encode_path_segment(segment: &str) -> String {
83:     let mut encoded = String::with_capacity(segment.len() * 3);
84:     for b in segment.as_bytes() {
85:         match b {
86:             b'a'..=b'z' | b'A'..=b'Z' | b'0'..=b'9' | b'-' | b'_' | b'.' | b'~' => {
87:                 encoded.push(*b as char);
88:             }
89:             _ => {
90:                 use std::fmt::Write;
91:                 let _ = write!(encoded, "%{:02X}", b);
92:             }
93:         }
94:     }
95:     encoded
96: }
97: 
98: /// Parses HTTP Range header.
99: /// Rejects suffix ranges on empty files, extra delimiters, and invalid numbers.
100: pub fn parse_range(header_val: &str, total_size: u64) -> Option<(u64, u64)> {
101:     if total_size == 0 {
102:         return None;
103:     }
104:     let trimmed = header_val.trim();
105:     if !trimmed.starts_with("bytes=") {
106:         return None;
107:     }
108:     let range_str = trimmed[6..].trim();
109:     if range_str.contains(',') {
110:         // Multi-range or trailing comma delimiters are not supported
111:         return None;
112:     }
113: 
114:     let mut bounds = range_str.split('-');
115:     let start_str = bounds.next()?.trim();
116:     let end_str = bounds.next()?.trim();
117:     if bounds.next().is_some() {
118:         // Extra hyphens (e.g., 0-10-20)
119:         return None;
120:     }
121: 
122:     if start_str.is_empty() && end_str.is_empty() {
123:         return None;
124:     }
125: 
126:     if start_str.is_empty() {
127:         if !end_str.chars().all(|c| c.is_ascii_digit()) {
128:             return None;
129:         }
130:         let suffix_len: u64 = end_str.parse().ok()?;
131:         if suffix_len == 0 {
132:             return None;
133:         }
134:         let start = total_size.saturating_sub(suffix_len);
135:         let end = total_size - 1;
136:         Some((start, end))
137:     } else {
138:         if !start_str.chars().all(|c| c.is_ascii_digit()) {
139:             return None;
140:         }
141:         let start: u64 = start_str.parse().ok()?;
142:         if start >= total_size {
143:             return None;
144:         }
145:         let end = if end_str.is_empty() {
146:             total_size - 1
147:         } else {
148:             if !end_str.chars().all(|c| c.is_ascii_digit()) {
149:                 return None;
150:             }
151:             let parsed_end: u64 = end_str.parse().ok()?;
152:             parsed_end.min(total_size - 1)
153:         };
154:         if start <= end {
155:             Some((start, end))
156:         } else {
157:             None
158:         }
159:     }
160: }
161: 
162: pub fn get_mime_type(ext: &str) -> &'static str {
163:     match ext {
164:         "jpg" | "jpeg" => "image/jpeg",
165:         "png" => "image/png",
166:         "webp" => "image/webp",
167:         "avif" => "image/avif",
168:         "gif" => "image/gif",
169:         "bmp" => "image/bmp",
170:         "svg" => "image/svg+xml",
171:         "ico" => "image/x-icon",
172:         "heic" => "image/heic",
173:         "heif" => "image/heif",
174:         "tif" | "tiff" => "image/tiff",
175:         "jxl" => "image/jxl",
176:         _ => "application/octet-stream",
177:     }
178: }

180: fn error_response(status: StatusCode, message: &str, req: &Request<Vec<u8>>) -> Response<Vec<u8>> {
181:     let mut builder = Response::builder()
182:         .status(status)
183:         .header("Content-Type", "text/plain; charset=utf-8");
184:     builder = apply_cors(builder, req);
185:     builder
186:         .body(message.as_bytes().to_vec())
187:         .unwrap_or_else(|_| Response::new(Vec::new()))
188: }

190: /// Verifies whether a path is an explicitly managed image artifact in the thumbnail or variant cache.
191: /// Prevents broad authorization of arbitrary files in the cache or data directory.
192: pub fn is_explicit_cache_artifact(app: &AppHandle, path: &Path) -> bool {
193:     let file_name = match path.file_name().and_then(|f| f.to_str()) {
194:         Some(f) => f,
195:         None => return false,
196:     };
197: 
198:     if !file_name.ends_with(".webp") {
199:         return false;
200:     }
201:     let is_thumb_or_variant = file_name.starts_with("t_") || file_name.starts_with("v_");
202:     if !is_thumb_or_variant {
203:         return false;
204:     }
205: 
206:     let parent = match path.parent() {
207:         Some(p) => p,
208:         None => return false,
209:     };
210:     let parent_name = match parent.file_name().and_then(|f| f.to_str()) {
211:         Some(p) => p,
212:         None => return false,
213:     };
214:     if parent_name != "thumbnails" && parent_name != "variants" {
215:         return false;
216:     }
217: 
218:     if let Ok(cache_dir) = app.path().app_cache_dir() {
219:         if let Ok(canonical_cache) = dunce::canonicalize(&cache_dir) {
220:             if parent.starts_with(&canonical_cache) || parent.starts_with(&cache_dir) {
221:                 return true;
222:             }
223:         } else if parent.starts_with(&cache_dir) {
224:             return true;
225:         }
226:     }
227:     if let Ok(app_dir) = app.path().app_data_dir() {
228:         let cache_dir = app_dir.join("cache");
229:         if let Ok(canonical_cache) = dunce::canonicalize(&cache_dir) {
230:             if parent.starts_with(&canonical_cache) || parent.starts_with(&cache_dir) {
231:                 return true;
232:             }
233:         } else if parent.starts_with(&cache_dir) {
234:             return true;
235:         }
236:     }
237:     false
238: }
239: 
240: pub fn is_in_thumbnail_cache(app: &AppHandle, path: &Path) -> bool {
241:     is_explicit_cache_artifact(app, path)
242: }
243: 
244: /// Constructs properly encoded mangaimg URLs where path segments are individually escaped,
245: /// ensuring Windows paths with spaces, Unicode characters, #, and % roundtrip safely without URI truncation.
246: pub fn path_to_mangaimg_url(path: &Path) -> String {
247:     let s = path.to_string_lossy().replace('\\', "/");
248:     let trimmed = s.trim_start_matches('/');
249:     if trimmed.len() >= 2
250:         && trimmed.as_bytes()[1] == b':'
251:         && trimmed.as_bytes()[0].is_ascii_alphabetic()
252:     {
253:         let drive = &trimmed[0..1];
254:         let rest = trimmed[2..].trim_start_matches('/');
255:         let segments: Vec<String> = rest
256:             .split('/')
257:             .filter(|seg| !seg.is_empty())
258:             .map(encode_path_segment)
259:             .collect();
260:         format!(
261:             "http://mangaimg.localhost/{drive}%3A/{}",
262:             segments.join("/")
263:         )
264:     } else {
265:         let segments: Vec<String> = trimmed
266:             .split('/')
267:             .filter(|seg| !seg.is_empty())
268:             .map(encode_path_segment)
269:             .collect();
270:         format!("http://mangaimg.localhost/{}", segments.join("/"))
271:     }
272: }
273: 
274: #[derive(Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
275: pub struct PageResourceParams {
276:     pub manga_id: String,
277:     pub version: String,
278:     pub transform: String,
279:     pub rel_path: String,
280: }
281: 
282: #[derive(Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
283: pub struct CoverResourceParams {
284:     pub manga_id: String,
285:     pub version: Option<String>,
286: }
287: 
288: pub fn parse_page_resource_uri(uri_path: &str) -> Option<PageResourceParams> {
289:     let rest = uri_path.strip_prefix("/page/")?;
290:     let parts: Vec<&str> = rest.splitn(4, '/').collect();
291:     if parts.len() < 4 {
292:         return None;
293:     }
294:     let manga_id = crate::commands::cache::percent_decode(parts[0]);
295:     let version = parts[1].to_string();
296:     let transform = parts[2].to_string();
297:     let rel_path = crate::commands::cache::percent_decode(parts[3]);
298:     if manga_id.is_empty() || rel_path.is_empty() {
299:         return None;
300:     }
301:     Some(PageResourceParams {
302:         manga_id,
303:         version,
304:         transform,
305:         rel_path,
306:     })
307: }
308: 
309: pub fn parse_cover_resource_uri(uri_path: &str) -> Option<CoverResourceParams> {
310:     let rest = uri_path.strip_prefix("/cover/")?;
311:     let trimmed = rest.trim_matches('/');
312:     let parts: Vec<&str> = trimmed.split('/').filter(|s| !s.is_empty()).collect();
313:     if parts.is_empty() {
314:         return None;
315:     }
316:     let manga_id = crate::commands::cache::percent_decode(parts[0]);
317:     if manga_id.is_empty() {
318:         return None;
319:     }
320:     let version = parts.get(1).map(|v| v.to_string());
321:     Some(CoverResourceParams { manga_id, version })
322: }
323: 
324: pub fn build_page_resource_url(
325:     manga_id: &str,
326:     version: &str,
327:     transform: &str,
328:     rel_path: &str,
329:     root: Option<&str>,
330: ) -> String {
331:     let enc_manga_id = encode_path_segment(manga_id);
332:     let enc_version = encode_path_segment(version);
333:     let enc_transform = encode_path_segment(transform);
334:     let clean_rel = rel_path.replace('\\', "/");
335:     let segments: Vec<String> = clean_rel
336:         .split('/')
337:         .filter(|s| !s.is_empty())
338:         .map(encode_path_segment)
339:         .collect();
340:     let enc_path = segments.join("/");
341:     let base_url = format!(
342:         "http://mangaimg.localhost/page/{}/{}/{}/{}",
343:         enc_manga_id, enc_version, enc_transform, enc_path
344:     );
345:     if let Some(r) = root {
346:         if !r.is_empty() {
347:             return format!("{}?root={}", base_url, encode_path_segment(r));
348:         }
349:     }
350:     base_url
351: }
352: 
353: pub fn build_cover_resource_url(manga_id: &str, version: Option<&str>) -> String {
354:     let enc_manga_id = encode_path_segment(manga_id);
355:     match version {
356:         Some(v) if !v.is_empty() => {
357:             format!(
358:                 "http://mangaimg.localhost/cover/{}/{}",
359:                 enc_manga_id,
360:                 encode_path_segment(v)
361:             )
362:         }
363:         _ => format!("http://mangaimg.localhost/cover/{}", enc_manga_id),
364:     }
365: }
366: 
367: pub fn parse_fit_dimensions(transform: &str) -> Option<(u32, u32)> {
368:     let rest = transform.strip_prefix("fit_")?;
369:     let mut parts = rest.split('x');
370:     let w_str = parts.next()?;
371:     let h_str = parts.next()?;
372:     let w: u32 = w_str.parse().ok()?;
373:     let h: u32 = h_str.parse().ok()?;
374:     if w == 0 || h == 0 {
375:         return None;
376:     }
377:     Some((w, h))
378: }
379: 
380: fn try_find_and_generate_cover(
381:     app: &AppHandle,
382:     base_dir: &Path,
383:     manga_id: &str,
384: ) -> Result<PathBuf, String> {
385:     if !base_dir.is_dir() {
386:         return Err("Base directory does not exist".into());
387:     }
388: 
389:     // Common cover filenames
390:     for name in &[
391:         "cover.jpg",
392:         "cover.jpeg",
393:         "cover.png",
394:         "cover.webp",
395:         "thumbnail.jpg",
396:         "thumbnail.jpeg",
397:         "thumbnail.png",
398:         "thumbnail.webp",
399:         "folder.jpg",
400:     ] {
401:         let candidate = base_dir.join(name);
402:         if candidate.is_file() {
403:             return crate::commands::image::generate_thumbnail_direct(app, &candidate, manga_id);
404:         }
405:     }
406: 
407:     if let Ok(entries) = std::fs::read_dir(base_dir) {
408:         let mut files: Vec<PathBuf> = entries
409:             .flatten()
410:             .map(|e| e.path())
411:             .filter(|p| p.is_file())
412:             .collect();
413:         files.sort_by(|a, b| {
414:             crate::commands::fs::natural_order(&a.to_string_lossy(), &b.to_string_lossy())
415:         });
416:         for path in files {
417:             if let Some(ext) = path.extension().and_then(|e| e.to_str()) {
418:                 if ALLOWED_IMAGE_EXTENSIONS.contains(&ext.to_lowercase().as_str()) {
419:                     return crate::commands::image::generate_thumbnail_direct(app, &path, manga_id);
420:                 }
421:             }
422:         }
423:     }
424: 
425:     if let Ok(entries) = std::fs::read_dir(base_dir) {
426:         let mut subdirs: Vec<PathBuf> = entries
427:             .flatten()
428:             .map(|e| e.path())
429:             .filter(|p| p.is_dir() && !crate::commands::fs::is_ignored_directory(p))
430:             .collect();
431:         subdirs.sort_by(|a, b| {
432:             crate::commands::fs::natural_order(&a.to_string_lossy(), &b.to_string_lossy())
433:         });
434:         for subdir in subdirs {
435:             if let Ok(sub_entries) = std::fs::read_dir(&subdir) {
436:                 let mut files: Vec<PathBuf> = sub_entries
437:                     .flatten()
438:                     .map(|e| e.path())
439:                     .filter(|p| p.is_file())
440:                     .collect();
441:                 files.sort_by(|a, b| {
442:                     crate::commands::fs::natural_order(&a.to_string_lossy(), &b.to_string_lossy())
443:                 });
444:                 for f in files {
445:                     if let Some(ext) = f.extension().and_then(|e| e.to_str()) {
446:                         if ALLOWED_IMAGE_EXTENSIONS.contains(&ext.to_lowercase().as_str()) {
447:                             return crate::commands::image::generate_thumbnail_direct(
448:                                 app, &f, manga_id,
449:                             );
450:                         }
451:                     }
452:                 }
453:             }
454:         }
455:     }
456: 
457:     Err("No image found to generate cover".into())
458: }
459: 
460: fn safe_name(s: &str) -> bool {
461:     !s.is_empty() && !s.contains(['/', '\\', ':']) && !s.contains("..")
462: }
463: 
464: pub fn handle_mangaimg_request(app: &AppHandle, req: Request<Vec<u8>>) -> Response<Vec<u8>> {
465:     // 0. Method restriction: only GET and HEAD are permitted
466:     if req.method() != "GET" && req.method() != "HEAD" {
467:         let mut builder = Response::builder()
468:             .status(StatusCode::METHOD_NOT_ALLOWED)
469:             .header("Allow", "GET, HEAD")
470:             .header("Content-Type", "text/plain; charset=utf-8");
471:         builder = apply_cors(builder, &req);
472:         return builder
473:             .body(
474:                 "Method not allowed. Only GET and HEAD are supported."
475:                     .as_bytes()
476:                     .to_vec(),
477:             )
478:             .unwrap_or_else(|_| Response::new(Vec::new()));
479:     }
480: 
481:     let uri_path = req.uri().path();
482: 
483:     // Support resource URLs (/cover/, /page/), opaque image ID endpoints, and canonical path requests
484:     let raw_target: PathBuf = if let Some(cover_params) = parse_cover_resource_uri(uri_path) {
485:         let safe_id = crate::commands::cache::sanitize_id(&cover_params.manga_id);
486:         let cache_dir = app
487:             .path()
488:             .app_cache_dir()
489:             .unwrap_or_else(|_| PathBuf::from("cache"));
490:         let thumb_path = cache_dir.join("thumbnails").join(format!("{safe_id}.webp"));
491: 
492:         if thumb_path.exists() {
493:             thumb_path
494:         } else {
495:             let local_path_opt = app
496:                 .try_state::<crate::db::DbState>()
497:                 .and_then(|db| db.get_local_path_sync(&cover_params.manga_id));
498:             if let Some(ref lp) = local_path_opt {
499:                 let base = PathBuf::from(lp);
500:                 match try_find_and_generate_cover(app, &base, &cover_params.manga_id) {
501:                     Ok(p) => p,
502:                     Err(_) => {
503:                         return error_response(
504:                             StatusCode::NOT_FOUND,
505:                             "Cover image not found",
506:                             &req,
507:                         );
508:                     }
509:                 }
510:             } else {
511:                 return error_response(StatusCode::NOT_FOUND, "Cover thumbnail not found", &req);
512:             }
513:         }
514:     } else if let Some(page_params) = parse_page_resource_uri(uri_path) {
515:         if let Err(e) = crate::validation::validate_relative_path(&page_params.rel_path) {
516:             return error_response(
517:                 StatusCode::BAD_REQUEST,
518:                 &format!("Invalid relative path in page URL: {e}"),
519:                 &req,
520:             );
521:         }
522: 
523:         let query_root = req.uri().query().and_then(|q| {
524:             q.split('&').find_map(|pair| {
525:                 pair.strip_prefix("root=")
526:                     .map(crate::commands::cache::percent_decode)
527:             })
528:         });
529: 
530:         let base_dir_str = match query_root {
531:             Some(ref r) if !r.trim().is_empty() => Some(r.clone()),
532:             _ => app
533:                 .try_state::<crate::db::DbState>()
534:                 .and_then(|db| db.get_local_path_sync(&page_params.manga_id)),
535:         };
536: 
537:         let base_dir_str = match base_dir_str {
538:             Some(s) => s,
539:             None => {
540:                 return error_response(
541:                     StatusCode::NOT_FOUND,
542:                     &format!(
543:                         "Manga '{}' not found or localPath unavailable",
544:                         page_params.manga_id
545:                     ),
546:                     &req,
547:                 );
548:             }
549:         };
550: 
551:         let base_dir = PathBuf::from(base_dir_str.trim());
552:         let allowed = app.state::<AllowedRoots>();
553:         if !allowed.is_allowed(&base_dir) {
554:             return error_response(
555:                 StatusCode::FORBIDDEN,
556:                 "Base root is not under an authorized library root",
557:                 &req,
558:             );
559:         }
560:         let resolved =
561:             match crate::commands::fs::resolve_relative_path(&base_dir, &page_params.rel_path) {
562:                 Ok(p) => p,
563:                 Err(e) => {
564:                     return error_response(
565:                         StatusCode::NOT_FOUND,
566:                         &format!("Page file not found: {e}"),
567:                         &req,
568:                     );
569:                 }
570:             };
571: 
572:         if !allowed.is_allowed(&resolved) {
573:             return error_response(
574:                 StatusCode::FORBIDDEN,
575:                 "Path is not under an authorized library root",
576:                 &req,
577:             );
578:         }
579: 
580:         match page_params.transform.as_str() {
581:             "raw" | "full" | "original" => resolved,
582:             "thumb" => {
583:                 match crate::commands::image::generate_image_variant_direct(
584:                     app, &resolved, 320, 420,
585:                 ) {
586:                     Ok(variant_path) => variant_path,
587:                     Err(_) => resolved,
588:                 }
589:             }
590:             "preview" | "low" => {
591:                 match crate::commands::image::generate_image_variant_direct(
592:                     app, &resolved, 1000, 1600,
593:                 ) {
594:                     Ok(variant_path) => variant_path,
595:                     Err(_) => resolved,
596:                 }
597:             }
598:             fit if fit.starts_with("fit_") => {
599:                 let (w, h) = parse_fit_dimensions(fit).unwrap_or((1000, 1600));
600:                 match crate::commands::image::generate_image_variant_direct(app, &resolved, w, h) {
601:                     Ok(variant_path) => variant_path,
602:                     Err(_) => resolved,
603:                 }
604:             }
605:             _ => resolved,
606:         }
607:     } else if let Some(id_part) = uri_path.strip_prefix("/thumb/") {
608:         let decoded = crate::commands::cache::percent_decode(id_part);
609:         if !safe_name(&decoded) {
610:             return error_response(StatusCode::BAD_REQUEST, "Bad name", &req);
611:         }
612:         let filename = if decoded.starts_with("t_") && decoded.ends_with(".webp") {
613:             decoded
614:         } else {
615:             let safe_id = crate::commands::cache::sanitize_id(&decoded);
616:             format!("{safe_id}.webp")
617:         };
618:         let cache_dir = app
619:             .path()
620:             .app_cache_dir()
621:             .unwrap_or_else(|_| PathBuf::from("cache"));
622:         cache_dir.join("thumbnails").join(filename)
623:     } else if let Some(hash_part) = uri_path.strip_prefix("/variant/") {
624:         let decoded = crate::commands::cache::percent_decode(hash_part);
625:         if !safe_name(&decoded) {
626:             return error_response(StatusCode::BAD_REQUEST, "Bad name", &req);
627:         }
628:         let filename = if decoded.ends_with(".webp") {
629:             decoded
630:         } else {
631:             format!("{decoded}.webp")
632:         };
633:         let cache_dir = app
634:             .path()
635:             .app_cache_dir()
636:             .unwrap_or_else(|_| PathBuf::from("cache"));
637:         cache_dir.join("variants").join(filename)
638:     } else {
639:         let decoded_path = decode_request_path(uri_path);
640:         if decoded_path.is_empty() {
641:             return error_response(StatusCode::BAD_REQUEST, "Missing file path", &req);
642:         }
643:         PathBuf::from(&decoded_path)
644:     };
645: 
646:     let resolved = match dunce::canonicalize(&raw_target) {
647:         Ok(p) => p,
648:         Err(_) => return error_response(StatusCode::NOT_FOUND, "Target file not found", &req),
649:     };
650: 
651:     // 1. Path allowlist checks (AllowedRoots or explicit cache artifact)
652:     let allowed = app.state::<AllowedRoots>();
653:     let is_allowed = allowed.is_allowed(&resolved) || is_explicit_cache_artifact(app, &resolved);
654:     if !is_allowed {
655:         return error_response(
656:             StatusCode::FORBIDDEN,
657:             "Path is not under an allowed library root or managed cache",
658:             &req,
659:         );
660:     }
661: 
662:     // 2. Extension check
663:     let ext = resolved
664:         .extension()
665:         .and_then(|s| s.to_str())
666:         .unwrap_or("")
667:         .to_lowercase();
668:     if !ALLOWED_IMAGE_EXTENSIONS.contains(&ext.as_str()) {
669:         return error_response(
670:             StatusCode::FORBIDDEN,
671:             "File extension is not an allowed image format",
672:             &req,
673:         );
674:     }
675: 
676:     // 3. Regular file and size check (100 MiB cap)
677:     // Open the descriptor once to eliminate TOCTOU race conditions between inspection and read.
678:     let mut file = match File::open(&resolved) {
679:         Ok(f) => f,
680:         Err(e) => {
681:             return error_response(
682:                 StatusCode::NOT_FOUND,
683:                 &format!("Failed to open file: {e}"),
684:                 &req,
685:             );
686:         }
687:     };
688: 
689:     let metadata = match file.metadata() {
690:         Ok(m) => m,
691:         Err(_) => {
692:             return error_response(
693:                 StatusCode::NOT_FOUND,
694:                 "Failed to inspect file metadata",
695:                 &req,
696:             );
697:         }
698:     };
699: 
700:     if !metadata.is_file() {
701:         return error_response(
702:             StatusCode::NOT_FOUND,
703:             "Target path is not a regular file",
704:             &req,
705:         );
706:     }
707: 
708:     let initial_size = metadata.len();
709:     if initial_size > MAX_FILE_READ_BYTES {
710:         return error_response(
711:             StatusCode::PAYLOAD_TOO_LARGE,
712:             "File exceeds 100 MiB safety limit",
713:             &req,
714:         );
715:     }
716: 
717:     let mtime_ms = metadata
718:         .modified()
719:         .ok()
720:         .and_then(|t| t.duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH).ok())
721:         .map(|d| d.as_millis())
722:         .unwrap_or(0);
723: 
724:     // Format as weak validator per RFC 9110 Section 8.8.3
725:     let etag = format!("W/\"{:x}-{:x}\"", mtime_ms, initial_size);
726:     let mime = get_mime_type(&ext);
727: 
728:     // 4. Conditional GET via ETag (304 Not Modified)
729:     if let Some(if_none_match) = req.headers().get("if-none-match") {
730:         if let Ok(val) = if_none_match.to_str() {
731:             if matches_if_none_match(val, &etag) {
732:                 let mut builder = Response::builder()
733:                     .status(StatusCode::NOT_MODIFIED)
734:                     .header("ETag", &etag)
735:                     .header("Cache-Control", "no-cache");
736:                 builder = apply_cors(builder, &req);
737:                 return builder
738:                     .body(Vec::new())
739:                     .unwrap_or_else(|_| Response::new(Vec::new()));
740:             }
741:         }
742:     }
743: 
744:     let is_head = req.method() == "HEAD";
745: 
746:     // 5. HTTP Range request handling (206 Partial Content) with 8 MiB chunk capping
747:     if let Some(range_header) = req.headers().get("range") {
748:         if let Ok(range_str) = range_header.to_str() {
749:             if let Some((start, end)) = parse_range(range_str, initial_size) {
750:                 let capped_end = end.min(start.saturating_add(MAX_RANGE_CHUNK_BYTES - 1));
751:                 let length = capped_end - start + 1;
752:                 let body = if is_head {
753:                     Vec::new()
754:                 } else {
755:                     if let Err(e) = file.seek(SeekFrom::Start(start)) {
756:                         return error_response(
757:                             StatusCode::INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR,
758:                             &format!("Seek error: {e}"),
759:                             &req,
760:                         );
761:                     }
762:                     let mut buffer = vec![0u8; length as usize];
763:                     if let Err(e) = file.read_exact(&mut buffer) {
764:                         return error_response(
765:                             StatusCode::INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR,
766:                             &format!("Read error: {e}"),
767:                             &req,
768:                         );
769:                     }
770:                     buffer
771:                 };
772: 
773:                 let mut builder = Response::builder()
774:                     .status(StatusCode::PARTIAL_CONTENT)
775:                     .header("Content-Type", mime)
776:                     .header("Content-Length", length.to_string())
777:                     .header(
778:                         "Content-Range",
779:                         format!("bytes {}-{}/{}", start, capped_end, initial_size),
780:                     )
781:                     .header("Accept-Ranges", "bytes")
782:                     .header("ETag", &etag)
783:                     .header("Cache-Control", "no-cache");
784:                 builder = apply_cors(builder, &req);
785:                 return builder
786:                     .body(body)
787:                     .unwrap_or_else(|_| Response::new(Vec::new()));
788:             } else {
789:                 let mut builder = Response::builder()
790:                     .status(StatusCode::RANGE_NOT_SATISFIABLE)
791:                     .header("Content-Range", format!("bytes */{}", initial_size));
792:                 builder = apply_cors(builder, &req);
793:                 return builder
794:                     .body(Vec::new())
795:                     .unwrap_or_else(|_| Response::new(Vec::new()));
796:             }
797:         }
798:     }
799: 
800:     // 6. Full content response (200 OK)
801:     let body = if is_head {
802:         Vec::new()
803:     } else {
804:         // Enforce the 100 MiB safety limit against the opened descriptor using take()
805:         let mut buffer = Vec::with_capacity(initial_size.min(MAX_FILE_READ_BYTES) as usize);
806:         let mut reader = (&mut file).take(MAX_FILE_READ_BYTES + 1);
807:         if let Err(e) = reader.read_to_end(&mut buffer) {
808:             return error_response(
809:                 StatusCode::INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR,
810:                 &format!("Failed to read file: {e}"),
811:                 &req,
812:             );
813:         }
814:         if buffer.len() as u64 > MAX_FILE_READ_BYTES {
815:             return error_response(
816:                 StatusCode::PAYLOAD_TOO_LARGE,
817:                 "File exceeds 100 MiB safety limit",
818:                 &req,
819:             );
820:         }
821:         buffer
822:     };
823: 
824:     let actual_size = if is_head {
825:         initial_size
826:     } else {
827:         body.len() as u64
828:     };
829:     let mut builder = Response::builder()
830:         .status(StatusCode::OK)
831:         .header("Content-Type", mime)
832:         .header("Content-Length", actual_size.to_string())
833:         .header("Accept-Ranges", "bytes")
834:         .header("ETag", &etag)
835:         .header("Cache-Control", "no-cache");
836:     builder = apply_cors(builder, &req);
837:     builder
838:         .body(body)
839:         .unwrap_or_else(|_| Response::new(Vec::new()))
840: }

873:     fn test_parse_range() {
874:         let total = 1000;
875:         // Standard range
876:         assert_eq!(parse_range("bytes=0-499", total), Some((0, 499)));
877:         // Open ended
878:         assert_eq!(parse_range("bytes=500-", total), Some((500, 999)));
879:         // Suffix range
880:         assert_eq!(parse_range("bytes=-200", total), Some((800, 999)));
881:         // Clamping end
882:         assert_eq!(parse_range("bytes=0-2000", total), Some((0, 999)));
883:         // Out of bounds
884:         assert_eq!(parse_range("bytes=1000-", total), None);
885:         assert_eq!(parse_range("bytes=1500-2000", total), None);
886:         // Invalid syntax
887:         assert_eq!(parse_range("items=0-10", total), None);
888:     }

135: pub const RESERVED_DOS_NAMES: &[&str] = &[
136:     "con", "prn", "aux", "nul", "com1", "com2", "com3", "com4", "com5", "com6", "com7", "com8",
137:     "com9", "lpt1", "lpt2", "lpt3", "lpt4", "lpt5", "lpt6", "lpt7", "lpt8", "lpt9",
138: ];
139: 
140: pub fn validate_relative_path(path: &str) -> Result<String, String> {
141:     if path.starts_with(' ') || path.ends_with(' ') {
142:         return Err(format!(
143:             "Path cannot start or end with whitespace: \"{path}\""
144:         ));
145:     }
146:     let trimmed = path.trim();
147:     if trimmed.is_empty() {
148:         return Err("Path must be a non-empty string.".to_string());
149:     }
150:     if trimmed.len() > LIMIT_PATH_CHARS {
151:         return Err(format!(
152:             "Relative path length (characters) exceeds the limit of {LIMIT_PATH_CHARS}. Use a smaller selection."
153:         ));
154:     }
155:     // Reject drive prefixes, alternate data streams, and URI schemes
156:     if trimmed.contains(':') {
157:         return Err(format!(
158:             "Path contains invalid colon or drive/stream prefix: \"{trimmed}\""
159:         ));
160:     }
161:     // Reject rooted paths, backslashes, and UNC/device syntax
162:     if trimmed.starts_with('/') || trimmed.ends_with('/') {
163:         return Err(format!(
164:             "Path cannot start or end with a slash: \"{trimmed}\""
165:         ));
166:     }
167:     if trimmed.contains('\\') {
168:         return Err(format!("Path must use forward slashes only: \"{trimmed}\""));
169:     }
170:     if trimmed.starts_with("//") {
171:         return Err(format!("Path cannot start with UNC prefix: \"{trimmed}\""));
172:     }
173:     if trimmed.contains('\0') {
174:         return Err("Path contains invalid null byte.".to_string());
175:     }
176:     if trimmed.chars().any(|c| c.is_control()) {
177:         return Err("Path contains invalid control character.".to_string());
178:     }
179: 
180:     let segments: Vec<&str> = trimmed.split('/').collect();
181:     if segments.len().saturating_sub(1) > LIMIT_DIRECTORY_DEPTH {
182:         return Err(format!(
183:             "Folder depth exceeds the limit of {LIMIT_DIRECTORY_DEPTH}. Use a smaller selection."
184:         ));
185:     }
186:     for seg in &segments {
187:         if seg.is_empty() || *seg == "." || *seg == ".." {
188:             return Err(format!(
189:                 "Path contains invalid traversal segment: \"{trimmed}\""
190:             ));
191:         }
192:         if seg.ends_with(' ') || seg.ends_with('.') {
193:             return Err(format!(
194:                 "Path segment cannot end with a space or dot: \"{seg}\""
195:             ));
196:         }
197:         let stem = seg.split('.').next().unwrap_or("").to_lowercase();
198:         if RESERVED_DOS_NAMES.contains(&stem.as_str()) {
199:             return Err(format!("Path segment uses reserved device name: \"{seg}\""));
200:         }
201:     }
202:     Ok(trimmed.to_string())
203: }
204: 
205: pub fn assert_image_dimensions(width: u32, height: u32) -> Result<(), String> {
206:     if width == 0 || height == 0 {
207:         return Err("Image dimensions must be positive integer pixels.".to_string());
208:     }
209:     let area = (width as u64).checked_mul(height as u64);
210:     match area {
211:         Some(a) if a <= 9_007_199_254_740_991 => Ok(()),
212:         _ => Err("Image area cannot be represented safely.".to_string()),
213:     }
214: }
215: 
216: pub fn validate_page(
217:     page: &mut ValidatedPage,
218:     seen_paths: &mut HashSet<String>,
219: ) -> Result<(), String> {
220:     let trimmed_name = page.name.trim();
221:     if trimmed_name.is_empty() {
222:         return Err("Page name must be a non-empty string.".to_string());
223:     }
224:     page.name = trimmed_name.to_string();
225: 
226:     let valid_path = validate_relative_path(&page.path)?;
227:     if seen_paths.contains(&valid_path) {
228:         return Err(format!(
229:             "Duplicate page path within chapter: \"{valid_path}\""
230:         ));
231:     }
232:     seen_paths.insert(valid_path.clone());
233:     page.path = valid_path;
234: 
235:     let is_pending_zero =
236:         page.dimensions_pending == Some(true) && page.width == 0 && page.height == 0;
237:     if !is_pending_zero {
238:         assert_image_dimensions(page.width, page.height)?;
239:     }
240: 
241:     if let Some(m) = page.modified {
242:         if !m.is_finite() || m < 0.0 {
243:             return Err("Page modified timestamp must be a non-negative number.".to_string());
244:         }
245:     }
246: 
247:     Ok(())
248: }
249: 
250: pub fn validate_position(pos: &mut ValidatedPosition, pages_count: usize) -> Result<(), String> {
251:     if pages_count == 0 {
252:         return Err("Position cannot be resolved against an empty chapter.".to_string());
253:     }
254: 
255:     if !pos.fraction.is_finite() {
256:         return Err("Position fraction must be a finite number.".to_string());
257:     }
258:     pos.fraction = pos.fraction.clamp(0.0, 1.0);
259: 
260:     if let Some(p) = &pos.path {
261:         pos.path = Some(validate_relative_path(p)?);
262:     }
263: 
264:     if let Some(ts) = pos.timestamp {
265:         if !ts.is_finite() {
266:             return Err("Position timestamp must be a finite number.".to_string());
267:         }
268:     }
269: 
270:     if (pos.page as usize) >= pages_count {
271:         pos.page = (pages_count - 1) as u32;
272:     }
273: 
274:     Ok(())
275: }
276: 
277: pub fn validate_chapter(
278:     chapter: &mut ValidatedChapter,
279:     chapter_index: usize,
280: ) -> Result<usize, String> {
281:     let trimmed_name = chapter.name.trim();
282:     if trimmed_name.is_empty() {
283:         return Err(format!(
284:             "Chapter {} name must be a non-empty string.",
285:             chapter_index + 1
286:         ));
287:     }
288:     chapter.name = trimmed_name.to_string();
289: 
290:     if chapter.pages.is_empty() {
291:         return Err(format!(
292:             "Chapter \"{}\" must contain at least one page.",
293:             chapter.name
294:         ));
295:     }
296: 
297:     let mut seen_paths = HashSet::new();
298:     for (idx, page) in chapter.pages.iter_mut().enumerate() {
299:         validate_page(page, &mut seen_paths).map_err(|e| {
300:             format!(
301:                 "Chapter \"{}\" page {} is invalid: {e}",
302:                 chapter.name,
303:                 idx + 1
304:             )
305:         })?;
306:     }
307: 
308:     let page_count = chapter.pages.len();
309: 
310:     if let Some(pos) = &mut chapter.position {
311:         validate_position(pos, page_count)?;
312:     }
313: 
314:     if let Some(bms) = &mut chapter.bookmarks {
315:         if bms.len() > LIMIT_BOOKMARKS_PER_CHAPTER {
316:             return Err(format!(
317:                 "Bookmarks per chapter exceeds the limit of {LIMIT_BOOKMARKS_PER_CHAPTER}. Use a smaller selection."
318:             ));
319:         }
320: 
321:         let mut seen_bms = HashSet::new();
322:         let mut valid_bms = Vec::new();
323:         for bm in bms.iter_mut() {
324:             if validate_position(bm, page_count).is_ok() {
325:                 let key = format!(
326:                     "{}:{}:{}",
327:                     bm.page,
328:                     bm.path.as_deref().unwrap_or(""),
329:                     bm.manga_bookmark.unwrap_or(false)
330:                 );
331:                 if seen_bms.insert(key) {
332:                     valid_bms.push(bm.clone());
333:                 }
334:             }
335:         }
336:         *bms = valid_bms;
337:     }
338: 
339:     Ok(page_count)
340: }
341: 
342: pub fn validate_manga(
343:     manga: &mut ValidatedManga,
344:     seen_ids: &mut HashSet<String>,
345: ) -> Result<usize, String> {
346:     let trimmed_id = manga.id.trim();
347:     if trimmed_id.is_empty() {
348:         return Err("Manga ID must be a non-empty string.".to_string());
349:     }
350:     if !seen_ids.insert(trimmed_id.to_string()) {
351:         return Err(format!(
352:             "Duplicate manga ID found in backup: \"{trimmed_id}\""
353:         ));
354:     }
355:     manga.id = trimmed_id.to_string();
356: 
357:     let trimmed_title = manga.title.trim();
358:     if trimmed_title.is_empty() {
359:         return Err("Manga title must be a non-empty string.".to_string());
360:     }
361:     manga.title = trimmed_title.to_string();
362: 
363:     if manga.chapters.is_empty() {
364:         return Err(format!(
365:             "Manga \"{}\" must contain at least one chapter.",
366:             manga.title
367:         ));
368:     }
369:     if manga.chapters.len() > LIMIT_CHAPTERS_PER_TITLE {
370:         return Err(format!(
371:             "Chapters per title exceeds the limit of {LIMIT_CHAPTERS_PER_TITLE}. Use a smaller selection."
372:         ));
373:     }
374: 
375:     let mut total_pages = 0;
376:     for (idx, ch) in manga.chapters.iter_mut().enumerate() {
377:         let ch_pages = validate_chapter(ch, idx)?;
378:         total_pages += ch_pages;
379:         if total_pages > LIMIT_PAGES_PER_TITLE {
380:             return Err(format!(
381:                 "Pages per title exceeds the limit of {LIMIT_PAGES_PER_TITLE}. Use a smaller selection."
382:             ));
383:         }
384:     }
385: 
386:     let chapter_count = manga.chapters.len();
387:     let last_chapter = manga.last_chapter.unwrap_or(0);
388:     manga.last_chapter = Some(last_chapter.min(chapter_count.saturating_sub(1)));
389: 
390:     let now = std::time::SystemTime::now()
391:         .duration_since(std::time::UNIX_EPOCH)
392:         .unwrap_or_default()
393:         .as_millis() as f64;
394: 
395:     manga.added_at = Some(
396:         manga
397:             .added_at
398:             .filter(|t| t.is_finite() && *t > 0.0)
399:             .unwrap_or(now),
400:     );
401:     manga.updated_at = Some(
402:         manga
403:             .updated_at
404:             .filter(|t| t.is_finite() && *t > 0.0)
405:             .unwrap_or(now),
406:     );
407: 
408:     if let Some(sort) = &manga.sort {
409:         if sort != "name" && sort != "reverse" && sort != "natural" {
410:             manga.sort = None;
411:         }
412:     }
413: 
414:     if manga.slug.as_ref().is_none_or(|s| s.trim().is_empty()) {
415:         manga.slug = Some(crate::commands::indexing::generate_slug(&manga.title));
416:     }
417: 
418:     Ok(total_pages)
419: }
420: 
421: pub fn normalize_settings(settings: &ValidatedSettings) -> serde_json::Value {
422:     let theme = match settings.theme.as_deref() {
423:         Some("light") => "light",
424:         Some("dark") => "dark",
425:         _ => "system",
426:     };
427:     let mode = match settings.mode.as_deref() {
428:         Some("single") => "single",
429:         _ => "continuous",
430:     };
431:     let layout = match settings.layout.as_deref() {
432:         Some("width") => "width",
433:         Some("height") => "height",
434:         Some("auto") => "auto",
435:         _ => "max",
436:     };
437:     let max_width = match settings.max_width {
438:         Some(920) | Some(1024) | Some(1280) | Some(0) => settings.max_width.unwrap(),
439:         _ => 920,
440:     };
441:     let seconds = settings
442:         .seconds
443:         .filter(|s| s.is_finite())
444:         .map(|s| s.clamp(0.5, 120.0))
445:         .unwrap_or(3.0);
446:     let advance = settings.advance.unwrap_or(true);
447:     let reduced = settings.reduced.unwrap_or(false);
448:     let low_quality = settings.low_quality.unwrap_or(false);
449:     let custom_seconds = settings
450:         .custom_seconds
451:         .unwrap_or_else(|| ![2.0, 3.0, 4.0, 5.0].contains(&seconds));
452:     let auto_hide_bars = settings.auto_hide_bars.unwrap_or(true);
453:     let resume_delay = settings
454:         .resume_delay
455:         .filter(|r| r.is_finite())
456:         .map(|r| r.clamp(0.5, 15.0))
457:         .unwrap_or(1.8);
458:     let direction = match settings.direction.as_deref() {
459:         Some("rtl") => "rtl",
460:         _ => "ltr",
461:     };
462:     let spread = settings.spread.unwrap_or(false);
463:     let single_cover = settings.single_cover.unwrap_or(true);
464:     let end_action = match settings.end_action.as_deref() {
465:         Some("stop") => "stop",
466:         Some("replay") => "replay",
467:         Some("next") => "next",
468:         _ => {
469:             if !advance {
470:                 "stop"
471:             } else {
472:                 "next"
473:             }
474:         }
475:     };
476:     let font_size_title = settings
477:         .font_size_title
478:         .filter(|f| f.is_finite())
479:         .map(|f| f.round().clamp(10.0, 36.0) as u32)
480:         .unwrap_or(15);
481:     let font_size_ui = settings
482:         .font_size_ui
483:         .filter(|f| f.is_finite())
484:         .map(|f| f.round().clamp(10.0, 36.0) as u32)
485:         .unwrap_or(15);
486:     let font_size_button = settings
487:         .font_size_button
488:         .filter(|f| f.is_finite())
489:         .map(|f| f.round().clamp(10.0, 36.0) as u32)
490:         .unwrap_or(15);
491:     let confirm_delete = settings.confirm_delete.unwrap_or(false);
492: 
493:     let mut map = serde_json::Map::new();
494:     map.insert("theme".into(), theme.into());
495:     map.insert("mode".into(), mode.into());
496:     map.insert("layout".into(), layout.into());
497:     map.insert("maxWidth".into(), max_width.into());
498:     map.insert("seconds".into(), seconds.into());
499:     map.insert("advance".into(), advance.into());
500:     map.insert("reduced".into(), reduced.into());
501:     map.insert("lowQuality".into(), low_quality.into());
502:     map.insert("customSeconds".into(), custom_seconds.into());
503:     map.insert("autoHideBars".into(), auto_hide_bars.into());
504:     map.insert("resumeDelay".into(), resume_delay.into());
505:     map.insert("direction".into(), direction.into());
506:     map.insert("spread".into(), spread.into());
507:     map.insert("singleCover".into(), single_cover.into());
508:     map.insert("endAction".into(), end_action.into());
509:     map.insert("fontSizeTitle".into(), font_size_title.into());
510:     map.insert("fontSizeUi".into(), font_size_ui.into());
511:     map.insert("fontSizeButton".into(), font_size_button.into());
512:     map.insert("confirmDelete".into(), confirm_delete.into());
513: 
514:     if let Some(sort) = &settings.shelf_sort {
515:         if sort == "recent" || sort == "title" || sort == "added" || sort == "progress" {
516:             map.insert("shelfSort".into(), sort.as_str().into());
517:         }
518:     }
519: 
520:     serde_json::Value::Object(map)
521: }
522: 
523: pub fn validate_backup_json(json_str: &str) -> Result<BackupPayload, String> {
524:     if json_str.len() > LIMIT_BACKUP_BYTES {
525:         return Err(format!(
526:             "Backup size (bytes) exceeds the limit of {LIMIT_BACKUP_BYTES}. Use a smaller selection."
527:         ));
528:     }
529: 
530:     let mut payload: BackupPayload = serde_json::from_str(json_str)
531:         .map_err(|e| format!("Invalid backup JSON structure: {e}"))?;
532: 
533:     if payload.version != 1 && payload.version != 2 {
534:         return Err("Invalid backup version. Supported versions: 1, 2.".to_string());
535:     }
536: 
537:     if payload.manga.len() > LIMIT_TITLES {
538:         return Err(format!(
539:             "Backup titles exceeds the limit of {LIMIT_TITLES}. Use a smaller selection."
540:         ));
541:     }
542: 
543:     let mut seen_ids = HashSet::new();
544:     let mut total_backup_pages = 0;
545: 
546:     for manga in payload.manga.iter_mut() {
547:         let pages = validate_manga(manga, &mut seen_ids)?;
548:         total_backup_pages += pages;
549:         if total_backup_pages > LIMIT_BACKUP_PAGES {
550:             return Err(format!(
551:                 "Backup pages exceeds the limit of {LIMIT_BACKUP_PAGES}. Use a smaller selection."
552:             ));
553:         }
554:     }
555: 
556:     Ok(payload)
557: }

559: #[cfg(test)]
560: mod tests {
561:     use super::*;
562: 
563:     #[test]
564:     fn test_validate_relative_path_traversal() {
565:         assert!(validate_relative_path("ch1/01.jpg").is_ok());
566:         assert!(validate_relative_path("ch1/chapter..jpg").is_ok());
567:         assert!(validate_relative_path("../ch1/01.jpg").is_err());
568:         assert!(validate_relative_path("/ch1/01.jpg").is_err());
569:         assert!(validate_relative_path("ch1/01.jpg/").is_err());
570:         assert!(validate_relative_path("ch1\\01.jpg").is_err());
571:         assert!(validate_relative_path("ch1/./01.jpg").is_err());
572:         assert!(validate_relative_path("ch1/../01.jpg").is_err());
573:         assert!(validate_relative_path("ch1/\001.jpg").is_err());
574:         // Windows prefix and stream security
575:         assert!(validate_relative_path("C:/ch1/01.jpg").is_err());
576:         assert!(validate_relative_path("ch1/01.jpg:stream").is_err());
577:         assert!(validate_relative_path("//server/share/01.jpg").is_err());
578:         assert!(validate_relative_path("nul.jpg").is_err());
579:         assert!(validate_relative_path("con").is_err());
580:         assert!(validate_relative_path("ch1./01.jpg").is_err());
581:         assert!(validate_relative_path("ch1/01.jpg.").is_err());
582:         assert!(validate_relative_path("ch1/01.jpg ").is_err());
583:         assert!(validate_relative_path(" ch1/01.jpg").is_err());
584:     }
585: 
586:     #[test]
587:     fn test_deny_unknown_fields_on_backup() {
588:         let json = r#"{
589:             "version": 2,
590:             "manga": [],
591:             "settings": {},
592:             "injectedKey": "malicious"
593:         }"#;
594:         assert!(validate_backup_json(json).is_err());
595:     }
596: 
597:     #[test]
598:     fn test_valid_backup_json_roundtrip() {
599:         let json = r#"{
600:             "version": 2,
601:             "manga": [
602:                 {
603:                     "id": "title-1",
604:                     "title": "Title 1",
605:                     "chapters": [
606:                         {
607:                             "name": "Chapter 1",
608:                             "pages": [
609:                                 { "name": "01.jpg", "path": "ch1/01.jpg", "width": 800, "height": 1200 }
610:                             ]
611:                         }
612:                     ]
613:                 }
614:             ],
615:             "settings": {
616:                 "theme": "dark",
617:                 "mode": "continuous"
618:             }
619:         }"#;
620: 
621:         let res = validate_backup_json(json);
622:         assert!(res.is_ok());
623:         let payload = res.unwrap();
624:         assert_eq!(payload.manga.len(), 1);
625:         assert_eq!(payload.manga[0].id, "title-1");
626:         assert_eq!(payload.manga[0].chapters[0].pages[0].width, 800);
627:     }

688:     #[test]
689:     fn test_backup_preserves_all_supported_fields_and_pending_dimensions() {
690:         let json = r#"{
691:             "version": 2,
692:             "manga": [
693:                 {
694:                     "id": "title-fields",
695:                     "title": "Title Fields",
696:                     "sort": "natural",
697:                     "revision": 7,
698:                     "chapters": [
699:                         {
700:                             "id": "ch-id-1",
701:                             "name": "Chapter 1",
702:                             "pages": [
703:                                 { "name": "01.jpg", "path": "ch1/01.jpg", "width": 0, "height": 0, "dimensionsPending": true }
704:                             ],
705:                             "position": {
706:                                 "page": 0,
707:                                 "fraction": 0.5,
708:                                 "path": "ch1/01.jpg",
709:                                 "mangaBookmark": true,
710:                                 "timestamp": 1731234567890.0
711:                             },
712:                             "bookmarks": [
713:                                 {
714:                                     "page": 0,
715:                                     "fraction": 0.0,
716:                                     "path": "ch1/01.jpg",
717:                                     "mangaBookmark": true,
718:                                     "timestamp": 1731234567890.0
719:                                 },
720:                                 {
721:                                     "page": 0,
722:                                     "fraction": 0.5,
723:                                     "path": "ch1/01.jpg",
724:                                     "mangaBookmark": false,
725:                                     "timestamp": 1731234567891.0
726:                                 }
727:                             ]
728:                         }
729:                     ]
730:                 }
731:             ],
732:             "settings": {
733:                 "theme": "dark",
734:                 "fontSizeTitle": 24,
735:                 "fontSizeUi": 18,
736:                 "fontSizeButton": 16,
737:                 "confirmDelete": true,
738:                 "shelfSort": "progress"
739:             }
740:         }"#;
741: 
742:         let payload = validate_backup_json(json).expect("Validation should pass");
743:         assert_eq!(payload.manga[0].sort.as_deref(), Some("natural"));
744:         assert_eq!(payload.manga[0].revision, Some(7));
745:         assert_eq!(payload.manga[0].chapters[0].id.as_deref(), Some("ch-id-1"));
746:         assert_eq!(
747:             payload.manga[0].chapters[0].pages[0].dimensions_pending,
748:             Some(true)
749:         );
750:         let bms = payload.manga[0].chapters[0].bookmarks.as_ref().unwrap();
751:         assert_eq!(
752:             bms.len(),
753:             2,
754:             "Both title bookmark and page bookmark on page 0 must be preserved"
755:         );
756:         let pos = payload.manga[0].chapters[0].position.as_ref().unwrap();
757:         assert_eq!(pos.manga_bookmark, Some(true));
758:         assert_eq!(pos.timestamp, Some(1731234567890.0));
759: 
760:         let norm = normalize_settings(&payload.settings);
761:         assert_eq!(norm["fontSizeTitle"], 24);
762:         assert_eq!(norm["fontSizeUi"], 18);
763:         assert_eq!(norm["fontSizeButton"], 16);
764:         assert_eq!(norm["confirmDelete"], true);
765:         assert_eq!(norm["shelfSort"], "progress");
766:     }
767: }
